### [1] [Interpretable EEG Microstate Discovery via Variational Deep Embedding: A Systematic Architecture Search with Multi-Quadrant Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10947)
*Saheed Faremi,Andrea Visentin,Luca Longo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conv-VaDE模型用于EEG微状态分析，通过架构搜索发现深度L=4是关键，最佳GEV达0.730，证明合理的架构设计比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的EEG微状态分析方法（如Modified K-Means）在电极空间直接操作，采用硬分配策略，缺乏潜在表示学习、生成解码器和将潜在配置解码为可验证头皮拓扑图的机制，限制了模型的透明度和可解释性。

Method: 提出卷积变分深度嵌入（Conv-VaDE）模型，在共享潜在空间中联合学习拓扑重建和概率软聚类；引入极性不变性方案；进行四维网格搜索（聚类数K=3-20、潜在维度、网络深度、通道宽度）系统揭示架构设计选择对EEG微状态表示的影响。

Result: 在LEMON静息态闭眼EEG数据集上评估，架构搜索显示深度L=4在所有18个最佳配置中一致出现，在K=4时获得最佳GEV=0.730和轮廓系数0.229；中等深度、紧凑通道宽度和小潜在维度的网络在整个K范围内占主导地位。

Conclusion: 有原则的架构搜索而非模型规模，是通过变分深度嵌入实现可解释和稳定EEG微状态发现的关键。

Abstract: EEG microstate analysis segments continuous brain electrical activity into brief, quasi-stable topographic configurations that reflect discrete functional brain states. Conventional approaches such as Modified K-Means operate directly in electrode space with hard assignment, offering no learned latent representation, no generative decoder, and no mechanism to decode latent configurations into verifiable scalp topographies, limiting both model transparency and interpretability. To address this, we present a Convolutional Variational Deep Embedding (Conv-VaDE) model that jointly learns topographic reconstruction and probabilistic soft clustering in a shared latent space. Conv-VaDE enables generative decoding of cluster prototypes into verifiable scalp topographies, replacing opaque hard partitioning with probabilistic soft assignment. A polarity invariance scheme and a four-dimensional grid search over cluster count (K from 3 to 20), latent dimensionality, network depth, and channel width are conducted to systematically reveal how each architectural design choice shapes the quality, stability, and interpretability of learned EEG microstate representations. The model is evaluated on the LEMON resting-state eyes-closed EEG dataset with ten participants using topographic template formation, clustering stability, and global explained variance (GEV). The architecture search reveals that depth L = 4 appears consistently across all 18 best-performing configurations, yielding a best-case GEV of 0.730 and a silhouette of 0.229 at K = 4 across the model sweeps, where moderately deep networks with compact channel widths and small latent dimensionality dominate across the full K range. These results establish that principled architecture search, rather than model scale, is the key to interpretable and stable EEG microstate discovery via variational deep embedding.

</details>


### [2] [QuIDE: Mastering the Quantized Intelligence Trade-off via Active Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10959)
*Xiantao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了QuIDE指标，将量化神经网络的压缩-精度-延迟权衡统一为单一分数I=(C×P)/log₂(T+1)，并在多种模型上验证了不同位宽的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏统一指标评估量化神经网络的效率，难以在压缩率、精度和延迟之间进行客观权衡和比较。

Method: 提出QuIDE评估框架，核心为智能指数I=(C×P)/log₂(T+1)，将压缩-精度-延迟三要素坍缩为单一分数；并提出精度门控变体I'以识别不可行配置。在SimpleCNN(MNIST/CIFAR)、ResNet-18(ImageNet-1K)和Llama-3-8B六种设置下进行实验验证。

Result: 发现任务依赖的帕累托膝盖点：4位量化对MNIST和大语言模型最优，8位是复杂CNN任务(ResNet-18/ImageNet)的最佳点(4位PTQ会导致精度灾难性下降)；精度门控变体I'能正确标记原始I会奖励的不可行配置。

Conclusion: QuIDE提供了可复现的评估协议和即用型混合精度搜索适应度函数，为量化神经网络效率评估建立了统一标准。

Abstract: There is currently no unified metric for evaluating the efficiency of quantized neural networks. We propose QuIDE, built around the Intelligence Index I = (C x P)/log_2(T+1), which collapses the compression-accuracy-latency trade-off into a single score. Experiments across six settings -- SimpleCNN (MNIST, CIFAR), ResNet-18 (ImageNet-1K), and Llama-3-8B -- show a task-dependent Pareto Knee. 4-bit quantization is optimal for MNIST and large LLMs, while 8-bit is the sweet spot for complex CNN tasks (ResNet-18 on ImageNet), where 4-bit PTQ collapses accuracy catastrophically. The accuracy-gated variant I' correctly flags these non-viable configurations that the raw I would reward. QuIDE provides a reproducible evaluation protocol and a ready-to-use fitness function for mixed-precision search.

</details>


### [3] [Steering Without Breaking: Mechanistically Informed Interventions for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10971)
*Hanhan Zhou,Shamik Roy,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离散扩散语言模型（DLM）的受控生成问题，揭示了均匀干预策略的不足，提出了一种自适应调度方法，在属性形成的关键步骤集中干预，显著提升了多属性控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散语言模型（DLM）受控生成方法沿用了自回归模型的均匀干预策略，即在所有去噪步骤上施加相同强度的干预。作者发现这种均匀调度会降低生成质量，且在联合控制多个属性时损害会加剧。因此，需要深入诊断失败原因并设计更有效的干预策略。

Method: 作者首先在四个DLM（124M-8B参数）上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），发现不同属性的"承诺"时间存在显著差异：主题属性在前2%的去噪步骤内即确定，而情感属性则在约20%的过程中逐渐形成。基于此发现，提出了自适应调度器（Adaptive Scheduler），该调度器将干预集中在属性活跃形成的步骤，避免在属性已固化或尚未出现的步骤上浪费干预能力，并给出了成本-控制权衡的闭式理论表征。

Result: 在四个DLM模型和七个控制任务上的实验表明：所提方法在保持生成质量的同时实现了精确控制。尤其在具有挑战性的三属性同时控制任务中，该方法达到高达93%的引导强度，相比最强基线提升高达15个百分点，且未出现均匀干预典型的质量退化问题。

Conclusion: 不同属性在离散扩散模型的去噪过程中遵循不同的时间承诺模式，均匀干预策略因忽视这一差异而导致效果下降。自适应调度策略通过在属性活跃形成阶段集中干预，在多属性联合控制任务中显著优于传统方法，证明了属性特异性的调度对于DLM受控生成的重要性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising all positions in parallel, offering an alternative to autoregressive models. Controlled generation methods for DLMs, imported from autoregressive models, apply uniform intervention at every denoising steps. We show this uniform schedule degrades quality, and the damage compounds when multiple attributes are steered jointly. To diagnose the failure, we train sparse autoencoders on four DLMs (124M-8B parameters) and find that different attributes commit on distinct schedules, varying in timing, sharpness, and magnitude. For instance, topic commits within the first 2\% of denoising, whereas sentiment emerges gradually over 20\% of the process. Consequently, uniform intervention wastes steering capacity on steps where the target attribute has already solidified or has yet to emerge. We propose a novel adaptive scheduler that concentrates interventions on the steps where an attribute is actively forming and leaves the rest of generation untouched. The cost-control trade-off admits a closed-form characterization: the advantage of adaptive over uniform scheduling is governed by a single dispersion statistic of the commitment distribution. Across four DLMs and seven steering tasks, our method achieves precise control without the degradation typical of uniform interventions. Especially on challenging simultaneous three-attribute control, it reaches up to 93\% steering strength, beating the strongest baseline by up to 15\% points while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [4] [SkillGen: Verified Inference-Time Agent Skill Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10999)
*Yuchen Ma,Yue Huang,Han Bao,Haomin Zhuang,Swadheen Shukla,Michel Galley,Xiangliang Zhang,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SkillGen框架，通过对比归纳从成功和失败的轨迹中自动生成可审计的技能，从而在不重新训练的情况下提升大语言模型（LLM）代理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高质量技能主要通过人工编写，成本高昂且效率低下。为了在不重新训练模型的前提下提升LLM代理能力，需要一种能够自动生成可复用、可控且可审计技能的方法。

Method: 提出SkillGen多智能体框架。该方法利用对比归纳（contrastive induction），同时分析成功与失败的轨迹，以识别可复用的成功模式和常见失败模式。框架生成候选技能并进行迭代优化。关键创新在于将技能建模为“干预”，通过对比有无技能在同一实例上的表现，量化技能带来的修复和回归影响。

Result: 在广泛的智能体和数据集测试中，SkillGen持续提升了留出集的性能表现，优于现有的技能生成基线方法，且生成的技能具有跨模型迁移能力。

Conclusion: SkillGen证明了自动生成高质量、可迁移技能的可行性，为提升LLM代理能力提供了一种高效、可审计且无需重新训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Skills are a promising way to improve LLM agent capabilities without retraining, while keeping the added procedure reusable and controllable. However, high-quality skills are still largely written by hand. We introduce SkillGen, a multi-agent framework that synthesizes a single auditable skill from trajectories generated by a base agent. The output is a human-readable artifact that can be inspected before use. Rather than merely summarizing trajectories, SkillGen leverages contrastive induction over both successful and failed trajectories to identify reusable success patterns, recurring failure modes, and behaviors that appear in nearby successes but are missing from failures. SkillGen then generates candidate skills and iteratively refines the skill. A key novelty in SkillGen is that we model agent skills as interventions to empirically verify the net effect of skills on the overall performance. Specifically, we compare outcomes on the same instances with and without the skill, so that we account for both repairs (cases where the skill fixes a baseline failure) and regressions (cases where the skill breaks a baseline success). Across a broad range of agents and datasets, SkillGen consistently improves held-out performance, outperforms existing skill-generation baselines, and produces skills that transfer across models.

</details>


### [5] [Improving the Performance and Learning Stability of Parallelizable RNNs Designed for Ultra-Low Power Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11855)
*Julien Brandoit,Arthur Fyon,Damien Ernst,Guillaume Drion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出累积记忆循环单元(CMRU)，通过累积更新公式解决BMRU的梯度阻断问题，在保持量化状态和持久记忆等超低功耗模拟实现优势的同时，显著提升了复杂序列任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双稳态记忆循环单元(BMRU)虽然能实现超低功耗的软硬件协同设计，但在复杂序列任务上性能落后于可并行化RNN。作者发现状态更新过程中的梯度阻断是关键限制因素，需要一种既能恢复梯度流又能保持持久记忆特性的解决方案。

Method: 识别出BMRU中状态更新时的梯度阻断问题，提出累积更新公式来恢复梯度流动，这创建了跨越时间的跳跃连接。该方法导出了累积记忆循环单元(CMRU)及其松弛变体αCMRU，在保持量化状态、持久记忆和抗噪声动态特性的同时改善了训练性能。

Result: 累积公式显著改善了收敛稳定性并降低了对初始化的敏感性。CMRU和αCMRU在小模型规模下于多种基准测试中匹配或优于线性循环单元(LRU)和最小门控循环单元(minGRU)，在需要离散长程保留的任务上具有特别优势，同时CMRU仍保持量化状态、持久记忆和抗噪声动态特性。

Conclusion: CMRU成功地在保持模拟实现所需的硬件友好特性(量化状态、持久记忆、抗噪声)的同时，通过累积更新机制解决了梯度阻断问题，实现了性能与功耗的有效平衡，为超低功耗序列学习提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Sequence learning is dominated by Transformers and parallelizable recurrent neural networks (RNNs) such as state-space models, yet learning long-term dependencies remains challenging, and state-of-the-art designs trade power consumption for performance. The Bistable Memory Recurrent Unit (BMRU) was introduced to enable hardware-software co-design of ultra-low power RNNs: quantized states with hysteresis provide persistent memory while mapping directly to analog primitives. However, BMRU performance lags behind parallelizable RNNs on complex sequential tasks. In this paper, we identify gradient blocking during state updates as a key limitation and propose a cumulative update formulation that restores gradient flow while preserving persistent memory, creating skip-connections through time. This leads to the Cumulative Memory Recurrent Unit (CMRU) and its relaxed variant, the $α$CMRU. Experiments show that the cumulative formulation dramatically improves convergence stability and reduces initialization sensitivity. The CMRU and $α$CMRU match or outperform Linear Recurrent Units (LRUs) and minimal Gated Recurrent Units (minGRUs) across diverse benchmarks at small model sizes, with particular advantages on tasks requiring discrete long-range retention, while the CMRU retains quantized states, persistent memory, and noise-resilient dynamics essential for analog implementation.

</details>


### [6] [More Than Meets the Eye: A Semantics-Aware Traffic Augmentation Framework for Generalizable Website Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11402)
*Youquan Xian,Xueying Zeng,Lingjia Meng,Lei Cui,Runhan Song,Wei Wang,Zhengquan Ding,Peng Liu,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SATA框架，通过应用层语义增强和跨层特征对齐解决网站指纹识别在地理和时间偏移下的泛化问题，在开放世界场景中显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的网站指纹识别方法虽然在封闭世界数据集上表现优异，但在真实环境中，特别是面临地理和时间偏移时，泛化能力严重不足。这一局限性主要源于两个挑战的耦合效应：应用层资源组成的可变性以及跨层封装导致的可观测特征不稳定性，这两者共同导致了底层应用语义与可观测流量特征之间的系统性偏移。

Method: 提出SATA（Semantics-Aware Traffic Augmentation）框架，包含两个核心组件：1）基于协议规则的应用层语义增强，扩展每个流内的资源组成模式和协议约束下的帧序列模式；2）通过知识蒸馏引入跨层特征对齐机制，将帧序列与包长度序列特征对齐，实现增强语义与可观测序列之间的跨层特征对齐。

Result: 在多样化和复杂场景下的广泛实验表明，SATA成功生成了训练集中不存在但测试集中真实存在的流量模式，显著提升了主流模型性能。特别是在开放世界设置中，SATA将准确率（ACC）提升了90.81%，AUROC提升了48.37%。

Conclusion: SATA框架有效解决了网站指纹识别在真实世界环境中的泛化难题，通过语义增强和跨层特征对齐机制，成功缓解了应用层语义与可观测特征之间的系统性偏移问题，为提升深度学习流量分析方法在实际部署中的鲁棒性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning-based website fingerprinting has emerged as an effective technique for inferring the websites users visit. Although existing methods achieve strong performance on closed-world datasets, they often fail to generalize to real-world environments, especially under geographic and temporal shifts. This limitation fundamentally stems from the coupled effects of two key challenges: application-layer resource composition variability and observable feature instability induced by cross-layer encapsulation. Intertwined, these factors induce systematic shifts between underlying application semantics and observable traffic features. To address the above challenges, we propose SATA , a semantics-aware traffic augmentation framework. Specifically, SATA first performs application-layer semantic augmentation based on protocol rules, expanding the resource composition patterns within each flow and frame sequence patterns under protocol constraints. Based on these augmented frame sequences, we further introduce a cross-layer feature alignment mechanism via knowledge distillation. It aligns frame sequence with packet-length sequence features, enabling cross-layer feature alignment between enhanced semantics and observable sequences. Extensive experiments show that SATA successfully generates traffic patterns that are absent from the training set but genuinely exist in the test set, and significantly improves the performance of mainstream models across diverse and complex scenarios. In particular, in open-world settings, SATA improves ACC by 90.81% and AUROC by 48.37%. The source code of the prototype system is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SATA-B6C2/.

</details>


### [7] [Rotation-Preserving Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10973)
*Hangzhan Jin,Tianwei Ni,Lu Li,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出旋转保留监督微调（RPSFT），通过约束预训练权重矩阵在主要奇异子空间上的投影旋转，以低成本方式有效缓解了标准SFT导致的分布外（OOD）泛化能力下降问题，实现了域内性能与OOD泛化的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）虽能提升域内性能，但常导致分布外（OOD）泛化能力下降。现有研究表明这与预训练权重矩阵主导奇异子空间的变化有关，但直接利用Hessian或Fisher信息识别损失敏感方向在大语言模型（LLM）规模下计算成本过高。

Method: 提出旋转保留监督微调（RPSFT）。该方法将预训练奇异子空间中的投影旋转作为Fisher敏感方向的高效代理，通过惩罚每个预训练权重矩阵在投影前k个奇异向量块上的变化，在不影响任务适应的前提下限制不必要的旋转。

Result: 在不同模型家族和规模的数学推理数据实验中，RPSFT相比标准SFT及强基线方法，改善了域内与OOD性能的权衡，更好地保留了预训练表征，并为下游强化学习微调提供了更强的初始化。

Conclusion: RPSFT是一种高效且有效的方法，通过保留预训练奇异子空间的投影旋转，在保持微调任务性能的同时显著缓解了OOD泛化退化问题。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves in-domain performance but can degrade out-of-domain (OOD) generalization. Prior work suggests that this degradation is related to changes in dominant singular subspaces of pretrained weight matrices. However, directly identifying loss-sensitive directions with Hessian or Fisher information is computationally expensive at LLM scale. In this work, we propose preserving projected rotations in pretrained singular subspaces as an efficient proxy for Fisher-sensitive directions, which we call Rotation-Preserving Supervised Fine-Tuning (RPSFT). RPSFT penalizes changes in the projected top-$k$ singular-vector block of each pretrained weight matrix, limiting unnecessary rotation while preserving task adaptation. Across model families and sizes trained on math reasoning data, RPSFT improves the in-domain/OOD trade-off over standard SFT and strong SFT baselines, better preserves pretrained representations, and provides stronger initializations for downstream RL fine-tuning. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/jinhangzhan/RPSFT.git}{https://github.com/jinhangzhan/RPSFT}.

</details>


### [8] [GRAFT-ATHENA: Self-Improving Agentic Teams for Autonomous Discovery and Evolutionary Numerical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11117)
*Juan Diego Toscano,Zhaojie Chai,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GRAFT-ATHENA自改进智能体框架，通过GRAFT方法将组合决策空间投影到分解概率树中，实现跨领域的方法论经验积累，在物理信息机器学习基准测试中超越人类和现有基线，并能自主发现新的数值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架将每个科学问题孤立处理，缺乏跨领域共享的方法论经验积累底座。科学发现可建模为将物理问题映射到数值解的概率决策序列，每个方法是方法论的组合，存在多种可行组合及结构性依赖，但现有系统无法有效学习和积累这些决策经验。

Method: 提出GRAFT-ATHENA自改进智能体框架，核心包括：1）GRAFF（图归约到自适应分解树）方法将组合决策空间投影到分解概率树，每个方法对应单一路径；2）参数足迹从指数级降至线性级；3）借鉴经典贝叶斯网络思想，分解是策略的I-map；4）路径作为度量空间中的唯一指纹嵌入，相似问题可从历史相似问题中学习；5）系统能自主扩展行动空间并提出正则化约束。

Result: 在标准物理信息机器学习（PIML）基准测试中，超越人类和现有智能体基线；在生产求解器上解决了复杂工程问题，包括从1968年报告重建阿波罗指令舱上马赫数10流动，以及恢复剪切稀化血液细胞流变学；系统自主提出了不适定逆问题的正则化约束，并发现了新的数值方法（具有指数收敛性的谱PINN）。

Conclusion: 该工作为自主实验室奠定了基础，使其能够随着解决的每个问题而变得更强大，实现了方法论经验跨领域的积累与迁移，展示了AI驱动的科学发现从孤立任务处理向持续学习系统的转变。

Abstract: Scientific discovery can be modeled as a sequence of probabilistic decisions that map physical problems to numerical solutions. Recent agentic AI systems automate individual scientific tasks by orchestrating LLM-driven planners, solvers, and evaluators. Each method is a combination of methodological actions, with many viable combinations for any given problem and structural dependencies between choices. However, existing frameworks treat each problem in isolation, with no shared substrate to accumulate methodological experience across domains. Here we show that GRAFT-ATHENA, a self-improving agentic framework, learns from past problems and autonomously expands its own action space across diverse domains. GRAFT (Graph Reduction to Adaptive Factored Trees) projects combinatorial decision spaces into factored probabilistic trees in which each method is a single path, taking the parameter footprint from exponential to linear. In the lineage of classical Bayesian networks, the factorization is an $I$-map of the policy, and the resulting paths embed as unique fingerprints in a metric space whose closeness lets each new problem learn from similar past ones. On canonical physics-informed machine learning (PIML) benchmarks, GRAFT-ATHENA improves over human and prior agentic baselines, and on production solvers, it tackles complex engineering problems such as reconstructing Mach-10 flow over the Apollo Command Module from a 1968 report and recovering shear-thinning blood-cell rheology. Notably, the system grows its own knowledge substrate, autonomously proposing regularization constraints for ill-posed inverse problems and discovering new numerical methods such as a spectral PINN with exponential convergence. These results provide a foundation for autonomous laboratories that grow more capable with every problem they solve.

</details>


### [9] [Search Your Block Floating Point Scales!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12464)
*Tanmaey Gupta,Hayden Prairie,Xiaoxia Wu,Reyna Abhyankar,Qingyang Wu,Austin Silveria,Pragaash Ponnusamy,Jue Wang,Ben Athiwaratkun,Leon Song,Tri Dao,Daniel Y. Fu,Chris De Sa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleSearch策略，通过细粒度搜索优化微缩放块浮点（BFP）格式的缩放因子选择，降低量化误差；并基于此提出ScaleSearchAttention算法，在保持性能的同时显著提升量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BFP算法使用基于块最大幅度的固定缩放因子，这在量化误差方面可能并非最优选择。随着GPU加速器对微缩放BFP格式的一流支持，有必要探索更优的缩放因子选择策略以减少量化误差。

Method: 提出ScaleSearch策略，利用微缩放格式中的尾数位进行细粒度搜索，针对给定分布最小化量化误差。该方法可与现有量化方法（如训练后量化PTQ和低精度注意力）集成。同时提出ScaleSearchAttention算法，结合ScaleSearch和改进的现有技术，确保因果语言建模的近零性能损失。

Result: ScaleSearch将NVFP4的量化误差降低27%，将Qwen3-8B在MATH500上的语言模型PTQ性能提升高达15分；ScaleSearchAttention将Llama 3.1 70B在Wikitext-2上的困惑度提升高达0.77点。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在提供量化精度改进的同时，能够紧密匹配基线性能，为生成模型的低精度推理提供了一种有效的优化方案。

Abstract: Quantization has emerged as a standard technique for accelerating inference for generative models by enabling faster low-precision computations and reduced memory transfers. Recently, GPU accelerators have added first-class support for microscaling Block Floating Point (BFP) formats. Standard BFP algorithms use a fixed scale based on the maximum magnitude of the block. We observe that this scale choice can be suboptimal with respect to quantization errors. In this work, we propose ScaleSearch, an alternative strategy for selecting these scale factors: using a fine-grained search leveraging the mantissa bits in microscaling formats to minimize the quantization error for the given distribution. ScaleSearch can be integrated with existing quantization methods such as Post Training Quantization and low-precision attention, and is shown to improve their performance. Additionally, we introduce ScaleSearchAttention, an accelerated NVFP4-based attention algorithm, which uses ScaleSearch and adapted prior techniques to ensure near-0 performance loss for causal language modeling. Experiments show that ScaleSearch reduces quantization error by 27% for NVFP4 and improves language model PTQ by up to 15 points for MATH500 (Qwen3-8B), while ScaleSearchAttention improves Wikitext-2 PPL by upto 0.77 points for Llama 3.1 70B. The proposed methods closely match baseline performance while providing quantization accuracy improvements.

</details>


### [10] [Vertex-Softmax: Tight Transformer Verification via Exact Softmax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10974)
*Navid Rezazadeh,Arash Gholami Davoodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Vertex-Softmax原语，证明了softmax区间约束问题的精确最优解位于约束框顶点，实现了对数线性复杂度；集成到CROWN验证器中显著提升了注意力模型的认证鲁棒性验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer注意力机制认证验证方法在松弛softmax函数时独立于下游目标，产生了不必要的松弛度，导致验证结果不够紧致。需要一种能够从分数区间获得最紧致且可靠边界的方法。

Method: 证明了分数盒问题的精确最优解在约束框顶点处达到，建立了阈值结构定理表明最优解位于按目标系数排序后的线性多个候选中，提出了Vertex-Softmax原语，具有序列长度上的对数线性复杂度，并证明了该方法是从分数区间获得的最紧致可靠边界。

Result: 将Vertex-Softmax集成到基于CROWN的验证器中，在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10注意力模型上显著提升了认证率，大幅收紧了下界，且以极低的计算成本持续匹配或超越alpha-CROWN和分支定界基线。

Conclusion: Vertex-Softmax是从分数区间获得的最紧致可靠边界，进一步改进需要利用额外的结构信息（如分数相关性、分数-值耦合）。该方法为Transformer注意力机制的认证验证提供了高效且最优的解决方案。

Abstract: Certified verification of transformer attention requires bounding the softmax function over interval constraints on the pre-softmax scores. Existing verifiers relax softmax ndependently of the downstream objective, leaving avoidable slack. We prove that the exact optimum of this score-box problem is attained at a vertex of the constraint box, and establish a threshold structure theorem showing that, after sorting the objective coefficients, the optimum lies among only linearly many candidates, yielding the Vertex-Softmax primitive with log-linear complexity in the sequence length. We further prove a formal optimality result showing that Vertex-Softmax is the tightest sound bound obtainable from score intervals alone, characterizing precisely what additional structure (score correlations, score-value coupling) is needed for further improvement. Integrated into a CROWN Convex Relaxation based Optimization for Worst-case Neurons)-style verifier with a formal soundness guarantee, Vertex-Softmax significantly improves certified rates and substantially tightens lower bounds across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 attention models, while consistently matching or outperforming alpha-CROWN and branch-and-bound baselines at a fraction of their cost.

</details>


### [11] [Hierarchical Multi-Scale Graph Neural Networks: Scalable Heterophilous Learning with Oversmoothing and Oversquashing Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10975)
*Md Sazzad Hossen,Avimanyu Sahoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HMH框架，通过层次化多视角Haar小波变换解决异配图神经网络中的节点度偏置和过度平滑问题，在节点和图分类任务上分别提升3%和7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中普遍存在异配图（相邻节点标签不同），但现有谱图神经网络方法存在两大关键问题：(1) 大度节点主导聚合过程，导致度偏置；(2) 多项式滤波器次优引入近似误差，混合远距离信号，导致过度平滑。这些缺陷严重限制了异配图的分类性能。

Method: 提出层次化多视角Haar(HMH)框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)异配感知编码器学习特征-结构感知的符号化亲和度；(2)基于嵌入引导构建软图层次结构，每层构建稀疏、正交、局部感知的Haar基，在频域应用可学习谱滤波器；(3)跳跃连接反池化层整合所有层级输出至原图，有效避免大度节点主导和长程信号瓶颈。方法具有近线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在节点分类任务上达到最高3%的性能提升，在图分类数据集上提升达7个百分点，同时保持线性可扩展性，在多个基准数据集上超越现有最优谱方法基线。

Conclusion: HMH框架通过层次化Haar小波基和软图层次结构，成功解决了异配图学习中的度偏置、过度平滑和信号挤压问题，为异配图神经网络提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Graphs with heterophily, where adjacent nodes carry different labels, are prevalent in real-world applications, from social networks to molecular interactions. However, existing spectral Graph Neural Network (GNN) approaches tailored for heterophilous graph classification suffer from hub-dominated (node with large degree) aggregation and oversmoothing, as their suboptimal polynomial filters introduce approximation errors and blend distant signals. To address the degree-biased aggregation and suboptimal polynomial filtering, we introduce a Hierarchical Multi-view HAAR (HMH), a novel spectral graph-learning framework that scales in near-linear time . HMH first learns feature- and structure-aware signed affinities via a heterophily-aware encoder, then constructs a soft graph hierarchy guided by these embeddings. At each hierarchical level, HMH constructs a sparse, orthonormal, and locality-aware Haar basis to apply learnable spectral filters in the frequency domain. Finally, skip-connection unpooling layers combine outputs from all hierarchical levels back into the original graph, effectively preventing hub domination and long-range signal bottleneck (over-squashing). Experimentation shows that HMH outperforms state-of-the-art spectral baselines, achieving up to a 3% improvement on node classification and 7% points on graph classification datasets, all while maintaining linear scalability.

</details>


### [12] [LEAP: Unlocking dLLM Parallelism via Lookahead Early-Convergence Token Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10980)
*Haohui Zhang,Zhiye Wang,Xiaoying Gan,Xinbing Wang,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAP，一种无需训练、即插即用的方法，通过检测去噪过程中早期收敛的token实现提前解码，将平均去噪步骤减少约30%，在保持模型精度的同时显著提升了扩散语言模型的并行解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的并行解码能力依赖于高置信度阈值下的条件独立性假设，但严格的置信度阈值限制了并行性的可扩展性。通过token级统计分析发现，大量token在去噪早期已收敛到正确预测但未达到标准置信度阈值，表明当前基于置信度的标准过于保守。

Method: 提出LEAP（Lookahead Early-Convergence Token Detection for Accelerated Parallel Decoding）方法，这是一种无需训练、即插即用的方法。该方法利用未来上下文过滤和多序列叠加来检测早期收敛的token，通过验证早期收敛与正确性之间的对齐关系，实现对早期收敛token的可靠提前解码。

Result: 跨领域基准测试表明LEAP显著降低了推理延迟和解码步骤。与基于置信度的解码相比，平均去噪步骤减少约30%。在GSM8K数据集上，LEAP与dParallel结合可将解码加速至每步7.2个token，同时保持模型精度。

Conclusion: LEAP有效打破了对高置信度先验的依赖，为并行解码提供了新的范式，使扩散语言模型能够在更宽松的收敛条件下实现高效的并行处理。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (dLLMs) have garnered significant attention for their potential in highly parallel processing. The parallel capabilities of existing dLLMs stem from the assumption of conditional independence at high confidence levels, which ensures negligible discrepancy between the marginal and joint distributions. However, the stringent confidence thresholds required to preserve accuracy severely constrain the scalability of parallelism. Through systematic token-level statistical analysis, we reveal that a substantial proportion of tokens converge to their correct predictions early in the denoising process yet fail to reach standard confidence thresholds, confirming that current confidence-based criteria are overly conservative. In response, we introduce LEAP (Lookahead Early-Convergence Token Detection for Accelerated Parallel Decoding). LEAP is a training-free, plug-and-play method that leverages future context filtering and multi-sequence superposition to detect early-converging tokens. By validating the alignment between early convergence and correctness, we enable reliable early decoding of these tokens. Benchmarking across diverse domains demonstrates that LEAP significantly lowers inference latency and decoding steps. Compared to confidence-based decoding, the average number of denoising steps is reduced by about 30%. On the GSM8K dataset, combining LEAP with dParallel accelerates decoding to 7.2 tokens per step while preserving model precision. LEAP effectively breaks the reliance on high-confidence priors, offering a novel paradigm for parallel decoding.

</details>


### [13] [Shaping Zero-Shot Coordination via State Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11688)
*Mingu Kang,Sunwoo Lee,Yonghyeon Jo,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了状态阻塞协调（SBC）框架，通过生成虚拟环境让智能体体验多样化的次优搭档策略，无需直接修改环境即可显著提升零样本协调能力及对人类搭档的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本协调（ZSC）方法主要依赖在训练中增加搭档多样性，但往往难以可靠地泛化至未见过的搭档，无法满足现实多智能体系统与人机协作的需求。

Method: 提出状态阻塞协调（SBC）框架，通过状态阻塞技术生成一系列虚拟环境，在不直接修改环境的前提下诱导多样化的交互场景，使智能体在训练中经历广泛的次优搭档策略。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，SBC在零样本协调任务上表现优异，展现出对未见搭档（尤其是人类搭档）的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SBC为解决零样本协调中的泛化难题提供了一种简单高效的新思路，证明了通过状态阻塞诱导多样交互场景的有效性，具有重要的理论价值与应用前景。

Abstract: Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) aims to enable agents to cooperate with independently trained partners without prior interaction, a key requirement for real-world multi-agent systems and human-AI collaboration. Existing approaches have largely emphasized increasing partner diversity during training, yet such strategies often fall short of achieving reliable generalization to unseen partners. We introduce State-Blocked Coordination (SBC), a simple yet effective framework that improves ZSC by inducing diverse interaction scenarios without direct environment modification. Specifically, SBC generates a family of virtual environments through state blocking, allowing agents to experience a wide range of suboptimal partner policies. Across multiple benchmarks, SBC demonstrates superior performance in zero-shot coordination, including strong generalization to human partners.

</details>


### [14] [$ξ$-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization via Ratio Reward Margin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10981)
*Zhengyuan Fan,Zhonghua Wu,Yuxuan Du,Qun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ξ-DPO方法，通过比率奖励边际解决SimPO中超参数β和γ联合调优困难的问题，实现了更易解释和确定的边际设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SimPO作为一种高效的无参考模型偏好优化方法，其超参数β和γ的联合调优仍是一个核心挑战。作者发现这一困难源于SimPO中的边际公式在不同奖励差距结构的数据集上难以解释，β隐式控制样本过滤，而γ的效果依赖于数据集的奖励差距结构。

Method: 提出ξ-DPO方法：首先通过等价变换重新表述偏好目标，将优化目标从最大化奖励差距似然转变为最小化奖励差距与最优边际之间的距离；然后将奖励重新定义为选中与拒绝响应之间的比率形式，有效抵消β的影响，产生有界且可解释的边际，称为比率奖励边际ξ。

Result: 与SimPO中的边际γ不同，ξ明确表示选中与拒绝响应之间的期望相对分离，可以从初始奖励差距分布中确定，避免了重复的试错调优。该方法消除了β的影响，提供了有界且可解释的边际。

Conclusion: ξ-DPO通过比率奖励边际的引入，成功解决了SimPO中超参数调优困难的问题，实现了边际的可解释性和可确定性，为偏好优化提供了更加鲁棒和实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reference-free preference optimization has emerged as an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning from human feedback, with Simple Preference Optimization(SimPO) demonstrating strong performance by eliminating the explicit reference model through a simple objective. However, the joint tuning of the hyperparameters $β$ and $γ$ in SimPO remains a central challenge. We argue that this difficulty arises because the margin formulation in SimPO is not easily interpretable across datasets with different reward gap structures. To better understand this issue, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of SimPO and find that $β$ implicitly controls sample filtering, while the effect of $γ$ depends on the reward gap structure of the dataset. Motivated by these observations, we propose $ξ$-DPO: Direct preference optimization via ratio reward margin. We first reformulate the preference objective through an equivalent transformation, changing the optimization target from maximizing the likelihood of reward gaps to minimizing the distance between reward gaps and optimal margins. Then, we redefine the reward in a ratio form between the chosen and rejected, which effectively cancels the effect of $β$ and yields a bounded and interpretable margin. This margin is called the ratio reward margin and is denoted by $ξ$. Unlike the margin $γ$ in SimPO, $ξ$ explicitly represents the desired relative separation between chosen and rejected responses and can be determined from the initial reward gap distribution, avoiding repeated trial-and-error tuning. ....

</details>


### [15] [Adaptive TD-Lambda for Cooperative Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11880)
*Yue Deng,Zirui Wang,Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体强化学习（MARL）中策略分布难以统计计算的问题，提出了一种基于参数化无似然密度比估计器和双经验回放池的自适应TD(λ)方法（ATD(λ)），并在QMIX和MAPPO算法上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MARL中，TD(λ)通过结合蒙特卡洛模拟和动态规划来平衡值估计的偏差与方差。现有单智能体强化学习方法将自适应λ值与策略分布关联，但在MARL场景下，由于巨大的联合动作空间和有限的转移数据，策略分布难以通过统计方法计算，这阻碍了自适应λ值的应用。

Method: 为了解决MARL中策略分布计算难题，本文提出使用一个参数化无似然密度比估计器，配合两个不同大小的经验回放池来替代直接的统计计算。这两个回放池分别存储代表过去策略和当前策略数据分布的历史轨迹。基于该估计器，研究者根据当前策略平稳分布下的似然度为状态-动作对分配自适应TD(λ)值（ATD(λ)）。

Result: 研究者将所提方法应用于基于价值的算法QMIX和基于Actor-Critic的算法MAPPO，在SMAC基准和Google Research Football Academy场景上进行了测试。实验结果表明，与使用静态λ值的基线方法相比，该方法具有一致且具有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ATD(λ)方法成功地解决了MARL中策略分布难以计算的问题，通过双回放池和密度比估计器实现了λ值的自适应调整，为MARL算法提供了一种有效提升性能的通用技术手段。

Abstract: TD($λ$) in value-based MARL algorithms or the Temporal Difference critic learning in Actor-Critic-based (AC-based) algorithms synergistically integrate elements from Monte-Carlo simulation and Q function bootstrapping via dynamic programming, which effectively addresses the inherent bias-variance trade-off in value estimation. Based on that, some recent works link the adaptive $λ$ value to the policy distribution in the single-agent reinforcement learning area. However, because of the large joint action space from multiple number of agents, and the limited transition data in Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning, the policy distribution is infeasible to be calculated statistically. To solve the policy distribution calculation problem in MARL settings, we employ a parametric likelihood-free density ratio estimator with two replay buffers instead of calculating statistically. The two replay buffers of different sizes store the historical trajectories that represent the data distribution of the past and current policies correspondingly. Based on the estimator, we assign Adaptive TD($λ$), \textbf{ATD($λ$)}, values to state-action pairs based on their likelihood under the stationary distribution of the current policy. We apply the proposed method on two competitive baseline methods, QMIX for value-based algorithms, and MAPPO for AC-based algorithms, over SMAC benchmarks and Gfootball academy scenarios, and demonstrate consistently competitive or superior performance compared to other baseline approaches with static $λ$ values.

</details>


### [16] [TMPO: Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization for Diverse and Efficient Diffusion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10983)
*Jiaming Li,Chenyu Zhu,Zhiyuan Ma,Nanxi Yi,Youjun Bao,Li Sun,Quanying Lv,Xiang Fang,Daizong Liu,Jianjun Li,Kun He,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TMPO方法，通过轨迹级奖励分布匹配替代标量奖励最大化，有效缓解RL对齐扩散模型中的奖励作弊问题，在保持生成多样性的同时实现奖励优化的最佳权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在将扩散模型对齐到下游任务时存在严重的奖励作弊问题，导致生成多样性和质量下降，表现为视觉模式崩塌和不可靠奖励的放大。根本原因是这些方法的模式寻求特性，即在最大化期望奖励时未能有效约束可接受轨迹上的概率分布，导致概率集中在少数高奖励路径上。

Method: 提出轨迹匹配策略优化（TMPO），引入Softmax轨迹平衡（Softmax-TB）目标函数，将K条轨迹的策略概率匹配到奖励诱导的玻尔兹曼分布。该目标继承了前向KL散度的模式覆盖特性，在优化奖励的同时保持对所有可接受轨迹的覆盖。此外，为减少大规模流匹配模型上的多轨迹训练时间，引入动态随机树采样，使轨迹共享去噪前缀并在动态调度的步骤处分枝，降低冗余计算。

Result: 在人类偏好、组合生成和文本渲染等多种对齐任务上进行了广泛实验，TMPO相比最先进方法在生成多样性上提升9.1%，在所有下游任务和效率指标上达到竞争性性能，实现了奖励与多样性之间的最佳权衡。

Conclusion: TMPO通过轨迹级分布匹配有效解决了现有RL对齐方法中的模式寻求问题，成功在奖励优化和生成多样性之间取得平衡，为扩散模型的任务对齐提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown extraordinary potential in aligning diffusion models to downstream tasks, yet most of them still suffer from significant reward hacking, which degrades generative diversity and quality by inducing visual mode collapse and amplifying unreliable rewards. We identify the root cause as the mode-seeking nature of these methods, which maximize expected reward without effectively constraining probability distribution over acceptable trajectories, causing concentration on a few high-reward paths. In contrast, we propose Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization (TMPO), which replaces scalar reward maximization with trajectory-level reward distribution matching. Specifically, TMPO introduces a Softmax Trajectory Balance (Softmax-TB) objective to match the policy probabilities of K trajectories to a reward-induced Boltzmann distribution. We prove that this objective inherits the mode-covering property of forward KL divergence, preserving coverage over all acceptable trajectories while optimizing reward. To further reduce multi-trajectory training time on large-scale flow-matching models, TMPO incorporates Dynamic Stochastic Tree Sampling, where trajectories share denoising prefixes and branch at dynamically scheduled steps, reducing redundant computation while improving training effectiveness. Extensive results across diverse alignment tasks such as human preference, compositional generation and text rendering show that TMPO improves generative diversity over state-of-the-art methods by 9.1%, and achieves competitive performance in all downstream and efficiency metrics, attaining the optimal trade-off between reward and diversity.

</details>

*Siddhant Dutta,Edward Tan Beng Wai,Soumick Sarker,Pasan Gunawardane,Jagath C. Rajapakse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个即插即用的框架，通过将ESM-2表示投影到蛋白质接触图并应用轻量级图神经网络SoftBlobGIN，在提升下游任务性能的同时实现了结构层面的可解释性。
