  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（如ESM-2）虽然在蛋白质功能预测任务上表现出色，但其特征编码在稠密的潜在空间中，结构和进化信号难以解释。现有方法缺乏直接可审计的结构解释能力。

Method: 提出一个即插即用的框架，将ESM-2表示投影到蛋白质接触图上，应用SoftBlobGIN——一种带有可微分Gumbel-softmax子结构池化的轻量级图同构网络，进行结构感知的消息传递，并学习用于下游预测任务的粗粒度功能子结构。该框架无需重新训练语言模型，仅增加约1.1M参数。

Result: 在酶分类任务上达到92.8%准确率和0.898 macro-F1；在结合位点检测任务上，残基AUROC从ESM-2线性探针的0.885提升至0.983；在Gene Ontology预测上达到Fmax=0.733，结合位点检测AUROC=0.969。GNNExplainer能够恢复生物学上有意义的活性位点残基、空间局部化的功能簇和催化接触模式。

Conclusion: 该框架作为蛋白质语言模型的可解释结构伴侣，能够产生直接可审计的结构解释，使模型预测更加透明和可审计，证明了结构解释无法仅从语言模型特征中恢复，为蛋白质功能预测提供了一种有效的可解释方法。

Abstract: Protein language models such as ESM-2 learn rich residue representations that achieve strong performance on protein function prediction, but their features remain difficult to interpret as structural $\&$ evolutionary signals are encoded in dense latent spaces. We propose a plug-$\&$-play framework that projects ESM-2 representations onto protein contact graphs $\&$ applies $\textbf{SoftBlobGIN}$, a lightweight Graph Isomorphism Network with differentiable Gumbel-softmax substructure pooling, to perform structure-aware message passing $\&$ learn coarse functional substructures for downstream prediction tasks. Across enzyme classification, SoftBlobGIN achieves 92.8\% accuracy $\&$ 0.898 macro-F1. Unlike post hoc analysis of protein language models alone, our method produces directly auditable structural explanations: GNNExplainer recovers biologically meaningful active-site residues, spatially localized functional clusters, $\&$ catalytic contact patterns. On binding-site detection, SoftBlobGIN improves residue AUROC from $0.885$ using an ESM-2 linear probe to $0.983$, indicating that these structural explanations are not recoverable from language-model features alone. Learned blob partitions provide an additional layer of interpretability by automatically grouping residues into functional substructures, with blobs containing annotated active-site residues showing $1.85\times$ higher importance than other blobs ($ρ{=}0.339$, $p{=}0.009$), without any active-site supervision. Our framework requires no retraining of the language model, adds only $\sim$1.1M parameters, $\&$ generalises across ProteinShake tasks, achieving $F_{\max}$ of $0.733$ on Gene Ontology prediction $\&$ AUROC of $0.969$ on binding-site detection. We position this as an interpretable structural companion to protein language models that makes their predictions more transparent $\&$ auditable.

</details>


### [18] [Predicting Decisions of AI Agents from Limited Interaction through Text-Tabular Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12411)
*Eilam Shapira,Moshe Tennenholtz,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种目标自适应的文本-表格预测框架，用于预测陌生AI代理的决策，通过引入LLM-as-Observer特征，在议价和谈判任务上显著优于直接LLM预测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI代理与陌生对手进行谈判和交易的现实场景中，对手的LLM、提示词、控制逻辑等均不可见，而每个决策都可能产生金钱后果，因此需要研究代理能否通过少量交互预测陌生对手的下一步决策。

Method: 将问题建模为目标自适应文本-表格预测任务，每个决策点表示为表格行，结合结构化游戏状态、报价历史和对话；提出LLM-as-Observer方法，将冻结的小型LLM作为编码器提取决策导向特征，而非直接用于少样本预测。

Result: 在13个前沿LLM代理上训练、91个支架代理上测试，完整模型在两项任务上优于直接LLM预测和游戏+文本特征基线；K=16时，Observer特征使响应预测AUC提高约4个百分点，议价报价预测误差降低14%。

Conclusion: 将对手预测建模为目标自适应文本-表格任务可实现有效适应，隐藏的LLM表示能够暴露直接提示无法呈现的决策相关信号。

Abstract: AI agents negotiate and transact in natural language with unfamiliar counterparts: a buyer bot facing an unknown seller, or a procurement assistant negotiating with a supplier. In such interactions, the counterpart's LLM, prompts, control logic, and rule-based fallbacks are hidden, while each decision can have monetary consequences. We ask whether an agent can predict an unfamiliar counterpart's next decision from a few interactions. To avoid real-world logging confounds, we study this problem in controlled bargaining and negotiation games, formulating it as target-adaptive text-tabular prediction: each decision point is a table row combining structured game state, offer history, and dialogue, while $K$ previous games of the same target agent, i.e., the counterpart being modeled, are provided in the prompt as labeled adaptation examples. Our model is built on a tabular foundation model that represents rows using game-state features and LLM-based text representations, and adds LLM-as-Observer as an additional representation: a small frozen LLM reads the decision-time state and dialogue; its answer is discarded, and its hidden state becomes a decision-oriented feature, making the LLM an encoder rather than a direct few-shot predictor. Training on 13 frontier-LLM agents and testing on 91 held-out scaffolded agents, the full model outperforms direct LLM-as-Predictor prompting and game+text features baselines. Within this tabular model, Observer features contribute beyond the other feature schemes: at $K=16$, they improve response-prediction AUC by about 4 points across both tasks and reduce bargaining offer-prediction error by 14%. These results show that formulating counterpart prediction as a target-adaptive text-tabular task enables effective adaptation, and that hidden LLM representations expose decision-relevant signals that direct prompting does not surface.

</details>


### [19] [AESOP: Adversarial Execution-path Selection to Overload Deep Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10987)
*Tingxi Li,Mingfang Ji,Ravishka Shemal Rathnasuriya,Simin Chen,Yitao Hu,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了动态推理管道中存在的路径感知攻击面，提出的AESOP框架可将FLOPs膨胀至2407倍，现有系统防御无法有效应对，迫使系统在吞吐量崩溃或高数据丢失率之间做选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习部署越来越多地将专用模型组合成动态推理管道，上游组件的中间预测决定下游组件的工作负载和输入。由于这些管道在严格的实时约束下运行，效率是系统可用性的基本要求。然而现有针对单模型的攻击方法无法利用管道结构的独特漏洞。

Method: 将此问题形式化为对抗性路径选择问题，并提出AESOP框架。该框架结合了漏洞引导的路径排序和自适应损失加权，专门针对动态推理管道的路径选择机制进行攻击。

Result: 在白盒设置下，AESOP实现了高达2407倍FLOPs膨胀和419倍延迟膨胀；在灰盒设置下实现58倍FLOPs膨胀和17倍延迟膨胀。与最强的单模型基线（117倍）相比，路径感知攻击产生了20倍的差距。在系统级防御下，攻击未被中和而是被重定向：管道被迫在吞吐量崩溃（0.578→0.006 input/s）或96.7%数据丢失之间做出选择。

Conclusion: 动态推理管道的结构创建了现有单模型防御方法无法应对的效率攻击面。即使在存在批处理、有界缓冲和置信度阈值防御的生产现实部署中，攻击仍然有效，迫使系统在可用性和完整性之间进行权衡，揭示了当前防御机制的局限性。

Abstract: Modern machine learning deployments increasingly compose specialized models into dynamic inference pipelines, where upstream components produce intermediate predictions that determine the workload and inputs of downstream components. The cost of processing an input is therefore not determined by any single model, but by two coupled factors: the per-inference cost of each invoked component and its workload volume. Because these pipelines run under hard real-time constraints, efficiency is a fundamental requirement for system availability. We show that this structure creates an efficiency-attack surface that existing methods targeting single models cannot exploit: on identical inputs and budgets, path-aware targeting inflates FLOPs by $2,407\times$ while the strongest single-model baseline achieves $117\times$ -- a $20\times$ gap attributable entirely to where the attack is directed. We formalize this as the adversarial path-selection problem and present AESOP, a framework combining vulnerability-guided path ranking with adaptive loss weighting. We evaluate AESOP on five pipelines plus a production-realistic deployment variant with batching, bounded buffering, and confidence-threshold defenses. AESOP achieves up to $2,407\times$ FLOPs and $419\times$ latency inflation in white-box setting and 58$\times$ FLOPs / 17$\times$ latency in gray-box settings. Under system-level defenses, the attack is not neutralized but redirected: pipelines are forced to choose between throughput collapse ($0.578 \to 0.006$ input/s) and $96.7\%$ data loss to sustain throughput.

</details>


### [20] [Seeing the Needle in the Haystack: Towards Weakly-Supervised Log Instance Anomaly Localization via Counterfactual Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10988)
*Yutszyuk Wong,Wentai Wu,Yuen-Ying Yeung,Weiwei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LogMILP框架，利用多示例学习结合原型引导和反事实扰动，在仅有包级标签的弱监督条件下实现了日志异常检测与细粒度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网络系统日志数据量巨大，实例级标注成本极高，导致细粒度异常定位困难。现有方法难以在粗粒度监督下准确识别具体异常日志条目。

Method: 提出弱监督框架LogMILP，基于多示例学习（MIL）仅使用包级标签。通过原型引导的结构化建模捕获关键模式，并引入反事实扰动一致性正则化，增强模型对关键日志条目的识别能力与定位可靠性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，LogMILP在包级异常检测性能上具有竞争力，同时在实例级异常定位方面显著提升了可靠性。

Conclusion: LogMILP有效解决了弱监督下日志异常细粒度定位难题，在保证检测性能的同时提供了更可靠的实例级定位能力，具有较强的实用性与可解释性。

Abstract: Log anomaly detection is a critical task for system operations and security assurance. However, in networked systems at scale, log data are generated at massive scale while instance-level annotations are prohibitively expensive, posing great difficulties to fine-grained anomaly localization. To address this challenge, we propose LogMILP (Log anomaly localization based on Multi-Instance Learning enhanced by prototypes and Perturbation), a weakly supervised framework that enables both bag-level anomaly detection and instance-level anomaly localization using only bag-level labels. Our method guides the model to pinpoint the critical log entries using prototype-guided structural modeling with counterfactual perturbation consistency regularization, thereby improving localization reliability and interpretability under coarse-grained supervision. Experimental results on three public datasets demonstrate that LogMILP achieves competitive detection performance while yielding significantly more reliable instance-level localization. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/YUK1207/LogMILP.

</details>


### [21] [SURGE: Surrogate Gradient Adaptation in Binary Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10989)
*Haoyu Huang,Boyu Liu,Linlin Yang,Yanjing Li,Yuguang Yang,Xuhui Liu,Canyu Chen,Zhongqian Fu,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SURGE框架，通过可学习的梯度补偿机制解决二值神经网络训练中的梯度不匹配问题，在多个任务上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二值神经网络训练方法（如STE及其变体）依赖手工设计的梯度近似，存在梯度不匹配和固定范围梯度裁剪导致的信息丢失问题。

Method: 提出SURGE框架，包含双路径梯度补偿器（DPGC）和自适应梯度缩放器（AGS）。DPGC为每个二值化层构建并行的全精度辅助分支，通过输出分解解耦梯度流；AGS基于最优尺度因子动态平衡分支间的梯度贡献。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测和语言理解任务上的实验表明，SURGE方法优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: SURGE通过辅助反向传播机制有效缓解了梯度不匹配问题，为二值神经网络的训练提供了一种理论上有依据、性能上优越的解决方案。

Abstract: The training of Binary Neural Networks (BNNs) is fundamentally based on gradient approximation for non-differentiable binarization operations (e.g., sign function). However, prevailing methods including the Straight-Through Estimator (STE) and its improved variants, rely on hand-crafted designs that suffer from gradient mismatch problem and information loss induced by fixed-range gradient clipping. To address this, we propose SURrogate GradiEnt Adaptation (SURGE), a novel learnable gradient compensation framework with theoretical grounding. SURGE mitigates gradient mismatch through auxiliary backpropagation. Specifically, we design a Dual-Path Gradient Compensator (DPGC) that constructs a parallel full-precision auxiliary branch for each binarized layer, decoupling gradient flow via output decomposition during backpropagation. DPGC enables bias-reduced gradient estimation by leveraging the full-precision branch to estimate components beyond STE's first-order approximation. To further enhance training stability, we introduce an Adaptive Gradient Scaler (AGS) based on an optimal scale factor to dynamically balance inter-branch gradient contributions via norm-based scaling. Experiments on image classification, object detection, and language understanding tasks demonstrate that SURGE performs best over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [22] [DisagMoE: Computation-Communication overlapped MoE Training via Disaggregated AF-Pipe Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11005)
*Zhichen Zeng,Chi-Chih Chang,Jiayi Wang,Zezhou Wang,Ningxin Zheng,Zheng Zhong,Cesar A. Stuardo,Dongyang Wang,Mohamed S. Abdelfattah,Haibin Lin,Banghua Zhu,Ang Li,Ziheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DisagMoE系统，通过解耦注意力层和FFN层到不同GPU组，并联合优化模型放置与调度，解决了MoE训练中专家并行的全对全通信瓶颈问题，实现了最高1.8倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家架构（MoE）在训练大规模模型时采用专家并行策略，但面临严重的全对全通信瓶颈问题。随着模型规模增长需要跨节点分布专家，有限的节点间网络带宽进一步加剧了这一瓶颈。现有工作通过重叠通信与计算来缓解问题，但由于注意力层和FFN层在计算-通信比例上的固有差异，仍存在网络延迟导致的停滞。

Method: DisagMoE采用解耦架构设计：(1) 将注意力层和FFN层分离到独立的GPU组；(2) 引入具有单向、多对多通信的多阶段流水线；(3) 基于计算-通信roofline模型平衡GPU和网络带宽在注意力组和FFN组之间的分配。系统基于Megatron-LM实现。

Result: 在16节点8xH800集群上对多种MoE模型进行评估，DisagMoE相比现有方法实现了最高1.8倍的训练加速，显著提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: DisagMoE通过硬件资源感知的解耦设计，有效解决了MoE训练中的通信瓶颈问题，为大规模MoE模型的高效训练提供了新的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures enable trillion-parameter LLMs with sparsely activated experts. Expert parallelism (EP) is a widely adopted MoE training strategy, but it suffers from severe all-to-all communication bottlenecks, which is exaggerated by the limited inter-node network bandwidth as the growing model size requires distributing experts across GPU nodes. Prior work focused on overlapping these all-to-all communications with feed-forward network (FFN) and self-attention computations, which often leaves residual network-bound stalls due to inherent imbalance in attention and FFN layers' computation-communication ratios. We present DisagMoE, a disaggregated MoE training system that jointly optimizes model placement and scheduling for maximal efficiency. DisagMoE separates attention and FFN layers into disjoint GPU groups, introduces a multi-stage pipeline with uni-directional, many-to-many communications, and employs a computation-communication roofline model to balance GPU and network bandwidth allocation among the attention and FFN groups. DisagMoE is implemented on Megatron-LM, and evaluation shows that DisagMoE improves training efficiency across multiple MoE models with up to 1.8x speedup on 16-node 8xH800 clusters.

</details>


### [23] [Test-Time Personalization: A Diagnostic Framework and Probabilistic Fix for Scaling Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10991)
*Linhai Zhang,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了测试时个性化（TTP）框架，通过在推理时采样N个候选并使用个性化奖励模型选择最佳结果，实现了随候选数量对数增长的效用提升，并提出了统一缩放定律来诊断和解决奖励模型的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法将推理视为单次过程，专注于构建更好的个性化模型或输入，而忽视了通过扩展推理时计算来实现个性化的潜力。本文旨在探索测试时个性化这一未被研究的方向。

Method: 提出测试时个性化（TTP）框架：从个性化策略模型采样N个候选，使用个性化奖励模型选择最佳结果。推导出统一缩放定律，将奖励模型的Best-of-N曲线分解为四个可测量量，揭示两种失效模式（用户级坍塌和查询级奖励黑客）。基于此提出概率个性化奖励模型，通过学习方差来缓解这两种失效模式。

Result: 理论证明oracle选择产生随候选数量对数增长的期望效用。标准奖励模型无法实现此潜力，但所提出的概率个性化奖励模型有效缓解了用户级坍塌和查询级奖励黑客两种失效模式。实验表明TTP在多个策略模型和个性化文本生成任务中实现了持续缩放，缩放定律与实际观察到的缩放曲线高度吻合。

Conclusion: 测试时个性化是一个有效的个性化方向，通过扩展推理时计算可以实现性能提升。统一缩放定律为理解和改进奖励模型提供了理论框架，概率个性化奖励模型成功解决了标准方法的局限性。

Abstract: Existing approaches to LLM personalization focus on constructing better personalized models or inputs, while treating inference as a single-shot process. In this work, we study Test-Time Personalization (TTP) along an unexplored axis: scaling inference-time computation by sampling N candidates from a personalized policy model and selecting the best with a personalized reward model. We prove that oracle selection yields expected utility growing logarithmically with the number of sampled candidates, establishing a theoretical ceiling for test-time scaling. However, standard reward models fail to realize this potential. To diagnose why, we derive a unified scaling law that decomposes any reward model's Best-of-N curve into four measurable quantities and reveals two failure modes, user-level collapse (near-constant prediction for some users) and query-level reward hacking (negative correlation with true quality for some queries). Guided by this law, we propose a probabilistic personalized reward model whose learned variance effectively mitigates both failure modes. Experiments confirm both elements of our framework: TTP delivers consistent scaling across multiple policy models and personalized text generation tasks, and our scaling law closely matches observed scaling curves across reward-model variants.

</details>


### [24] [Finite Volume-Informed Neural Network Framework for 2D Shallow Water Equations: Rugged Loss Landscapes and the Importance of Data Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11001)
*Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Data-Guided FVM-PINN框架，用有限体积法损失替代传统强形式残差，解决了浅水方程中物理驱动训练易陷入平凡解的问题，发现少量数据即可显著提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）采用强形式残差公式，在处理浅水方程（SWE）时存在显著局限：无法强制执行局部守恒性、难以处理间断解，且无法利用实际应用中常见的边界适应非结构化网格。这些缺陷限制了PINNs在真实流体动力学问题中的应用效果。

Method: 提出了"Data-Guided FVM-PINN"框架，核心创新包括：（1）用可微的、well-balanced的Roe黎曼求解器有限体积法（FVM）损失替代强形式残差；（2）在非结构化网格上评估损失；（3）通过损失景观诊断揭示纯物理驱动训练存在浅层平凡解陷阱；（4）引入稀疏数据指导打破退化的损失景观；（5）采用时间窗口分解与渐进式初始条件交接策略处理长时间仿真。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）纯物理FVM-PINN训练在现实2D问题上常失败，网络坍缩至几乎满足残差但与真实流动无关的低动量平凡态；（2）添加稀疏数据将损失景观分离度从7倍提升至310倍；（3）在2D block-in-channel基准测试中，仅200个随机速度测量使速度场L2误差降低22倍，50个测量仍能降低7倍；（4）控制消融实验表明FVM-PINN损失在稀疏数据条件下降低速度场L2误差约23%，在密集数据条件下作用中性；（5）在Savannah河段真实案例（1306单元、3600秒仿真、五个Manning区域）中成功构建准确代理模型。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将领域知识（有限体积法）与数据驱动方法结合的重要性。纯物理驱动的FVM-PINN因损失景观退化易陷入平凡解，而少量测量数据可有效打破这种退化。所提框架成功弥合了PINN理想化表述与实际计算流体力学需求之间的差距，为真实世界浅水流动问题提供了实用的代理建模解决方案。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are a simple surrogate-modelling paradigm for partial differential equations, but their standard strong-form residual formulation is ill suited to the shallow water equations (SWE). It cannot enforce local conservation, handle discontinuities, or leverage the boundary-conforming unstructured meshes used in real-world applications. We introduce ``Data-Guided FVM-PINN'', a framework that replaces the strong-form residual with a differentiable, well-balanced Roe Riemann-solver finite-volume (FVM) loss evaluated on unstructured meshes. The major finding is that physics-only FVM-PINN training often fails on realistic 2D problems: the network collapses to a trivial low-momentum state that nearly satisfies the FVM-PINN residual but bears no resemblance to the true flow. A loss-landscape diagnostic shows that the FVM-PINN loss at zero momentum is only about $7\times$ larger than at the trained solution, a shallow basin that an ordinary optimizer falls into; adding even sparse data turns this into a $310\times$ separation, breaking the degeneracy. On a 2D block-in-channel benchmark, just $200$ random velocity measurements drop the velocity-field $L_2$ error by $22\times$ versus physics-only; $50$ measurements still deliver a $7\times$ reduction. A controlled ablation isolates the contribution of the FVM-PINN loss: it reduces velocity-field $L_2$ by $\sim$$23\%$ in the sparse-data regime and is essentially neutral when dense reference data is available. On a real-world Savannah River reach ($1306$ cells, $3600$~s simulation, five Manning zones), the framework constructs an accurate surrogate from SRH-2D anchor data, with time-window decomposition reducing error monotonically via progressive initial-condition handoff.

</details>


### [25] [RT-Transformer: The Transformer Block as a Spherical State Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11007)
*Peter Racioppo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从单一几何估计问题出发，推导出Transformer核心组件（注意力、残差连接、归一化）的自然形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示Transformer架构组件的数学本质，探索这些组件是否源于统一的几何或统计原理，而非仅作为独立的架构设计选择。

Method: 将潜在状态建模为超球面上的方向向量，噪声定义在当前估计点的切平面上，构建了一个精度加权的方向推断框架。在此几何设定下，推导出各组件的数学对应关系。

Result: 发现注意力机制用于聚合证据，残差连接实现状态的增量更新，归一化将更新后的状态重新投影回超球面。这三者自然地由几何估计问题的结构推导得出。

Conclusion: Transformer的核心组件并非任意的架构选择，而是几何估计问题内在结构的必然产物，这为理解Transformer提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We show that the core components of the Transformer block -- attention, residual connections, and normalization -- arise naturally from a single geometric estimation problem. Modeling the latent state as a direction on the hypersphere, with noise defined in the tangent plane at the current estimate, yields a precision-weighted directional inference procedure in which attention aggregates evidence, residual connections implement incremental state updates, and normalization retracts the updated state back onto the hypersphere. Together, these components follow from the geometry of the estimation problem rather than being introduced as independent architectural choices.

</details>


### [26] [When and How to Canonize: A Generalization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11008)
*Yonatan Sverdlov,Benjamin Friedman,Snir Hordan,Nadav Dym*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了非不变骨干网络通过规范化和群平均实现不变性的理论框架，证明了规范化方法的泛化误差界介于结构不变模型和非不变基准之间，并首次从理论上解释了Hilbert曲线序列化在点云处理中的成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不变性架构是处理对称数据的标准方法，但通过群平均或规范化在非不变骨干网络上实现不变性的替代策略受到越来越多的关注。然而，这些替代策略的理论泛化性质仍不清楚，缺乏对其泛化误差的理论理解。

Method: 作者引入了一个理论框架，通过界定覆盖数来分析规范化方法和群平均方法的泛化误差。该框架建立了泛化层次结构，比较了规范化模型、结构不变模型、群平均模型和非不变基准模型的误差界。作者还将此框架应用于排列群和点云处理，分析不同规范化方法的覆盖数增长特性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1)建立了严格的泛化层次结构：规范化模型的误差界最好情况下等于结构不变和群平均模型的误差界，最坏情况下等于非不变基准的误差界；(2)存在最优规范化能达到最优误差界，也存在差规范化达到非不变误差界，这取决于规范化的正则性；(3)证明了字典序排序的覆盖数随点云维度呈指数增长，而Hilbert曲线规范化保证多项式增长，首次为Hilbert曲线序列化的经验成功提供了形式化理论支持。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析揭示了规范化方法的泛化性质，证明了规范化方法的理论保证取决于其正则性。研究为点云处理中Hilbert曲线序列化的成功提供了首个形式化理论依据，并通过实验验证了理论主张的正确性。

Abstract: While invariant architectures are standard for processing symmetric data, there is growing interest in achieving invariance by applying group averaging or canonization to non-invariant backbones. However, the theoretical generalization properties of these alternative strategies remain poorly understood. We introduce a theoretical framework to analyze the generalization error of these methods by bounding their covering numbers. We establish a rigorous generalization hierarchy: the error bounds of canonized models are at best equal to the error bounds of structurally invariant and group-averaged models, and at worst equal to the bounds of non-invariant baselines. Furthermore, we show that there exist optimal canonizations which attain the optimal error bounds, and poor canonizations which attain the non-invariant error bounds, and that this depends on the regularity of the canonization. Finally, applying this framework to permutation groups in point cloud processing, we rigorously prove that the covering number of lexicographical sorting grows exponentially with point cloud dimension, whereas Hilbert curve canonization guarantees polynomial growth. This provides the first formal theoretical justification for the empirical success of Hilbert curve serialization in state-of-the-art point cloud architectures. We conclude with experiments that support our theoretical claims.
  Code is available at https://github.com/yonatansverdlov/Canonization

</details>


### [27] [ACSAC: Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic with Causal Transformer Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11009)
*Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Hongtu Zhou,Hang Yu,Yanping Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACSAC算法，通过因果Transformer评论家自适应选择动作块大小，解决了长视野稀疏奖励任务中固定块大小导致的响应性与时间一致性矛盾，在离线和离线到在线RL设置中均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野稀疏奖励任务中，单步TD学习存在自举误差累积问题。现有的基于动作块的Actor-Critic方法使用固定块大小，无法平衡响应性与时间一致性：大块降低对新观测的响应，小块导致动作不连贯，需要针对任务调参。

Method: 提出自适应块大小Actor-Critic（ACSAC）算法。该方法利用因果Transformer评论家评估不同大小动作块的期望回报，在每个块边界处自适应选择能最大化期望回报的块大小，实现灵活的、依赖状态的块大小选择，无需针对特定任务调参。同时证明了ACSAC的Bellman算子是收缩算子。

Result: 在OGBench上的实验表明，ACSAC在离线强化学习和离线到在线强化学习两种设置下的长视野稀疏奖励操作任务中均取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: ACSAC成功解决了固定动作块大小的局限性，通过自适应机制实现了响应性与时间一致性的动态平衡，为长视野稀疏奖励任务提供了一种无需任务特定调参的有效解决方案，并提供了理论收敛性保证。

Abstract: Long-horizon, sparse-reward tasks pose a fundamental challenge for reinforcement learning, since single-step TD learning suffers from bootstrapping error accumulation across successive Bellman updates. Actor-critic methods with action chunking address this by operating over temporally extended actions, which reduce the effective horizon, enable fast value backups, and support temporally consistent exploration. However, existing methods rely on a fixed chunk size and therefore cannot adaptively balance reactivity against temporal consistency. A large fixed chunk size reduces responsiveness to new observations, while a small one produces incoherent motions, forcing task-specific tuning of the chunk size. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic (ACSAC). ACSAC leverages a causal Transformer critic to evaluate expected returns for action chunks of different sizes. At each chunk boundary, it adaptively selects the chunk size that maximizes the expected return, supporting flexible, state-dependent chunk sizes without task-specific tuning. We prove that the ACSAC Bellman operator is a contraction whose unique fixed point is the action-value function of the adaptive policy. Experiments on OGBench demonstrate that ACSAC achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon, sparse-reward manipulation tasks across both offline RL and offline-to-online RL settings.

</details>


### [28] [A Comparative Study of Federated Learning Aggregation Strategies under Homogeneous and Heterogeneous Data Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11010)
*Antonios Makris,Christos Dousis,Emmanouil Kritharakis,Stavros Bouras,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦学习中的主流聚合策略在 homogeneous 和 heterogeneous 数据分布下进行了全面的实验比较，揭示了不同策略在准确率、鲁棒性和系统效率方面的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习的性能受服务器端聚合策略影响显著，聚合策略直接决定模型的学习性能、鲁棒性和系统行为。目前缺乏在 heterogeneous 数据分布下对主流聚合策略的系统化实验比较分析，因此需要深入探究不同聚合机制的表现和权衡。

Method: 采用基准图像分类数据集，在 homogeneous 和 heterogeneous 数据分布条件下，对广泛使用的联邦聚合策略进行全面实验比较。分析不同聚合机制在不同数据异质性程度下的响应，考察其对中心化准确率、损失函数的影响，同时测量聚合时间、训练时间和通信时间等系统效率指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，各聚合策略在不同数据集和数据分布下呈现显著差异的权衡关系。策略的有效性取决于数据集特征和运行条件，没有单一策略能在所有场景下表现最优。不同聚合策略在面对数据异质性时展现出不同的鲁棒性和效率特性。

Conclusion: 聚合策略的选择需要根据具体的应用场景、数据集特征和系统约束进行权衡决策。研究为联邦学习实践者在不同数据分布条件下选择合适的聚合策略提供了实验依据和指导建议。

Abstract: Federated Learning has emerged as a transformative paradigm for collaborative machine learning across distributed environments. However, its performance is strongly influenced by the aggregation strategy used to combine local model updates at the server, which directly affects learning performance, robustness, and system behavior. This work presents a comprehensive experimental comparison of widely used federated aggregation strategies under both homogeneous and heterogeneous data distributions. Using benchmark image classification datasets, we analyze how different aggregation mechanisms respond to varying degrees of data heterogeneity, examining their impact on centralized accuracy and loss, and system-level efficiency metrics, including aggregation, training, and communication time. The results demonstrate that aggregation strategies exhibit distinct trade-offs across datasets and data distributions, with their effectiveness varying according to dataset characteristics and operating conditions.

</details>


### [29] [LoopUS: Recasting Pretrained LLMs into Looped Latent Refinement Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11011)
*Taekhyun Park,Yongjae Lee,Dohee Kim,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LoopUS框架，通过将预训练LLM转换为循环架构来提升推理性能，无需从头训练循环模型或破坏性改造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升LLM推理性能的循环计算方法通常需要从头训练循环模型或进行破坏性改造，这涉及巨大的计算成本并可能损害预训练能力。因此，需要一种能够高效转换标准预训练LLM为循环架构的方法。

Method: LoopUS将预训练LLM重构为编码器、循环推理块和解码器三部分，通过四个核心组件实现：(1)基于阶段性表示动态的块分解；(2)输入依赖的选择性门控以缓解隐藏状态漂移；(3)随机深度监督实现长递归范围内的内存高效学习；(4)置信度头用于自适应早退。这些机制协同工作，在避免计算瓶颈和表示崩溃的同时实现稳定的潜在循环。

Result: LoopUS通过稳定的潜在循环提升了LLM的推理导向性能，且无需延长生成轨迹或从头进行循环训练，成功将标准非循环模型转换为循环形式。

Conclusion: LoopUS框架提供了一种高效的后训练解决方案，能够在保持预训练能力的同时，通过循环架构显著提升模型的推理性能，避免了传统方法的高计算成本和模型能力损失问题。

Abstract: Looped computation shows promise in improving the reasoning-oriented performance of LLMs by scaling test-time compute. However, existing approaches typically require either training recurrent models from scratch or applying disruptive retrofits, which involve substantial computational costs and may compromise pretrained capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{Looped Depth Up-Scaling} (LoopUS), a post-training framework that converts a standard pretrained LLM into a looped architecture. As a key technical contribution, LoopUS recasts the pretrained LLM into an encoder, a looped reasoning block, and a decoder. It operationalizes this latent-refinement architecture through four core components: (1) block decomposition, guided by staged representation dynamics; (2) an input-dependent selective gate to mitigate hidden-state drift; (3) random deep supervision for memory-efficient learning over long recursive horizons; and (4) a confidence head for adaptive early exiting. Collectively, these mechanisms transform a standard non-looped model into a looped form while stabilizing it against both computational bottlenecks and representation collapse. Through stable latent looping, LoopUS improves reasoning-oriented performance without extending the generated traces or requiring recurrent training from scratch. For more details, see https://thrillcrazyer.github.io/LoopUS

</details>


### [30] [Backbone-Equated Diffusion OOD via Sparse Internal Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11014)
*Yadang Alexis Rouzoumka,Jean Pinsolle,Eugénie Terreaux,Christèle Morisseau,Jean-Philippe Ovarlez,Chengfang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MBE协议实现扩散模型OOD检测器的公平比较，并引入CFS检测器，仅需少量低噪声内部激活即可达到优异性能，证明OOD信号集中在稀疏内部状态中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的OOD检测器缺乏公平比较基准，其结论会随骨干网络选择、损坏参数化和测试时预算而变化，导致难以准确评估不同方法的真实性能。

Method: 提出Mutualized Backbone-Equated (MBE)协议，统一不同扩散骨干网络的损坏级别和测试时成本；引入Canonical Feature Snapshots (CFS)检测器，仅使用极少量低噪声级别的冻结扩散骨干网络内部激活进行OOD检测。

Result: 在CIFAR规模基准上，单次前向的最强CFS变体为CFS(1x2)，更小的仅解码器变体也具有高度竞争力。实验表明冻结扩散骨干网络暴露的OOD信号集中在少量稀疏内部状态中，无需完整去噪轨迹或高容量下游头部。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的OOD检测能力主要来源于少量稀疏内部状态而非完整去噪过程；通过条件编码器-解码器互补性、对角分数分离和低噪声损坏稳定性可解释这一现象；MBE协议为未来扩散OOD检测研究提供了公平比较标准。

Abstract: Fair comparison between diffusion-based OOD detectors is challenging, as conclusions can vary with backbone choice, corruption parameterization, and test-time budget. We address this issue through a Mutualized Backbone-Equated (MBE) protocol that aligns canonical corruption levels and logical test-time cost across diffusion backbones. Within this setting, we introduce Canonical Feature Snapshots (CFS), a family of detectors that probes a frozen diffusion backbone using only a tiny number of native internal activations at canonical low-noise levels. On a controlled CIFAR-scale benchmark, the strongest one-forward CFS variant is CFS(1x2), while an even smaller decoder-only variant remains highly competitive. This shows that much of the relative-OOD signal exposed by frozen diffusion backbones is concentrated in a small number of sparse internal states, rather than requiring full denoising trajectories or high-capacity downstream heads. We further provide a local diagnostic theory explaining these observations through conditional encoder-decoder complementarity, diagonal-score separation, and low-noise corruption stability. The official implementation is available at https://github.com/RouzAY/cfs-diffusion-ood/.

</details>


### [31] [Simpson's Paradox in Behavioral Curves: How Aggregation Distorts Parametric Models of User Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11017)
*Chao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现聚合行为曲线存在辛普森悖论导致的系统性扭曲（生存偏差引起），在Goodreads和Amazon数据集中分别产生3倍和5.3倍的峰值估计偏差，并提出合成零校准方法解决分类假阳性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为曲线建模（拟合参与度-暴露量数据的参数函数）在推荐系统、广告投放和临床剂量确定中是标准做法。然而，聚合数据是否会引入系统性扭曲尚不清楚，这对从聚合曲线估计个体行为参数的实践提出了质疑。

Method: 1. 在Goodreads（330万用户，9个类型）、Amazon电子产品（1800万评论）和MovieLens-25M三个数据集上分析个体与聚合行为曲线的差异；2. 使用MovieLens-25M（D≈1）作为阴性对照，验证生存偏差而非聚合本身是作用机制；3. 测试扭曲对类别粒度、参与度操作化和分类器校准的稳健性；4. 开发合成零校准方法解决每用户分类中的假阳性问题。

Result: 1. Goodreads数据中，个体用户在约11次暴露时达到峰值，而聚合曲线在约34次暴露时达峰，存在3倍差距；2. Amazon电子产品数据呈现5.3倍的扭曲；3. MovieLens-25M作为阴性对照未显示显著扭曲，证实生存偏差是关键机制；4. 扭曲对类别粒度、参与度操作化和分类器校准具有稳健性；5. 每用户分类存在32%的假阳性率问题。

Conclusion: 聚合行为曲线在存在差异流失的情况下会产生系统性扭曲（辛普森悖论），生存偏差是核心机制。合成零校准可有效解决分类假阳性问题。该发现适用于所有从聚合曲线估计个体行为参数且存在差异流失的场景。

Abstract: Behavioral curve modeling -- fitting parametric functions to engagement-versus-exposure data -- is standard practice in recommendation, advertising, and clinical dosing. We show that aggregation introduces a systematic distortion: Simpson's paradox in behavioral curves. On Goodreads (3.3M users, 9 genres), individual users peak at n* approximately 11 exposures while the aggregate peaks at n* approximately 34 -- a 3x gap driven by survival bias. Amazon Electronics (18M reviews) shows a 5.3x distortion. MovieLens-25M (D approximately 1) serves as a negative control, confirming that survival bias -- not aggregation per se -- is the operative mechanism. The distortion is robust to category granularity, engagement operationalization, and classifier calibration. We develop Synthetic Null Calibration to address a 32% false positive rate in per-user classification. Our findings apply wherever individual behavioral parameters are estimated from aggregate curves under differential attrition.

</details>


### [32] [Efficient LLM Reasoning via Variational Posterior Guidance with Efficiency Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11019)
*Zizhao Chen,Yuying Li,Siting Lin,Lianxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受认知科学启发，提出VPG-EA框架，通过变分推理与双流架构解决大模型思维链推理中的过度思考问题，显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在使用思维链进行复杂推理时存在“过度思考”现象，严重降低了推理效率；现有的强化学习方法通过精心设计的奖励函数压缩推理链，导致高质量样本在探索空间中极其稀疏，形成了先验策略的采样瓶颈。

Method: 受认知科学启发，理论上证明了由参考答案引导的后验分布比先验分布具有更高的期望效用，能够突破高质量样本的采样瓶颈。为此，将高效推理形式化为变分推理问题，引入效率感知的证据下界作为理论基础，并提出VPG-EA框架。该框架采用参数共享的双流架构同时实例化后验分布和先验策略；通过跨视图评估过滤伪高效路径，并通过变分蒸馏将后验的高效模式单向迁移至先验策略。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和7B规模上的实验表明，VPG-EA在综合效率指标ε³上分别比各模型规模上的最强基线提升了8.73%和12.37%。

Conclusion: VPG-EA框架成功解决了思维链推理中的效率瓶颈问题，通过理论指导的变分推理方法实现了高质量样本的有效利用，为提升大模型推理效率提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Although large language models rely on chain-of-thought for complex reasoning, the overthinking phenomenon severely degrades inference efficiency. Existing reinforcement learning methods compress reasoning chains by designing elaborate reward functions, which renders high-quality samples extremely sparse in the exploration space and creates a sampling bottleneck for the prior policy. Inspired by cognitive science, we theoretically prove that a posterior distribution guided by reference answers achieves higher expected utility than the prior distribution, thus capable of breaking through the sampling bottleneck of high-quality samples. However, the posterior distribution is unavailable during inference. To this end, we formalize efficient reasoning as a variational inference problem and introduce an efficiency-aware evidence lower bound as the theoretical foundation. Based on this, we propose the VPG-EA framework. It adopts a parameter-shared dual-stream architecture to instantiate both the posterior distribution and the prior policy; after filtering out pseudo-efficient paths via cross-view evaluation, it unidirectionally transfers the posterior's efficient patterns to the prior policy through variational distillation. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and 7B scales demonstrate that VPG-EA improves the comprehensive efficiency metric epsilon cubed by 8.73% and 12.37% over the strongest baselines on each model size, respectively.

</details>


### [33] [Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning: Explicit Dual Ascent using Local Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11020)
*Anish Diwan,Davide Tateo,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了信任区域逆强化学习（TRIRL）算法，通过利用信任区域最优策略的理论见解，在每次迭代中仅依赖当前策略的局部搜索即可显式优化对偶目标，从而弥合了经典双重上升IRL与现代对抗方法之间的差距，实现了奖励函数和策略的单调改进，无需每轮完全求解RL问题，且避免了对抗方法的训练不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的双重上升逆强化学习（IRL）虽然保证了单调性能提升，但每次迭代都需要完全求解一个强化学习问题来计算对偶梯度，计算成本高昂；而现代对抗方法虽然避免了这一成本，却牺牲了稳定性和单调对偶改进。因此，本研究旨在弥合这两种方法之间的差距，在不完全求解RL问题的前提下实现奖励函数和策略的单调改进。

Method: 提出信任区域逆强化学习（TRIRL）算法。核心理论见解是：对于奖励函数更新的信任区域最优策略，在同一方向上的更小更新可以是全局最优的。利用这一更小的更新，可以在仅依赖当前策略局部搜索的情况下显式优化对偶目标，从而避免每轮完全求解RL问题，同时保持训练稳定性。

Result: TRIRL算法在多个挑战性任务中表现优于最先进的模仿学习方法，其聚合四分位均值提升了2.4倍；同时，所恢复的奖励函数能够适应系统动态变化，具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TRIRL成功结合了经典IRL方法的单调改进保证与对抗方法的计算效率，避免了训练不稳定性，学习到了传统意义上的奖励函数——即可以通过全局优化来匹配专家演示，为逆强化学习领域提供了一种高效且稳定的新方法。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) is typically formulated as maximizing entropy subject to matching the distribution of expert trajectories. Classical (dual-ascent) IRL guarantees monotonic performance improvement but requires fully solving an RL problem each iteration to compute dual gradients. More recent adversarial methods avoid this cost at the expense of stability and monotonic dual improvement, by directly optimizing the primal problem and using a discriminator to provide rewards. In this work, we bridge the gap between these approaches by enabling monotonic improvement of the reward function and policy without having to fully solve an RL problem at every iteration. Our key theoretical insight is that a trust-region-optimal policy for a reward function update can be globally optimal for a smaller update in the same direction. This smaller update allows us to explicitly optimize the dual objective while only relying on a local search around the current policy. In doing so, our approach avoids the training instabilities of adversarial methods, offers monotonic performance improvement, and learns a reward function in the traditional sense of IRL--one that can be globally optimized to match expert demonstrations. Our proposed algorithm, Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning (TRIRL), outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning methods across multiple challenging tasks by a factor of 2.4x in terms of aggregate inter-quartile mean, while recovering reward functions that generalize to system dynamics shifts.

</details>


### [34] [A Switching System Theory of Q-Learning with Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11021)
*Donghwan Lee,Han-Dong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于联合谱半径（JSR）为线性函数逼近Q-learning建立了切换系统框架，推导了均值动态的精确线性切换模型，并将收敛性分析与切换系统稳定性联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Q-learning收敛性分析多基于单步范数界限，可能过于保守。本文旨在建立更精确的切换系统视角，通过联合谱半径（JSR）提供更紧的收敛性判定条件，并统一分析多种Q-learning变体。

Method: 首先推导线性函数逼近Q-learning均值动态的精确线性切换模型，将收敛性问题转化为切换系统的稳定性问题；然后推广到随机线性Q-learning（包括i.i.d.观测和马尔可夫观测情形）；利用JSR构造稳定性判据，该判据捕捉了切换模式的乘积特性；最后将该框架应用于正则化Q-learning分析。

Result: 建立了Q-learning with LFA的精确线性切换系统模型；JSR判据相比传统单步范数界限更少保守性；该框架统一了投影Bellman方程、有限差分随机策略切换和切换系统稳定性分析；为正则化Q-learning提供了JSR视角的理论分析工具。

Conclusion: 切换系统与JSR理论为强化学习算法分析提供了新颖且有效的工具，能够建立更精确的收敛性判据，并为算法设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: This paper develops a switching-system interpretation of Q-learning with linear function approximation (LFA) based on the joint spectral radius (JSR). We derive an exact linear switched model for the mean dynamics and relate convergence to stability of the corresponding switched system. The same construction is then used for stochastic linear Q-learning with independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) observations and with Markovian observations. Although exact JSR computation is difficult in general, the certificate captures products of switching modes and can be less conservative than one-step norm bounds. The framework also yields a JSR-based view of regularized Q-learning with LFA. The resulting analysis connects projected Bellman equations, finite-difference stochastic-policy switching, and switched-system stability in a single parameter-space formulation.

</details>


### [35] [ASD-Bench: A Four-Axis Comprehensive Benchmark of AI Models for Autism Spectrum Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11091)
*Shubhankit Singh,Hassan Shaikh,Kuldeep Raghuwanshi,Keshav Bulia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了ASD-Bench基准测试框架，系统评估了机器学习、深度学习和基础模型在三个年龄组的自闭症筛查表现，发现成人分类表现最佳、青少年最具挑战性，并揭示了特征重要性随年龄变化的模式，强调多维度评估在临床AI中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)筛查工具存在三大局限：(1)单一架构评估，(2)受限的评估维度，(3)几乎完全聚焦于成人群体。这些限制遮蔽了对早期干预至关重要的年龄特异性诊断模式，因此需要开发一个系统性的基准测试框架来全面评估不同年龄群体的ASD筛查模型。

Method: 研究引入ASD-Bench基准框架，在四个维度（预测性能、校准、可解释性、对抗鲁棒性）上评估多种模型配置。数据集包含4,068条AQ-10记录，覆盖三个年龄组：儿童(1-11岁)、青少年(12-16岁)、成人(17-64岁)。评估模型包括经典机器学习模型(XGBoost、AdaBoost、Random Forest、Logistic Regression)、神经网络(MLP)、深度表格Transformer(TabNet、TabTransformer、FT-Transformer)以及TabPFN v2。研究还提出了启发式聚合惩罚(HAP)指标，这是一种代价敏感度量，对假阴性给予更重惩罚并纳入交叉验证方差以确保部署稳定性。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1)成人分类表现最佳(17个模型中10个达到完美F1和AUC)，青少年任务最难(F1上限0.837 vs 儿童0.915)；(2)特征重要性层级随年龄组变化：儿童以A9(社交动机)为主导，青少年以A5(模式识别)为首，成人呈现更平坦的重要性分布，与发展性社交遮掩现象一致；(3)准确性与校准存在分离：AdaBoost在成人组达到F1=1.000但ECE=0.302，证明单一指标评估在临床AI中不足。研究为不同年龄组提供了具体的部署建议。

Conclusion: 研究强调多维度评估框架在临床AI开发中的必要性，证明单一指标评估不足以衡量模型质量。年龄特异性特征模式为早期干预策略提供了新见解。所有发现应被解释为基于问卷标签的概念验证证据，而非临床验证的诊断性能。

Abstract: Automated ASD screening tools remain limited by single-architecture evaluations, axis-restricted assessment, and near-exclusive focus on adult cohorts, obscuring age-specific diagnostic patterns critical for early intervention. We introduce ASD-Bench, a systematic tabular benchmark evaluating ML, deep learning, and foundation model configurations across three age cohorts (children 1-11 yr, adolescents 12-16 yr, adults 17-64 yr) on four axes: predictive performance, calibration, interpretability, and adversarial robustness. Applied to a curated v3 dataset of 4,068 AQ-10 records, our benchmark spans classical models (XGBoost, AdaBoost, Random Forest, Logistic Regression), neural networks (MLP), deep tabular transformers (TabNet, TabTransformer, FT-Transformer), and TabPFN v2. We introduce the Heuristic Aggregate Penalty (HAP): a cost-sensitive metric penalising false negatives more heavily and incorporating cross-validation variance for deployment stability. Adult classification yields high performance (10/17 models achieve perfect F1 and AUC), while adolescents present a harder task (F1 ceiling 0.837 vs. 0.915 for children). Feature hierarchies shift across cohorts: A9 (social motivation) dominates for children, A5 (pattern recognition) leads for adolescents, and adults exhibit a flatter importance profile consistent with developmental social masking. Accuracy and calibration are dissociated: AdaBoost achieves F1=1.000 on adults with ECE=0.302, confirming single-metric evaluation is insufficient for clinical AI. Cohort-specific deployment recommendations are provided. All findings should be interpreted as proof-of-concept evidence on questionnaire-derived labels rather than clinically validated diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [36] [Enabling Performant and Flexible Model-Internal Observability for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11093)
*Nengneng Yu,Sixian Xiong,Yibo Zhao,Wei Wang,Zaoxing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DMI-Lib，一个高速深度模型检查器，通过异步可观测性底层架构将内部可观测性解耦，在保持低延迟和高GPU内存效率的同时实现实时访问模型内部状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的推理时工作负载越来越依赖于及时访问模型的内部状态，但现有的方法在提供可观测性时会引入显著的延迟开销，影响推理性能，因此需要一种高效的方法来实现内部可观测性。

Method: 提出了DMI-Lib，采用异步可观测性底层架构，包括：(1) Ring^2 GPU-CPU内存抽象用于捕获和暂存张量；(2) 策略控制的主机后端用于导出张量。该设计将内部可观测性作为一等系统原语，通过异步方式从推理热路径中解耦，支持在丰富的内部信号空间和多样化推理后端中放置观察点，同时保持服务优化并遵守严格的GPU内存预算。

Result: 实验表明DMI-Lib在离线批量推理中仅产生0.4%-6.8%的开销，在中等在线服务中平均开销为6%，与具有类似可观测性功能的现有基线相比，延迟开销降低了2倍至15倍。

Conclusion: DMI-Lib成功实现了高效、低开销的深度模型内部状态检查，为推理时工作负载提供了实用的内部可观测性解决方案，显著优于现有方法，并已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Today's inference-time workloads increasingly depend on timely access to a model's internal states. We present DMI-Lib, a high-speed deep model inspector that treats internal observability as a first-class systems primitive, decoupling it from the inference hot path via an asynchronous observability substrate built from Ring^2, a GPU-CPU memory abstraction for capturing and staging tensors, and a policy-controlled host backend that exports them. DMI-Lib enables the placement of observation points across a rich space of internal signals and diverse inference backends while preserving serving optimizations and adhering to tight GPU memory budgets. Our experiments demonstrate that DMI-Lib incurs only 0.4%--6.8% overhead in offline batch inference and an average of 6% in moderate online serving, reducing latency overhead by 2x-15x compared to existing baselines with similar observability features. DMI-Lib is open-sourced at https://github.com/ProjectDMX/DMI.

</details>


### [37] [Newton's Lantern: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Finetuning AC Power Flow Warm Start Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11102)
*Shourya Bose,Helgi Hilmarsson,Dhruv Suri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Newton's Lantern方法，通过强化学习微调流程解决神经网络热启动在重负荷场景下泛化差的问题，在多个基准测试中实现了唯一的全收敛率和最小的平均迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督学习方法在接近电压崩溃的重负荷场景下泛化能力差，论文通过理论分析发现监督回归在鞍结分岔附近的失效模式：Newton-Raphson迭代次数下界取决于热启动误差的方向而非幅度，且当潮流雅可比矩阵最小奇异值缩小时该界变得无效。

Method: 提出Newton's Lantern微调流程，结合群组相对策略优化和学习到的奖励模型，奖励模型在基础模型预测的扰动上进行训练，直接使用迭代次数本身作为监督信号。

Result: 在IEEE 118-bus、GOC 500-bus和GOC 2000-bus基准测试中，Newton's Lantern是唯一在所有测试快照上都收敛的方法，同时达到了最小的平均迭代次数。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了监督学习热启动在重负荷场景下的失效问题，证明了基于迭代次数直接优化的强化学习方法优于传统监督回归方法。

Abstract: Neural warm starts can sharply reduce the number of Newton-Raphson iterations required to solve the AC power flow problem, but existing supervised approaches generalize poorly on heavily loaded instances near voltage collapse. We prove a lower bound on the Newton-Raphson iteration count that depends on the direction of the warm start error rather than on its magnitude, and show as a corollary that the bound becomes vacuous as the smallest singular value of the power-flow Jacobian shrinks, identifying the failure mode of supervised regression near the saddle-node bifurcation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce Newton's Lantern, a finetuning pipeline that combines group relative policy optimization with a learned reward model trained on perturbations of the base model's predictions, using the iteration count itself as the supervisory signal. Across IEEE 118-bus, GOC 500-bus, and GOC 2000-bus benchmarks, Newton's Lantern is the only method that converges on every test snapshot while attaining the smallest mean iteration count.

</details>


### [38] [Language Modeling with Hyperspherical Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11125)
*Justin Deschenaux,Caglar Gulcehre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S-FLM，一种在超球面上进行的潜在流语言模型，通过旋转向量避免了现有FLM的高计算开销，在大词汇推理任务上显著提升了连续流语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散模型使用因子化分布采样，表达能力不及自回归模型；而近期的流语言模型(FLMs)虽然避免了因子化采样，但需要在one-hot向量上操作，导致训练成本随词汇量增加而剧增，且高斯噪声缺乏清晰的语义解释。

Method: 提出S-FLM，在超球面(S^{d-1})的潜在空间中进行建模。通过学习速度场来旋转向量，使用交叉熵损失进行训练，避免了显式构建one-hot向量带来的计算开销。

Result: S-FLM在大词汇推理任务上显著提升了连续流语言模型的性能；在标准温度采样(T=1)下缩小了与掩码扩散模型的差距，但在优化的低温度(T=0.1)解码下仍存在差距。

Conclusion: S-FLM成功解决了现有流语言模型的计算开销问题，在大词汇推理场景中表现优异，但在低温度解码设置下与掩码扩散模型相比仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Discrete Diffusion Language Models progressed rapidly as an alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, motivated by their parallel generation abilities. However, for tractability, discrete diffusion models sample from a factorized distribution, which is less expressive than AR. Recent Flow Language Models (FLMs) apply continuous flows to language, transporting noise to data with a deterministic ODE that avoids factorized sampling. FLMs operate on one-hot vectors whose dimension scales with the vocabulary size, making FLMs costly to train. Moreover, since all distinct one-hot embeddings are equidistant in $\ell_2$, adding Gaussian noise does not have a clear semantic interpretation (unlike images, where Gaussian noise progressively degrades structure). We introduce $\mathbb{S}$-FLM, a latent FLM in the hypersphere. $\mathbb{S}$-FLM generates sequences by rotating vectors in $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ along a velocity field learned with cross-entropy, avoiding the overhead of materializing one-hot vectors. Previous FLMs match AR in Generative Perplexity (Gen.\ PPL), but samples with high likelihood are not necessarily correct in verifiable domains such as math and code. $\mathbb{S}$-FLM substantially improves continuous flow language models on large-vocabulary reasoning and closes the gap to masked diffusion under standard-temperature sampling ($T=1$), while a gap remains under optimized low-temperature ($T=0.1$) decoding.

</details>


### [39] [HEPA: A Self-Supervised Horizon-Conditioned Event Predictive Architecture for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11130)
*Jonas Petersen,Gian-Alessandro Lombardi,Riccardo Maggioni,Camilla Mazzoleni,Federico Martelli,Philipp Petersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEPA通过联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）预训练因果Transformer编码器，再冻结编码器微调预测器，在14个基准测试中击败主流时序模型，同时显著降低对标注数据和调参的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列中的关键事件（如涡轮机故障、心律失常）预测至关重要，但由于事件罕见且标注成本高昂，导致标记数据极其稀缺。

Method: 提出HEPA架构，包含两个核心步骤：(1)利用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）预训练因果Transformer编码器，通过条件预测器预测未来表征而非未来值，迫使编码器从无标签数据中捕获可预测的时序动态；(2)冻结编码器，仅针对目标事件微调预测器，生成单调生存累积分布函数（CDF）。

Result: 在固定架构和优化器超参数的情况下，HEPA在11个领域的14个基准测试中（涵盖水污染、网络攻击检测、波动率机制等），至少在10个基准上超越了PatchTST、iTransformer、MAE和Chronos-2等领先时序架构，且调参量减少一个数量级，在生命周期数据集上所需标注数据也减少一个数量级。

Conclusion: HEPA通过解耦表征学习与事件预测，利用无标签数据预训练和参数冻结策略，有效解决了关键事件预测中标注数据稀缺的问题，展现出优异的跨领域泛化能力和数据效率。

Abstract: Critical events in multivariate time series, from turbine failures to cardiac arrhythmias, demand accurate prediction, yet labeled data is scarce because such events are rare and costly to annotate. We introduce HEPA (Horizon-conditioned Event Predictive Architecture), built on two key principles. First, a causal Transformer encoder is pretrained via a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA): a horizon-conditioned predictor learns to forecast future representations rather than future values, forcing the encoder to capture predictable temporal dynamics from unlabeled data alone. Second, we freeze the encoder and finetune only the predictor toward the target event, producing a monotonic survival cumulative distribution function (CDF) over horizons. With fixed architecture and optimiser hyperparameters across all benchmarks, HEPA handles water contamination, cyberattack detection, volatility regimes, and eight further event types across 11 domains, exceeding leading time-series architectures including PatchTST, iTransformer, MAE, and Chronos-2 on at least 10 of 14 benchmarks, with an order of magnitude fewer tuned parameters and, on lifecycle datasets, an order of magnitude less labeled data.

</details>


### [40] [Steerable Neural ODEs on Homogeneous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11133)
*Emma Andersdotter,Daniel Persson,Fredrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了齐性空间上的可导向神经常微分方程，这是流形NODEs的几何扩展，能够实现对向量值特征的连续等变动力学学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形神经常微分方程缺乏处理具有局部对称群变换特征的机制，亟需一种几何框架来学习齐性空间上向量值特征的连续时间等变动力学。

Method: 将特征解释为关联向量丛的截面，将其演化建模为平行移动，构建包含流形上的流方程和特征上的导向方程的耦合ODE系统，通过G不变向量场和联络保证G等变性。

Result: 证明了当生成流的向量场和支配平行移动的联络均为G不变时，可导向NODEs具有G等变性；展示了该框架能统一包含现有NODE模型和李群上的连续正则流。

Conclusion: 该工作为齐性空间上一般向量值特征的连续时间等变动力学学习提供了几何基础。

Abstract: We introduce steerable neural ordinary differential equations on homogeneous spaces $M=G/H$. These models constitute a novel geometric extension of manifold neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs) that transport associated feature vectors transforming under the local symmetry group $H$. We interpret features as sections of associated vector bundles over $M$, and describe their evolution as parallel transport. This results in a coupled system of ODEs consisting of a flow equation on $M$ and a steering equation acting on features. We show that steerable NODEs are $G$-equivariant whenever the vector field generating the flow and the connection governing parallel transport are both $G$-invariant. Furthermore, we demonstrate how steerable NODEs incorporate existing NODE models and continuous normalizing flows on Lie groups. Our framework provides the geometric foundation for learning continuous-time equivariant dynamics of general vector-valued features on homogeneous spaces.

</details>


### [41] [Spurious Correlation Learning in Preference Optimization: Mechanisms, Consequences, and Mitigation via Tie Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11134)
*Christian Moya,Alex Semendinger,Guang Lin,Elliott Thornley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面分析了偏好学习方法（如DPO）导致模型依赖虚假特征的问题，揭示了其产生机制与后果，并提出了一种基于“平局数据”的数据增强策略来有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好学习方法（如直接偏好优化DPO）会导致模型依赖虚假相关性，进而引发阿谀奉承和长度偏差等问题，并可能造成严重的目标泛化错误。作者旨在从理论层面统一分析这一现象的机制、后果及解决方案。

Method: 针对对数线性策略，理论分析表明标准偏好学习目标通过“平均虚假偏差”和“因果-虚假相关性泄漏”两个渠道诱导对虚假特征的依赖。基于此，提出了“平局训练”（tie training），这是一种利用平局（效用相等的偏好对）进行数据增强的策略，通过数据驱动的正则化来选择性抑制虚假学习。

Result: 理论分析表明，对虚假特征的依赖会导致模型在分布偏移面前存在不可约减的脆弱性，单纯增加训练数据无法降低这种依赖。实验验证了“平局训练”方法能够在不损害因果学习的前提下，有效减少对虚假特征的依赖。该方法在神经网络和大语言模型上同样有效。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上揭示了偏好学习中虚假特征依赖的根源，证明了其带来的分布偏移脆弱性，并提出了理论保证的缓解策略。所提出的“平局训练”方法为构建更鲁棒、更符合人类意图的语言模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Preference learning methods such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) are known to induce reliance on spurious correlations, leading to sycophancy and length bias in today's language models and potentially severe goal misgeneralization in future systems. In this work, we provide a unified theoretical analysis of this phenomenon, characterizing the mechanisms of spurious learning, its consequences on deployment, and a provable mitigation strategy. Focusing on log-linear policies, we show that standard preference-learning objectives induce reliance on spurious features at the population level through two channels: mean spurious bias and causal--spurious correlation leakage. We then show that this reliance creates an irreducible vulnerability to distribution shift: more data from the same training distribution fails to reduce the model's dependence on spurious features. To address this, we propose tie training, a data augmentation strategy using ties (equal-utility preference pairs) to introduce data-driven regularization. We demonstrate that this approach selectively reduces spurious learning without degrading causal learning. Finally, we validate our theory on log-linear models and provide empirical evidence that both the spurious learning mechanisms and the benefits of tie training persist for neural networks and large language models.

</details>


### [42] [Rank Is Not Capacity: Spectral Occupancy for Latent Graph Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11142)
*Nikolaos Nakis,Panagiotis Promponas,Konstantinos Tsirkas,Katerina Mamali,Eftychia Makri,Leandros Tassiulas,Nicholas A. Christakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Spectra方法，用谱分布的有效秩替代传统潜空间维度作为分析单元，使模型容量成为可训练参数而非超参数，在多个网络上实现了预测精度与模型容量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习方法将潜空间维度作为固定超参数进行调优，且学习到的潜变量仅具有旋转和缩放不变性，导致名义维度无法真实反映模型行为。此外，在过参数化设置下缺乏对模型容量的有效控制手段。

Method: 提出谱前缀提取与容量导向表示分析方法：1) 构造迹归一化的半正定核矩阵，使其谱在不同拟合之间可比；2) 将归一化特征值视为单纯形上的分布，用Shannon有效秩量化学习容量；3) 在训练过程中通过单一标量控制已实现维度，利用二分法将其调节至任意期望值；4) 从理论上证明了已实现维度的局部正则性和单调性。

Result: 在协作网络、社交网络、生物网络和基础设施网络上的实验表明：1) 与强链接预测基线相比具有竞争性表现；2) 能够追踪精度-容量前沿，可视化预测精度与已实现维度之间的权衡关系；3) 通过谱前缀生成对齐的低容量视图；4) 在过参数化设置下提供了对模型容量的有效控制。

Conclusion: 通过将容量从训练超参数转变为拟合模型的固有属性，该方法为过参数化设置下的图表示学习提供了更规范、可控的分析框架。

Abstract: Graph representation learning has become a standard approach for analyzing networked data, with latent embeddings widely used for link prediction, community detection, and related tasks. Yet a basic design choice, the latent dimension, is still treated as a brittle hyperparameter, fixed before training and tuned by held-out performance. Learned factors are also identifiable only up to rotation and rescaling, so the nominal rank rarely coincides with the quantity that governs model behavior. We propose Spectral Prefix Extraction and Capacity-Targeted Representation Analysis (Spectra), which replaces rank as the unit of analysis with the spectrum of a learned positive semidefinite kernel, trace-normalized so that spectra are comparable across fits. The normalized eigenvalues form a distribution on the simplex, and their Shannon effective rank acts both as a summary of learned capacity and as a controllable training-time coordinate: a single scalar shapes this realized dimension during training, and bisection targets any desired value within the rank cap. To theoretically support that, we show local regularity and monotonicity of the realized-dimension profile. Across collaboration, social, biological, and infrastructure networks, Spectra traces performance--capacity frontiers that make the trade-off between predictive accuracy and realized dimension visible. It performs competitively with strong link-prediction baselines, yields aligned lower-capacity views of the same fitted model through spectral prefixes, and provides a principled handle on capacity in the overparameterized regime. Capacity thus becomes a property of the fitted model rather than a hyperparameter of the training.

</details>


### [43] [CORE: Cyclic Orthotope Relation Embedding for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11159)
*Yingqi Zeng,Luying Wang,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CORE模型，通过将实体和关系嵌入到无边界的环面流形上来解决基于区域的KGC模型中的边界约束问题，实现了链路预测性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于区域的知识图谱补全模型在优化过程中受到绝对边界约束的限制，而无约束时关系区域又会无限扩张，这两种情况都会影响模型性能。

Method: 提出CORE（循环正交关系嵌入）模型，将实体和关系嵌入到无边界的环面流形上；将关系表示为环面上的循环正交体，使区域能够平滑地绕过空间边界；引入自适应宽度正则化以防止区域无条件扩张。

Result: 理论分析证明CORE能够捕获包含和交集等复杂关系模式；在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，CORE在链路预测准确率方面取得了具有高度竞争力的性能，特别是在密集语义环境中显著提升了预测精度。

Conclusion: 通过将知识图谱嵌入到环面流形上，CORE成功克服了传统区域嵌入模型的边界约束问题，为知识图谱补全任务提供了一种有效的新方法。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion (KGC) aims to automatically infer missing facts in multi-relational data by mapping entities and relations into continuous representation spaces. Recent region-based embedding models have shown great promise in capturing complex logical patterns by representing relations as geometric regions. However, these models inevitably suffer from absolute boundary constraints during optimization. Conversely, without such constraints, relation regions expand indefinitely. To address the limitation, we propose \textbf{CORE} (Cyclic Orthotope Relation Embedding), a novel KGC model that embeds entities and relations onto a boundary-less torus manifold.CORE represents relations as cyclic orthotopes on the torus manifold, allowing regions to seamlessly wrap around spatial boundaries to ensure smooth gradient conduction. Furthermore, an adaptive width regularization is introduced to prevent unconditional region expansion. Theoretical analysis proves that CORE can capture various complex relation patterns such as subsumption and intersection. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that CORE achieves highly competitive performance, significantly improving link prediction accuracy in dense semantic environments.

</details>


### [44] [Interpretability Can Be Actionable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11161)
*Hadas Orgad,Fazl Barez,Tal Haklay,Isabelle Lee,Marius Mosbach,Anja Reusch,Naomi Saphra,Byron Wallace,Sarah Wiegreffe,Eric Wong,Ian Tenney,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出可解释性研究缺乏实际影响的核心问题在于缺乏以“行动性”为导向的评估标准，提出了一个包含具体性和验证性两个维度的可操作性框架，旨在将研究重心转向能够指导实际决策和干预的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度神经网络的可解释性研究发展迅速，但人们日益担忧该领域的大部分工作未能产生实际影响，这引发了对其相关性和实用性的质疑。作者认为，造成这一问题的核心原因不在于缺乏新方法，而在于缺乏能够连接研究与实际应用的评估标准。

Method: 作者提出了“行动性可解释性”（actionable interpretability）的概念，将其定义为洞察力在可解释性研究之外实现具体决策和干预的程度。研究构建了一个评估框架，包含两个维度：具体性（concreteness）和验证性（validation），并识别了目前阻碍实现现实影响的障碍。同时，文章确定了五个可解释性具有独特优势的应用领域，并提出了与实际结果相一致的评估标准。

Result: 研究构建了一个可行动解释性的理论框架，明确了具体性和验证性两个评估维度，识别了阻碍实际应用的障碍，并提出了针对五个特定领域的评估标准，这些标准与实际结果紧密对齐。

Conclusion: 本文的目标并非贬低探索性研究的价值，而是确立“行动性”作为可解释性研究的核心目标。通过建立以实际应用为导向的评估体系，推动可解释性研究产生真正的现实影响。

Abstract: Interpretability aims to explain the behavior of deep neural networks. Despite rapid growth, there is mounting concern that much of this work has not translated into practical impact, raising questions about its relevance and utility. This position paper argues that the central missing ingredient is not new methods, but evaluation criteria: interpretability should be evaluated by actionability--the extent to which insights enable concrete decisions and interventions beyond interpretability research itself. We define actionable interpretability along two dimensions--concreteness and validation--and analyze the barriers currently preventing real-world impact. To address these barriers, we identify five domains where interpretability offers unique leverage and present a framework for actionable interpretability with evaluation criteria aligned with practical outcomes. Our goal is not to downplay exploratory research, but to establish actionability as a core objective of interpretability research.

</details>


### [45] [COSMOS: Model-Agnostic Personalized Federated Learning with Clustered Server Models and Pseudo-Label-Only Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11165)
*Ben Rachmut,Luise Ge,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COSMOS框架，通过伪标签通信和服务器端聚类实现模型无关的个性化联邦学习，理论证明可带来指数级个性化风险收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法难以同时处理架构异构性和统计异构性，虽然已有方法尝试通过客户端聚类和知识蒸馏解决，但仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出COSMOS模型无关框架：客户端训练本地模型并预测公共数据；服务器根据预测相似性聚类客户端，为每个组训练特定模型，并将模型蒸馏回客户端；仅通过伪标签通信实现服务器端个性化。

Result: 提供首个理论分析证明从学习到的聚类模型进行蒸馏可实现指数级个性化风险收敛，超越模型无关联邦学习中常见的收敛到平稳点保证；实验表明COSMOS持续优于所有模型无关基线，并与最先进的个性化联邦学习方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 服务器端个性化学习与伪标签结合是高度异构环境中可扩展、模型无关联邦学习的有前景范式。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) in heterogeneous environments remains challenging because client models often differ in both architecture and data distribution. While recent approaches attempt to address this challenge through client clustering and knowledge distillation, simultaneously handling architectural and statistical heterogeneity remains difficult. We introduce COSMOS, a model-agnostic framework that enables server-side personalization using only pseudo-label communication. Clients train local models and predict on the public data; the server clusters clients by prediction similarity, trains a cluster-specific model for each group using its own compute, and distills the resulting models back to clients. We provide the first theoretical analysis showing that distillation from the learned cluster models can yield exponential personalization risk contraction, going beyond the convergence-to-stationarity guarantees typically provided in model-agnostic FL. Experiments across benchmarks demonstrate that COSMOS consistently outperforms all model-agnostic FL baselines while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art personalized FL methods. More broadly, our results highlight personalized server-side learning with pseudo-labels as a promising paradigm for scalable and model-agnostic federated learning in highly heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [46] [Unlearning with Asymmetric Sources: Improved Unlearning-Utility Trade-off with Public Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11170)
*Ahmed Mehdi Inane,Vincent Quirion,Gintare Karolina Dzugaite,Ioannis Mitliagkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不对称朗之万遗忘框架，利用公开数据将遗忘成本抑制O(1/n_pub^2)倍，实现大规模数据删除同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有噪声认证机器遗忘面临严峻瓶颈：认证遗忘所需的噪声幅度通常会破坏模型效用，尤其在大规模删除请求场景下。虽然利用公开数据是差分隐私中缓解此矛盾的常用技术，但其在遗忘领域的应用尚未被探索。

Method: 引入不对称朗之万遗忘框架，通过注入公开数据来降低隐私成本。理论证明公开数据注入可将遗忘成本抑制O(1/n_pub^2)倍，建立新的控制机制：通过增加公开数据量来缓解对高噪声的需求及相关的效用损失。同时分析了分布不匹配的现实场景，刻画公开数据与私有数据源之间的分布偏移对效用的影响。

Result: ALU能够实现常数级数据集比例的大规模遗忘——这是标准对称方法变得不切实际的场景——同时保持高效用。使用变分Rényi散度和成员推理攻击的实验评估证实，ALU在合理的分布偏移下能有效抵御隐私攻击并保持效用。

Conclusion: 公开数据可作为机器遗忘中的有效资源，提供严格的计算优势优于重新训练，为隐私保护与模型效用之间的平衡提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Noise-based certified machine unlearning currently faces a hard ceiling: the noise magnitude required to certify unlearning typically destroys model utility, particularly for large-scale deletion requests.
  While leveraging public data is a standard technique in differential privacy to relax this tension, its role in unlearning remains unexplored. We address this gap by introducing Asymmetric Langevin Unlearning (ALU), a framework that uses public data to mitigate privacy costs. We prove that public data injection suppresses the unlearning cost by a factor of $O(1/n_{\mathrm{pub}}^2)$, guaranteeing a strict computational advantage over retraining. This establishes a new control mechanism: practitioners can mitigate the need for high noise-and the associated utility loss-by increasing the volume of public data. Crucially, we analyze the realistic setting of distribution mismatch, explicitly characterizing how shifts between public and private sources impact utility.
  We show that ALU enables mass unlearning of constant dataset fractions -- a regime where standard symmetric methods become impractical -- while maintaining high utility. Empirical evaluations using variational Rényi divergence and membership inference attacks confirm that ALU effectively thwarts privacy attacks while preserving utility under reasonable distribution shifts.

</details>


### [47] [Optimistic Dual Averaging Unifies Modern Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11172)
*Thomas Pethick,Wanyun Xie,Roman Machacek,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SODA框架，统一了Muon、Lion、AdEMAMix和NAdam等先进优化器，并实现了免调优权重衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进优化器（如Muon、Lion等）缺乏统一的理论视角，且权重衰减超参数通常需要繁琐的手动调整。

Method: 提出SODA（Optimistic Dual Averaging的泛化），从乐观优化的角度统一了多种主流优化器，并据此设计了一个实用的SODA包装器，采用理论推导的$1/k$衰减 schedule 消除权重衰减调参。

Result: 在不同规模和训练时长的实验中，SODA在不引入额外超参数调优的情况下，持续提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: SODA为理解现代优化器提供了统一框架，并能作为一个通用的即插即用组件，免去权重衰减调参并提升训练效果。

Abstract: We introduce SODA, a generalization of Optimistic Dual Averaging, which provides a common perspective on state-of-the-art optimizers like Muon, Lion, AdEMAMix and NAdam, showing that they can all be viewed as optimistic instances of this framework. Based on this framing, we propose a practical SODA wrapper for any base optimizer that eliminates weight decay tuning through a theoretically-grounded $1/k$ decay schedule. Empirical results across various scales and training horizons show that SODA consistently improves performance without any additional hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [48] [Oversmoothing as Representation Degeneracy in Neural Sheaf Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11178)
*Arif Dönmez,Axel Mosig,Ellen Fritsche,Katharina Koch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过箭图表示论视角分析神经层束扩散（NSD），发现过平滑问题源于表示几何的退化，并提出非均匀茎维度和矩映射正则化作为解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经层束扩散（NSD）虽然通过学习的限制映射定义了任务自适应的几何结构，但其扩散极限的表示论结构（即整体截面空间）仍然不明确。作者旨在建立箭图表示论与NSD的对应关系，深入理解NSD的表示论结构，并解释过平滑现象的本质。

Method: 1. 建立图上胞腔束与关联箭图表示之间的对应关系，使学习的束几何成为有限维表示空间中的点；
2. 证明关联箭图表示的直和分解诱导扩散极限的调和空间分解；
3. 利用几何不变量论中的稳定性和矩映射原理，引入矩映射启发的正则化器；
4. 分析等茎架构中的结构性障碍，研究非均匀茎维度的影响。

Result: 1. 发现过平滑问题可解释为表示退化：学习的束可能向低复杂度分量坍缩，其整体截面无法保留判别信息；
2. 揭示等茎架构中存在结构性障碍：当$d_v = d_e$时，可学习稳定性参数的可接受性强制所有对象分量位于稳定性墙壁上；
3. 非均匀茎维度可消除这一障碍，使自适应稳定性有意义；
4. 异质性基准实验验证：打破茎对称性可减少方差或改善验证行为，自适应稳定性在特定矩形设置中更有效。

Conclusion: 本文成功将NSD框架重构为基于表示几何的理论体系，将过平滑重新定义为学习束扩散底层表示几何的退化现象。通过引入非均匀茎维度和矩映射正则化器，可以有效缓解过平滑问题，为理解和发展GNN提供了新的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Neural Sheaf Diffusion (NSD) generalizes diffusion-based Graph Neural Networks by replacing scalar graph Laplacians with sheaf Laplacians whose learned restriction maps define a task-adapted geometry. While the diffusion limit of NSD is known to be the space of global sections, the representation-theoretic structure of this harmonic space remains largely implicit. We develop a quiver-theoretic interpretation of NSD by identifying cellular sheaves on graphs with representations of the associated incidence quiver. Under this correspondence, learned sheaf geometries become points in a finite-dimensional representation space. We show that direct-sum decompositions of the underlying incidence-quiver representation induce decompositions of the harmonic space reached in the diffusion limit. This gives an algebraic interpretation of oversmoothing as representation degeneration: learned sheaves may collapse toward low-complexity summands whose global sections fail to preserve discriminative information. Building on this viewpoint, we connect sheaf diffusion to stability and moment-map principles from Geometric Invariant Theory. We introduce moment-map-inspired regularizers that bias restriction maps toward balanced representation geometries, and identify a structural obstruction in equal-stalk architectures: when $d_v = d_e$, admissibility for learnable stability parameters forces the trivial all-object summand onto a stability wall. Non-uniform stalk dimensions remove this obstruction, making adaptive stability meaningful. Experiments on heterophilic benchmarks are consistent with this mechanism: breaking stalk symmetry can reduce variance or improve validation behavior, and adaptive stability becomes more effective in selected rectangular settings. Overall, our framework reframes oversmoothing as a degeneration phenomenon in the representation geometry underlying learned sheaf diffusion.

</details>


### [49] [Muon is Not That Special: Random or Inverted Spectra Work Just as Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11181)
*Zakhar Shumaylov,Nathaël Da Costa,Peter Zaika,Bálint Mucsányi,Alex Massucco,Yoav Gelberg,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Yarin Gal,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了Muon优化器成功的几何叙事，提出Freon优化器族和荒谬的Kaon优化器，证明精确的几何结构不是影响优化性能的关键因素，真正重要的是对齐度和下降潜力这两个局部量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在非欧几里得优化中的经验成功通常被归因于与二阶方法和线性最小化预言机（LMO）理论的相似性，本文质疑这一几何解释是否真正揭示了优化性能的核心机制。

Method: 1）提出基于Schatten（拟）范数的Freon优化器族，使用最优的QDWH迭代近似方法；2）设计用随机噪声替代奇异值的Kaon优化器；3）在随机随机特征模型中分析对齐度和下降潜力两个局部量。

Result: Freon在GPT-2上的最佳Schatten参数严格位于拟范数区域，无法被任何酉不变的LMO表示；Kaon虽无几何结构但性能与Muon相当且保持收敛保证；性能由对齐度和下降潜力两个局部量控制，Muon成功在于保证步长最优性而非追踪全局几何。

Conclusion: 精确的几何结构对优化性能不是关键因素，优化器性能由局部量（对齐度和下降潜力）控制，Muon的成功源于保证步长最优性，而非追踪理想的全局几何。

Abstract: The recent empirical success of the Muon optimizer has renewed interest in non-Euclidean optimization, typically justified by similarities with second-order methods, and linear minimization oracle (LMO) theory. In this paper, we challenge this geometric narrative through three contributions, demonstrating that precise geometric structure is not the key factor affecting optimization performance. First, we introduce Freon, a family of optimizers based on Schatten (quasi-)norms, powered by a novel, provably optimal QDWH-based iterative approximation. Freon naturally interpolates between SGD and Muon, while smoothly extrapolating into the quasi-norm regime. Empirically, the best-performing Schatten parameters for GPT-2 lie strictly within the quasi-norm regime, and thus cannot be represented by any unitarily invariant LMO. Second, noting that Freon performs well across a wide range of exponents, we introduce Kaon, an absurd optimizer that replaces singular values with random noise. Despite lacking any coherent geometric structure, Kaon matches Muon's performance and retains classical convergence guarantees, proving that strict adherence to a precise geometry is practically irrelevant. Third, having shown that geometry is not the primary driver of performance, we demonstrate it is instead controlled by two local quantities: alignment and descent potential. Ultimately, each optimizer must tune its step size around these two quantities. While their dynamics are difficult to predict a-priori, evaluating them within a stochastic random feature model yields a precise insight: Muon succeeds not by tracking an ideal global geometry, but by guaranteeing step-size optimality.

</details>


### [50] [CATS: Cascaded Adaptive Tree Speculation for Memory-Limited LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11186)
*Yuning Han,Yangchenchen Jin,Dylan Zhao,Jingwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CATS框架，在内存受限设备上实现高效的自推测解码，无需额外显存即可获得最高5.08倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法假设HBM足够容纳目标模型和草稿模型，但在内存受限的边缘设备上该假设失效。自回归解码固有的内存带宽瓶颈在有限DRAM环境下更加严峻。

Method: 提出CATS（CAscaded speculaTive decoding for memory-constrained deviceS），一种自推测解码框架。基于内存预算和参数卸载模式进行级联验证与校正，确保峰值内存占用仅等于目标模型本身，无需额外空间存储草稿模型。

Result: 在五种基准测试和真实边缘设备上评估，CATS实现最高5.08倍实际加速，无生成质量损失；在边缘内存约束下性能超越SOTA方法最高1.45倍。

Conclusion: CATS成功解决了内存受限场景下的推测解码问题，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升推理速度，为边缘设备上的LLM部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Auto-regressive decoding in Large Language Models (LLMs) is inherently memory-bound: every generation step requires loading the model weights and intermediate results from memory (e.g., High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) for GPU servers), making throughput bottlenecked by memory bandwidth rather than compute. Speculative decoding addresses this by enabling parallel verification of multiple draft tokens, effectively amortizing the cost of each target-model call. However, existing speculative decoding methods are designed under the assumption that HBM is sufficiently large to hold both the target model and an auxiliary draft model simultaneously -- an assumption that breaks down on memory-constrained devices such as edge platforms with limited DRAM. We analyze the inference bottleneck in this memory-limited regime and propose CATS, a self-speculative decoding framework that conducts cascaded verification and correction based on the memory budget and parameter offloading patterns on memory-limited devices. This design maximizes token acceptance rate and end-to-end speedup while keeping the peak memory footprint on the device equal to that of the target model alone. We evaluate CATS on different models across five benchmarks on real edge devices. CATS can achieve a wall-clock speedup of up to 5.08x with no degradation in generation quality, outperforming the SOTA method by up to 1.45x under edge memory constraints.

</details>

Result: 论文成功开发了能够捕捉蛋白质结构层次特性的深度学习架构，以及能够高效搜索蛋白质复合物序列空间的算法，这些方法在改进复合物结构预测和蛋白质序列设计方面取得了进展。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and designing protein complex structures is a central problem in computational structural biology, with broad implications for understanding cellular function and developing therapeutics. This thesis investigates two fundamental aspects of this problem using deep learning: domain-specific architectures that capture the hierarchical nature of protein structures, and search algorithms that efficiently navigate the vast sequence spaces of protein complexes to identify interacting homologs for improving complex structure prediction and to design protein sequences.

</details>


### [52] [Variational Linear Attention: Stable Associative Memory for Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11196)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变分线性注意力（VLA），将记忆更新重构为在线正则化最小二乘问题，并通过归一化写入方向使状态范数自限制，在降低记忆状态Frobenius范数的同时显著提升多查询联想召回性能，且实现O(T)复杂度的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准线性注意力虽将计算复杂度从二次降至O(T)，但其记忆状态的Frobenius范数随序列长度线性增长，导致存储的关联之间产生渐进性干扰，限制了模型的长序列建模能力与召回准确性。

Method: 引入变分线性注意力（VLA），将记忆更新形式化为在线正则化最小二乘问题，利用Sherman-Morrison秩一公式自适应维护惩罚矩阵；通过将写入方向归一化为单位长度，证明递归雅可比矩阵的谱范数在所有序列长度和注意力头维度下均为1（命题2），且状态范数在有界输入下自限制（命题1）；并实现Triton融合内核以加速计算。

Result: 理论证明VLA具有状态范数自限制性质；在T=1,000时，VLA将状态Frobenius范数降低至标准线性注意力的109倍；在多查询联想召回任务中，当n_pairs<d_h（每头有效记忆范围内）取得近完美的精确匹配准确率；在增大记忆负载时，召回性能显著高于DeltaNet和标准线性注意力，在每头容量边界处保持62%准确率；Triton融合内核相比顺序Python实现加速14倍，在约43,000 tokens时延迟低于softmax注意力。

Conclusion: VLA通过在线正则化最小二乘视角与写入方向归一化，有效抑制了线性注意力中记忆状态范数增长导致的干扰问题，兼具理论稳定性与实际效率，为长序列建模提供了一种更鲁棒且高效的线性注意力变体。

Abstract: Linear attention reduces the quadratic cost of softmax attention to $\mathcal{O}(T)$, but its memory state grows as $\mathcal{O}(T)$ in Frobenius norm, causing progressive interference between stored associations. We introduce \textbf{Variational Linear Attention} (VLA), which reframes the memory update as an online regularised least-squares problem with an adaptive penalty matrix maintained via the Sherman-Morrison rank-1 formula. We prove that normalising the write direction to unit length gives the recurrence Jacobian spectral norm exactly $1$ for all sequence lengths and head dimensions (Proposition 2), and that the state norm is self-limiting under bounded inputs (Proposition 1). Empirically, VLA reduces $\|S_t\|_F$ by $109\times$ relative to standard linear attention at $T{=}1{,}000$, achieves near-perfect exact-match accuracy on multi-query associative recall within the effective per-head memory regime ($n_\text{pairs} < d_h$), maintaining substantially higher retrieval performance than DeltaNet and standard linear attention under increasing memory load, and maintains 62\% accuracy at the per-head capacity boundary. A Triton-fused kernel achieves $14\times$ speedup over sequential Python and $\mathcal{O}(T)$ scaling, crossing below softmax attention latency at approximately 43\,000 tokens.

</details>


### [53] [FeatMap: Understanding image manipulation in the feature space and its implications for feature space geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11203)
*Elias B. Krey,Nils Neukirch,Nils Strodthoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过对输入图像进行多种变换（几何、光度、局部掩码和语义编辑），探索了深度神经网络中间特征表示的几何结构。研究发现，即使是简单的共享线性映射也能有效学习从原始特征到变换后特征的映射，这表明特征空间在一定程度上呈现线性结构组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的中间特征表示是网络表达能力和适应性的基础，但其几何结构仍然缺乏深入理解。作者希望通过研究输入空间的各种变换如何映射到特征空间，来间接揭示特征表示的几何特性。

Method: 1. 在输入空间应用多种变换：几何变换、光度变换、局部掩码以及使用生成式图像编辑模型进行语义操作\n2. 设计不同类型的映射：从线性到非线性、从局部到全局的映射\n3. 评估映射的重建质量和映射表示的语义内容\n4. 分析不同特征层的映射特性，包括权重与偏差的主导地位以及线性变换的有效秩

Result: 1. 所有考虑的变换都可以学习到有效的特征空间映射\n2. 虽然全局Transformer模型通常效果最好，但共享线性模型在单个特征向量上也能达到几乎相同的重建质量，即使对于复杂的语义操作也是如此\n3. 不同特征层的映射表现出不同的权重/偏差主导性和有效秩特性\n4. 这些结果支持特征空间在一定程度上近似线性组织的假设

Conclusion: 特征空间在一定程度上呈现线性结构组织，简单的线性映射就能有效捕捉复杂的语义变换。该研究还展示了生成式图像编辑模型可以通过输入操作来加深对特征空间的理解，为未来研究提供了新的方法和视角。

Abstract: Intermediate feature representations represent the backbone for the expressivity and adaptability of deep neural networks. However, their geometric structure remains poorly understood. In this submission, we provide indirect insights into this matter by applying a broad selection of manipulations in input space, ranging from geometric and photometric transformations to local masking and semantic manipulations using generative image editing models, and assess the feasibility of learning a mapping in the feature space, mapping from the original to the manipulated feature map. To this end, we devise different types of mappings, from linear to non-linear and local to global mappings and assess both the reconstruction quality of the mapping as well as the semantic content of the mapped representations. We demonstrate the feasibility of learning such mappings for all considered transformations. While global (transformer) models that operate on the full feature map often achieve best results, we show that the same can be achieved with a shared linear model operating on a single feature vector typically with very little degradation in reconstruction quality, even for highly non-trivial semantic manipulations. We analyze the corresponding mappings across different feature layers and characterize them according to dominance of weight vs. bias and the effective rank of the linear transformations. These results provide hints for the hypothesis that the feature space is to a first degree of approximation organized in linear structures. From a broader perspective, the study demonstrates that generative image editing models might open the door to a deeper understanding of the feature space through input manipulation.

</details>


### [54] [The Scaling Law of Evaluation Failure: Why Simple Averaging Collapses Under Data Sparsity and Item Difficulty Gaps, and How Item Response Theory Recovers Ground Truth Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11205)
*Jung Min Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基准评估中常用的简单平均法在稀疏数据和高难度差异条件下会产生严重误导性排名，而项目反应理论（IRT）模型能保持高排名准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI和安全关键领域的基准评估普遍采用简单平均法进行排名，但这种方法在评估矩阵稀疏且项目难度差异大时可能产生误导性结果，这一问题在Physical AI等新兴领域尤为突出。

Method: 通过四个领域（GLUE自然语言处理、临床试验、自动驾驶安全、网络安全）的受控模拟实验，系统比较简单平均法与两参数逻辑斯蒂项目反应理论（2PL IRT）模型的排名准确性；并在稀疏度S∈[0, 0.70]和难度差异D∈[0.5, 5.0]范围内进行150条件网格扫描分析。

Result: 简单平均法的Spearman秩相关系数从100%覆盖率的ρ=1.000下降到67%覆盖率（高难度异质性）时的ρ=0.809；排名误差形成显著的S×D交互作用失败曲面（γ₃=+0.20, t=13.05）；而2PL IRT模型在所有条件下均保持ρ≥0.993的排名准确性。

Conclusion: 简单平均法在评估矩阵稀疏且难度差异大的场景下存在系统性缺陷，IRT模型提供了一种稳健的替代方案；这对Physical AI等不完整评估矩阵和极端难度差距的领域具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Benchmark evaluation across AI and safety-critical domains overwhelmingly relies on simple averaging.
