We demonstrate that this practice produces substantially misleading rankings when two conditions co-occur: (1) the evaluation matrix is sparse and (2) items vary substantially in difficulty. Through controlled simulation experiments across four domains -- NLP (GLUE), clinical drug trials, autonomous vehicle safety, and cybersecurity -- we show that Spearman rank correlation $ρ$ between simple-average rankings and ground-truth rankings degrades from $ρ= 1.000$ at 100% coverage to $ρ= 0.809$ at 67% coverage with high difficulty heterogeneity (mean over 20 seeds). A standard two-parameter logistic (2PL) Item Response Theory (IRT) model maintains $ρ\geq 0.996$ across all conditions. A 150-condition grid sweep over sparsity $S \in [0, 0.70]$ and difficulty gap $D \in [0.5, 5.0]$ confirms that ranking error forms a failure surface with a strong $S \times D$ interaction ($γ_3 = +0.20$, $t = 13.05$), while IRT maintains $ρ\geq 0.993$ throughout. We discuss implications for Physical AI benchmarking, where evaluation matrices are often incomplete and difficulty gaps are extreme.

</details>


### [55] [Measuring Five-Nines Reliability: Sample-Efficient LLM Evaluation in Saturated Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11209)
*Eungyeup Kim,Chenchen Gu,Vashisth Tiwari,J. Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交叉熵方法（CEM）的高效采样框架，通过集中于易失效输入来评估大语言模型的极端可靠性，在保持估计精度的同时将所需推理次数减少高达156倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试显示大语言模型在多项任务上表现近乎完美，但这种饱和掩盖了对可靠性的严格评估需求。在可靠性关键应用中，"五个九"(99.999%)与"三个九"(99.9%)可靠性的差距意味着失效数量增加一个数量级。然而，用标准蒙特卡洛方法估计如此罕见的失效概率需要巨大的推理成本，在有限计算预算下不可行。

Method: 观察到LLM失效呈现强系统性模式：在参数化输入空间中，小部分输入不成比例地导致了大多数失效。基于此，提出通过交叉熵方法（CEM）学习集中于易失效输入的采样分布，从而高效估计极端可靠性。

Result: 在三个LLM（Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct、gpt-oss-20b-low和Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite）上，使用参数化GSM8K模板进行评估，相比朴素均匀采样，所需推理次数减少高达156.22倍。估计结果显示，在标准基准上准确率难以区分的模型，其估计失效率可能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 可靠性是区别于准确率的独立且可测量的模型质量维度。该框架实现了对LLM极端可靠性的评估，为可靠性敏感应用中的模型选择提供了新的评估维度，填补了现有基准测试的空白。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks demonstrate the near-perfect performance of large language models (LLMs) on various tasks, this apparent saturation often obscures the need for rigorous evaluation of their reliability. In real-world deployment, however, achieving extremely high reliability (e.g., "five-nines" (99.999%) vs. "three-nines" (99.9%)) is fundamentally critical, as this gap results in an order-of-magnitude increase in failures, which is catastrophic in reliability-critical applications. Still, estimating such a rare failure probability with tight confidence bounds requires prohibitively large LLM inference sizes, making standard Monte Carlo evaluation infeasible under limited compute budgets. In this paper, we observe that LLM failures exhibit strong systematic patterns: across broad parameterized input spaces, a small subset of inputs disproportionately accounts for the majority of failures. Leveraging this observation, we propose to learn a sampling distribution concentrated on failure-prone inputs via the cross-entropy method (CEM). We evaluate our framework on three LLMs, Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct, gpt-oss-20b-low, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, across parameterized GSM8K templates and achieve up to 156.22x reduction in required inferences compared to naive uniform sampling. Our estimates reveal that models with indistinguishable accuracy on standard benchmarks can differ substantially in estimated failure rates, underscoring that reliability is a distinct and measurable axis of model quality. Our simple yet practical framework enables the evaluation of extreme reliability in LLMs, a distinct and underexplored dimension of evaluation beyond existing benchmarks, for their growing use in reliability-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [56] [Enforcing Constraints in Generative Sampling via Adaptive Correction Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11214)
*Noah Trupin,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应校正调度策略，通过状态依赖的方式智能分配投影预算，在生成采样过程中动态选择投影时机，以75%更少的校正次数获得了71.2%的全步校正收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬约束生成采样方法采用二元框架（终端投影或每步投影），忽略了投影会改变后续更新所依赖的状态分布这一根本问题。延迟投影产生的样本虽可行但与预期采样动力学不一致，仅保证可行性不足以保持预期的约束采样动力学。

Method: 将约束执行形式化为生成展开上的校正调度问题，使用单步约束缺陷作为几何失配的局部信号，引入自适应校正调度——一种状态依赖策略，将投影预算分配给最强烈扰动轨迹的步骤。终端投影和步进投影是该策略族的极限情况。

Result: 在受控流形展开和学习投影扩散采样器上，自适应调度在匹配投影预算下改善了成本-精度前沿，以75%更少的校正次数恢复了71.2%的全步校正收益。

Conclusion: 约束时机是生成采样中的一类设计变量，仅执行可行性不足以保持预期的约束采样动力学，约束时机设计应作为生成采样的核心考量因素。

Abstract: Hard constraints in generative sampling are typically enforced by projection, applied either once at the end of sampling or after every update. This binary framing overlooks a fundamental issue: projection changes the distribution of states which future updates depend on. As a result, delayed projection can produce samples that are feasible but inconsistent with the intended sampling dynamics, even after final projection. We formalize constraint enforcement as a correction scheduling problem over the generative rollout. Using one-step constraint defect as a local signal of geometric mismatch, we introduce adaptive correction scheduling, a state-dependent policy that allocates projection budget to the steps that most strongly perturb the trajectory. Terminal and stepwise projection arise as limiting cases of this family. Across controlled manifold rollouts and a learned projected diffusion sampler, adaptive scheduling improves the cost-accuracy frontier at matched projection budgets, recovering 71.2% of full stepwise benefit with 75% fewer corrections. These results show that constraint timing is a first-class design variable in generative sampling, and that enforcing feasibility alone is insufficient to preserve the intended constrained sampling dynamics.

</details>


### [57] [Leveraging RAG for Training-Free Alignment of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11217)
*John T. Halloran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的在线对齐算法RAG-Pref，通过检索增强生成利用对比信息，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下，显著提升LLM对智能体攻击的拒绝能力（平均提升3.7倍），同时保持通用人类偏好对齐任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的大语言模型（LLM）对齐算法通常依赖偏好对进行后训练，虽然广泛用于建立安全护栏和通用人类偏好对齐，但存在两大不足：一是需要大量计算资源；二是针对近期出现的智能体攻击（agentic attacks），其拒绝护栏能力显著不足。

Method: 引入检索增强生成偏好对齐算法（RAG-Pref）。这是一种基于RAG的简单对齐算法，属于在线（免训练）方法。其核心机制是在推理过程中，通过条件化优选和非优选样本（preferred and dispreferred samples），利用对比信息来引导模型输出。该方法与现有即用型软件包兼容，并可与离线（基于训练的）对齐算法结合使用。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的LLM上的实验表明：RAG-Pref与离线对齐算法结合时，对智能体攻击的拒绝率平均提升了3.7倍。相比之下，其他在线对齐算法的平均提升为2.9倍，仅使用离线对齐的平均提升仅为1.5倍。此外，与其他在线对齐方法形成鲜明对比的是，RAG-Pref在通用人类偏好对齐任务上也提升了性能，且未显著增加总体计算需求。

Conclusion: RAG-Pref是一种高效且有效的LLM对齐方法。它成功解决了传统对齐算法计算开销大且对新型攻击防御能力弱的问题，证明了利用对比信息进行检索增强是一种在不牺牲通用对齐性能或计算效率的前提下，显著提升模型安全护栏能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) alignment algorithms typically consist of post-training over preference pairs. While such algorithms are widely used to enable safety guardrails and align LLMs with general human preferences, we show that state-of-the-art alignment algorithms require significant computational resources while being far less capable of enabling refusal guardrails for recent agentic attacks. Thus, to improve refusal guardrails against such attacks without drastically increasing computational overhead, we introduce Retrieval Augmented Generation for Pref erence alignment (RAG-Pref), a simple RAG-based alignment algorithm which conditions on preferred and dispreferred samples to leverage contrastive information during inference. RAG-Pref is online (training-free), compatible with off-the-shelf packages, and, when combined with offline (training-based) alignment algorithms, enables more than an average 3.7 factor improvement in agentic attack refusals across five widely used LLMs, compared to 2.9 for other online alignment algorithms and 1.5 for offline alignment alone. We conclude by showing that, in stark contrast to other online alignment methods, RAG-Pref similarly increases performance on general human-preference alignment tasks and does not drastically increase overall computational requirements.

</details>


### [58] [ADMM-Q: An Improved Hessian-based Weight Quantizer for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11222)
*Ryan Lucas,Mehdi Makni,Xiang Meng,Adam Deng,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADMM-Q，一种基于组合ADMM的新型权重量化算法，在低比特（如W2A4）场景下显著降低LLM困惑度，可作为GPTQ等方法的即插即用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流后训练量化方法（如GPTQ和RTN）在激进量化水平（低于4比特）下模型性能显著下降，亟需一种在低比特下仍能保持高精度的量化算法。

Method: 将层级量化建模为组合优化问题，采用组合型交替方向乘子法（ADMM）框架。通过算子分裂过程连续更新权重以最小化层级重建误差，并逐步强制执行量化约束；引入惩罚调度、预条件化和局部搜索后处理等加速技巧；模块化设计，可与范围裁剪、旋转、激活缩放等技术无缝组合。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，以ADMM-Q替代GPTQ：W3A16权重量化下WikiText-2困惑度从12.85降至10.06；W4A8 SmoothQuant流程下从9.29降至8.68；W2A4KV4 SpinQuant流程下从66.11大幅降至19.42。

Conclusion: ADMM-Q在保证收敛的前提下，有效解决了低比特量化下的性能退化问题，具备通用性与可扩展性，为LLM压缩提供了更优的权重量化基线方案。

Abstract: Quantization is an effective strategy to reduce the storage and computation footprint of large language models (LLMs). Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a leading approach for compressing LLMs. Popular weight quantization procedures, including GPTQ and RTN, suffer in model utility, especially at aggressive quantization levels (sub-4-bit). We propose ADMM-Q, a novel weight quantization algorithm that considers the layer-wise quantization problem. Our algorithm is based on a combinatorial variant of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM). Our operator-splitting procedure updates weights continuously to minimize the layer-wise reconstruction error, while gradually enforcing the quantization constraints with convergence guarantees. We propose additional algorithmic enhancements (e.g., penalty scheduling, preconditioning, and a local search post-processing step) to make ADMM-Q efficient at LLM scale. ADMM-Q is modular and can be used as a drop-in replacement for any weight quantizer within existing quantization pipelines: ADMM-Q is fully composable with existing techniques including range clipping, learned or random rotations, and activation scaling. Using ADMM-Q in place of GPTQ on Qwen3-8B, we decrease WikiText-2 perplexity in: (i) the W3A16 weight-only setting (12.85 $\rightarrow$ 10.06); (ii) the W4A8 SmoothQuant procedure (9.29 $\rightarrow$ 8.68); and (iii) the W2A4KV4 SpinQuant procedure (66.11 $\rightarrow$ 19.42).

</details>


### [59] [LiBaGS: Lightweight Boundary Gap Synthesis for Targeted Synthetic Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11231)
*Abhishek Moturu,Anna Goldenberg,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LiBaGS，一种轻量级、生成器无关的合成数据选择方法，通过多指标评分和边界间隙分配规则，有针对性地筛选位于决策边界附近的高质量合成样本，有效提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据的价值取决于其能否填补训练分布中影响下游任务的关键缺失部分。现有的简单增加数据或仅基于不确定性选择的方法往往效率不高，因此需要一种能够精准定位决策边界稀疏区域并筛选高质量样本的方法。

Method: 提出了LiBaGS（Lightweight Boundary-gap Guided Selection）方法，包含四个核心组件：1）通过结合决策边界邻近度、预测不确定性、真实数据密度和支持有效性对候选合成样本进行评分；2）使用边界间隙分配规则，有针对性地选择位于决策边界附近稀疏但现实的样本；3）引入边际价值停止规则自动判断何时停止添加合成样本；4）为模糊边界附近的样本分配软标签，并使用多样性目标避免冗余样本选择。

Result: 实验结果表明，LiBaGS在准确性上优于传统的过采样方法、硬数据增强、基于不确定性和密度的消融方法，以及有针对性的生成选择标准。该方法在多个数据集和任务上展现了更好的性能提升。

Conclusion: LiBaGS证明了有针对性地选择合成样本比单纯增加数据量更有效。通过综合考虑样本的信息量和真实性，并聚焦于决策边界的稀疏区域，可以显著提升模型性能，为合成数据在机器学习中的应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Synthetic data is useful only when the added samples fill missing parts of the training distribution that matter for the downstream task. We introduce LiBaGS, a lightweight, generator-agnostic method for targeted synthetic training data selection. LiBaGS scores candidate synthetic samples by combining decision-boundary proximity, predictive uncertainty, real-data density, and support validity, so that selected samples are both informative and likely to remain on the real data manifold. We then use a boundary-gap allocation rule that targets sparse but realistic decision-boundary neighborhoods, rather than simply adding more data or selecting only the most uncertain candidates. LiBaGS also learns when enough synthetic samples have been added through a marginal-value stopping rule, assigns softer labels near ambiguous boundaries, and uses a diversity objective to avoid redundant near-duplicate selections. Experiments show that LiBaGS improves accuracy over classical oversampling, hard augmentation, uncertainty and density ablations, and targeted-generation selection criteria.

</details>


### [60] [A Comparative Study of Model Selection Criteria for Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11233)
*Ali Soltani,Gabriel Kronberger,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca,Mattia Billa,Alessandro Lucantonio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了符号回归中多种模型选择准则（AIC、AICc、BIC、MDL及Bootstrap）的性能，发现MDL在多数数据集上表现最佳，能选出测试误差最低且长度最短的模型，且MDL与BIC选择真实表达式的概率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在符号回归中，从遗传算法生成的帕累托最优解集中自动选择泛化能力强的模型仍是一个开放问题。尽管文献中存在多种基于信息论和贝叶斯方法的选择准则，但目前缺乏在不同数据条件下对这些准则性能的全面比较。

Method: 研究在七个添加高斯噪声的合成数据集上，系统比较了五种常用的模型选择准则：AIC、AICc、BIC、最小描述长度（MDL）以及Efron的Bootstrap估计。通过对真实函数进行扰动生成候选表达式，根据泛化误差和选中真实表达式的概率来评估各准则的性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，MDL在大多数数据集上表现最为稳健，能够一致地识别出测试误差最低且表达式长度最短的模型。尽管没有单一准则在所有结果中占绝对优势，但MDL和BIC在选中真实表达式（ground-truth）的概率上表现最好。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，最小描述长度（MDL）是符号回归模型选择中一种高效且可靠的准则，能够有效平衡准确性与复杂性，选出泛化能力强的模型。在追求识别真实模型结构的场景下，MDL和BIC是首选准则。

Abstract: Effective model selection is critical in symbolic regression (SR) to identify mathematical expressions that balance accuracy and complexity, and have low expected error on unseen data. Many modern implementations of genetic programming (GP) for SR generate a set of Pareto optimal candidate solutions, but reliable automatic selection of solutions that generalize well remains an open issue. Current literature offers various information-theoretic and Bayesian approaches, yet comprehensive comparisons of their performance across different data regimes are limited. This study presents a systematic empirical comparison of widely used selection criteria: the Akaike information criterion (AIC), the corrected AIC (AICc), the Bayesian information criterion (BIC), minimum description length (MDL), as well as Efron's bootstrap estimate for the in-sample prediction error on seven synthetic datasets with Gaussian noise. We rank candidate expressions generated by perturbing ground-truth functions to assess generalization error and selection probability of the ground-truth expression. Our findings reveal that MDL consistently identifies models with the lowest test error and the shortest length across most datasets. While no single criterion dominates all results, MDL and BIC produced the highest probability of selecting the ground-truth expressions.

</details>


### [61] [Internalizing Curriculum Judgment for LLM Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11235)
*Han Zheng,Yining Ma,Karthick Gunasekaran,Bharathan Balaji,Zheng Du,Shiv Vitaladevuni,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: METIS提出了一种将课程学习判断内化为LLM原生能力的框架，通过预测提示词的信息量动态调整训练分配，在多个基准上实现了优越性能并加速收敛达67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM强化微调(RFT)中，课程学习方法依赖于手工设计的启发式规则或辅助模型来进行课程判断，存在与策略训练动态不匹配的风险。需要一种能够内在化课程判断、与训练过程紧密对齐的新方法。

Method: 提出METIS框架，将课程判断内化为模型的原生能力。核心发现是提示内奖励方差可以有效衡量提示词的信息量。METIS基于近期训练结果作为轻量级上下文学习示例来预测该指标，利用这种内在自我判断动态决定训练分配。同时，通过联合优化标准RFT奖励和自我判断奖励，形成判断与优化的闭环，实现元认知学习。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成到智能体函数调用等多个离散和连续RFT基准测试中，METIS始终表现优异，同时将收敛速度提升高达67%。通过绕过手工启发式规则和辅助模型，建立了一个简单、闭环且高效的课程内化范式。

Conclusion: METIS成功实现了课程判断的内化，建立了简单、闭环、高效的LLM强化微调课程学习范式，为LLM训练中的元认知能力发展提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: In LLM Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT), curriculum learning drives both efficiency and performance. Yet, current methods externalize curriculum judgment via handcrafted heuristics or auxiliary models, risking misalignment with the policy's training dynamics. In this paper, we introduce METIS (METacognitive Internalized Self-judgment), a novel framework that internalizes curriculum judgment as a native capability. Leveraging a critical observation that within-prompt reward variance effectively gauges prompt informativeness, METIS predicts this metric based on recent training outcomes as lightweight in-context learning examples. This intrinsic self-judgment then dynamically dictates the training allocation. Moreover, METIS closes the loop between judgment and optimization by jointly optimizing the standard RFT rewards and a self-judgment reward. This allows the policy to learn what to learn next, as a form of metacognition. Across extensive discrete and continuous RFT benchmarks from mathematical reasoning, code generation, to agentic function-calling, METIS consistently delivers superior performance while accelerating convergence by up to 67%. By bypassing handcrafted heuristics and auxiliary models, our work establishes a simple, closed-loop, and highly efficient curriculum internalization paradigm for LLM reinforcement fine-tuning.

</details>


### [62] [DeconDTN-Toolkit: A Library for Evaluation and Enhancement of Robustness to Provenance Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11237)
*Yongsen Tan,Zhecheng Sheng,Xiruo Ding,Serguei V. S. Pakhomov,Trevor Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了数据来源偏移与反事实不变性、不变学习之间的理论联系，提出了鲁棒学习目标，并开发了DeconDTN-Toolkit工具包用于评估和修复数据来源偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管分布偏移研究已有大量工作，但数据来源偏移（provenance shift）——即部署时数据源与标签之间的关系发生变化——仍然缺乏深入理解和有效解决方案，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 建立数据来源偏移、反事实不变性和不变学习三者之间的形式化联系，据此推导出面向鲁棒性的学习目标；同时开发DeconDTN-Toolkit评估与修复工具套件，用于模拟不同程度的数据来源偏移，同时保持现有基准的训练协议和基础设施。

Result: 揭示了经验风险最小化（ERM）在数据来源偏移下的脆弱性，提出了一种鲁棒的分布外性能指标，并对现有算法进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: 本文为刻画数据来源混淆问题提供了理论基础和实践工具，并实现了缓解该问题的方法，为后续研究奠定了理论与工具基础。

Abstract: Despite the burgeoning body of work on distribution shifts, provenance shift-where the relationship between data source and label changes at deployment-remains poorly understood and under-addressed. In this paper, we establish a formal connection between provenance shift, counterfactual invariance, and invariant learning to derive a learning objective for robustness. We then introduce \textsc{DeconDTN-Toolkit}, a specialized evaluation and remediation suite designed to simulate provenance shifts of varying degrees while maintaining the training protocol and the infrastructure of existing benchmarks. We reveal the vulnerability of Empirical Risk Minimization under provenance shift, introduce a robust out-of-distribution performance indicator, and conduct a comprehensive evaluation on existing algorithms. Our work provides both the theoretical grounding and the practical tools necessary to characterize the problem of confounding by provenance, and implementations of methods to mitigate it.

</details>


### [63] [Extending Kernel Trick to Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11239)
*Zhenhuan Sun,Shahrokh Valaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了影响函数的对偶表示，计算复杂度与数据集规模而非模型规模成正比，适用于大模型小数据集场景，但仅限于可线性化模型且需构建大矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始影响函数在参数空间评估的计算复杂度与模型规模相关，当模型规模远大于数据集规模或参数空间评估不可行时，原始方法效率低下或无法使用，因此需要一种更高效的替代方法。

Method: 提出影响函数的对偶表示（dual representation），其计算复杂度与数据集规模成正比；通过分析推导和实验验证，使用该对偶形式估计数据点移除引起的参数变化、模型输出和损失变化。

Result: 分析与实验表明，当模型规模相对于数据集规模较大，或在参数空间评估原始影响函数不可行时，该对偶表示可作为原始影响函数的高效替代方案；但该方法仅适用于可线性化模型（训练过程行为可被线性化近似），且需要构建一个规模随模型输出维度与数据集大小乘积增长的矩阵。

Conclusion: 对偶影响函数为大模型小数据集场景提供了高效可行的影响估计方法，但其适用范围受限于可线性化模型和矩阵存储开销，在大输出维度或大数据集下可能面临挑战。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a dual representation of the influence functions, whose computational complexity scales with dataset size rather than model size. Both analytically and experimentally, we show that this representation can be an efficient alternative to the original influence functions for estimating changes in parameters, model outputs and loss due to data point removal, when model size is large relative to dataset size, or when evaluating the original influence functions in parameter space is infeasible. The dual representation, however, is limited to linearizable models, which are models whose behavior can be approximated by their linearizations throughout training, and requires materializing a matrix, whose size grows with the product of model output dimension and dataset size.

</details>


### [64] [Support-Proximity Augmented Diffusion Estimation for Offline Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11246)
*Yonghan Yang,Ye Yuan,Zipeng Sun,Linfeng Du,Bowei He,Haolun Wu,Can Chen,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPADE框架，通过扩散模型建模前向似然并结合校准模块和支撑邻近正则化，解决了离线黑盒优化中的分布外外推问题，在Design-Bench和LLM数据混合优化基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线黑盒优化面临分布外(OOD)外推的根本性挑战。现有方法存在局限性：逆方法难以处理从分数到设计的映射的病态性质；前向方法往往缺乏分布表达能力，无法有效量化不确定性。

Method: 提出SPADE框架，以前向代理建模的视角重新思考，使用扩散模型建模前向似然p(y|x)，包含两个关键增强：(1)校准扩散估计模块，强制统计矩和成对排序的全局一致性；(2)支撑邻近正则化机制，通过kNN密度估计隐式内化数据流形约束p(x)。

Result: 理论上证明了正则化方法与最大化具有有效设计先验的贝叶斯后验具有一阶等价性。实验上，SPADE在Design-Bench任务和LLM数据混合优化基准上均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SPADE成功地将前向代理建模重新构架为条件生成建模问题，通过扩散模型和针对性增强模块有效解决了离线黑盒优化中的核心挑战，为该领域提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Offline black-box optimization aims to discover novel designs with high property scores using only a static dataset, a task fundamentally challenged by the out-of-distribution (OOD) extrapolation problem. Existing approaches typically bifurcate into inverse methods, which struggle with the ill-posed nature of mapping scores to designs, and forward methods, which often lack the distributional expressivity to quantify uncertainty effectively. In this work, we propose SPADE (Support-Proximity Augmented Diffusion Estimation), a novel framework that reimagines forward surrogate modeling through the lens of conditional generative modeling. SPADE models the forward likelihood p(y|x) using a diffusion model, but with two critical enhancements to tailor it for optimization: (1) a Calibrated Diffusion Estimation module that enforces global consistency in statistical moments and pairwise rankings, and (2) a Support-Proximity Regularization mechanism that implicitly internalizes the data manifold constraint p(x) via kNN-based density estimation. Theoretically, we prove that our regularization is first-order equivalent to maximizing a Bayesian posterior with a valid design prior. Empirically, SPADE achieves state-of-the-art performance across Design-Bench tasks and an LLM data mixture optimization benchmark.

</details>


### [65] [A Proof-of-Concept Simulation-Driven Digital Twin Framework for Decision-Aware Diabetes Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11247)
*Zarrin Monirzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个概念验证性的数字孪生框架，利用基准临床数据、合成时间增强和持续葡萄糖监测（CGM）分析进行仿真驱动的糖尿病建模，旨在生成可解释的模拟轨迹，并为未来的医疗数字孪生研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的糖尿病预测模型通常仅关注临床验证的结果，而缺乏对病程轨迹和干预措施效果的动态模拟能力。本研究旨在弥合预测模型与决策支持之间的差距，开发一个能够生成可解释的模拟轨迹并支持反事实仿真分析的框架。

Method: 该研究构建了一个数字孪生框架，整合了公开的基准临床数据，并采用合成时间增强技术来扩展数据场景。研究利用持续葡萄糖监测（CGM）数据进行分析，通过结合预测模型与反事实仿真，模拟了不同的时间行为和干预效果。

Result: 评估结果表明，该框架能够成功地将预测与反事实仿真相结合，展示了生成可解释的时间轨迹和模拟干预措施的可行性，证明了该方法在决策感知分析方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究虽然尚不声称具备临床就绪状态，但成功展示了仿真驱动数字孪生系统在医疗领域的可行性。该工作为未来在医疗保健领域开发更成熟、具有临床应用价值的仿真驱动数字孪生系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a proof-of-concept digital twin framework for simulation-driven diabetes modeling using benchmark clinical data, synthetic temporal augmentation, and illustrative continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) analysis. Unlike traditional predictive models, the framework focuses on generating interpretable simulated trajectories rather than clinically validated outcomes. Evaluation is conducted using a public dataset combined with controlled synthetic scenarios to illustrate temporal behavior and intervention effects. Results illustrate the feasibility of integrating prediction with counterfactual simulation for decision-aware analysis. This work does not claim clinical readiness but provides a foundation for future research on simulation-driven digital twin systems in healthcare.

</details>


### [66] [Curriculum Learning-Guided Progressive Distillation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11260)
*Jincheng Cao,Fanzhi Zeng,Leqi Liu,Aryan Mokhtari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLPD框架，通过联合考虑数据学习顺序和教师-学生容量匹配问题，成功解决了“强教师无法训练出强学生”的反直觉现象，显著提升了知识蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识蒸馏方法忽视了训练数据的学习顺序和教师-学生模型间的容量不匹配这两个关键因素，导致了“更强的教师模型反而无法训练出更好的学生模型”这一反直觉现象，限制了蒸馏性能的上限。

Method: 提出课程学习引导的渐进式蒸馏框架（CLPD）。该方法构建显式课程，按从易到难的顺序组织训练数据；同时应用隐式课程，通过逐步调度容量递增的教师模型来调整监督信号。该框架具有模块化特性，可无缝集成到标准蒸馏算法中。

Result: 在推理基准测试上的实验结果表明，CLPD在多种设置下始终优于标准蒸馏、仅使用数据排序以及仅使用教师调度的方法，证明了联合考虑数据排序和教师容量的有效性。

Conclusion: 在将推理能力蒸馏到小型语言模型时，联合考虑数据排序和教师容量至关重要。CLPD框架通过显式组织数据难度和隐式调整教师强度，有效对齐了两者，为解决容量不匹配问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is a key technique for transferring the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) into smaller, more efficient student models. Existing distillation approaches often overlook two critical factors: the learning order of training data and the capacity mismatch between teacher and student models. This oversight limits distillation performance, as manifested by the counter-intuitive phenomenon where stronger teachers fail to produce better students. In this work, we propose Curriculum Learning-Guided Progressive Distillation (CLPD), a unified framework that explicitly accounts for both factors by aligning data difficulty with teacher strength. CLPD constructs an explicit curriculum by organizing training examples from easy to hard, while simultaneously applying an implicit curriculum over supervision signals by progressively scheduling teachers of increasing capacity. Our framework is modular and can be integrated into standard distillation algorithms with minimal overhead. Empirical results on the reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CLPD consistently outperforms standard distillation, data ordering alone, and teacher scheduling alone across multiple settings. These findings highlight the importance of jointly considering data ordering and teacher capacity when distilling reasoning abilities into small language models.

</details>


### [67] [Latent Chain-of-Thought Improves Structured-Data Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11262)
*Carson Dudley,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将“思维链”（CoT）概念引入时间序列和表格数据，提出了一种潜在思维链机制。实验表明，该方法在36个数据集上显著优于基准模型，证明了CoT是扩展结构化数据测试时计算的有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受思维链（Chain-of-thought）和测试时计算在增强语言模型推理能力方面取得成功的启发，本文旨在探索将潜在思维链、模型深度及循环机制应用于时间序列和表格数据的预测任务中，以提升结构化数据的处理性能。

Method: 提出了一种循环方案：结构化数据Transformer在进行初始前向传播后，将其查询位置的隐藏状态压缩为反馈标记，并将其附加到输入中进行再次处理。这允许在最终预测前进行多轮潜在计算，从而实现对潜在思维链的模拟。

Result: 在36个数据集（9个时间序列、27个表格数据）的实验中，潜在思维链模型在8/9的时间序列数据集上取得提升（平均增益+10.99%），在22/27的表格数据集上取得提升（平均增益+5.31%）。总体而言，CoT模型在各项设置中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 思维链是扩展结构化数据测试时计算的一个有效维度，能够显著提升模型在时间序列预测和表格预测任务上的性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought and more broadly test-time compute are known to augment the expressive capabilities of language models and have led to major innovations in reasoning. Motivated by this success, this paper explores latent chain-of-thought as well as the impact of depth and looping for time-series and tabular data. We propose a recurrent scheme in which a structured-data transformer, after an initial forward pass, compresses its query-position hidden states into feedback tokens that are appended to the input and processed again, allowing multiple rounds of latent computation before prediction. We compare CoT models against a same-depth no-CoT baseline, a deeper baseline matched to the CoT model in effective depth, and a looped transformer with weight-tied recurrence but no additional chain-of-thought tokens. Across 36 datasets in time-series forecasting and tabular prediction, latent chain-of-thought improves over the baseline on 8/9 time-series datasets (+10.99\% average gain) and 22/27 tabular datasets (+5.31\% average gain). Across both settings, the CoT models perform the best on average. These results demonstrate that chain-of-thought is a useful axis for scaling test-time compute for structured data.

</details>


### [68] [Localization Boosting for Growth Markets: Mitigating Cross-Locale Behavioral Bias in Learning-to-Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11272)
*Suryaa Veerabathiran Seran,Ashwin Naresh Kumar,Tracy Holloway King,Jing Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多语言场景下LTR模型存在的跨地域曝光偏差问题，提出了结合行为监督、VLM相关性信号和本地化提升的多目标框架，有效提升了相关性与本地内容可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adobe Express在国际扩张中，LTR模型主要基于美国用户的行为反馈训练，导致非美国地区的模板被美国流行内容主导。这种跨地域曝光偏差抑制了本地内容的可发现性，降低了增长地区的排序质量。

Method: 提出了一种多目标框架，结合了行为监督（点击数据）、VLM（视觉-语言模型）评分的相关性标签作为辅助监督，以及本地化感知的提升机制，以解耦曝光偏差与语义监督。

Result: 在五个地区进行的实验表明，该模型在提升内容相关性的同时，恢复了稳定的本地化内容可见性，证明了将曝光与语义监督解耦的重要性。

Conclusion: 单纯的点击训练会抑制具有语义信息的本地化特征，而引入VLM辅助监督虽能改善语义对齐但无法保持本地内容可见性；所提出的多目标框架成功平衡了相关性与本地化需求。

Abstract: Adobe Express is expanding internationally, but the US has a disproportionately large content supply and interaction volume. Learning-to-rank (LTR) models trained primarily on behavioral feedback inherit this imbalance: templates popular in US are over-served in non-US locales. This cross-locale exposure bias suppresses local content discoverability and degrades ranking quality in growth locales.
  We show that click-only training suppresses semantically informative localization features. Adding vision-language model (VLM) graded relevance labels as auxiliary supervision alongside clicks improves semantic alignment but does not preserve local content visibility. We propose a multi-objective framework combining behavioral supervision, VLM-derived relevance signals, and locale-aware boosting. Across five locales, the resulting model improves relevance while restoring stable localization, demonstrating the importance of disentangling exposure from semantic supervision.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Similarity: Temporal Operator Attention for Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11287)
*Jevon Twitty,Vinh Pham,Nitiwith Rotchanarak,Viresh Pati,Yubin Kim,Shihao Yang,Jiecheng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时序算子注意力（TOA）框架，通过引入可学习的序列空间算子解决标准注意力机制在处理符号变换和振荡变换时的能力不足，在多个时间序列任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中存在一个持续的悖论：结构简单的MLP和线性模型往往优于高容量的Transformer。作者认为这种差距源于序列建模原语的不匹配：许多时间序列动态由全局时序算子（如滤波和谐波结构）控制，而标准注意力将每个输出表示为输入的凸组合，限制了其表示时间信号处理中基本的符号变换和振荡变换的能力。作者将这种局限性形式化为softmax注意力中的单纯形约束混合瓶颈，这在算子驱动的时间序列任务中尤为限制性。

Method: 提出时序算子注意力（TOA）框架，该框架通过显式的、可学习的序列空间算子增强注意力机制，能够在保持输入依赖自适应性的同时实现跨时间的直接符号混合。为了使密集的$N \times N$算子实用化，引入了随机算子正则化（Stochastic Operator Regularization），这是一种高方差丢弃机制，能够稳定训练并防止平凡的记忆化。该方法被集成到标准骨干网络如PatchTST和iTransformer中。

Result: 在预测、异常检测和分类基准测试中，TOA在集成到PatchTST和iTransformer等标准骨干网络时持续提升性能，特别是在重建密集型任务中表现出强劲的提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，显式算子学习是有效时间序列建模的关键要素，为理解和改进时间序列模型架构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: A persistent paradox in time-series forecasting is that structurally simple MLP and linear models often outperform high-capacity Transformers. We argue that this gap arises from a mismatch in the sequence-modeling primitive: while many time-series dynamics are governed by global temporal operators (e.g., filtering and harmonic structure), standard attention forms each output as a convex combination of inputs. This restricts its ability to represent signed and oscillatory transformations that are fundamental to temporal signal processing. We formalize this limitation as a simplex-constrained mixing bottleneck in softmax attention, which becomes especially restrictive for operator-driven time-series tasks. To address this, we propose $\textbf{Temporal Operator Attention (TOA)}$, a framework that augments attention with explicit, learnable sequence-space operators, enabling direct signed mixing across time while preserving input-dependent adaptivity. To make dense $N \times N$ operators practical, we introduce Stochastic Operator Regularization, a high-variance dropout mechanism that stabilizes training and prevents trivial memorization. Across forecasting, anomaly detection, and classification benchmarks, TOA consistently improves performance when integrated into standard backbones such as PatchTST and iTransformer, with particularly strong gains in reconstruction-heavy tasks. These results suggest that explicit operator learning is a key ingredient for effective time-series modeling.

</details>


### [70] [Quotient-Categorical Representations for Bellman-Compatible Average-Reward Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11289)
*Ege C. Kaya,Aliasghar Pourghani,Vijay Gupta,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个具有严格理论保证的平均奖励分布强化学习框架，通过商空间构造解决了偏置定义的不适定问题，并在多种设定下证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均奖励强化学习中，偏置的定义仅在一个加性常数内是确定的，这种对称性使得在实数域上构建直接的分布型类比变得不适定（ill-posed），阻碍了分布强化学习方法在平均奖励设定下的应用。

Method: 作者引入了商空间（quotient-space）表述，将状态索引的偏置分布识别为具有共同平移对称性的等价类，并提出了一种尊重该对称性的分类参数化方法。在此基础上，定义了投影平均奖励分布算子，并研究了采样递归及其均值场映射。对于未知增益的情况，设计了带有在线增益估计器的耦合递归方案。

Result: 在理想化的中心奖励设定下，单状态时序差分更新在独立同分布和马尔可夫采样下均具有几乎必然收敛性和有限迭代残差界。对于未知增益情形，证明了耦合方案的非扩展性和马尔可夫收敛性。此外，证明了同步精确更新在商分布层面上是增益无关的。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将分布强化学习扩展到平均奖励设定，提供了严谨的商空间数学框架，揭示了理想商分布与实际网格分类表示之间的结构差异，为后续算法设计和理论分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Average-reward reinforcement learning requires estimating the gain and the bias, which is defined only up to an additive constant. This makes direct distributional analogues ill-posed on the real line. We introduce a quotient-space formulation in which state-indexed bias laws are identified up to a common translation, together with a categorical parameterization that respects this symmetry. On this quotient-categorical space, we define a projected average-reward distributional operator and show that it is well-defined, non-expansive in a coordinate Cramér metric, and admits fixed points. We then study sampled recursions whose mean-field maps are asynchronous relaxations of this operator. In an idealized centered-reward setting, a one-state temporal-difference update enjoys almost sure convergence together with finite-iteration residual bounds under both i.i.d. and Markovian sampling. When the gain is unknown, we augment the recursion with an online gain estimator, and prove non-expansiveness and Markovian convergence of the resulting coupled scheme. Finally, we show that synchronous exact updates are gain-independent at the quotient-law level, isolating a structural contrast between ideal quotient distributions and practical fixed-grid categorical representations.

</details>


### [71] [Optimal Representations for Generalized Contrastive Learning with Imbalanced Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11291)
*Thuan Nguyen,Shuchin Aeron,D. Richard Brown,Prakash Ishwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论刻画了类别不平衡下对比学习最优表示的几何结构，发现了Minority Collapse现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Neural Collapse理论仅适用于类别平衡的场景，而实际应用中类别不平衡普遍存在。因此需要研究类别不平衡情况下对比学习最优表示的几何特性。

Method: 针对类别不平衡场景和一大类对比学习损失函数，理论证明了同类样本会塌缩到类均值，其几何结构呈现由类别比例决定的角对称性，可通过凸优化问题求解；针对极端不平衡情况进行了解析分析。

Result: 证明了同类样本塌缩到类均值，几何结构具有角对称性；发现了Minority Collapse现象：当类别不平衡超过阈值时，少数类样本会塌缩到单一向量，该阈值取决于损失函数的正则性和负样本数量。

Conclusion: 本文为类别不平衡下的对比学习提供了理论框架，揭示了Minority Collapse这一重要现象，为理解和改进不平衡场景下的表示学习提供了理论基础，并指出了若干开放性问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a computable characterization of the geometry of optimal representations in Contrastive Learning (CL) when the classes are imbalanced. When classes are balanced and the representation dimension is greater than the number of classes, it is well-known that the optimal representations exhibit Neural Collapse (NC), i.e., representations from the same class collapse to their class means and the class means form an Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF). For imbalanced classes and a large, generalized family of CL losses, we prove that the optimal representations of all samples from the same class collapse to their class means and their geometry exhibits an angular symmetry structure that is determined by the relative class proportions. In general, we show that the geometry can be determined by solving a convex optimization problem. Exploiting this symmetry structure, we analytically investigate a special case where class imbalance is extreme and prove that CL exhibits a phenomenon called Minority Collapse (MC) where all samples from the minority classes (classes with small probabilities) collapse into a single vector, whenever the class imbalance exceeds a threshold, which in turn depends on the regularity properties of the CL loss used and on the number of negative samples. Numerical results are provided to illustrate these phenomena and corroborate the theoretical results. We conclude by identifying a number of open problems.

</details>


### [72] [Primal Generation, Dual Judgment: Self-Training from Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11299)
*Yizhu Jiao,Ruixiang Zhang,Richard Bai,Jiawei Han,Ronan Collobert,Yizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DuST框架，利用测试时采样产生的对偶判断空间进行自训练，通过排序学习将判断能力转化为生成能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代码生成在原始空间训练，反馈稀疏（仅有通过/失败信号）；测试时采样产生的候选程序对比信息在推理后被丢弃，但这些相对正确性信息能提供更丰富的训练信号。

Method: DuST框架：从模型自身分布采样候选程序，通过沙盒执行标注正确性，保留成功与失败混合的组，使用GRPO训练模型按执行正确性对候选排序。训练目标纯粹是判别式的，不直接奖励生成正确程序。

Result: 在五个模型（4B-30B）上，DuST持续提升LiveCodeBench Best-of-4性能。Qwen3-30B-Thinking上：判断质量提升+6.2 NDCG，pass@1提升+3.1，Best-of-4提升+4.1；训练模型的单次采样达到基础模型Best-of-4性能。SFT对比实验验证了在线策略RL是对偶学习迁移到生成能力的关键机制。

Conclusion: 对偶判断空间的自训练能有效同时提升模型的判断能力和生成能力，在线策略强化学习是对偶空间学习迁移回原始生成空间的关键机制。

Abstract: Code generation is typically trained in the primal space of programs: a model produces a candidate solution and receives sparse execution feedback, often a single pass/fail bit. Test-time scaling enriches the inference procedure by sampling multiple candidates and judging among them, but the comparative information this process reveals is discarded after inference. We argue that this information defines a dual judgment space that provides a far richer training signal: the model learns not from an isolated success or failure, but from the relative correctness structure across its own plausible attempts, identifying which succeed, which fail, and what distinguishes them. We introduce DuST (Dual Self-Training), a framework for self-training from the dual judgment space. DuST samples candidate programs from the model's own distribution, labels them through sandbox execution, retains groups containing both successes and failures, and trains the model to rank candidates by execution correctness using GRPO. The objective is purely discriminative: the model is never directly rewarded for generating correct programs. Dual self-training improves both judgment and generation. Across five models spanning two families and three scales (4B to 30B), DuST consistently improves Best-of-4 test-time scaling on LiveCodeBench. For Qwen3-30B-Thinking on LiveCodeBench v6, judgment quality improves by +6.2 NDCG, single-sample pass@1 improves by +3.1, and Best-of-4 accuracy improves by +4.1. The trained model's single rollout matches the base model's Best-of-4 performance. SFT on the same ranking data improves judgment without improving generation, confirming that on-policy RL is the mechanism that transfers dual-space learning back into primal generation.

</details>


### [73] [A Theory of Time-Sensitive Language Generation: Sparse Hallucination Beats Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11302)
*Atul Ganju,Travis McVoy,Shaddin Dughmi,Shang-Hua Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有截止期限的语言生成问题。证明了强一致性生成器无法实现及时生成，但在幻觉率随时间消失的弱条件下，可以实现对任何超线性截止函数的最优密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习中，归纳偏置通常倾向于更简单或更合理的输出。基于此，研究者希望更高排名的字符串能够更早被生成，并引入了截止函数的概念——根据字符串在目标语言中的排名来规定其必须被生成的最后期限。

Method: 研究在全局字符串偏好排序下的语言生成问题，在追求广度的同时增加了及时性要求。首先证明了一致性生成器无法实现及时生成，然后研究了在幻觉率随时间消失这一最温和的自然松弛条件下，如何规避不可能性结果。

Result: 证明了最终一致性生成器在强意义上无法实现及时生成。在幻觉率随时间消失的条件下，可以针对任何超线性截止函数实现最优密度。同时证明了这一结果的紧致性：线性截止函数和消失幻觉率条件下无法实现及时生成。

Conclusion: 及时生成与强一致性不兼容，但通过允许幻觉率随时间消失这一温和放松，可以实现超线性截止条件下的最优生成性能，揭示了及时性要求的理论边界。

Abstract: We study language generation in the limit under a global preference ordering on strings, as introduced by Kleinberg and Wei. As in [arXiv:2504.14370, arXiv:2511.05295], we aim for \emph{breadth}, but impose an additional requirement of timeliness: higher-ranked strings should be generated earlier. A string is then only credited if it is generated before a deadline, where its deadline is defined by a function that maps a string's rank in the target language to the time by which it must be produced. This is in keeping with a central consideration in machine learning, where inductive bias favors ``simpler'' or ``more plausible'' outputs, all else being equal. We show that timely generation is impossible in a strong sense for eventually consistent generators -- the protagonists of most prior related work. Under what is perhaps the mildest natural relaxation of consistency, a hallucination rate that vanishes over time, we show that we can circumvent our impossibility result. In particular, we can achieve optimal density with respect to any superlinear deadline function. We also show this is tight by ruling out timely generation with linear deadlines and vanishing hallucination rate.

</details>


### [74] [Couple to Control: Joint Initial Noise Design in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11311)
*Jing Jia,Liyue Shen,Guanyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出耦合初始噪声设计框架，通过构造边际标准高斯但样本间存在依赖关系的初始噪声，在无额外采样成本下提升扩散模型的生成多样性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型假设批量生成的初始噪声相互独立，作者认为该假设仅为更广泛有效联合噪声设计的一个特例，缺乏对样本间依赖关系的显式控制，限制了生成多样性与可控性。

Method: 提出耦合初始噪声设计框架，保持每个噪声边际分布为标准高斯以确保与预训练扩散模型兼容，同时根据任务需求显式设计样本间的依赖结构，将初始噪声控制从单样本种子选择扩展为多样本依赖结构设计。

Result: 排斥型高斯耦合在SD1.5、SDXL和SD3上显著提升生成多样性，同时保持提示词对齐与图像质量；在相同采样成本下匹配或超越近期测试时噪声优化基线；子空间耦合支持固定前景的背景生成，可调节前景保真度与背景多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 耦合初始噪声为扩散模型提供了一个通用的噪声设计框架，统一了现有方法并催生新的构造，无需额外采样成本即可提升生成效果，且可作为优化流程的结构化初始化，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion models typically generate image batches from independent Gaussian initial noises. We argue that this independence assumption is only one choice within a broader class of valid joint noise designs. Instead, one can specify a coupling of the initial noises: each noise remains marginally standard Gaussian, so the pretrained diffusion model receives the same single-sample input distribution, while the dependence across samples is chosen by design. This reframes initial-noise control from selecting or optimizing individual seeds to designing the dependence structure of a multi-sample gallery. This view gives a general framework for initial-noise design, covering several existing methods as special cases and leading naturally to new coupled-noise constructions. Coupled noise can improve generation on its own without adding sampling cost, and it is flexible enough to serve as a structured initialization for optimization-based pipelines when additional computation is available. Empirically, repulsive Gaussian coupling improves gallery diversity on SD1.5, SDXL, and SD3 while largely preserving prompt alignment and image quality. It matches or outperforms recent test-time noise-optimization baselines on several diversity metrics at the same sampling cost as independent generation. Subspace couplings also support fixed-object background generation, producing diverse, natural backgrounds compared with specialized inpainting baselines, with a tunable trade-off in foreground fidelity.

</details>


### [75] [Error whitening: Why Gauss-Newton outperforms Newton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11316)
*Maricela Best McKay,Nathan P. Lawrence,Brian Wetton,R. Bhushan Gopaluni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从函数空间视角揭示了广义高斯-牛顿(GGN)方法的核心优势——“误差白化”效应，即通过投影消除参数化扭曲($JJ^\top$被单位矩阵替代)，从而在监督学习、物理信息深度学习等领域显著优于牛顿法、Adam和Muon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管高斯-牛顿矩阵被视为Hessian的正半定近似，但实证证据显示高斯-牛顿下降优于牛顿法。作者旨在从函数空间视角解释这一反直觉现象的理论根源。

Method: 采用函数空间视角进行理论分析：1) 证明GGN矩阵将函数空间中的牛顿方向投影到模型切空间；2) 揭示预测-目标失配的演化受参数化矩阵$JJ^\top$影响；3) 提出并验证“误差白化”效应——投影有效将$JJ^\top$替换为单位矩阵，消除参数化扭曲。

Result: 理论发现：GGN通过投影消除模型参数化带来的失真，使预测-目标失配仅由损失结构和优化器投影决定，这一特性严格区分了高斯-牛顿法与牛顿法。实证验证：在监督学习、物理信息深度学习和近似动态规划案例中，高斯-牛顿优化器准确遵循理论预测的函数空间动态，性能优于牛顿法、Adam和Muon。

Conclusion: 高斯-牛顿法的优越性源于其独特的“误差白化”性质，能在函数空间中消除参数化带来的扭曲影响，这为其在多种深度学习任务中的优异表现提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The Gauss-Newton matrix is widely viewed as a positive semidefinite approximation of the Hessian, yet mounting empirical evidence shows that Gauss-Newton descent outperforms Newton's method. We adopt a function space perspective to analyze this phenomenon. We show that the generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) matrix projects the Newton direction in function space onto the model's tangent space, while a Jacobian-only variant obtained by applying the least squares Gauss-Newton matrix to non-least squares losses projects the function space loss gradient onto this same tangent space. Both projections eliminate distortions from the model's parameterization. Specifically, the evolution of the prediction-target mismatch depends on the model's parameterization through the matrix $JJ^\top$ where $J$ is the Jacobian of the model with respect to its parameters. The projections effectively replace $JJ^\top$ with the identity. We call this effect error whitening. Once the parameterization is removed, the prediction-target mismatch evolves according to dynamics dictated by the structure of the loss and the projection produced by the optimizer. Error whitening is a special property of Gauss-Newton descent that rigorously distinguishes it from Newton's method. We empirically demonstrate that Gauss-Newton optimizers follow the theoretically predicted function space dynamics and outperforms Newton's method, Adam, and Muon across case studies spanning supervised learning, physics-informed deep learning, and approximate dynamic programming.

</details>


### [76] [$\varepsilon$-Good Action Identification in Fixed-Budget Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11324)
*Yinan Li,Tuan Nguyen,Kwang-Sung Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次针对固定预算下的最大-最小动作识别问题提出了ε不可知算法，证明了错误识别概率呈指数衰减，并揭示了该问题与标准多臂老虎机在难度结构上的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究由近似规划问题驱动，旨在解决深度为2的最大-最小树结构中的固定预算最大-最小动作识别问题。学习者需在有限样本预算内，识别出其最小叶节点值最大的子树。已有工作主要集中在标准最佳臂识别，而针对最大-最小树结构的固定预算识别问题尚无有效的算法保证。

Method: 提出了一种ε不可知算法，该算法无需ε作为输入，但能对所有有意义的ε获得实例相关的误差界限。算法通过合理分配样本到各叶节点，利用跨子树和子树内部的差距信息，实现高效的子树识别。当每个子树仅有一个叶节点时，问题退化为标准固定预算最佳臂识别，分析结果涵盖了已知的减半风格方法，并给出了Successive Rejects方法的新保证。

Result: 主要结果表明，错误识别概率以exp(-Θ̃(T/H₂(ε)))的速率衰减，其中H₂(ε)刻画了跨子树和子树内部差距。算法在退化为标准问题时，恢复了已知ε-good保证并提供了新的理论结果。下界方面，证明了最大-最小识别问题具有与标准K臂老虎机不同的难度结构。

Conclusion: 本文首次为固定预算下的最大-最小动作识别问题提供了可证明的算法保证，填补了该领域的理论空白。所提算法具有ε不可知性和良好的理论性质，为蒙特卡洛树搜索中的近似规划问题提供了新的理论基础和方法支持。

Abstract: We study the fixed-budget max-min action identification problem in depth-2 max-min trees, an important special case of Monte Carlo Tree Search. A learner sequentially allocates $T$ samples to leaves and then recommends a subtree whose minimum leaf value is largest. Motivated by approximate planning, we focus on $\varepsilon$-good subtree identification, where any subtree whose min value is within $\varepsilon$ of the optimal maximin value is acceptable.
  Our main contribution is an $\varepsilon$-agnostic algorithm: it does not require $\varepsilon$ as input, but achieves instance-dependent error bounds for every meaningful $\varepsilon$. We show that the misidentification probability decays as $\exp(-\widetildeΘ(T/H_2(\varepsilon)))$, where $H_2(\varepsilon)$ captures both cross-subtree and within-subtree gaps. When each subtree has a single leaf, the problem reduces to standard fixed-budget best-arm identification, and our analysis recovers, up to accelerating factors, known $\varepsilon$-good guarantees for halving-style methods while giving a new $\varepsilon$-good guarantee for Successive Rejects.
  On the lower-bound side, we provide complementary positive and negative results showing that max-min identification has a different hardness structure from standard $K$-armed bandits. To our knowledge, this is the first provable fixed-budget algorithmic guarantee for max-min action identification.

</details>


### [77] [Neural Statistical Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11327)
*Daniel Xu,Yuxin Xie,Minghao Guo,Haixu Wu,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经统计函数模型，可直接推断连续工况范围内的统计量，无需显式采样，在减少100倍模型评估的同时实现强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典深度学习针对单样本推理，实际应用中常需重复推断以估计不确定性或极值分析的统计量，导致显著延迟。

Method: 引入前缀统计概念，将积分、分位数和极值等多样统计函数转化为区间条件框架；利用前缀统计与单样本回归间的原理性恒等式作为学习目标；从预训练单样本预测器和稀疏数据样本中学习。

Result: 在动力系统累积能量、气动响应分位数、碰撞过程最大应力等复杂物理过程统计估计中表现优异，模型评估量减少达100倍。

Conclusion: 神经统计函数为复杂决策任务中的统计量估计提供了高效替代方案，显著降低计算成本并保持强性能。

Abstract: Classical deep learning typically operates on individual cases. Despite its success, real-world usage often requires repeated inference to estimate statistical quantities for complex decision-making tasks involving uncertainty or extreme-value analysis, resulting in substantial latency. We introduce neural statistical functions, a new family of models learned from pre-trained single-sample predictors and scattered data samples, which can directly infer statistics over continuous operating condition ranges without explicit sampling. By introducing the notion of prefix statistics, we transform and unify diverse statistical functions (e.g., integrals, quantiles, and maxima) into an interval-conditional framework, in which a principled identity between the prefix statistics and the individual-case regression serves as the learning objective. Neural statistical functions achieve strong performance in estimating essential statistics of complex physical processes, including accumulated energy in dynamical systems, quantiles of aerodynamic responses, and maximum stress in crash processes, while achieving up to a 100$\times$ reduction in model evaluations.

</details>


### [78] [Epistemic Uncertainty for Test-Time Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11328)
*Kainat Riaz,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Umer,Ayesha Mohsin,Aqib Riaz,Ali Subhan,John M. Cioffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UG-TTT通过维护一组低秩适配器并利用其预测分歧作为探索奖励，解决了强化学习中因惩罚高方差突变而导致的最大奖励平台期问题，从而更有效地发现科学创新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用大语言模型进行自动化科学发现时，标准的强化学习方法倾向于惩罚高方差突变，导致策略优先选择熟悉模式。这使得平均奖励增加但最大奖励陷入平台期，难以探索到真正新颖的解决方案。为了克服这一限制，需要一种信号来区分未探索区域与本质上就困难的问题。

Method: 提出UG-TTT方法，在冻结的基础模型上维护一组低秩适配器的小型集成。通过计算集成预测与权重假设之间的互信息来量化每个token的分歧，以此分离认知不确定性。将此分歧度量作为探索奖励整合到策略梯度中，并引入核范数正则化器以确保适配器之间保持差异，从而在训练过程中持续保留探索信号。

Result: 在四个科学发现基准测试中，UG-TTT在三个任务上提高了最大奖励，并保持了显著更高的解决方案多样性。消融研究证实，正则化器对于维持这种行为至关重要。

Conclusion: UG-TTT成功解决了标准强化学习在探索新颖方案时的局限性，通过量化和管理认知不确定性，有效引导策略探索训练覆盖不足的区域，证明了其在促进真正的科学发现方面的有效性。

Abstract: Automated scientific discovery using large language models relies on identifying genuinely novel solutions. Standard reinforcement learning penalizes high-variance mutations, which leads the policy to prioritize familiar patterns. As a result, the maximum reward plateaus even as the average reward increases. Overcoming this limitation requires a signal that distinguishes unexplored regions from intrinsically difficult problems. This necessitates measuring disagreement across independently adapted weight hypotheses rather than relying on a single network's confidence. UG-TTT addresses this challenge by maintaining a small ensemble of low-rank adapters over a frozen base model. The per-token disagreement, quantified as the mutual information between ensemble predictions and weight hypotheses, isolates epistemic uncertainty and identifies positions where insufficient coverage leads to adapter divergence rather than intrinsic problem difficulty. This measure is incorporated as an exploration bonus into the policy gradient, directing the policy toward positions where persistent adapter disagreement signals low training coverage, the same frontier where genuine discovery is possible. A nuclear norm regularizer ensures the adapters remain distinct from one another, thereby preserving the exploration signal throughout training. Across four scientific discovery benchmarks, UG-TTT increases the maximum reward on three tasks, maintains substantially higher solution diversity, and an ablation study confirms that the regularizer is essential for sustaining this behavior.

</details>


### [79] [VERDI: Single-Call Confidence Estimation for Verification-Based LLM Judges via Decomposed Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11334)
*Jasmine Qi,Danylo Dantsev,Muyang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERDI方法通过分解推理过程提取置信度信号，解决了LLM-as-Judge系统中token对数概率不可用或不可靠的问题，在多个基准测试上取得了显著的AUROC提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-Judge系统被广泛用于自动评估，但从业者缺乏可靠的方法来判断法官的判决何时应该被信任。标准的后验置信度信号——token对数概率存在两个关键问题：(1)许多商业LLM无法提供；(2)即使可访问，在结构化JSON输出中也会饱和至0.999以上。因此需要一种新的方法来获取LLM法官的置信度估计。

Method: 提出VERDI（验证分解推理）方法，从结构化法官已经产生的推理轨迹中提取置信度，无需额外的推理调用。VERDI将每个验证式评估分解为子检查，并导出三个结构信号：步骤判决一致性、声明级别边际和证据基础分数，然后结合Platt缩放的逻辑回归进行融合。此外还展示了3300万参数的NLI模型可以提供可扩展的正则表达式提取替代方案。

Result: 在三个公共基准测试上，VERDI在GPT-4.1-mini上达到AUROC 0.72-0.91，在GPT-5.4-mini上达到0.66-0.80。对于答案token对数概率反校准的Qwen3.5-4B/9B/27B模型（错误时置信度更高，AUROC 0.32-0.49），VERDI达到0.56-0.70。在生产系统的八个评分标准上验证（事实性评分标准AUROC 0.73-0.88），展示了跨模型迁移能力（AUROC 0.66-0.69）。

Conclusion: VERDI为LLM-as-Judge系统提供了一种有效的置信度估计方法，无需依赖不可靠或不可用的token对数概率，能够在多种模型和场景下提供可靠的置信度信号，具有良好的跨模型迁移性和可扩展性。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems are widely deployed for automated evaluation, yet practitioners lack reliable methods to know when a judge's verdict should be trusted. Token log-probabilities, the standard post-hoc confidence signal, are unavailable for many commercial LLMs and, even when accessible, saturate above 0.999 with structured JSON output.
  We introduce VERDI (VERification-Decomposed Inference), a method that extracts confidence from the reasoning trace a structured judge already produces, with no additional inference calls. VERDI decomposes each verification-style evaluation into sub-checks and derives three structural signals: Step-Verdict Alignment, Claim-Level Margin, and Evidence Grounding Score. We combine them with Platt-scaled logistic regression.
  On three public benchmarks, VERDI achieves AUROC 0.72-0.91 on GPT-4.1-mini and 0.66-0.80 on GPT-5.4-mini. On Qwen3.5-4B/9B/27B, where answer-token logprobs are anti-calibrated (higher confidence on errors, AUROC 0.32-0.49), VERDI achieves 0.56-0.70. We additionally validate on a production system with eight rubrics (AUROC 0.73-0.88 on factual rubrics), demonstrate cross-model transfer (AUROC 0.66-0.69), and show that a 33M-parameter NLI (Natural Language Inference) model provides a scalable alternative to regex extraction.

</details>


### [80] [Physics-Informed Teacher-Student Ensemble Learning for Traffic State Estimation with a Varying Speed Limit Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11346)
*Archie J. Huang,Dongdong Wang,Shaurya Agarwal,Mohamed Abdel-Aty,Md Mahmudul Islam,Muhammad Shahbaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成教师-学生训练框架的物理信息深度学习（PIDL）方法，用于解决可变限速（VSL）场景下的交通状态估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PIDL训练架构无法适应实施可变限速（VSL）的高速公路上不断变化的交通特性。为了填补这一空白，需要一个能够处理VSL导致的交通流异质性的新方法。

Method: 提出一个新颖的集成框架，结合了教师-学生集成训练与PIDL神经网络。该框架利用PIDL将流量守恒定律的物理约束局部编码到教师模型中，学生模型则使用多层感知机（MLP）分类器来识别交通特性，并选择合适的PIDL神经网络集成成员进行交通状态估计。

Result: 案例研究结果验证了所提出的集成方法在可变限速场景下的有效性，其在交通状态估计任务上的表现优于其他流行的基线方法（以相对L2误差为指标）。

Conclusion: 该集成框架为捕捉可变限速的异质性并准确解决交通状态估计问题提供了一个自然的解决方案，证明了其在交通管理应用中的优越性。

Abstract: Physics-informed deep learning (PIDL) neural networks have shown their capability as a useful instrument for transportation practitioners in utilizing the underlying relationship between the state variables for traffic state estimation (TSE). Another efficient traffic management approach is implementing varying speed limits (VSLs) on transportation corridors to control traffic and mitigate congestion. However, the existing training architecture of PIDL in the literature cannot accommodate the changing traffic characteristics on a freeway with VSL. To tackle this challenge, we propose a novel framework integrating teacher-student ensemble training with PIDL neural networks for TSE under VSL scenarios. The physics of flow conservation law is encoded locally in the teacher models by PIDL, and the student model uses a multi-layer perceptron classifier (MLP) to identify traffic characteristics and selects the ensemble member of PIDL neural networks for TSE. This integrated framework provides a natural solution for capturing the heterogeneity of VSL and accurately addressing the TSE problem. The case study results validate the proposed ensemble approach, demonstrating its superior performance in TSE compared to other popular baseline methods, as indicated by relative L2 error.

</details>


### [81] [Gradient-Free Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11347)
*Jeongsol Kim,Hongeun Kim,Jian Wang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ZeNO框架，通过零阶优化实现了扩散和流模型的奖励对齐，无需梯度反向传播，适用于不可微场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散和流模型奖励对齐方法依赖多步随机轨迹，难以扩展到确定性生成器；而现有的噪声空间优化方法需要通过生成器和奖励管道进行反向传播，限制了其在不可微设置中的应用。

Method: 提出ZeNO（零阶噪声优化）框架，将噪声优化问题表述为路径积分控制问题，仅需零阶奖励评估即可估计，无需梯度计算。当使用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck参考过程实例化时，更新过程与隐式针对奖励倾斜分布的Langevin动力学相关联。

Result: ZeNO在多种生成器和奖励函数上实现了有效的推理时扩展并展现出强劲性能，包括在蛋白质结构生成任务中的应用，而该任务中反向传播是不可行的。

Conclusion: ZeNO成功实现了无需梯度的奖励对齐，扩展了奖励优化方法在不可微场景下的适用性，为确定性生成器和复杂奖励函数的优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Existing reward alignment methods for diffusion and flow models rely on multi-step stochastic trajectories, making them difficult to extend to deterministic generators. A natural alternative is noise-space optimization, but existing approaches require backpropagation through the generator and reward pipeline, limiting applicability to differentiable settings. To address this, here we present ZeNO (Zeroth-order Noise Optimization), a gradient-free framework that formulates noise optimization as a path-integral control problem, estimable from zeroth-order reward evaluations alone. When instantiated with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck reference process, the update connects to Langevin dynamics implicitly targeting a reward-tilted distribution. ZeNO enables effective inference-time scaling and demonstrates strong performance across diverse generators and reward functions, including a protein structure generation task where backpropagation is infeasible.

</details>


### [82] [gym-invmgmt: An Open Benchmarking Framework for Inventory Management Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11355)
*Reza Barati,Qinmin Vivian Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了gym-invmgmt基准环境，用于在不同压力条件下公平比较库存管理策略。随机规划表现最佳但计算成本高，PPO-Transformer在强化学习方法中表现最优，策略排名受信息获取、需求变化、拓扑结构和策略表示共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库存策略比较难以解释，因为性能评估结果同时依赖于评估协议和策略本身。拓扑结构、需求机制、信息获取、可行性约束、短缺处理和KPI定义的差异都会改变方法的排名，缺乏统一的跨范式评估框架。

Method: 开发了gym-invmgmt，这是一个与Gymnasium兼容的OR-Gym库存管理扩展，用于可审计的跨范式评估。该基准在共享CoreEnv转移、奖励、动作约束和KPI协议下评估优化、启发式和学习控制器，通过22个核心场景网格加上四个补充MARL模式行来变化压力条件。

Result: 信息化的随机规划在可获得预测的情况下提供最强的非oracle参考基准，但在线计算成本显著更高。在学习控制器中，PPO-Transformer以快速推理实现最强的学习策略质量，Residual RL提供竞争性混合性能。PPO-GNN在默认发散拓扑上高度竞争但在串行拓扑上鲁棒性较差。模仿学习在平稳机制下表现良好但在需求变化下退化。有界LLM策略参数基线更适合作为诊断控制器而非自主库存优化器。

Conclusion: 该基准识别了场景条件下的领先方法，同时表明性能取决于信息获取、需求变化、拓扑结构和策略表示的共同作用，为库存管理策略选择提供了系统化的评估框架。

Abstract: Inventory-policy comparisons are often difficult to interpret because performance depends on the evaluation contract as much as on the policy itself. Differences in topology, demand regime, information access, feasibility constraints, shortage treatment, and Key Performance Indicator (KPI) definitions can change method rankings. We present gym-invmgmt, a Gymnasium-compatible extension of the OR-Gym inventory-management lineage for auditable cross-paradigm evaluation. The benchmark evaluates optimization, heuristic, and learned controllers under a shared CoreEnv transition, reward, action-bound, and KPI contract, while varying stress conditions through a 22-scenario core grid plus four supplemental MARL-mode rows. Within these released scenarios, informed stochastic programming provides the strongest non-oracle reference, reflecting the value of scenario hedging under forecast access, but at substantially higher online computational cost. Among learned controllers, the Proximal Policy Optimization Transformer variant (PPO-Transformer) achieves the strongest learned-policy quality at fast inference, while Residual Reinforcement Learning (Residual RL) provides competitive hybrid performance. The graph neural network variant (PPO-GNN) is highly competitive on the default divergent topology but less robust on the serial topology. Imitation learning performs well in stationary regimes but degrades under demand shift, and the bounded Large Language Model (LLM) policy-parameter baseline is best interpreted as a diagnostic controller rather than an autonomous inventory optimizer. Overall, the benchmark identifies scenario-conditioned leaders while showing that performance depends jointly on information access, demand shift, topology, and policy representation.

</details>


### [83] [The tractability landscape of diffusion alignment: regularization, rewards, and computational primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11361)
*Ankur Moitra,Andrej Risteski,Dhruv Rohatgi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于原语的扩散模型推理时奖励对齐方法，证明KL距离下线性指数倾斜足以对齐凸低维奖励，而Wasserstein距离下近端传输预言机可处理凹或低维Lipschitz奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在推理时将预训练扩散模型转换为偏好特定奖励的采样器，同时保持与原始分布的接近性。由于缺乏统一的分布距离度量标准，不同选择会导致不同的奖励对齐分布和算法问题。

Method: 提出基于原语的方法：不假设能从任意奖励对齐分布采样，而是研究哪些简单算法原语足以实现对非平凡奖励类的对齐。针对KL距离使用线性指数倾斜作为原语，针对Wasserstein距离使用近端传输预言机。

Result: 证明了线性指数倾斜（形式为q(x)∝p(x)exp(⟨θ,x⟩)）可作为对齐凸低维奖励的充分原语；近端传输预言机（求解argmax_y{r(y)-λc(x,y)}）可有效实现凹或低维Lipschitz奖励r(x)=f(Ax)的对齐。

Conclusion: 对齐时选择的分布距离度量会影响所需的计算原语和可处理的奖励类别，为扩散模型奖励对齐提供了理论基础和算法指导。

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment asks how to turn a pre-trained diffusion model with base law $p$ into a sampler that favors a reward $r$ while remaining close to $p$. Since there is no canonical distributional distance for this closeness constraint, different choices lead to different "reward-aligned" laws and, just as importantly, different algorithmic problems. We develop a primitive-based approach to reward alignment: rather than assuming arbitrary reward-aligned laws can be sampled, we ask which simple algorithmic primitives suffice to implement alignment for non-trivial reward classes. If closeness is measured in KL distance, the target law is $q(x) \propto p(x) \exp(λ^{-1}r(x))$. For this setting, we show that linear exponential tilts of the form $q(x)\propto p(x)\exp(\langle θ, x \rangle)$ -- which according to recent work [MRR26] can be efficiently sampled from -- are a sufficient primitive for aligning to a very broad class of convex low-dimensional rewards. If closeness is measured in Wasserstein distance, the corresponding primitive is a proximal transport oracle: given $x$, solve $\mbox{argmax}_y \{r(y)- λc(x,y)\}$. This oracle can be efficiently implemented for concave or low-dimensional Lipschitz rewards $r(x)=f(Ax)$. Together, these results illustrate that the choice of distribution distance for alignment affects the computational primitive and the tractable reward class.

</details>


### [84] [Causal Fairness for Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11362)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于生存分析公平性的因果框架，能够将生存差异分解为直接、间接和虚假路径的贡献，并应用于ICU患者预后的种族差异分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平机器学习研究主要关注静态场景，而时间维度下的公平性问题，特别是生存/时间事件分析，研究相对不足。当前公平生存分析方法采用统计公平性定义，即使拥有无限数据也无法识别产生差异的因果机制。

Method: 提出一个非参数因果框架，包含四个步骤：(1) 使用图模型形式化关于删失和无混杂的必要假设；(2) 恢复给定协变量的条件生存函数；(3) 应用因果约简定理重构问题以便进行因果路径分解；(4) 高效估计效应。该方法能将生存差异分解为直接、间接和虚假路径的贡献。

Result: 开发了一个可解释的因果框架，能够提供关于差异为何产生以及如何随时间演变的、人类可理解的解释，并将该方法应用于分析ICU入院后预后中种族差异的时间演变。

Conclusion: 该因果框架填补了时间维度下公平性分析的研究空白，为理解生存分析中差异的因果机制提供了新视角，有助于在医疗等高风险领域实现更公平的决策。

Abstract: In the data-driven era, large-scale datasets are routinely collected and analyzed using machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) to inform decisions in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, employment, and criminal justice, raising concerns about the fairness behavior of these systems. Existing works in fair ML cover tasks such as bias detection, fair prediction, and fair decision-making, but largely focus on static settings. At the same time, fairness in temporal contexts, particularly survival/time-to-event (TTE) analysis, remains relatively underexplored, with current approaches to fair survival analysis adopting statistical fairness definitions, which, even with unlimited data, cannot disentangle the causal mechanisms that generate disparities. To address this gap, we develop a causal framework for fairness in TTE analysis, enabling the decomposition of disparities in survival into contributions from direct, indirect, and spurious pathways. This provides a human-understandable explanation of why disparities arise and how they evolve over time. Our non-parametric approach proceeds in four steps: (1) formalizing the necessary assumptions about censoring and lack of confounding using a graphical model; (2) recovering the conditional survival function given covariates; (3) applying the Causal Reduction Theorem to reframe the problem in a form amenable to causal pathway decomposition; (4) estimating the effects efficiently. Finally, our approach is used to analyze the temporal evolution of racial disparities in outcome after admission to an intensive care unit (ICU).

</details>


### [85] [LPDP: Inference-Time Reward Control for Variable-Length DNA Generation with Edit Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11368)
*Jeongchan Kim,Yunkyung Ko,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LPDP，一种无需训练的推理时奖励控制方法，通过局部重排序和离散规划优化DNA编辑流，实现了变长DNA序列生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奖励引导的DNA生成框架主要针对固定长度序列空间，无法有效处理变长DNA序列的生成问题。而编辑流（Edit Flows）具有通过生物学上合理的插入、删除和替换操作生成变长DNA的潜力，因此需要一种在推理时对变长DNA编辑动作生成器进行奖励控制的方法。

Method: 提出局部扰动离散规划（LPDP）方法，这是一种无需训练、感知中间状态和动作的局部重新求解算子。在每次引导展开步骤中，LPDP对单步根编辑进行评分，保留近优根带，并通过在每个保留根的子序列周围求解有界局部离散程序来重新排序。该局部程序利用编辑动作的类型几何特性，专注于连贯的替换、插入或删除子图，并通过硬Max备份或软log-sum-exponential（LSE）备份聚合局部延续。

Result: 在两个场景中验证了LPDP的有效性：增强子优化的前置奖励倾斜（早期编辑对建立全局调控序列结构至关重要）和外显子-内含子-外显子修复的后置奖励倾斜（后期编辑微调剪接边界上下文）。

Conclusion: LPDP为变长DNA序列生成提供了一种有效的推理时奖励控制方法，能够根据不同任务需求灵活调整编辑策略，在增强子优化和基因序列修复任务中展现出良好的应用前景。

Abstract: We study the application of recent Edit Flows for inference-time reward control for DNA sequence generation. Unlike most reward-guided DNA generation frameworks, which operate on fixed-length sequence spaces, Edit Flows have a potential to generate variable-length DNA through biologically plausible insertion, deletion, and substitution operations. In particular, we propose Local Perturbation Discrete Programming (LPDP), a training-free, intermediate-state and action-aware local re-solving operator for variable-length DNA edit-action generators at inference time. More specifically, at each guided rollout step, LPDP scores one-step root edits, retains a near-best root band, and re-ranks each retained root by solving a bounded local discrete program around its child sequence. This local program uses the typed geometry of edit actions to focus on coherent substitution, insertion, or deletion subgraphs, and aggregates local continuations with either a hard Max backup or a soft log-sum-exponential (LSE) backup. We instantiate LPDP in two regimes: front-loaded reward tilting for enhancer optimization, where early edits are critical for establishing global regulatory sequence structure, and back-loaded reward tilting for exon-intron-exon inpainting, where late edits fine-tune splice-boundary contexts.

</details>


### [86] [Test-Time Compute for Dense Retrieval: Agentic Program Generation with Frozen Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11374)
*Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明测试时计算不仅适用于大型推理模型，也能显著提升小型嵌入模型的性能。通过搜索发现了一种无需参数的通用推理程序，在多个模型家族上实现了统计显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为测试时计算仅对大型推理模型有益，本文旨在挑战这一假设，探索测试时计算是否也能帮助小型嵌入模型。由于现代嵌入模型通常从大型LLM蒸馏而来并继承其表示空间，作者推测冻结的嵌入模型应能从额外的推理计算中受益而无需重新训练。

Method: 采用基于代理的程序搜索循环，在冻结的嵌入API上探索259个候选推理程序，跨越90个进化代。通过系统性搜索和验证，在Pareto前沿上寻找最优推理策略。

Result: 发现所有Pareto最优解都收敛到一个统一的代数形式：局部top-K文档的softmax加权质心与查询的插值。该无参数默认方法在跨越十倍参数范围的七个嵌入模型家族上，nDCG@10指标均获得统计显著提升，且在held-out全BEIR验证集上确认了每个测试模型的性能增益。

Conclusion: 测试时计算对小型嵌入模型同样有效。存在一个简单、无参数的推理程序（softmax加权质心与查询插值），能够在无需重新训练的情况下普遍提升多种嵌入模型的检索性能，证明了蒸馏继承的表示空间可以有效利用额外推理计算。

Abstract: Test-time compute is widely believed to benefit only large reasoning models. We show it also helps small embedding models. Most modern embedding checkpoints are distilled from large LLM backbones and inherit their representation space; a frozen embedding model should therefore benefit from extra inference compute without retraining. Using an agentic program-search loop, we explore 259 candidate inference programs over a frozen embedding API across ninety generations. The entire Pareto frontier collapses onto a single algebra: a softmax-weighted centroid of the local top-K documents interpolated with the query. This parameter-free default lifts nDCG@10 statistically significantly across seven embedding-model families spanning a tenfold parameter range, with held-out full-BEIR validation confirming the lift on every model tested.

</details>


### [87] [TRACE: Temporal Routing with Autoregressive Cross-channel Experts for EEG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11380)
*Fan Ma,Qier An,Peng Chen,Lingfei Qian,Xiang Lan,Mingyang Jiang,Zhiling Gu,Xenophon Papademetris,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACE，一种脑电图(EEG)自回归预训练框架，通过在保持瞬时跨通道相干性的同时允许不同时间机制激活不同计算，解决了EEG信号多通道和非平稳特性带来的表示学习挑战，在多个下游基准测试中取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电图(EEG)信号的表示学习面临固有挑战：EEG信号本质上是多通道的且非平稳的。同一时刻观察到的通道提供了神经活动的耦合测量，而相关的时间动态在不同情境下存在差异。现有架构在时间上应用统一计算或独立路由每个通道块，这与EEG的结构特性不匹配。

Method: 提出TRACE自回归EEG预训练框架，从因果上下文预测未来EEG块，同时执行时间自适应和跨通道相干计算。在每个时间步骤，TRACE从因果跨通道历史中推导专家路由决策，并将该决策联合应用于该步骤的所有通道，从而保持瞬时跨通道相干性，同时允许不同时间机制激活不同的计算。路由定义在可用通道集和因果时间上下文上，使得TRACE能够兼容具有不同通道数、导联方式、序列长度和记录领域的异构预训练。

Result: 在八个下游EEG基准测试中进行了评估，包括两种设置：下游领域仅作为无标签预训练数据，以及下游数据集在预训练期间完全未见。TRACE在多个基准测试中取得了最佳结果，同时在运动想象和临床事件分类任务中保持竞争力，消融实验支持了跨通道时间路由的重要性。

Conclusion: TRACE成功解决了EEG信号的多通道和非平稳特性带来的表示学习挑战，通过时间自适应和跨通道相干计算机制，在保持跨通道相干性的同时实现了不同时间机制的计算差异化，为EEG预训练提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning transferable representations for electroencephalography (EEG) remains challenging because EEG signals are inherently multi-channel and non-stationary. Channels observed at the same time provide coupled measurements of neural activity, while the relevant temporal dynamics vary across contexts. This structure is poorly matched by architectures that apply uniform computation across time or route each channel patch independently. To this end, we propose TRACE, an autoregressive EEG pre-training framework that predicts future EEG patches from causal context while performing temporally adaptive and cross-channel coherent computation. At each temporal step, TRACE derives an expert routing decision from the causal cross-channel history and applies it jointly to all channels at that step. This preserves instantaneous cross-channel coherence while allowing different temporal regimes to activate different computation. Since routing is defined over the available channel set and causal temporal context, TRACE is compatible with heterogeneous pre-training across corpora with different channel counts, montages, sequence lengths, and recording domains. Across eight downstream EEG benchmarks, TRACE is evaluated in both settings: when downstream domains are seen only as unlabeled pre-training data and when downstream datasets are completely unseen during pre-training. It obtains the best results on several benchmarks while remaining competitive on motor imagery and clinical event classification tasks, with ablations supporting the importance of cross-channel temporal routing.

</details>


### [88] [Behavioral Mode Discovery for Fine-tuning Multimodal Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11387)
*Alberta Longhini,David Emukpere,Jean-Michel Renders,Seungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督模式发现框架，通过利用互信息作为内在奖励来正则化RL微调过程，在提升预训练生成式策略任务成功率的同时，有效解决了行为多模态丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习（RL）的生成式策略（如扩散策略）微调方法虽然能提升任务性能，但往往会导致动作分布坍缩，即多样化的行为模式会退化为单一的奖励最大化模式，从而丢失了策略原有的多模态特性。

Method: 提出了一种无监督模式发现框架，用于发掘生成式策略中潜在的行为模式；利用发现的模式计算互信息并将其作为内在奖励，以此正则化强化学习微调过程，从而在优化任务表现的同时约束行为多样性。

Result: 在机器人操作任务上的实验表明，该方法在任务成功率上持续优于传统微调方法，并且成功保留了更丰富的多模态动作分布。

Conclusion: 通过无监督模式发现与互信息正则化的结合，该方法有效平衡了任务性能提升与行为多样性保持之间的矛盾，为生成式策略的微调提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We address the problem of fine-tuning pre-trained generative policies with reinforcement learning (RL) while preserving the multimodality of their action distributions. Existing methods for RL fine-tuning of generative policies (e.g., diffusion policies) improve task performance but often collapse diverse behaviors into a single reward-maximizing mode. To mitigate this issue, we propose an unsupervised mode discovery framework that uncovers latent behavioral modes within generative policies. The discovered modes enable the use of mutual information as an intrinsic reward, regularizing RL fine-tuning to enhance task success while maintaining behavioral diversity. Experiments on robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms conventional fine-tuning approaches, achieving higher success rates and preserving richer multimodal action distributions.

</details>


### [89] [MuonQ: Enhancing Low-Bit Muon Quantization via Directional Fidelity Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11396)
*Yupeng Su,Ruijie Zhang,Ziyue Liu,Yequan Zhao,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MuonQ，一个针对Muon优化器的4比特量化训练框架，通过方向保真度优化解决了Muon对量化误差敏感的问题，在保持训练性能的同时将优化器状态内存减少了7.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器虽然通过梯度正交化实现了显著的计算节省，但其优化器状态对量化误差非常敏感：由于正交化丢弃了奇异值的幅度信息而只保留方向信息，即使奇异向量方向上的微小量化误差也会在更新中被放大，导致难以进行低比特量化。

Method: 提出了MuonQ框架，基于方向保真度优化原则，包含三个关键技术：(1)预量化归一化，确保每一步引入相同幅度的量化误差，防止累积误差形成优选方向；(2)结构分解，通过幂迭代分别量化主导奇异分量，确保量化误差仅扰动奇异值幅度而不旋转奇异向量方向；(3)μ律压扩量化，为密集分布的动量值分配更高分辨率，将量化目标从异常值保留转向密集区域区分性。

Result: 在GPT风格和LLaMA风格模型上的预训练实验表明，MuonQ在4比特精度下的训练损失和下游任务准确率与全精度Muon高度匹配，同时将优化器状态内存减少高达7.3倍。

Conclusion: MuonQ成功实现了Muon优化器状态的稳定4比特量化，为大语言模型训练提供了一种内存高效的解决方案，证明了方向保真度优化原则在优化器量化中的有效性。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer has emerged as a compelling alternative to Adam for training large language models, achieving remarkable computational savings through gradient orthogonalization. However, Muon's optimizer state is more sensitive to quantization errors: because the orthogonalization discards the magnitudes of singular values and retains only directional information, even small quantization errors in singular vector directions are amplified in the update. In this work, we propose MuonQ, a low-bit Muon training framework built on the principle of directional fidelity optimization. First, we apply a pre-quantization normalization so that each step introduces quantization errors of the same magnitude, preventing the accumulated error from developing a preferred direction. Second, we introduce a structural decomposition that separately quantizes the dominant singular components via power iteration, ensuring that quantization errors perturb only singular value magnitudes rather than rotating singular vector directions. Third, we adopt $μ$-law companding quantization to allocate higher resolution to densely packed momentum values, shifting the quantization objective from outlier preservation to dense-region distinguishability. Together, these techniques enable stable 4-bit quantization of Muon's optimizer states. Pre-training experiments on GPT-style and LLaMA-style models demonstrate that MuonQ at 4-bit precision closely matches full-precision Muon in both training loss and downstream task accuracy, while reducing optimizer state memory by up to 7.3 $\times$. Our code is available at https://github.com/YupengSu/MuonQ.

</details>


### [90] [fg-expo: Frontier-guided exploration-prioritized policy optimization via adaptive kl and gaussian curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11403)
*Mingxiong Lin,Zhangquan Gong,Maowen Tang,Qian Li,Chuangchuang Wang,Jian Ma,Sutian Huang,Kai Tang,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FG-ExPO通过自适应KL惩罚和高斯课程采样改进GRPO算法，在数学推理任务上显著提升模型性能，AIME 2025 pass@32指标提升13.34个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO算法存在两个被忽视的低效问题：(1)固定的KL系数在模型需要偏离参考策略时过度限制策略探索；(2)均匀问题采样忽略了中等难度问题能产生最有信息量的梯度信号这一事实。

Method: 提出FG-ExPO方法，集成两个轻量级组件：(1)准确率条件KL缩放(AKL)，根据批次平均准确率通过平滑非线性函数调整KL惩罚强度，模型表现差时放松约束，表现好时加强约束；(2)高斯课程采样(GCS)，按以中等准确率(约0.5)为中心的高斯分布为问题分配采样权重，聚焦模型的学习前沿。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和Qwen3-8B-Base模型上，于六个主流数学推理基准测试中FG-ExPO持续优于原始GRPO。AIME 2025 pass@32指标绝对提升13.34（从63.33%提升至76.67%），8B模型平均pass@32增益2.66。pass@32相比pass@1的显著更大提升验证了FG-ExPO在固定推理预算下扩大了模型的有效探索空间。

Conclusion: FG-ExPO通过自适应调整KL约束和优化问题采样策略，有效提升了强化学习在LLM数学推理中的训练效率，证明了动态探索控制和课程学习的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the standard paradigm for LLM mathematical reasoning, with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) serving as the dominant algorithm. We identify two overlooked inefficiencies inherent in GRPO. First, a fixed KL coefficient overly restricts policy exploration at moments when the model needs to diverge significantly from the reference policy. Second, uniform question sampling overlooks that moderately difficult problems produce the most informative gradient signals. We propose FG-ExPO, short for Frontier-Guided Exploration-Prioritized Policy Optimization, which integrates two lightweight components. Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL) adjusts the KL penalty strength through a smooth nonlinear function of batch average accuracy, loosening the constraint when the model performs poorly and strengthening it when the model achieves satisfactory results. Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS) assigns sampling weights to questions following a Gaussian distribution centered at a moderate accuracy level around 0.5, focusing model training on its learning frontier. We conduct evaluations on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and Qwen3-8B-Base across six mainstream mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that FG-ExPO consistently outperforms vanilla GRPO. It delivers an absolute improvement of 13.34 on the AIME 2025 pass@32 metric, rising from 63.33 percent to 76.67 percent, and obtains an average pass@32 gain of 2.66 on the 8B model. The substantially larger performance gains observed on pass@32 compared to pass@1 verify that FG-ExPO enlarges the model's effective exploration space under a fixed inference budget.

</details>


### [91] [20/20 Vision Language Models: A Prescription for Better VLMs through Data Curation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11405)
*Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Alvin Deng,Aldo Carranza,Alex Fang,Amro Abbas,Anshuman Suri,Brett Larsen,Daniel Zayas,Darren Teh,David Schwab,Diego Kiner,Fan Pan,Jack Urbanek,Jason Lee,Jason Telanoff,Josh Wills,Kaleigh Mentzer,Luke Merrick,Maximilian Böther,Parth Doshi,Paul Burstein,Pratyush Maini,Ties Robroek,Tony Jiang,Vidhi Jain,Vineeth Dorna,Zhengping Wang,Bogdan Gaza,Ari Morcos,Matthew Leavitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据筛选对视觉语言模型(VLM)性能的影响，发现在保持架构、训练方法和计算量不变的情况下，仅通过优化训练数据即可显著提升模型性能。在2B规模上，精选数据训练的模型以约17倍更少的计算量超越了InternVL3.5-2B，接近Qwen3-VL-2B的性能。数据筛选还能提升模型的可靠性、泛化能力和行为质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据筛选已证明能提升语言模型和对比图文预训练的质量-计算边界，但其在视觉语言模型(VLM)中的作用尚未明确。本文旨在探索在固定架构、训练配方和计算资源的情况下，仅通过数据筛选能在多大程度上提升VLM性能。

Method: 研究者将数据筛选流程应用于MAmmoTH-VL单图像子集，在保持架构、训练方法和计算量不变的前提下，仅改变训练数据进行对比实验。同时开发了高保真VLM评估套件DatBench，涵盖9个能力维度。

Result: 精选数据训练的模型在20个公开VLM基准测试上平均提升11.7个百分点，在DatBench的9个能力轴上平均提升11.3个百分点。2B模型以约17倍更少计算量超越InternVL3.5-2B(9.9pp)，以约87倍更少计算量接近Qwen3-VL-2B(差距1.8pp)。此外还带来四项改进：(1)可靠性提升67%，跨训练种子标准差降低；(2)OOD泛化能力提升7.2pp，多图像推理能力意外提升；(3)更诚实、具体、简洁的回复行为；(4)在推理成本上实现帕累托优势，4B模型以3.3倍更低的响应FLOPs匹配Qwen3-VL-4B的准确率。

Conclusion: 数据筛选是构建更好VLM的高杠杆工具，能以最多约150倍更少的训练计算量达到接近前沿模型的准确率，为VLM的高效训练提供了重要途径。

Abstract: Data curation has shifted the quality-compute frontier for language-model and contrastive image-text pretraining, but its role for vision-language models (VLMs) is far less established.
