We ask how far data curation alone can take VLM performance, holding architecture, training recipe, and compute fixed and varying only the training data. Our pipeline, applied to the MAmmoTH-VL single-image subset, lifts performance by +11.7pp on average across 20 public VLM benchmarks (spanning grounding, VQA, OCR/documents, captioning, spatial/3D, counting, charts, math, brand-ID, and multi-image reasoning) and by +11.3pp on average across all nine capability axes of DatBench, our high-fidelity VLM eval suite. At 2B, our curated model surpasses InternVL3.5-2B by 9.9pp at ~17x less training compute and closes the gap to Qwen3-VL-2B to within 1.8pp at ~87x less compute, from pretraining alone. Beyond accuracy, curation delivers four further properties: (1) Reliability: per-capability std across training seeds drops by ~67% and the lift survives a 4k-to-16k context-length sweep; (2) OOD generalization: the 9-eval OOD average rises by +7.2pp, and multi-image BLINK rises by +3.09pp despite single-image-only training, with Visual Correspondence gaining +11.8pp; (3) Behavioral gains beyond benchmarks: across ~1,100 open-ended queries the curated 2B is more honest and more specific than the matched-compute baseline, and more concise and less refusal-prone than a frontier 2B reference; (4) Pareto-dominance on inference cost: at every scale (1B, 2B, 4B) the curated model raises accuracy while lowering response FLOPs vs. the matched-compute baseline, and the curated 4B matches near-frontier accuracy at 3.3x lower response FLOPs than Qwen3-VL-4B. Data curation is a high-leverage tool for building better VLMs, reaching near-frontier accuracy at up to ~150x less training compute.

</details>


### [92] [A Boundary-Aware Non-parametric Granular-Ball Classifier Based on Minimum Description Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11406)
*Zeqiang Xian,Caihui Liu,Yong Zhang,Wenjing Qiu,Duoqian Miao,Witold Pedrycz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最小描述长度原则的粒球分类器（MDL-GBC），通过描述长度准则统一粒球的构建决策，解决了现有方法依赖手工规则导致决策不透明的问题，在18个数据集上取得了最优的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的粒球分类方法通常依赖手工设计的质量度量、邻域规则或启发式的分裂与停止准则，这降低了局部构建决策的透明度，并阻碍了对边界敏感区域的显式建模。

Method: 将类条件粒球构建表述为最小描述长度（MDL）原则下的局部模型选择问题。对每个当前粒球，在统一的描述长度准则下比较三种候选解释：单球模型、双球模型和核心-边界模型，据此决定粒球是保留、几何分裂还是细化为核心与边界敏感的子球，使局部构建决策与MDL分类机制保持一致。预测时采用类级混合编码规则，通过比较类别编码成本分配测试样本。

Result: 在18个基准数据集上的实验表明，MDL-GBC与经典分类器和代表性粒球方法相比具有竞争力的分类性能，获得了最佳的平均Accuracy、Macro-F1和平均排名。

Conclusion: MDL-GBC为传统启发式粒球分类策略提供了一种有效且可解释的替代方案，证明了基于MDL原则的粒球构建方法的有效性。

Abstract: Existing granular-ball classification methods are often driven by handcrafted quality measures, neighborhood rules, or heuristic splitting and stopping criteria, which may reduce the transparency of local construction decisions and hinder explicit modeling of boundary-sensitive regions. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Minimum Description Length based Granular-Ball Classifier (MDL-GBC), a boundary-aware non-parametric and interpretable granular-ball classifier. MDL-GBC formulates class-conditional granular-ball construction as a local model selection problem under the Minimum Description Length principle. For each class, samples from the target class provide positive class evidence, while samples from the remaining classes provide negative boundary evidence. For each current granular ball, three candidate explanations are compared under a unified description-length criterion: a single-ball model, a two-ball model, and a core-boundary model. The selected model determines whether the ball is retained, geometrically split, or refined into core and boundary-sensitive child balls, thereby making local construction decisions consistent with the MDL-based classification mechanism. During prediction, a class-level mixture coding rule aggregates stable granular balls of the same class and assigns the test sample by comparing class-wise coding costs. Experiments on 18 benchmark datasets show that MDL-GBC achieves competitive classification performance against classical classifiers and representative granular-ball-based methods, obtaining the best average Accuracy, Macro-F1, and average rank. These results indicate that MDL-GBC provides an effective and interpretable alternative to conventional heuristic granular-ball classification strategies.

</details>


### [93] [MaskTab: Scalable Masked Tabular Pretraining with Scaling Laws and Distillation for Industrial Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11408)
*Bo Zheng,Yudong Chen,Zihua Xiong,Shuai Fang,Peidong He,Yang Yang,Sheng Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MaskTab，一个专为工业级表格数据设计的统一预训练框架，通过编码缺失值、混合监督预训练和MoE增强损失，在基准测试中显著优于现有方法，并能有效蒸馏为轻量级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据是金融、医疗等高风险决策系统的核心，但工业级表格数据存在高维、大量缺失值、标签稀缺等挑战。尽管基础模型在视觉和语言领域取得了革命性进展，表格学习仍依赖手工特征，缺乏通用的自监督框架。

Method: 提出MaskTab框架，包含三个核心设计：1）通过专用的可学习token编码缺失值，区分结构性缺失与随机丢弃；2）采用双路径架构联合优化掩码重建与任务特定监督的混合预训练方案；3）引入MoE增强损失，自适应地将特征路由到专门的子网络。

Result: 在工业级基准测试中，相比已有最优方法，MaskTab在严格扩展条件下实现了+5.04% AUC和+8.28% KS的提升；蒸馏为轻量级模型后，在延迟和可解释性约束下仍获得+2.55% AUC和+4.85% KS的提升，并增强了对分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明表格数据可以通过基础模型方法进行处理，前提是充分尊重其结构性特征，为工业级表格数据学习提供了一条有效的通用预训练路径。

Abstract: Tabular data forms the backbone of high-stakes decision systems in finance, healthcare, and beyond. Yet industrial tabular datasets are inherently difficult: high-dimensional, riddled with missing entries, and rarely labeled at scale. While foundation models have revolutionized vision and language, tabular learning still leans on handcrafted features and lacks a general self-supervised framework. We present MaskTab, a unified pre-training framework designed specifically for industrial-scale tabular data. MaskTab encodes missing values via dedicated learnable tokens, enabling the model to distinguish structural absence from random dropout. It jointly optimizes a hybrid supervised pre-training scheme--utilizing a twin-path architecture to reconcile masked reconstruction with task-specific supervision--and an MoE-augmented loss that adaptively routes features through specialized subnetworks. On industrial-scale benchmarks, it achieves +5.04% AUC and +8.28% KS over prior art under rigorous scaling. Moreover, its representations distill effectively into lightweight models, yielding +2.55% AUC and +4.85% KS under strict latency and interpretability constraints, while improving robustness to distribution shifts. Our work demonstrates that tabular data admits a foundation-model treatment--when its structural idiosyncrasies are respected.

</details>


### [94] [Generative Diffusion Prior Distillation for Long-Context Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11414)
*Nilushika Udayangani,Kishor Nandakishor,Marimuthu Palaniswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成扩散先验蒸馏框架（GDPD），利用扩散模型学习教师特征的生成先验，通过后验采样为目标教师表示提供合理的长程信息，从而将完整时间序列分类器的泛化能力迁移到部分序列分类器中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列分类器通常假设推理时拥有完整序列，但实际应用中的延迟和成本限制往往只能获取部分前缀序列。部分数据中缺乏类别判别性模式，严重阻碍了分类器的泛化能力。现有知识蒸馏方法在处理由训练数据差异（完整vs部分）导致的泛化差距时，教师的全上下文特征对学生短上下文特征而言往往过于强大，造成监督信号不匹配。

Method: 提出生成扩散先验蒸馏（GDPD）框架：1）将短上下文学生特征视为目标完整上下文特征的退化观测；2）受扩散模型迭代恢复能力的启发，学习教师特征的扩散生成先验；3）利用该先验进行后验采样，生成能最好解释学生特征中缺失长程信息的目标教师表示；4）优化学生特征使其相对于这些目标具有最小退化；5）为每个学生特征提供任务相关长上下文知识的分布。

Result: 在多种早期设置、数据集和架构上进行了广泛实验，验证了GDPD在完整到部分蒸馏任务中的有效性。该方法成功弥合了由训练数据差异导致的泛化差距，提升了部分时间序列分类的性能。

Conclusion: GDPD通过生成扩散先验为部分时间序列分类提供了渐进、多样和集体的教师监督，有效解决了传统知识蒸馏在处理数据差异时的局限性，为实际场景中的早期分类任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: While traditional time-series classifiers assume full sequences at inference, practical constraints (latency and cost) often limit inputs to partial prefixes. The absence of class-discriminative patterns in partial data can significantly hinder a classifier's ability to generalize. This work uses knowledge distillation (KD) to equip partial time series classifiers with the generalization ability of their full-sequence counterparts. In KD, high-capacity teacher transfers supervision to aid student learning on the target task. Matching with teacher features has shown promise in closing the generalization gap due to limited parameter capacity. However, when the generalization gap arises from training-data differences (full versus partial), the teacher's full-context features can be an overwhelming target signal for the student's short-context features. To provide progressive, diverse, and collective teacher supervision, we propose Generative Diffusion Prior Distillation (GDPD), a novel KD framework that treats short-context student features as degraded observations of the target full-context features. Inspired by the iterative restoration capability of diffusion models, we learn a diffusion-based generative prior over teacher features. Leveraging this prior, we posterior-sample target teacher representations that could best explain the missing long-range information in the student features and optimize the student features to be minimally degraded relative to these targets. GDPD provides each student feature with a distribution of task-relevant long-context knowledge, which benefits learning on the partial classification task. Extensive experiments across earliness settings, datasets, and architectures demonstrate GDPD's effectiveness for full-to-partial distillation.

</details>


### [95] [FastUMAP: Scalable Dimensionality Reduction via Bipartite Landmark Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11428)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FastUMAP，一种基于地标的快速降维方法，通过稀疏点-地标模糊图和Nystrom谱预热启动，在保持合理精度的同时显著提升运行速度，适用于重复探索性分析场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际的高维数据探索性分析中，分析师经常需要因预处理、子集或超参数变化而反复运行降维方法，而标准的非线性降维方法很快会成为性能瓶颈。现有的降维方法在重复使用场景下效率不足，需要一种能够平衡运行时间和保真度的快速方法。

Method: FastUMAP是一种基于地标的降维方法，包含三个关键步骤：(1)构建稀疏点-地标模糊图；(2)从诱导的地标亲和度计算Nystrom谱预热启动；(3)在二部图上使用UMAP风格的目标函数优化所有样本坐标。地标比例r = m/n提供了直接的方式来权衡运行时间和保真度。

Result: 在9个基准数据集（样本数从178到70,000）上的实验表明，FastUMAP在7个数据集上运行时间最短。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集（n=70000）上，FastUMAP运行时间约4.6秒，而Barnes-Hut t-SNE需要约73-75秒；准确率方面，FastUMAP达到91.4%的平均kNN准确率，而最强的准确率基线为94.6%。

Conclusion: FastUMAP应该被视为重复探索性嵌入的快速选项，而不是精度优先方法的替代品。它适用于需要反复运行降维的探索性分析场景，在合理牺牲一定精度的情况下大幅提升运行效率。

Abstract: Exploratory analysis of high-dimensional data rarely stops at a single embedding. In practice, analysts rerun dimensionality reduction after changing preprocessing, subsets, or hyperparameters, and standard nonlinear methods can quickly become the bottleneck. We introduce FastUMAP (Bipartite Manifold Approximation and Projection), a landmark-based method designed for this repeated-use setting. FastUMAP builds a sparse point-landmark fuzzy graph, computes a Nystrom spectral warm start from the induced landmark affinity, and then refines all sample coordinates with a UMAP-style objective on the bipartite graph. The landmark ratio r = m/n provides a direct way to trade runtime against fidelity. On 9 benchmark datasets spanning 178 to 70,000 samples, FastUMAP has the lowest runtime on 7 datasets in our reported default-implementation comparison on one workstation. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST (n=70000), it runs in about 4.6 seconds, compared with about 73--75 seconds for Barnes--Hut t-SNE, while reaching 91.4% mean kNN accuracy versus 94.6% for the strongest accuracy baseline. FastUMAP is therefore best viewed as a fast option for repeated exploratory embedding, rather than as a replacement for accuracy-first methods.

</details>


### [96] [Deep Minds and Shallow Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11448)
*Su Hyeong Lee,Risi Kondor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Neural representations are not unique objects. Even when two systems realize the same downstream computation, their hidden coordinates may differ by reparameterization. A probe family intended to reveal structure already present in a representation should therefore be stable under the relevant representation symmetries rather than be tied to a particular basis. We study this group action in the tractable exact setting of the final readout layer, where equivalent realizations induce affine changes of hidden coordinates. The resulting symmetry principle singles out a unique hierarchy of shallow coordinate-stable probes, with linear probes as its degree-1 member. We also show that a natural object for cross-model probe transfer is a shared probe-visible quotient--the representation modulo directions invisible to the probe family--rather than the full hidden state. Experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks support both predictions, showing where degree-2 probes help beyond linear ones and how quotient-based transfer enables coverage-aware monitor portability across model families. These results point toward a broader geometric representation theory of neural probing, with coverage-aware monitor transfer as a concrete operational consequence.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Prediction: Interval Neural Networks for Uncertainty-Aware System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11460)
*Mehmet Ali Ferah,Tufan Kumbasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个构建和训练区间神经网络(INNs)的系统框架，用于不确定性感知的系统辨识，包含两种训练策略，能够同时实现高精度的点预测和可靠的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统辨识方法难以捕捉非线性行为，而现有的深度学习方法缺乏不确定性量化，无法保证预测的可靠性，因此需要开发能够量化不确定性的神经网络模型。

Method: 将标准神经网络扩展为区间神经网络，开发了区间LSTM和NODE模型，通过区间算术传播不确定性；提出两种训练策略：级联INN(C-INN)将已训练的标准NN转换为INN，联合INN(J-INN)联合优化预测精度和区间精度；引入通道弹性概念分析参数不确定性表示。

Result: 在多个系统辨识数据集上的实验表明，C-INN在点预测精度方面表现优异，J-INN产生更准确且校准更好的预测区间，两者均优于现有的不确定性感知基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效整合了深度学习与不确定性感知建模，为系统辨识提供了一种可靠的解决方案，能够在保证预测精度的同时提供可靠的不确定性量化。

Abstract: System identification (SysID) is critical for modeling dynamical systems from experimental data, yet traditional approaches often fail to capture nonlinear behaviors. While deep learning offers powerful tools for modeling such dynamics, incorporating uncertainty quantification is essential to ensure reliable predictions. This paper presents a systematic framework for constructing and training interval Neural Networks (INNs) for uncertainty-aware SysID. By extending crisp neural networks into interval counterparts, we develop Interval LSTM and NODE models that propagate uncertainty through interval arithmetic without probabilistic assumptions. This design allows them to represent uncertainty and produce prediction intervals. For training, we propose two strategies: Cascade INN (C-INN), a two-stage approach converting a trained crisp NN into an INN, and Joint INN (J-INN), a one-stage framework jointly optimizing prediction accuracy and interval precision. Both strategies employ uncertainty-aware loss functions and parameterization tricks to ensure reliable learning. Comprehensive experiments on multiple SysID datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of both approaches and benchmark their performance against well-established uncertainty-aware baselines: C-INN achieves superior point prediction accuracy, whereas J-INN yields more accurate and better-calibrated prediction intervals. Furthermore, to reveal how uncertainty is represented across model parameters, the concept of channel-wise elasticity is introduced, which is used to identify distinct patterns across the two training strategies. The results of this study demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively integrates deep learning with uncertainty-aware modeling.

</details>


### [98] [Drop the Act: Probe-Filtered RL for Faithful Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11467)
*Swapnil Parekh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProFIL方法，通过探测后承诺步骤来减少推理模型中的“推理表演”，提高思维链的忠实度并缩短链长度，同时保持或提升任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型会在内部已确定答案后进行事后合理化，产生“推理表演”——看似深思熟虑但实际无助于正确性的步骤。这浪费推理token、污染可解释性并掩盖模型实际计算过程。

Method: ProFIL（探测过滤强化学习）作为GRPO的扩展：1）在冻结的基础模型上训练多头注意力探测器，仅从内部激活检测后承诺步骤（使用验证器派生标签，无需人工标注）；2）在GRPO训练中，探测分数超过阈值的rollout将其优势值归零，从而抑制推理表演。

Result: 在四个推理领域（GSM8K、LiveCodeBench、ToolUse、MMLU-Redux）和两种模型架构（Llama-8B、Qwen-7B）上，ProFIL将后承诺表演减少11-100%，忠实度分数提升（如LiveCodeBench上+24pp），链长度缩短4-19%，同时保持或提升任务准确性，优于长度惩罚基线。

Conclusion: 在冻结基础模型上训练的探测器（使用验证器派生标签）能提供稳定信号，在抑制推理表演的同时抵抗先前工作预测的RL混淆失效模式，实现了语义承诺检测而非简单的链压缩。

Abstract: Reasoning models post-hoc rationalize answers they have already committed to internally, producing chains of *reasoning theater*: deliberative-looking steps that contribute nothing to correctness. This wastes inference tokens, pollutes interpretability, and obscures what the model actually computed. We introduce **ProFIL** (**Pro**be-**Fil**tered Reinforcement Learning) to *reduce theater, increase chain-of-thought faithfulness, and shrink chain length* in a single, drop-in extension to Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). A multi-head attention probe is trained *once* on the *frozen* base model to detect post-commitment steps from internal activations alone; during GRPO, rollouts whose probe score exceeds a threshold have their advantage zeroed. *Our central finding is that a probe trained on a frozen base, with verifier-derived labels and no human annotation, provides a stable signal that suppresses theater while resisting the RL-obfuscation failure mode predicted by prior work.* Across four reasoning domains (GSM8K, LiveCodeBench, ToolUse, MMLU-Redux) and two model architectures (Llama-8B, Qwen-7B), ProFIL reduces post-commitment theater by **11--100%**, raises faithful-fraction (e.g., +24pp on LiveCodeBench under an independent Claude 3.7 Sonnet judge), and shortens chains by 4--19%, all while preserving or improving task accuracy. ProFIL also beats a matched length-penalty GRPO baseline, isolating the gain as semantic commitment-detection rather than chain compression. Probe weights, training configurations, and rollouts are released across all four domains.

</details>


### [99] [Robust Multi-Agent Path Finding under Observation Attacks: A Principled Adversarial-Plus-Smoothing Training Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11469)
*Riad Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式多智能体路径规划(MAPF)中PPO策略对输入扰动脆弱的问题，提出Adv-PPO和Adv-PPO+MACER两种训练方法，显著提升了策略在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，成功率和成功率分别恢复到59.2%和77.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式MAPF中，使用PPO训练的共享神经策略在干净观察下表现良好，但对输入扰动极度脆弱：单个智能体受到微小扰动就可能改变其动作，进而阻塞邻居智能体，导致整个团队陷入拥堵。现有策略缺乏对抗性鲁棒性。

Method: 提出两种训练方案：（1）Adv-PPO：在训练过程中针对自身输入的最坏情况扰动进行对抗训练，并根据对抗扰动下的性能选择检查点；（2）Adv-PPO+MACER：在Adv-PPO检查点基础上，使用基于随机平滑认证半径的平滑项进行微调。两种方法都保持相同的网络结构和部署流程。

Result: 在POGEMA环境（8x8地图，4个智能体）上：未保护的PPO策略干净成功率为95.8%，但在最强攻击下仅2.5%；Adv-PPO将最坏情况成功率恢复至59.2%（干净性能损失1个百分点）；Adv-PPO+MACER进一步恢复至77.5%±6.0%（干净性能损失不到1个百分点）。实验还包括攻击曲线、认证动作稳定性检查和并排展示失败模式与修复效果的可视化。

Conclusion: 通过对抗训练和随机平滑技术，可以显著提升分布式MAPF策略的对抗鲁棒性，同时几乎不损失干净环境下的性能。这两种方法为构建更可靠的分布式多智能体系统提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-agent path finding (MAPF) routes a team of agents on a shared grid, each acting from its own local view. The standard solution trains one shared neural policy with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), a popular on-policy reinforcement learning algorithm. Such a policy works well on clean observations, but a small input perturbation on one agent often changes its action, which then blocks a neighbour, and the team jams. In this paper we present two training recipes that keep the same network and the same deployment loop, yet make the policy hold up under perturbed observations. The first recipe, Adv-PPO, trains the shared policy against worst-case perturbations of its own input and selects the checkpoint by performance under adversarial perturbation. The second recipe, Adv-PPO+MACER, fine-tunes that checkpoint with a small on-policy smoothness term whose gradient follows the certified radius of randomized smoothing. On POGEMA with 8x8 maps and four agents, the unprotected PPO policy reaches 95.8% clean success but only 2.5% under the strongest attack. Adv-PPO recovers worst-case success to 59.2% at one percentage point of clean cost. Adv-PPO+MACER recovers it to 77.5% +/- 6.0% across three independent seeds at less than one percentage point of clean cost. We support these numbers with per-attack curves, a certified action-stability sanity check (which measures the smoothed-policy wrapper, not the deployed argmax policy), and side-by-side rollout storyboards that show the failure mode and the fix inside one environment instance.

</details>


### [100] [On the Approximation Complexity of Matrix Product Operator Born Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11471)
*Chao Li,Zerui Tao,Yuchen Cong,Jian Xu,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对矩阵乘积算符玻恩机(MPO-BMs)的可近似性进行了完整刻画：证明了连续设定下其KL近似是NP难的，但在特定局部结构下可实现高效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPO-BMs作为一种可处理的张量网络概率模型，其高效近似能力的理论边界尚不清晰，缺乏对其在何种情况下难以近似、何种情况下可高效学习的系统性刻画。

Method: 从负面和正面两个角度进行理论分析：(1)通过复杂性理论证明连续设定下KL近似的NP困难性；(2)针对基于分数的变分推断，在损失诱导哈密顿量满足局部性和谱隙条件下，证明结构化目标（如路径图马尔可夫随机场）存在多项式键维度的MPO-BM近似；(3)证明在相同局部结构下，多项式数量的分数查询足以估计诱导哈密顿量。

Result: 连续设定下MPO-BMs的KL近似是NP难的，排除了最坏情况下通用高效近似的可能性；对于具有局部结构的目标分布，存在多项式键维度的MPO-BM近似，且具有可证明的KL保证；多项式数量的分数查询足以获得这些保证。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上完整刻画了MPO-BMs可近似性的边界：在最坏情况下是计算困难的，但在具有局部结构的目标分布下可以实现高效学习，为理解张量网络概率模型的计算能力提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Matrix product operator Born machines (MPO-BMs) are tractable tensor-network models for probabilistic modeling, but their efficient approximation capability remains unclear. We characterize this boundary from both negative and positive perspectives. First, we prove that KL approximation is NP-hard for MPO-BMs in the continuous setting, ruling out universal efficient approximation in the worst case. Second, for score-based variational inference, we show that, under a locality and spectral-gap conditions on the loss-induced Hamiltonian, structured targets (e.g., path-graph Markov random fields) admit MPO-BM approximations with polynomial bond dimension and provable KL guarantees. Third, under the same locality structure, we prove that polynomially many score queries suffice to estimate the induced Hamiltonian and obtain such guarantees. Our results provide a theoretical characterization of when MPO-BMs are fundamentally hard to approximate and when they become efficiently learnable.

</details>


### [101] [Efficient Adjoint Matching for Fine-tuning Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11480)
*Jeongwoo Shin,Dongsoo Shin,Joonseok Lee,Jaewoong Choi,Jaemoo Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了高效伴随匹配（EAM）方法，通过将随机最优控制问题重构为线性基础漂移形式，解决了伴随匹配（AM）在奖励微调中的计算瓶颈，实现了4倍加速且性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的伴随匹配（AM）方法虽然为扩散和流模型的奖励微调提供了基于随机最优控制的原理性框架，但存在两个主要计算瓶颈：(i) 需要在无记忆动力学下进行随机模拟完整的生成轨迹，导致大量函数评估；(ii) 需要沿每条采样轨迹进行伴随状态的后向ODE模拟。这些低效性与预训练模型继承的非平凡基础漂移密切相关。

Method: 提出高效伴随匹配（EAM），通过将随机最优控制问题重构为带有线性基础漂移和相应修改的终端代价的形式。该重构消除了两个低效源：(i) 允许使用少步确定性ODE求解器进行训练时采样；(ii) 产生闭式伴随解，无需后向伴随模拟。

Result: 在标准文本到图像奖励微调基准测试中，EAM的收敛速度比AM快4倍。在PickScore、ImageReward、HPSv2.1、CLIPScore和Aesthetics等多个评估指标上，EAM达到或超越AM的性能表现。

Conclusion: EAM成功解决了AM方法的计算效率问题，通过引入线性基础漂移重构，既保持了原理性框架的优势，又大幅提升了训练效率，为扩散和流模型的奖励微调提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reward fine-tuning has become a common approach for aligning pretrained diffusion and flow models with human preferences in text-to-image generation. Among reward-gradient-based methods, Adjoint Matching (AM) provides a principled formulation by casting reward fine-tuning as a stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem. However, AM inevitably requires a substantial computational cost: it requires (i) stochastic simulation of full generative trajectories under memoryless dynamics, resulting in a large number of function evaluations, and (ii) backward ODE simulation of the adjoint state along each sampled trajectory. In this work, we observe that both bottlenecks are closely tied to the \textit{non-trivial base drift} inherited from the pretrained model. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Efficient Adjoint Matching (EAM)}, which substantially improves training efficiency by reformulating the SOC problem with a \textit{linear base drift} and a correspondingly modified \textit{terminal cost}. This reformulation removes both sources of inefficiency; it enables training-time sampling with a few-step deterministic ODE solver and yields a closed-form adjoint solution that eliminates backward adjoint simulation. On standard text-to-image reward fine-tuning benchmarks, EAM converges up to 4x faster than AM and matches or surpasses it across various metrics including PickScore, ImageReward, HPSv2.1, CLIPScore and Aesthetics.

</details>


### [102] [Adaptive Calibration in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11490)
*Junyan Liu,Haipeng Luo,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套在线校准预测算法，能够自动适应环境的非平稳程度，在校准误差上实现了从i.i.d.到对抗性环境的平滑插值，填补了现有方法在近平稳环境下的性能空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线预测校准研究主要关注完全对抗性环境，导致算法在更温和的近平稳环境中表现次优。这种差距引发了核心问题：能否设计出校准误差能够自动适应环境非平稳程度的算法？

Method: 在先前工作基础上，引入基于轮次（epoch）的调度策略，并创新性地提出对预测空间的非均匀划分方法，在潜在真值附近分配更精细的分辨率。

Result: 算法在T轮和未知非平稳度量C（定义为均值结果的最小ℓ1偏差）下，对ℓ1校准误差达到Õ(√T + (TC)^(1/3))，对ℓ2和伪KL校准误差达到Õ((1+C)^(1/3))。

Conclusion: 该工作成功实现了校准误差对环境非平稳性的自动适应，在平稳情形（C=0）匹配最优速率，在完全对抗情形（C=T）恢复已知保证，为在线预测校准提供了更灵活的理论保障。

Abstract: Making calibrated online predictions is a central challenge in modern AI systems. Much of the existing literature focuses on fully adversarial environments where outcomes may be arbitrary, leading to conservative algorithms that can perform suboptimally in more benign settings, such as when outcomes are nearly stationary. This gap raises a natural question: can we design online prediction algorithms whose calibration error automatically adapts to the degree of non-stationarity in the environment, smoothly interpolating between i.i.d. and adversarial regimes? We answer this question in the affirmative and develop a suite of algorithms that achieve adaptive calibration guarantees under multiple calibration measures. Specifically, with $T$ being the number of rounds and $C\in[0,T]$ being an unknown non-stationary measure defined as the minimal $\ell_1$ deviation of the mean outcomes, our algorithms attain $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+(TC)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for $\ell_1$ calibration error and $\widetilde{O}((1+C)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for both $\ell_2$ and pseudo KL calibration error. These bounds match the optimal rates in the stationary case ($C=0$) and recover known guarantees in the fully adversarial regime ($C=T$). Our approach builds on and extends prior work [Hu et al., 2026, Luo et al., 2025], introducing an epoch-based scheduling together with a novel non-uniform partition of the prediction space that allocates finer resolution near the underlying ground truth.

</details>


### [103] [Understanding and Preventing Entropy Collapse in RLVR with On-Policy Entropy Flow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11491)
*Huimin Xu,Shuai Zhao,Xiaobao Wu,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OPEFO方法，通过Token级熵流视角解决RLVR训练中的熵坍塌问题，在六项数学推理基准上提升了训练稳定性和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR算法（如GRPO）存在熵坍塌问题，导致过早确定性和优化不稳定；现有的熵正则化和比率裁剪等补救措施要么是粗粒度控制，要么依赖近似同策略训练，效果有限。

Method: 从Token级熵流视角重新审视熵坍塌，发现熵减少的Token持续超过熵增加的Token，导致熵流严重失衡；提出同策略熵流优化（OPEFO），这是一种自适应熵流平衡机制，根据熵变化的贡献重新缩放熵增加和熵减少的更新，同时保持严格的同策略性。

Result: 在六项数学推理基准测试上的实验表明，OPEFO提高了训练稳定性和最终性能。

Conclusion: Token级熵流视角为现有RLVR算法中的熵坍塌提供了统一解释，强调了平衡熵动态的重要性；OPEFO通过自适应平衡熵流有效解决了该问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning ability of large language models. However, widely used RLVR algorithms, such as GRPO, often suffer from entropy collapse, leading to premature determinism and unstable optimization. Existing remedies, including entropy regularization and ratio-based clipping heuristics, either control entropy in a coarse-grained manner or rely on approximate on-policy training. In this paper, we revisit entropy collapse from a token-level entropy flow perspective. Our analysis reveals that entropy-decreasing tokens consistently outweigh entropy-increasing ones, resulting in a severely imbalanced entropy flow. This perspective provides a unified explanation of entropy collapse in existing RLVR algorithms and highlights the importance of balancing entropy dynamics. Motivated by this analysis, we propose On-Policy Entropy Flow Optimization (OPEFO), an adaptive entropy flow balancing mechanism that rescales entropy-increasing and entropy-decreasing updates according to their contributions to entropy change, while remaining strict on-policy. Experiments on six mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPEFO improves training stability and final performance. We will release the code and models upon publication.

</details>


### [104] [CTFusion: A CTF-based Benchmark for LLM Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11504)
*Dongjun Lee,Ga-eun Bae,Insu Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CTFusion，一个基于Live CTFs的流式评估框架，用于解决现有CTF基准测试中存在的数据污染和潜在作弊问题，为LLM网络安全智能体提供了更可靠的评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTF基准测试普遍复用已有挑战题目，导致存在数据污染（LLM可能在训练中已见过答案）和潜在作弊风险。作者通过实验证实了这些问题，因此需要一种新的、未被污染的评估方法来准确衡量LLM智能体在网络安全任务中的真实能力。

Method: 提出CTFusion框架，采用Live CTFs进行实时评估。技术实现上：(1) 在单一团队账户下保持各智能体的独立性；(2) 仅转发每个挑战的第一个正确flag以降低对竞赛的影响；(3) 基于广泛使用的CTFd平台实现为模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器，确保对不同CTF赛事和智能体类型的广泛适用性。

Result: 通过在3个大语言模型、2个智能体和5场Live CTFs上的实验验证，发现现有CTF基准测试在评估基于LLM的智能体时存在不可靠性，而CTFusion能够提供更稳健的评估结果。作者已将CTFusion作为开源项目发布。

Conclusion: CTFusion成功解决了传统CTF基准测试的数据污染问题，为网络安全智能体的评估提供了一个可靠、适用性广的解决方案，有助于推动该领域的未来研究发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled agentic systems for complex, multi-step tasks; cybersecurity is emerging as a prominent application. To evaluate such agents, researchers widely adopt Capture The Flag (CTF) benchmarks. However, current CTF benchmarks reuse existing challenges, which exposes them to data contamination and potential cheating. Notably, we confirmed these issues in practice by integrating web search tools into an existing agent. To address these limitations, we present CTFusion, a streaming evaluation framework built on Live CTFs. To achieve this, CTFusion preserves per-agent independence under a single team account and reduces competition impact by forwarding only the first correct flag per challenge. Moreover, we implement CTFusion as a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server on the widely used CTFd platform, which offers broad applicability to diverse CTF events and agent types. Through experiments with three LLMs, two agents, and five Live CTFs, we demonstrate that existing CTF benchmarks can be unreliable in assessing LLM-based agents, while CTFusion can serve as a robust solution for evaluating cybersecurity agents. We release CTFusion as open source to foster future research in this area.

</details>


### [105] [EqOD: Symmetry-Informed Stability Selection for PDE Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11524)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为EqOD的全自动偏微分方程（PDE）数据驱动辨识方法，通过结合伽利略不变性检测和随机化LASSO稳定性选择两种候选库缩减机制，在噪声环境下有效减少误报并提升发现准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动PDE发现方法依赖对候选微分算子库的稀疏回归，但候选库过大时在观测噪声下会产生大量假阳性，过小则可能遗漏真实项。如何在保证不遗漏真实项的前提下缩减候选库、降低噪声敏感性和假阳性率，是该领域亟待解决的问题。

Method: EqOD首先对轨迹数据进行弱形式结构测试以检测伽利略不变性；若检测到，则应用作者证明的伽利略排除结果，使用对称性缩减库，剔除不可能出现在控制方程中的项。若未检测到，则采用经典假阳性界引导的随机化LASSO稳定性选择进行库缩减。同时，设计基于残差的回退机制，确保性能不低于全库基线。方法在显式自治性与库假设下有理论保障。

Result: 在8个PDE、4个噪声水平下，EqOD在20%噪声的热方程上获得F1=1.000±0.000，显著优于WF-LASSO（0.475±0.181）、官方PySINDy 2.0（0.000）和WSINDy重实现（0.789）。在严格准则下，EqOD在32个实验单元中胜出7个，WF-LASSO无一胜出，其余25个为平局。在全部32个单元中，EqOD在23个单元上优于PySINDy 2.0.0（PySINDy的5次胜出均在反应PDE上）。在WeakIdent和PINN-SR数据集的外部验证中，EqOD在所有5个纯净基准上获得F1=1.000。另报告了NLS、2D、耦合系统和圆柱绕流扩展。

Conclusion: EqOD通过融合伽利略不变性驱动的库缩减与稳定性选择，实现了更鲁棒、自动化的PDE发现，在高噪声场景下显著超越现有方法。伽利略库缩减在特定假设下有严格证明，稳定性选择由经典假阳性界支撑，但针对相关PDE设计矩阵的形式化保证仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Data-driven identification of partial differential equations (PDEs) relies on sparse regression over a candidate library of differential operators, where larger libraries inflate false positives under observation noise and smaller libraries risk missing true terms. We introduce Equivariant Operator Discovery (EqOD), a fully automatic method combining two library reduction mechanisms. When Galilean invariance is detected from trajectory data via a weak-form structural test, EqOD uses the symmetry-reduced library, eliminating terms that our Galilean exclusion result proves to be absent from the governing equation. Otherwise, it applies randomized LASSO stability selection guided by classical false-positive bounds. A residual-based fallback prevents degradation below the full-library baseline. On 8 PDEs at 4 noise levels, EqOD attains $F_1 = 1.000 \pm 0.000$ on Heat at $20\%$ noise, where WF-LASSO obtains $0.475 \pm 0.181$, official PySINDy 2.0 obtains $0.000$, and the WSINDy reimplementation obtains $0.789$. Under the strict criterion that the mean F1 difference exceeds the larger of the two standard deviations, EqOD wins 7 of 32 cells. WF-LASSO wins none, and the remaining 25 cells are ties. Across all 32 cells, EqOD outperforms PySINDy 2.0.0 in 23 of 32 cells, and all 5 PySINDy wins occur on reaction PDEs. External validation on WeakIdent and PINN-SR datasets gives $F_1 = 1.000$ on all 5 clean benchmarks. NLS, 2D, coupled-system, and cylinder-wake extensions are reported. The Galilean library reduction is proved under explicit autonomy and library assumptions. The stability-selection step is motivated by classical false-positive bounds, while formal guarantees for correlated PDE design matrices remain open.

</details>


### [106] [OverNaN: NaN-Aware Oversampling for Imbalanced Learning with Meaningful Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11525)
*Amanda S Barnard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OverNaN，一个保留缺失值结构的轻量级过采样框架，用于处理不完整且不平衡的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将缺失值视为缺陷并删除或插补，但这会破坏缺失模式所携带的信息，尤其在类别不平衡的数据集中会导致偏差和泛化能力下降。

Method: OverNaN扩展现有合成过采样方法，使其直接在不完整特征向量上操作，允许根据明确策略保留、传播或选择性插值缺失值，将缺失性视为特征空间的一部分。

Result: 通过代表性示例证明，在过采样过程中可以保留有意义的缺失性，而不会引入人为的确定性。

Conclusion: OverNaN适用于科学和工程领域中不可避免的缺失数据场景，为处理小型、不完整且不平衡的数据集提供了新思路。

Abstract: Missing values are routinely treated as defects to be eliminated through deletion or imputation prior to machine learning. In many applied domains, however, missingness itself carries information, reflecting experimental constraints, measurement choices, or systematic mechanisms tied to the data-generating process. Eliminating or masking this structure can distort class boundaries, introduce bias, and reduce generalisability; particularly in imbalanced datasets where minority classes are already under-represented. OverNaN is a lightweight, NaN-aware oversampling framework designed to address class imbalance without erasing missingness structure. It extends common synthetic oversampling methods to operate directly on incomplete feature vectors, allowing missing values to be preserved, propagated, or selectively interpolated according to explicitly defined strategies. Rather than repairing missing data, OverNaN treats missingness as part of the feature space over which synthetic samples are generated. This paper situates OverNaN within the broader landscape of imbalanced learning, missing-data handling, and NaN-tolerant algorithms. Using representative examples included with the software, we demonstrate that meaningful missingness can be retained during oversampling without introducing artificial certainty. OverNaN is intended for practitioners working with small, incomplete, and imbalanced datasets in scientific and engineering domains where missingness is unavoidable and often informative.

</details>


### [107] [FERMI: Exploiting Relations for Membership Inference Against Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11527)
*Abtin Mahyar,Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Yuhan Liu,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FERMI，一种针对多关系表格扩散模型的成员推理攻击方法，通过利用父表辅助信息显著提升了攻击性能，揭示了现有单表攻击方法低估了多表场景下的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为表格数据合成的主流方法并广泛用于共享敏感记录，但其隐私保护效果尚存疑。现有的成员推理攻击仅适用于单表场景，忽略了现实敏感数据的多关系结构，无法充分利用关联表的辅助信息来评估隐私风险。

Method: 提出FERMI（FEature-mapping for Relational Membership Inference）方法，通过特征映射技术将单表特征与关系成员信号相结合。该方法解决了一个实际设置：辅助信息仅在训练攻击模型时可用，而在推理时攻击者仅能观察到目标表的记录属性值。

Result: 在三种表格扩散架构和三个真实世界关系数据集上的实验表明，FERMI一致地优于单表基线方法。在白盒设置下，TPR@0.1FPR相比单表基线提升高达53%；在黑盒设置下提升高达22%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当前单表成员推理攻击方法严重低估了多关系表格扩散模型的隐私风险，需要开发新的隐私保护机制来应对关系数据中的隐私泄露问题。

Abstract: Diffusion models are the leading approach for tabular data synthesis and are increasingly used to share sensitive records. Whether they actually protect privacy has become a pressing question. Membership inference attacks are the standard tool for this purpose, yet existing attacks assume a single-table setting and ignore the multi-relational structure of real sensitive data. A core challenge in assessing privacy risks from membership inference attacks in multi-table settings is how to leverage auxiliary information from relations associated with the target table, such as its parent tables. Particularly, we study a practical setting in which such auxiliary information is available only when training the attack model. At inference time, the attacker observes only the attribute values of the target record from the target table. We propose FERMI (FEature-mapping for Relational Membership Inference), which resolves this gap by enriching single-table features with relational membership signal. Across three tabular diffusion architectures and three real-world relational datasets, FERMI consistently improves attack performance over single-table baselines, with TPR@$0.1$FPR rising by up to 53% over the single-table baseline in the white-box setting and 22% in the black-box setting.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Narrow Transformation as a Single-Model Ensemble: Boundary Conditions, Mechanisms, and Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11530)
*Tatsuhito Hasegawa,Taisei Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在参数预算近似匹配的条件下，单模型集成(SME)中模型容量的分配策略效果依赖于数据量：低数据场景下“多窄路径”配置更优，高数据场景下“单宽路径”配置更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模型集成(SME)可在单个网络内近似深度集成的优势，但在参数预算近似匹配的约束下，尚不清楚应将模型容量集中于单条宽路径还是重新分配为多条窄且独立的成员，本文旨在探究这一问题。

Method: 提出Multi-Narrow (MN)变换，将基线CNN转换为具有路径独立分支的窄路径SME，同时近似保持参数预算；系统比较了Single-Wide和Multi-Narrow配置在不同训练数据规模、架构和数据集上的表现，并分析了内部表示。

Result: MN的有效性高度依赖数据量：在数据丰富的场景下，弱分区或基线宽模型更优；而在低数据场景下，高分区MN模型持续优于基线。该趋势在多种CNN架构和图像分类数据集上复现。表示分析显示高MN模型学习到更多样且冗余更低的路径特征，在低数据场景下该多样性被广泛利用并改善泛化，而在数据丰富场景下训练变得不平衡且预测仅由少数路径主导。

Conclusion: 明确了Multi-Narrow变换生效的条件与原因，为在有限预算下如何在宽度与成员数量之间分配模型容量提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Single-model ensembles (SMEs) have attracted attention as a way to approximate some of the benefits of deep ensembles within a single network. However, under an approximately matched parameter budget, it remains unclear whether model capacity should be concentrated in a single wide pathway or redistributed into many narrow and independent members. We investigate this question through the Multi-Narrow (MN) transformation, which converts a baseline CNN into an SME of narrow, path-wise independent branches while approximately preserving the dominant parameter budget. We systematically compare Single-Wide and Multi-Narrow configurations across different training-data regimes, architectures, and datasets. The results show that the effectiveness of MN is strongly data-dependent: weakly partitioned or baseline-wide models are preferable in data-rich settings, whereas highly partitioned MN models consistently outperform the baseline in low-data settings. This tendency is reproduced across multiple CNN architectures and image-classification datasets, suggesting that it is not specific to a single benchmark or model family. Analysis of internal representations shows that high-MN models learn more diverse and less redundant path-wise features. In low-data regimes, this diversity is broadly utilized and improves generalization, whereas in data-rich regimes, training becomes imbalanced and prediction is dominated by a small subset of paths. These findings clarify when and why Multi-Narrow transformation is effective, and provide practical guidance for allocating model capacity between width and member multiplicity under a limited budget.

</details>


### [109] [Primal-Dual Policy Optimization for Linear CMDPs with Adversarial Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11535)
*Kihyun Yu,Seoungbin Bae,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次针对在线有限视界对抗性线性约束马尔可夫决策过程提出了一个原对偶策略优化算法，达到了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$的次线性遗憾和约束违反界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDPs）研究主要集中在随机设置上，其中损失和成本是固定的或从固定分布中抽取。然而，这种表述本质上容易受到对抗性变化环境的影响。为了克服这一局限性，本文研究了对抗性线性CMDPs，旨在解决更具挑战性的环境设置。

Method: 提出了一种原对偶策略优化算法，该算法引入并运行一类新策略——加权LogSumExp softmax策略，专门设计用于适应对抗性选择的损失函数。算法包含两个新颖的算法组件：(i) 周期性策略混合；(ii) 正则化对偶更新，这些组件有效控制了覆盖数和对偶变量。

Result: 算法在对抗性线性CMDPs设置下达到了次线性的遗憾和约束违反界限，两者均被$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$所界定，其中$K$表示回合数。数值实验结果验证了算法性能的理论发现。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了对抗性环境下的线性CMDPs问题，提出了首个在此设置下达到次线性遗憾和约束违反界限的算法，主要贡献在于建立了加权LogSumExp softmax策略的新覆盖数论证，并设计了周期性策略混合和正则化对偶更新两个关键算法组件。

Abstract: Existing work on linear constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs) has primarily focused on stochastic settings, where the losses and costs are either fixed or drawn from fixed distributions. However, such formulations are inherently vulnerable to adversarially changing environments. To overcome this limitation, we propose a primal-dual policy optimization algorithm for online finite-horizon {adversarial} linear CMDPs, where the losses are adversarially chosen under full-information feedback and the costs are stochastic under bandit feedback. Our algorithm is the \emph{first} to achieve sublinear regret and constraint violation bounds in this setting, both bounded by $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. The algorithm introduces and runs with a new class of policies, which we call weighted LogSumExp softmax policies, designed to adapt to adversarially chosen loss functions. Our main result stems from the following key contributions: (i) a new covering number argument for the weighted LogSumExp softmax policies, and (ii) two novel algorithmic components -- periodic policy mixing and a regularized dual update -- which allow us to effectively control both the covering number and the dual variable. We also report numerical results that validate our theoretical findings on the performance of the algorithm.

</details>


### [110] [Fast MoE Inference via Predictive Prefetching and Expert Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11537)
*Ankit Jyothish,Ali Jannesari,Aishwarya Sarkar,Joseph Zuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态专家复制策略，通过预测并复制过载专家实现并发处理，解决MoE推理中的负载不平衡问题，在保持90-95%性能的同时实现近100% GPU利用率，推理速度提升最高3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在大语言模型中广泛应用，但推理过程存在GPU利用率低、负载不平衡和延迟高的问题，主要源于专家激活的稀疏性导致多个token等待同一专家计算。

Method: 提出动态专家复制策略，预测可能过载的专家并在即将处理的token批次中提前复制这些专家，使复制的专家能够跨层并发处理批次token，从而提高并行性并减少GPU空闲时间。

Result: 在Switch-base-128和Switch-base-256等大规模MoE模型上的实验表明，该方法实现近100%的GPU利用率，推理速度提升最高3倍，同时保持约90-95%的基准架构性能。

Conclusion: 动态专家复制策略有效解决了MoE推理中的负载不平衡问题，显著提升GPU利用率和推理速度，为MoE模型的实际部署提供了可行的优化方案。

Abstract: The Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture has become a fundamental building block in state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), improving domain-specific expertise in LLMs and scaling model capacity without proportionally increasing their computational overhead. However, MoE inference often suffers from suboptimal GPU utilization, load imbalance, and elevated latency arising from multiple tokens waiting on the same experts for their computation which arises from sparsity of expert activation. To address these challenges, we propose a dynamic expert replication strategy that predicts which experts are likely to be overloaded and replicates them for upcoming batches of tokens. The replicated experts process batch tokens concurrently across layers, which leads to improved parallelism, shorter GPU idle time, and significantly faster inference. Experimental evaluations conducted on large-scale MoE models, including Switch-base-128 and Switch-base-256, demonstrate that our method achieves near-complete GPU utilization (approx 100%), leading to upto 3x improvement in inference speed while preserving approximately 90-95% of the performance of baseline architectures

</details>


### [111] [Sharpen Your Flow: Sharpness-Aware Sampling for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11547)
*Aditi Gupta,Soon Hoe Lim,Annan Yu,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SharpEuler，一种无需训练的采样器，通过离线分析速度场的快速变化区域来优化时间步分配，在不增加计算成本的情况下提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型的快速生成需要在有限的神经网络评估预算内进行有效积分，核心问题在于如何决定采样器在各时间步的步数分配，而不仅仅是积分方法本身。

Method: SharpEuler通过离线分析预训练模型，沿校准轨迹估计学习速度场变化最快的区域，使用有限差分估计定义求解器感知的锐度分布，经平滑和分位数变换生成任意推理预算的时间步网格，测试时仍采用常规Euler积分。

Result: 实验表明SharpEuler在固定计算预算下提升样本质量，减少模式间泄漏并增加模式覆盖率，具有相同的模型评估次数。

Conclusion: 通过数值原理（轨迹加速度是Euler离散化误差的主要来源）、变分原理（推导基于锐度的幂律时间步密度）和统计保证（有限样本校准采样器在终端分布水平稳定），证明了SharpEuler的有效性和稳定性。

Abstract: Flow matching models generate samples by numerically integrating a learned velocity field, with each integration step requiring a neural network evaluation. Fast generation therefore requires using a small fixed evaluation budget effectively: the key question is not only how to integrate the flow, but where the sampler should spend its steps. We propose SharpEuler, a training-free sampler that profiles a pretrained model offline by estimating where the learned velocity field changes most rapidly along calibration trajectories. This finite-difference estimate defines a solver-aware sharpness profile, which is smoothed and converted by a quantile transform into a timestep grid for any desired inference budget. At test time, sampling remains ordinary Euler integration with the same number of model evaluations as a uniform schedule. We justify SharpEuler using three principles: a numerical principle identifying trajectory acceleration as the leading source of Euler discretization error, a variational principle deriving sharpness-based power-law timestep densities, and a statistical guarantee showing that the finite-sample calibrated sampler is stable at the terminal distribution level. Our experiments show that SharpEuler improves sample quality at fixed budgets, reducing inter-mode leakage and increasing mode coverage.

</details>


### [112] [VNDUQE: Information-Theoretic Novelty Detection using Deep Variational Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11551)
*Aryan Gondkar,Hayder Radha,Yiming Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VNDUQE方法，基于深度变分信息瓶颈进行OOD检测，通过KL散度和预测熵的互补策略实现95.3% AUROC，较基线提升32个百分点，并降低校准误差38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中检测分布外（OOD）样本至关重要。现有最大软最大概率（MSP）基线方法缺乏理论基础且存在校准不准问题，需要更可靠、理论完备的检测方法。

Method: 提出VNDUQE方法，基于深度变分信息瓶颈（VIB）显式约束表征的信息流。在MNIST上训练并保留部分数字类别，采用信息论度量（KL散度和预测熵）进行OOD检测，并设计并行检测策略融合两种指标。

Result: KL散度在远OOD检测上达到100% AUROC，预测熵在近OOD检测上达到94.7% AUROC。并行策略实现95.3%平均AUROC和92% TPR@5%FPR，较MSP基线分别提升10.3和31.9个百分点。信息瓶颈压缩使期望校准误差降低38%。

Conclusion: 信息论约束能够产生根本性更可靠的置信度估计，KL散度和预测熵提供互补的检测信号。该方法直接支持计算昂贵的主动学习场景，为oracle查询提供原则性的阈值选择机制。

Abstract: Detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) samples is critical for safe deployment of neural networks in safety-critical applications. While maximum softmax probability (MSP) provides a simple baseline, it lacks theoretical grounding and suffers from miscalibration. We propose VNDUQE (VIB-based Novelty Detection and Uncertainty Quantification for Nondestructive Evaluation), which investigates novelty detection through the Deep Variational Information Bottleneck (VIB), which explicitly constrains information flow through learned representations. We train VIB models on MNIST with held-out digit classes and evaluate OOD detection using information-theoretic metrics: KL divergence and prediction entropy. Our results reveal complementary detection signals: KL divergence achieves perfect detection (100\% AUROC on noise) on far-OOD samples (noise, domain shift), while prediction entropy excels at near-OOD detection (94.7\% AUROC on novel digit classes). A parallel detection strategy combining both metrics achieves 95.3\% average AUROC and 92\% true positive rate at 5\% false positive rate, which is a 32 percentage point improvement over baseline MSP (85.0\% AUROC, 60.1\% TPR). Compression via the information bottleneck principle ($β=10^{-3}$) reduces Expected Calibration Error by 38\%, demonstrating that information-theoretic constraints produce fundamentally more reliable uncertainty estimates. These findings directly support active learning with expensive computational oracles, where well-calibrated novelty detection enables principled threshold selection for oracle queries.

</details>


### [113] [A Controlled Counterexample to Strong Proxy-Based Explanations of OOD Performance: in a Fixed Pretraining-and-Probing Setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11554)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过构建反例证明，任务无关的结构代理排名可能与下游任务的OOD探针准确率排名不一致，从而揭示了代理解释方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练语料库迁移性能解释常依赖任务无关的结构代理，但这类解释的前提是代理必须能够捕捉对下游任务真正重要的结构。本文旨在验证这一前提是否成立，即结构代理的排名是否必然与OOD（分布外）探针准确率的排名一致。

Method: 在一个受控的预训练与探针设置中，首先提供了一个形式化的结构构造，使结构量、操作代理和任务相关结构三者分离。随后在合成序列模型实验中实例化该机制，对比代理排名与OOD准确率排名，并通过辅助诊断和消融实验验证机制。

Result: 在主要的全样本评估下，三个随机种子中有两个出现OOD准确率排名与代理排名相反的情况。辅助诊断和消融实验均支持该解释。这表明即使在对受控设置中，用于衡量总学习结构的代理也可能无法追踪驱动OOD性能的任务相关结构。

Conclusion: 本反例并未全盘否定基于结构的解释方法，但明确界定了强代理解释的边界：任务无关的结构代理并不必然反映真正影响任务性能的结构，代理排名可能与实际性能排名不一致。

Abstract: Task-agnostic structure proxies are often used to interpret why one pretraining corpus transfers better than another, but such explanations require the proxy to track the structure that matters for the downstream task. We test this requirement in a fixed pretraining-and-probing setup motivated by computationally bounded notions of learned structure, including epiplexity. The core question is whether a proxy ranking of two pretraining datasets must agree with their ranking by OOD probe accuracy. We show that it need not. First, we give a controlled construction in which a formal structure quantity, its operational proxy, and the task-relevant structure for a target family separate. We then instantiate the same mechanism in a synthetic sequence-model experiment: under the primary all-sample evaluation, the OOD accuracy ranking reverses the proxy ranking in two of three seeds, with auxiliary diagnostics and ablations supporting the same interpretation. The counterexample does not reject structure-based explanations in general; it identifies a boundary on strong proxy-based explanations. A proxy for total learned structure can fail to track the task-relevant structure that drives OOD performance, even in a controlled setting.

</details>


### [114] [A Composite Activation Function for Learning Stable Binary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11558)
*Seokhun Park,Choeun Kim,Kwanho Lee,Sehyun Park,Insung Kong,Yongdai Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了重尾激活函数（HTAF），一种Heaviside函数的平滑近似方法，成功解决了二值激活网络训练中的梯度优化难题，并基于此构建了可解释的隐式概念瓶颈模型（ICBM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值激活表示在计算效率、内存效率和可解释性方面具有显著优势，但Heaviside激活函数的不可微性阻碍了标准的基于梯度的优化，使得训练具有挑战性。现有方法难以在保持二值化优势的同时实现稳定的梯度训练。

Method: 提出重尾激活函数（HTAF）作为Heaviside函数的平滑近似，将其构造为sigmoid双曲正切复合函数。理论上证明HTAF在零输入附近保持较大的梯度质量，同时在尾部区域展现较慢的梯度衰减特性，从而支持稳定的梯度优化。该方法可应用于脉冲神经网络、二值神经网络和深度Heaviside神经网络。

Result: HTAF成功实现了对多种网络架构（脉冲神经网络、二值神经网络、深度Heaviside神经网络）的稳定训练。基于HTAF构建的隐式概念瓶颈模型（ICBM）在多个图像数据集上实现了稳定的离散化，预测性能达到或超过标准模型水平。

Conclusion: HTAF为二值激活神经网络提供了一种有效的训练解决方案，在保持计算和内存效率的同时实现了稳定的梯度优化。ICBM模型展示了HTAF在构建可解释性图像模型方面的潜力，为离散特征表示学习开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Activation functions play a central role in neural networks by shaping internal representations. Recently, learning binary activation representations has attracted significant attention due to their advantages in computational and memory efficiency, as well as interpretability. However, training neural networks with Heaviside activations remains challenging, as their non-differentiability obstructs standard gradient-based optimization. In this paper, we propose Heavy Tailed Activation Function (HTAF), a smooth approximation to the Heaviside function that enables stable training with gradient-based optimization. We construct HTAF as a sigmoid hyperbolic tangent composite function and theoretically show that it maintains a large gradient mass around zero inputs while exhibiting slower gradient decay in the tail regions. We show that Spiking Neural Networks, Binary Neural Networks and Deep Heaviside neural Networks can be trained stably using HTAF with gradient-based optimization. Finally, we introduce Implicit Concept Bottleneck Models (ICBMs), an interpretable image model that leverages HTAF to induce discrete feature representations. Extensive experiments across various architectures and image datasets demonstrate that ICBM enables stable discretization while achieving prediction performance comparable to or better than standard models.

</details>


### [115] [OUI as a Structural Observable: Towards an Activation-Centric View of Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11570)
*Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Jose I. Mestre,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Manuel F. Dolz,Jose Duato,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出过拟合-欠拟合指标（OUI）作为一种基于激活函数的早期、无标签信号，能够在收敛前揭示网络训练状态，为理解深度网络内部结构演化提供了首个实用观测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活函数赋予深度网络表达能力，但目前训练评估主要依赖损失、准确率等外部指标，网络内部结构的演化过程长期未被观测。研究者希望找到一种能够反映网络内部训练状态的实用观测指标。

Method: 提出过拟合-欠拟合指标（OUI），作为一种基于激活函数的早期、无标签信号，用于监测网络内部结构演化。在监督学习、强化学习（PPO actor-critic）和在线控制等多个场景中验证其有效性。

Result: OUI在监督学习中能够预测权重衰减机制，在强化学习中早期区分PPO的学习率机制，在在线控制中可驱动层级权重衰减自适应。实验表明OUI能够早期识别训练进入良好或不良状态。

Conclusion: 结合激活模式比参数更早稳定的证据，这些结果指向一个更广阔的研究方向：以激活为中心的训练动力学理论。OUI正成为通向这一理论的实证基础。

Abstract: Activation functions are what make deep networks expressive: without them, the model collapses to a linear map. Yet we still evaluate training mostly from the outside, through loss, accuracy, return, or final calibration, while the internal structural evolution of the network remains largely unobserved. In this paper, we argue that the Overfitting--Underfitting Indicator (OUI) should be understood as a first practical observable of that internal structure. Across our recent results, OUI consistently appears as an early, label-free, activation-based signal that reveals whether a network is entering a poor or promising training regime before convergence. In supervised learning, it anticipates weight decay regimes; in reinforcement learning, it discriminates learning-rate regimes early in PPO actor--critic; and in online control, it can drive layer-wise weight decay adaptation. Read together with recent evidence that activation patterns tend to stabilize earlier than parameters, these results suggest a broader research direction: an activation-centric theory of training dynamics. OUI is becoming an empirical foothold toward this theory.

</details>


### [116] [FedOUI: OUI-Guided Client Weighting for Federated Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11571)
*Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Jose I. Mestre,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Manuel F. Dolz,Jose Duato,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FedOUI，一种基于过拟合-欠拟合指标（OUI）的联邦学习聚合规则，利用客户端模型的内部激活结构信息进行加权聚合，在强异构和噪声客户端条件下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法通常基于数据集大小或梯度级别标准聚合客户端更新，忽略了客户端模型在训练过程中如何组织其输入空间的内部信号，这限制了聚合质量。

Method: 引入基于激活且无需标签的过拟合-欠拟合指标（OUI）。每个客户端发送本地更新及在固定探测批次上计算的OUI值，服务器估计每轮OUI分布，通过平滑重加权规则为结构性异常客户端分配较低权重。

Result: 在CIFAR-10强非独立同分布（non-IID）划分和噪声客户端条件下，与FedAvg、FedProx及梯度对齐基线相比，FedOUI在强异构环境下取得了最显著的性能提升，且保持轻量级和可解释性。

Conclusion: 内部激活结构可为联邦聚合提供除客户端规模和梯度几何之外的有用信息，基于OUI的加权能有效提升聚合质量。

Abstract: Federated learning usually aggregates client updates using dataset size or gradient-level criteria, while overlooking internal signals about how each client model is organizing its input space during training. We introduce FedOUI, a simple aggregation rule based on the Overfitting-Underfitting Indicator (OUI), an activation-based and label-free metric. Each participating client sends its local update together with a OUI value computed on a fixed probe batch, and the server estimates the round-wise OUI distribution to assign lower weights to structurally atypical clients through a smooth reweighting rule. We evaluate FedOUI on CIFAR-10 under strong non-IID partitioning and noisy-client conditions, comparing it with FedAvg, FedProx, and a gradient-alignment baseline. The clearest gains appear under strong heterogeneity, where OUI-based weighting improves aggregation quality while remaining lightweight and interpretable. These results show that internal activation structure can provide useful information for federated aggregation beyond client size and gradient geometry.

</details>


### [117] [Stargazer: A Scalable Model-Fitting Benchmark Environment for AI Agents under Astrophysical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15664)
*Xinge Liu,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin,Kristen Menou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Stargazer，一个基于径向速度时间序列数据的可扩展动态基准环境，用于评估AI智能体在物理约束模型拟合任务上的表现。对8个前沿智能体的评估显示，它们虽然能获得良好的统计拟合，但常常无法恢复正确的物理系统参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体的兴起，需要具有内置反馈的动态基准环境，在科学任务上评估这些智能体的研究工作能力，特别是物理学基础的模型拟合能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含120个任务的动态迭代基准环境，涵盖三个难度级别，包括20个真实档案案例，从高信噪比单行星系统到需要复杂低信噪比分析的多行星配置。对8个前沿AI智能体进行了系统性评估。

Result: 评估发现智能体在数值优化和物理约束遵循之间存在差距：虽然智能体经常能达到良好的统计拟合，但经常无法恢复正确的物理系统参数，即使配备基础技能也是如此。增加测试时计算仅带来边际收益，过多的token使用往往反映递归失败循环而非有意义的探索。

Conclusion: Stargazer为在具有实际研究相关性的模型拟合问题上训练、评估、支撑和扩展策略提供了机会。设计模拟驱动环境的方法论可推广到跨科学领域的许多其他模型拟合问题。

Abstract: The rise of autonomous AI agents suggests that dynamic benchmark environments with built-in feedback on scientifically grounded tasks are needed to evaluate the capabilities of these agents in research work. We introduce Stargazer, a scalable environment for evaluating AI agents on dynamic, iterative physics-grounded model-fitting tasks using inference on radial-velocity (RV) time series data. Stargazer comprises 120 tasks across three difficulty tiers, including 20 real archival cases, covering diverse scenarios ranging from high-SNR single-planet systems to complex multi-planetary configurations requiring involved low-SNR analysis. Our evaluation of eight frontier agents reveals a gap between numerical optimization and adherence to physical constraints: although agents often achieve a good statistical fit, they frequently fail to recover correct physical system parameters, a limitation that persists even when agents are equipped with vanilla skills. Furthermore, increasing test-time compute yields only marginal gains, with excessive token usage often reflecting recursive failure loops rather than meaningful exploration. Stargazer presents an opportunity to train, evaluate, scaffold, and scale strategies on a model-fitting problem of practical research relevance today. Our methodology to design a simulation-driven environment for AI agents presumably generalizes to many other model-fitting problems across scientific domains. Source code and the project website are available at https://github.com/AIPS-UofT/Stargazer and https://aips-uoft.github.io/Stargazer/, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [Learning Weakly Communicating Average-Reward CMDPs: Strong Duality and Improved Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11586)
*Kihyun Yu,Beomhan Baek,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱连通平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程的强对偶性，并基于此提出了裁剪值迭代算法，将遗憾和约束违反界限改进至O(T^{2/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱连通假设下的无限时域平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程。在缺乏线性规划公式且非凸的情况下，强对偶性是否成立尚不明确；同时现有的学习算法遗憾和约束违反界限有待改进。

Method: 首先通过仔细利用占用测度集的几何结构，证明了弱连通平均奖励CMDP在平稳策略下的强对偶性。其次，基于强对偶结果，将裁剪值迭代扩展到约束设置并适应有限时域近似，提出了原始-对偶裁剪值迭代算法来学习弱连通平均奖励线性CMDP。通过开发基于强对偶的新方法，将复合拉格朗日遗憾分解为单独的遗憾和约束违反界限进行分析。

Result: 成功证明了弱连通平均奖励CMDP在平稳策略下的强对偶性。所提出的算法实现了O(T^{2/3})的遗憾和约束违反界限，优于当时已知的最优界限。

Conclusion: 即使在弱连通设置下缺乏线性规划公式和非凸性，强对偶性仍然成立。基于此的原始-对偶裁剪值迭代算法通过有限时域近似稳定对偶变量，能够有效学习弱连通平均奖励线性CMDP并取得改进的性能界限。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon average-reward constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs) under the weakly communicating assumption. Our contributions are twofold. First, we establish strong duality for weakly communicating average-reward CMDPs over stationary policies with finite state and action spaces. Despite the absence of a linear programming formulation and the resulting nonconvexity under the weakly communicating setting, we show that strong duality still holds by carefully exploiting the geometric structure of the occupation measure set. Second, building on this result, we propose a primal--dual clipped value iteration algorithm for learning weakly communicating average-reward linear CMDPs. Our algorithm achieves regret and constraint violation bounds of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, improving upon the best known bounds, where $T$ denotes the number of interactions. Our approach extends clipped value iteration to the constrained setting and adapts it to a finite-horizon approximation, which stabilizes the dual variable and is crucial for achieving improved regret bounds. To analyze this, we develop a novel approach based on strong duality that enables the decomposition of the composite Lagrangian regret into separate bounds on regret and constraint violation.

</details>


### [119] [SoK: Unlearnability and Unlearning for Model Dememorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11592)
*Mengying Zhang,Derui Wang,Ruoxi Sun,Xiaoyu Xia,Shuang Hao,Minhui Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地分析了机器学习中两种关键的模型去记忆化方法——不可学习性和机器遗忘，揭示了它们共同的浅层去记忆化脆弱性及其相互影响，并提供了首个关于去记忆化深度的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的两种模型去记忆化防御方法（不可学习性和机器遗忘）虽然都旨在保护数据隐私，但其脆弱性和共同基础尚不明确。两者都存在浅层去记忆化问题，即在权重扰动下可能虚假地声称已降低数据可学习性或实现遗忘。此外，输入扰动可能影响下游遗忘的有效性，而遗忘操作可能意外恢复被不可学习性隐藏的领域知识。目前缺乏针对浅层去记忆化的形式化理论保证。

Method: 本文提出了首个整合分析框架，包含三个核心贡献：(i) 提出了不可学习性和可扩展遗忘方法的统一分类体系；(ii) 对代表性方法进行了实证评估，揭示其鲁棒性、相互作用及浅层去记忆化问题；(iii) 提出了首个针对经过认证遗忘处理的模型的去记忆化深度的理论保证。

Result: 研究发现了领先方法在鲁棒性、相互作用和浅层去记忆化方面的具体表现，证明了当前防御方法的脆弱性。同时，首次建立了针对去记忆化深度的理论保证，为统一机器学习生命周期中的去记忆化机制奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 本研究为跨机器学习生命周期统一去记忆化机制奠定了理论基础，有助于实现对敏感知识更深层次的遗忘状态，为构建更可靠的数据隐私保护方案提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Advanced model dememorization methods, including availability poisoning (unlearnability) and machine unlearning, are emerging as key safeguards against data misuse in machine learning (ML). At the training stage, unlearnability embeds imperceptible perturbations into data before release to reduce learnability. At the post-training stage, unlearning removes previously acquired information from models to prevent unauthorized disclosure or use. While both defenses aim to preserve the right to withhold knowledge, their vulnerabilities and shared foundations remain unclear. Specifically, both unlearnability and unlearning suffer from issues such as shallow dememorization, leading to falsely claimed data learnability reduction or forgetting in the presence of weight perturbations. Moreover, input perturbations may affect the effectiveness of downstream unlearning, while unlearning may inadvertently recover domain knowledge hidden by unlearnability. This interplay calls for deeper investigation. Finally, there is a lack of formal guarantees to provide theoretical insights into current defenses against shallow dememorization. In this Systematization of Knowledge, we present the first integrated analysis of model dememorization approaches leveraging unlearnability and unlearning. Our contributions are threefold: (i) a unified taxonomy of unlearnability and scalable unlearning methods; (ii) an empirical evaluation revealing the robustness, interplay, and shallow dememorization of leading methods; and (iii) the first theoretical guarantee on dememorization depth for models processed through certified unlearning. These results lay the foundation for unifying dememorization mechanisms across the ML lifecycle to achieve a deeper immemor state for sensitive knowledge.

</details>


### [120] [EpiCastBench: Datasets and Benchmarks for Multivariate Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11598)
*Madhurima Panja,Danny D'Agostino,Huitao Li,Tanujit Chakraborty,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EpiCastBench，一个包含40个精选多元流行病数据集的大规模基准测试框架，用于解决流行病预测领域缺乏高质量基准的问题，并在此框架下对15种多元预测模型进行了全面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动决策在公共卫生领域的普及使得流行病预测成为关键研究领域。尽管多元预测模型比传统单变量方法能更好地捕捉复杂时间依赖关系，但稳健的流行病预测方法发展受到制约，主要原因是缺乏涵盖多种传染病和地理区域的高质量多元数据集基准。

Method: 构建了EpiCastBench大规模基准测试框架，包含40个精选的（相关的）多元流行病数据集。这些数据集涵盖广泛的传染病，在时间粒度、序列长度和稀疏性方面具有多样性特征。建立了标准化评估设置以确保可重现性和公平比较，包括统一的预测范围、一致的预处理流程、多样的性能指标和统计显著性检验。在此框架下对15种多元预测模型进行了综合评估，涵盖从统计基线到最先进的深度学习和基础模型。

Result: 对数据集进行了分析以识别其全局特征和结构模式。基于建立的标准化评估框架，完成了对15种多元预测模型的全面评估比较。所有数据集和代码已在Kaggle和GitHub上公开发布。

Conclusion: EpiCastBench为流行病预测领域提供了首个大规模多元数据集基准，填补了该领域高质量基准缺失的空白。该框架通过标准化评估设置确保了研究的可重现性和公平比较，为未来流行病预测方法的发展提供了重要基础设施支持。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of data-driven decision-making in public health has established epidemic forecasting as a critical area of research. Recent advances in multivariate forecasting models better capture complex temporal dependencies than conventional univariate approaches, which model individual series independently. Despite this potential, the development of robust epidemic forecasting methods is constrained by the lack of high-quality benchmarks comprising diverse multivariate datasets across infectious diseases and geographical regions. To address this gap, we present EpiCastBench, a large-scale benchmarking framework featuring 40 curated (correlated) multivariate epidemic datasets. These publicly available datasets span a wide range of infectious diseases and exhibit diverse characteristics in terms of temporal granularity, series length, and sparsity. We analyze these datasets to identify their global features and structural patterns. To ensure reproducibility and fair comparison, we establish standardized evaluation settings, including a unified forecasting horizon, consistent preprocessing pipelines, diverse performance metrics, and statistical significance testing. By leveraging this framework, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 15 multivariate forecasting models spanning statistical baselines to state-of-the-art deep learning and foundation models. All datasets and code are publicly available on Kaggle (https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/aimltsf/epicastbench) and GitHub (https://github.com/aimltsf/EpiCastBench).

</details>


### [121] [Targeted Tests for LLM Reasoning: An Audit-Constrained Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11599)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个审计约束的协议用于针对性推理评估，并在该协议下实例化了CAPS采样器，虽然协议能有效识别模型错误并排除伪影，但CAPS在审计后的收益上并未优于均匀采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的固定推理基准仅评估规范提示，但语义有效的呈现变化仍可能改变模型行为。现有提示变异研究缺乏审计，可能将真正的模型错误与无效扰动、提取伪影和不匹配的搜索程序混合在一起，导致评估结果不准确。

Method: 提出了一个审计约束的协议：提示变体由有限组件语法生成、确定性渲染、在固定查询预算下评估，并仅在通过语义和提取审计后才计为模型错误。在此协议下实例化了CAPS（一种基于分数的提示组件采样器），并与等预算均匀采样进行对比。

Result: 在三个审计切片中，该协议成功识别了确认的模型错误提示键，同时排除了格式化和提取伪影。然而，匹配比较并未显示CAPS在审计后的收益或独特提示键发现方面优于均匀采样。

Conclusion: 贡献在于方法论层面：针对性的提示变异可以在可重构、可审查、预算匹配的协议下进行研究，代理引导的策略应通过审计后的收益而非原始不匹配计数或选定示例来评判。

Abstract: Fixed reasoning benchmarks evaluate canonical prompts, but semantically valid changes in presentation can still change model behavior. Studies of prompt variation can reveal such failures, but without audit they can mix genuine model errors with invalid perturbations, extraction artifacts, and unmatched search procedures. We propose an audit-constrained protocol for targeted reasoning evaluation. Prompt variants are generated from a finite component grammar, rendered deterministically, evaluated under a fixed query budget, and counted as model errors only after semantic and extraction audit. Within this protocol we instantiate Component-Adaptive Prompt Sampling (CAPS), a score-based sampler over prompt components, and compare it with equal-budget uniform component sampling under the same task bank, renderer, model interface, decoding settings, and audit procedure. Across three audited slices, the protocol identifies confirmed model-error prompt keys while excluding formatting and extraction artifacts, but matched comparisons do not show that CAPS improves audited yield or unique prompt-key discovery over uniform sampling. The contribution is methodological: targeted prompt variation can be studied under a reconstructable, reviewable, budget-matched protocol, and proxy-guided policies should be judged by audited yield rather than raw mismatch counts or selected examples alone.

</details>


### [122] [Anti-Self-Distillation for Reasoning RL via Pointwise Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11609)
*Guobin Shen,Xiang Cheng,Chenxiao Zhao,Lei Huang,Jindong Li,Dongcheng Zhao,Xing Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Anti-Self-Distillation (AntiSD)，通过反转自蒸馏的优化方向并引入熵触发门控机制，在数学推理任务上以2-10倍更少的训练步数达到基线性能，最终准确率提升高达11.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的同策略自蒸馏方法在数学推理任务中表现不稳定。通过逐点互信息分析发现，特权上下文会导致教师模型对解题过程中已隐含的结构性token过度自信，而对驱动多步搜索的深思token（如"Wait"、"Let"、"Maybe"）信心不足，这正是导致自蒸馏失败的根本原因。

Method: 提出Anti-Self-Distillation (AntiSD)，与传统自蒸馏最小化师生分布散度不同，AntiSD通过增大散度来反转每个token的优化符号，从而获得自然有界的优势。同时设计熵触发门控机制，当教师熵坍缩时自动禁用该项，形成可直接替换默认自蒸馏的即插即用方案。

Result: 在4B到30B参数规模的五个模型上进行实验，在数学推理基准测试中，AntiSD仅需GRPO基线2到10倍的训练步数即可达到相同准确率，最终准确率最高提升11.5个点。

Conclusion: AntiSD为语言模型的规模化自我提升开辟了新路径，使模型能够通过自身的训练信号进行推理能力的自举式进化，摆脱了对更强外部教师的依赖。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation, where a student is pulled toward a copy of itself conditioned on privileged context (e.g., a verified solution or feedback), offers a promising direction for advancing reasoning capability without a stronger external teacher. Yet in math reasoning the gains are inconsistent, even when the same approach succeeds elsewhere. A pointwise mutual information analysis traces the failure to the privileged context itself: it inflates the teacher's confidence on tokens already implied by the solution (structural connectives, verifiable claims) and deflates it on deliberation tokens ("Wait", "Let", "Maybe") that drive multi-step search. We propose Anti-Self-Distillation (AntiSD), which ascends a divergence between student and teacher rather than descending it: this reverses the per-token sign and yields a naturally bounded advantage in one step. An entropy-triggered gate disables the term once the teacher entropy collapses, completing a drop-in replacement for default self-distillation. Across five models from 4B to 30B parameters on math reasoning benchmarks, AntiSD reaches the GRPO baseline's accuracy in 2 to 10x fewer training steps and improves final accuracy by up to 11.5 points. AntiSD opens a path to scalable self-improvement, where a language model bootstraps its own reasoning through its training signal.

</details>


### [123] [From Generic Correlation to Input-Specific Credit in On-Policy Self Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11613)
*Guobin Shen,Lei Huang,Xiang Cheng,Chenxiao Zhao,Jindong Li,Dongcheng Zhao,Xing Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CREDIT方法，通过对比学习基线分离输入特定组件，在编码、科学推理和工具使用基准测试中实现了最优综合性能，且计算开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在策略自蒸馏在后训练语言模型中取得了成功，但其奖励信号的实际含义和信用分配机制仍不清晰，需要理论解释来指导方法改进。

Method: 从后验兼容性角度分析，发现自蒸馏词元奖励是贝叶斯滤波增量，其轨迹和等于响应与反馈在给定输入下的点互信息。提出CREDIT方法，利用批量对比基线分离输入特定组件，在序列层面实现对比点互信息目标。

Result: 在两个模型家族的编码、科学推理和工具使用基准测试中，CREDIT在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下取得了最强的综合性能表现。

Conclusion: 该研究为自蒸馏奖励提供了理论解释，将其与点互信息和贝叶斯滤波联系起来，并基于此提出了有效的CREDIT方法，为后训练语言模型提供了新的理论指导和实践方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation has emerged as a promising paradigm for post-training language models, in which the model conditions on environment feedback to serve as its own teacher, providing dense token-level rewards without external teacher models or step-level annotations. Despite its empirical success, what this reward actually measures and what kind of credit it assigns remain unclear. Under a posterior-compatibility interpretation of feedback conditioning, standard in the implicit-reward literature, we show that the self-distillation token reward is a Bayesian filtering increment whose trajectory sum is exactly the pointwise mutual information between the response and the feedback given the input. This pMI can be raised by input-specific reasoning or by input-generic shortcuts, so we further decompose the teacher log-probability along the input axis. Based on this analysis, we propose CREDIT (Contrastive REward from DIsTillation), which isolates the input-specific component with a batch-contrastive baseline. At the sequence level, CREDIT is a teacher-side surrogate for a contrastive pMI objective that also penalizes responses remaining likely under unrelated inputs. Across coding, scientific reasoning, and tool-use benchmarks on two model families, CREDIT delivers the strongest aggregate performance at negligible additional compute.

</details>


### [124] [MIST: Reliable Streaming Decision Trees for Online Class-Incremental Learning via McDiarmid Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11617)
*Phu-Hoa Pham,Chi-Nguyen Tran,Nguyen Lam Phu Quy,Dao Sy Duy Minh,Huynh Trung Kiet,Long Tran-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MIST算法，通过K无关的McDiarmid置信半径、贝叶斯继承协议和KLL分位数草图三大创新，解决了流式决策树在在线类增量学习中因类别数K增长导致的分裂准则失效和知识迁移缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式决策树虽天然适合开放世界持续学习（局部更新、有界内存、静态边界），但在在线类增量学习中仍面临两大耦合失效：类别数K增加时分裂准则可靠性下降；分裂时缺乏知识迁移。根源在于信息增益范围随log₂K缩放，导致基于Hoeffding的置信半径必然依赖K，结构上无法实现K无关的分裂准则。

Method: 提出MIST（McDiarmid增量流式树），包含三个核心组件：1）适用于Gini分裂的紧致K无关McDiarmid置信半径，作为结构正则化器；2）贝叶斯继承协议，通过截断高斯矩将父节点统计量投影到子节点，在分裂最保守时方差减少保证最强；3）每叶节点KLL分位数草图，支持连续阈值评估和几何自适应叶预测，仅需单一数据结构。

Result: 在标准表格数据流和压力测试中，MIST在近高斯基准上与全局参数方法竞争力相当，在非高斯几何（SOTA基准崩溃的场景）下展现独特鲁棒性。这证明了K无关分裂准则与有效知识迁移机制的有效性。

Conclusion: MIST成功解决了流式决策树在在线类增量学习中的根本性局限，实现了真正的类别数无关分裂准则和有效的知识迁移，为开放世界持续学习提供了一个理论上严谨、实践中鲁棒的非参数解决方案，拓展了流式决策树的适用边界。

Abstract: Streaming decision trees are natural candidates for open-world continual learning, as they perform local updates, enjoy bounded memory, and static decision boundaries. Despite these, they still fail in online class-incremental learning due to two coupled miscalibrations: (i) their split criterion grows unreliable as the class count K expands, and (ii) the absence of knowledge transfer at split time. Both failures share a common root: the range of Information Gain intrinsically scales with log2 K. Consequently, any Hoeffding-style confidence radius derived from it must inevitably grow with the class count, making a K-independent split criterion structurally impossible, taking away the potential benefits of applying streaming decision trees to continual learning. To fix this issue, we present MIST (McDiarmid Incremental Streaming Tree), which resolves both failures through three integrated components: (i) a tight, K-independent McDiarmid confidence radius for Gini splitting that acts as a structural regulariser; (ii) a Bayesian inheritance protocol that projects parent statistics to child nodes via truncated-Gaussian moments, with variance reduction guarantees strongest precisely when splitting is most conservative; and (iii) per-leaf KLL quantile sketches that support both continuous threshold evaluation and geometry-adaptive leaf prediction from a single data structure. On standard and stress-test tabular streams, MIST is competitive with global parametric methods on near-Gaussian benchmarks and uniquely robust on non-Gaussian geometry where SOTA benchmarks collapse.

</details>


### [125] [Evolutionary Task Discovery: Advancing Reasoning Frontiers via Skill Composition and Complexity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11666)
*Liqin Ye,Yanbin Yin,Michael Galarnyk,Yuzhao Heng,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Evolutionary Task Discovery (EvoTD)框架，通过在算法技能和复杂度属性的双轴流形上进行定向搜索，结合结构化进化算子和动态最近发展区过滤器，系统性地合成高质量训练数据以扩展LLM的推理边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练范式（如RLVR）的推理能力提升受限于训练数据的多样性与复杂度。主流数据合成方法（非结构化变异或探索）面临同质性崩溃问题，无法系统性地扩展推理边界。

Method: 提出EvoTD框架，将数据合成视为双轴流形（算法技能轴与复杂度属性轴）上的定向搜索。设计结构化进化算子：(1) 交叉算子合成新型技能组合以增强多样性；(2) 参数化变异算子调节结构约束（如输入规模、树深度）以驱动泛化。关键性地集成动态最近发展区(ZPD)过滤器，确保任务位于模型可学习区域内。

Result: EvoTD在实验中显著提升了模型推理能力，且该增益在不同模型架构、预训练范式和模型规模上均表现出一致的泛化性，证明了结构化进化课程能有效支持推理能力提升。

Conclusion: 结构化进化课程可以有效支持大语言模型推理能力的提升，为数据合成提供了一种系统性的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The reasoning frontier of Large Language Models (LLMs) has advanced significantly through modern post-training paradigms (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR)). However, the efficacy of these methods remains fundamentally constrained by the diversity and complexity of the training data. One practical solution is data synthesis; yet, prevalent methods relying on unstructured mutation or exploration suffer from homogeneity collapse, failing to systematically expand the reasoning frontier. To overcome this, we propose Evoutionary Task Discovery (EvoTD), a framework that treats data synthesis as a directed search over a dual-axis manifold of Algorithmic Skills and Complexity Attributes. We introduce structured evolutionary operators to navigate this space: a Crossover operator that synthesizes novel skill compositions to enhance diversity, and a Parametric Mutation operator that scales structural constraints (e.g., input size, tree depth) to drive robust generalization. Crucially, we integrate a dynamic Zone of Proximal Development filter, ensuring tasks lie within the learnable region of the model. Empirically, EvoTD delivers substantial reasoning gains that generalize consistently across model architectures, pretraining regimes, and scales, demonstrating that structured evolutionary curricula can effectively support reasoning improvement. We release our code on https://github.com/liqinye/EvoTD.

</details>


### [126] [Partial Model Sharing Improves Byzantine Resilience in Federated Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11684)
*Ehsan Lari,Reza Arablouei,Stefan Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分模型共享的拜占庭容错联邦共形预测方法，通过在训练和校准两个阶段同时进行防护，实现了在抵抗恶意攻击的同时保持通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒联邦共形预测方法主要关注校准阶段的防护，而忽略了联邦训练阶段的安全性，且面临通信开销大的问题。针对这一局限性，本文旨在提出一种能够同时保护训练和校准阶段，并降低通信成本的方法。

Method: 该方法采用部分模型共享策略，每轮仅交换部分模型参数。在训练阶段，部分共享限制了攻击面并抑制了恶意更新；在校准阶段，客户端将非一致性分数压缩为直方图特征向量，服务器通过基于距离的恶意评分检测拜占庭客户端，并仅使用良性客户端的数据估计共形分位数。

Result: 在多种拜占庭攻击场景下的实验表明，与标准联邦共形预测相比，该方法实现了更接近名义覆盖率的性能，且预测区间显著更紧，验证了其鲁棒性和通信高效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架成功地为联邦不确定性量化提供了一个既鲁棒又通信高效的解决方案，证明了在训练和校准阶段同时进行防护的重要性。

Abstract: We propose a Byzantine-resilient federated conformal prediction (FCP) method that leverages partial model sharing, where only a subset of model parameters is exchanged each round. Unlike existing robust FCP approaches that primarily harden the calibration stage, our method protects both the federated training and conformal calibration phases. During training, partial sharing inherently restricts the attack surface and attenuates poisoned updates while reducing communication. During calibration, clients compress their non-conformity scores into histogram-based characterization vectors, enabling the server to detect Byzantine clients via distance-based maliciousness scores and to estimate the conformal quantile using only benign contributors. Experiments across diverse Byzantine attack scenarios show that the proposed method achieves closer-to-nominal coverage with substantially tighter prediction intervals than standard FCP, establishing a robust and communication-efficient approach to federated uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [127] [Slicing and Dicing: Configuring Optimal Mixtures of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11689)
*Margaret Li,Sneha Kudugunta,Danielle Rothermel,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对MoE架构的核心设计选择进行了系统性大规模研究（超过2000次预训练实验），发现专家数量和粒度是影响模型性能的关键因素，其他设计选择影响较小，建议简化MoE设计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合专家（MoE）架构研究中，核心设计选择（如专家数量、粒度、共享专家、负载均衡、token丢弃等）通常仅在狭窄的配置范围内被单独或成对研究，缺乏对这些选择之间相互作用及其独立优化可行性的系统性探索。

Method: 对超过2000次预训练实验进行系统性研究，模型规模高达66亿总参数。实验中全面变化总专家数、专家维度、层内异构专家大小、共享专家大小及负载均衡机制等多个维度进行消融分析。
