Result: 研究发现：（1）在所有活跃参数规模下，性能随总MoE参数增加持续提升，即使在极端活跃专家参数比（如128）下亦然；（2）最优专家大小几乎与总参数量无关，仅取决于活跃参数量；（3）共享专家、异构专家及负载均衡设置对性能影响较小，但无丢弃路由（dropless routing）带来稳定收益。

Conclusion: MoE架构设计应优先关注专家数量和粒度，其他设计选择对最终质量的影响微乎其微，从而提出了一个更简化的MoE设计配方。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have become standard in large language models, yet many of their core design choices - expert count, granularity, shared experts, load balancing, token dropping - have only been studied one or two at a time over narrow configuration ranges. It remains an open question whether these choices can be optimized independently, without considering interactions. We present the first systematic study of over 2,000 pretraining runs spanning models up to 6.6B total parameters, in which we exhaustively vary total experts, expert dimension, heterogeneous expert sizing within a single layer, shared expert size and load-balancing mechanisms. We find that at every active-parameter scale that we study, performance consistently improves with total MoE parameters even at extreme active expert parameter ratios like 128.Further, the optimal expert size is nearly invariant to total parameter count and depends only on active parameter count. Third, we see that other choices like shared experts, heterogeneous experts and load-balancing settings have small effects relative to expert count and granularity, although dropless routing yields a consistent gain. Overall, our results suggest a simpler recipe: focus on expert count and granularity, other choices have minimal effect on final quality.

</details>


### [128] [Compositional Neural Operators for Multi-Dimensional Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11691)
*Hamda Hmida,Hsiu-Wen Chang,Youssef Mesri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出组合神经算子(CompNO)框架，将复杂偏微分方程分解为预训练的基础模块库，通过聚合器组装来解决2D物理系统，提高了适应性、可解释性和模块复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高保真数值求解PDE计算成本高昂，现有机器学习方法泛化能力不足。典型科学基础模型的编码-解码方法存在预训练成本高和可解释性有限的问题。

Method: 提出组合神经算子(CompNO)框架：(1)将复杂PDE分解为预训练的基础模块库，包含对流、扩散、非线性对流块和泊松求解器；(2)通过带有聚合器的适应模块组装这些专家模块；(3)聚合器通过最小化数据损失和基于控制方程的物理残差来学习非线性相互作用。

Result: 在对流-扩散方程、Burgers方程和不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程上验证表明，从基础算子学习显著提高了适应性、增强模型可解释性，并促进了适应新物理系统时预训练模块的复用。

Conclusion: 模块化组合方法为科学计算提供了一种高效、可解释且可复用的PDE求解范式，有效平衡了预训练成本与模型性能。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) govern diverse physical phenomena, yet high-fidelity numerical solutions are computationally expensive and Machine Learning approaches lack generalization. While Scientific Foundation Models (SFMs) aim to provide universal surrogates, typical encoding-decoding approaches suffer from high pretraining costs and limited interpretability. In this paper, we propose Compositional Neural Operators (CompNO) for 2D systems, a framework that decomposes complex PDEs into a library of Foundation Blocks. Each block is a specialized Neural Operator pretrained on elementary physics. This modular library contains convection, diffusion, and nonlinear convection blocks as well as a Poisson Solver, enabling the framework to address the pressure-velocity coupling. These experts are assembled via an Adaptation Block featuring an Aggregator. This aggregator learns nonlinear interactions by minimizing data loss and physics-based residuals driven from governing equations. The proposed approach has been evaluated on the Convection-Diffusion equation, the Burgers' equation, and the Incompressible Navier-Stokes equation. Our results demonstrate that learning from elementary operators significantly improves adaptability, enhances model interpretability and facilitates the reuse of pretrained blocks when adapting to new physical systems.

</details>


### [129] [Augmented Lagrangian Method for Last-Iterate Convergence for Constrained MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11694)
*Michael Lu,Max Qiushi Lin,Mo Chen,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于非精确增广拉格朗日方法的通用框架，实现了约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的策略优化具有可证明的末次迭代收敛性，涵盖了表格设定、对数线性策略以及复杂的非线性策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束马尔可夫决策过程理论保证通常适用于混合策略，但部署混合策略计算和内存开销巨大，实际应用中往往只能部署单一（末次迭代）策略。近期关于末次迭代收敛的理论研究多局限于表格设定或实际中很少使用的算法变体，存在理论与实践的脱节。

Method: 利用经典的非精确增广拉格朗日（AL）方法构建了一个通用框架。首先在表格设定下，使用投影Q上升法（PQA）求解AL子问题；随后将结果推广至对数线性策略，采用高效的投影PQA变体；最后将框架扩展至复杂的非线性策略。

Result: 在表格设定下建立了全局末次迭代收敛性；在对数线性策略下实现了与先前工作具有可比性保证的末次迭代收敛；证明了该框架可以扩展至复杂的非线性策略，并在连续控制任务上进行了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于增广拉格朗日方法的框架成功地弥合了理论与实践的差距，为约束马尔可夫决策过程提供了一个具有可证明末次迭代收敛性且可扩展至复杂非线性策略的有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for infinite-horizon, discounted constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs). While existing theoretical guarantees typically hold for the mixture policy, deploying such a policy is computationally and memory intensive. This leads to a practical mismatch where a single (last-iterate) policy must be deployed. Recent theoretical works have thus focused on proving last-iterate convergence, but are largely limited to the tabular setting or to algorithmic variants that are rarely used in practice. To address this, we use the classic inexact augmented Lagrangian ($\texttt{AL}$) method from constrained optimization, and propose a general framework with provable last-iterate convergence for CMDPs. We first focus on the tabular setting and propose to solve the $\texttt{AL}$ sub-problem with projected Q-ascent ($\texttt{PQA}$). Combining the theoretical guarantees of $\texttt{PQA}$ and the standard $\texttt{AL}$ analysis enables us to establish global last-iterate convergence. We generalize these results to handle log-linear policies, and demonstrate that an efficient, projected variant of $\texttt{PQA}$ can achieve last-iterate convergence with comparable guarantees as prior work. Finally, we demonstrate that our framework scales to complex non-linear policies, and evaluate it on continuous control tasks.

</details>


### [130] [GRAFT: Graph-Tokenized LLMs for Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11706)
*Xinyi Gao,Xinyu Ren,Junliang Yu,Tong Chen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAFT框架，通过图词元化将工具依赖图内化到语言模型表示空间中，解决了现有方法中工具选择与子任务意图不对齐及错误累积问题，在复杂工具规划任务上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将工具依赖建模为外部图结构并通过检索或提示注入方式与LLM结合，但这种匹配范式常导致工具选择与子任务结构不对齐，产生违反图约束的规划；且存在错误累积问题，早期错误的工具选择会导致后续预测偏离有效执行路径。

Method: 提出GRAFT框架，将工具图内化到语言模型中：将每个工具节点映射为专用特殊词元，在表示空间中学习有向工具依赖关系；引入在策略工具上下文蒸馏，在模型自身采样的轨迹上训练，同时蒸馏逐步规划信号。

Result: 实验表明，GRAFT在精确序列匹配和依赖合法性方面达到最先进性能，支持更可靠的复杂工作流LLM工具规划。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过图词元化策略有效解决了工具依赖建模问题，为复杂多步骤任务中的LLM工具规划提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to complete complex tasks by selecting and coordinating external tools across multiple steps. This requires aligning tool choices with subtask intent while satisfying directional execution dependencies among tools. To do this, existing methods model these dependencies as tool graphs and incorporate the graphs with LLMs through retrieval, serialization, or prompt-level injection. However, these external graph-use strategies all follow a matching paradigm, which often fails to align tool choices with the underlying subtask structure, producing semantically plausible plans that violate graph constraints. This issue is further exacerbated by error accumulation, where an early incorrect tool selection shifts the plan into an invalid graph state and causes subsequent predictions to drift away from the valid execution path. To address these challenges, we propose GRAFT, a graph-tokenized language model framework for dependency-aware tool planning. GRAFT internalizes the tool graph by mapping each tool node to a dedicated special token and learning directed tool dependencies within the representation space. It further introduces on-policy tool context distillation, training the model on its own sampled trajectories while distilling stepwise planning signals. Experiments show that GRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance in exact sequence matching and dependency legality, supporting more reliable LLM tool planning in complex workflows.

</details>


### [131] [Unlocking Compositional Generalization in Continual Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11710)
*Phu-Quy Nguyen-Lam,Phu-Hoa Pham,Dao Sy Duy Minh,Chi-Nguyen Tran,Huynh Trung Kiet,Long Tran-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的持续学习范式，通过将表示学习与组合推理严格解耦，利用自监督ViT的patch级语义几何特性，在训练时优化整体类别身份，在推理时动态组合保留的槽位，实现了少样本学习中更好的新概念泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续学习方法存在结构性冲突：要么将场景折叠为全局嵌入，要么使用部件级匹配目标使表示过于依赖已见模式，导致无法泛化到真正的新概念。对象中心表示在少样本学习中的潜力（将场景分解为可匹配的对象级部分）在实际应用中难以实现。

Method: 提出了一种双阶段策略框架：1）训练阶段，利用自监督Vision Transformers的patch级语义几何特性，将槽位表示完全优化为整体类别身份，保持高度可泛化的对象级几何结构；2）推理阶段，动态组合保留的槽位以匹配新场景。该方法将表示学习与组合推理严格解耦，冻结的主干网络防止表示漂移，轻量级整体优化保持特征的新概念迁移能力。

Result: 在标准持续学习基准测试上进行了广泛实验，实现了最先进的新概念泛化能力和最小遗忘。该方法在未见概念泛化方面达到了最佳性能。

Conclusion: 通过严格解耦表示学习与组合推理，并利用自监督ViT的内在语义几何特性，成功解决了持续学习中的结构性冲突问题。冻结主干网络和整体优化策略的双重结构优势确保了表示的稳定性和泛化能力，为少样本学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Object-centric representations promise a key property for few-shot learning: Rather than treating a scene as a single unit, a model can decompose it into individual object-level parts that can be matched and compared across different concepts. In practice, this potential is rarely realized. Continual learners either collapse scenes into global embeddings, or train with part-level matching objectives that tie representations too closely to seen patterns, leaving them unable to generalize to truly novel concepts. In this paper, we identify this fundamental structural conflict and pioneer a new paradigm that strictly decouples representation learning from compositional inference. Leveraging the inherent patch-level semantic geometry of self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs), our framework employs a dual-phase strategy. During training, slot representations are optimized entirely toward holistic class identity, preserving highly generalizable, object-level geometries. At inference, preserved slots are dynamically composed to match novel scenes. We demonstrate that this paradigm offers dual structural benefits: The frozen backbone naturally prevents representation drift, while our lightweight, holistic optimization preserves the features' capacity for novel-concept transfer. Extensive experiments validate this approach, achieving state-of-the-art unseen-concept generalization and minimal forgetting across standard continual learning benchmarks.

</details>


### [132] [Debiased Model-based Representations for Sample-efficient Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11711)
*Jiafei Lyu,Zichuan Lin,Scott Fujimoto,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Zongqing Lu,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DR.Q算法，通过最大化互信息和使用衰退优先经验回放，解决了现有基于模型的表示方法中信息捕获不足和早期经验过拟合的问题，在连续控制基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型的表示方法在嵌入潜在动态信息时存在两个关键问题：无法捕获相关变量的足够信息，以及容易过拟合到经验回放缓冲区中的早期经验。这些问题会导致表示和actor-critic学习中的偏差，从而导致性能下降。

Method: 提出DR.Q（Debiased model-based Representations for Q-learning）算法，该方法包含两个核心组件：1) 显式最大化当前状态-动作对表示与下一状态之间的互信息，同时最小化它们之间的偏差；2) 采用衰退优先经验回放（faded prioritized experience replay）来采样转换。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准测试上使用单一超参数集进行评估，DR.Q能够匹配或超越最近的强基线方法，在某些情况下大幅度优于这些基线。

Conclusion: DR.Q算法成功解决了基于模型的表示方法中的偏差问题，通过显式最大化互信息和改进的经验回放策略，实现了更好的性能，证明了该方法在离线策略actor-critic学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Model-based representations recently stand out as a promising framework that embeds latent dynamics information into the representations for downstream off-policy actor-critic learning. It implicitly combines the advantages of both model-free and model-based approaches while avoiding the training costs associated with model-based methods. Nevertheless, existing model-based representation methods can fail to capture sufficient information about relevant variables and can overfit to early experiences in the replay buffer. These incur biases in representation and actor-critic learning, leading to inferior performance. To address this, we propose Debiased model-based Representations for Q-learning, tagged DR.Q algorithm. DR.Q explicitly maximizes the mutual information between the representations of the current state-action pair and the next state besides minimizing their deviations, and samples transitions with faded prioritized experience replay. We evaluate DR.Q on numerous continuous control benchmarks with a single set of hyperparameters, and the results demonstrate that DR.Q can match or surpass recent strong baselines, sometimes outperforming them by a large margin. Our code is available at https://github.com/dmksjfl/DR.Q.

</details>


### [133] [Block-R1: Rethinking the Role of Block Size in Multi-domain Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11726)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并解决了扩散大语言模型在多领域强化学习后训练中的块大小冲突问题，构建了数据集Block-R1-41K和基准Block-R1，并提出了一种简单有效的跨域后训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扩散大语言模型的强化学习后训练中，块大小是决定并行解码粒度和影响RL优化（如GRPO）中rollout轨迹的关键因素。现有研究多关注单一领域的推理效果，忽略了多领域场景下不同领域对最优块大小需求差异导致的冲突问题。该冲突会严重影响基于rollout的RL方法的训练效果，因此亟需从领域冲突视角研究块大小的选择。

Method: 1. 形式化定义了多领域dLLM强化学习中的领域块大小冲突问题；2. 构建了包含41K样本的数据集Block-R1-41K，为每个样本标注最佳训练块大小，并基于此提出块大小冲突分数以量化领域冲突；3. 建立了新的基准Block-R1，支持单领域和跨领域的灵活RL后训练；4. 提出一种简单有效的跨域后训练方法，采用样本级最佳训练块大小进行训练。

Result: 在Block-R1基准上进行了广泛实验，覆盖13个不同数据集、7种最新RL算法和多种dLLM骨干网络，验证了所提方法的有效性。实验表明考虑块大小冲突并采用样本级最佳块大小的训练方法能够有效提升跨域后训练性能。

Conclusion: 本文首次从领域冲突视角系统研究了dLLM强化学习后训练中的块大小问题，提出的形式化定义、量化指标、基准和方法为该方向的研究提供了重要基础。实验结果证明了样本级最佳块大小训练方法的有效性，为多领域场景下的dLLM后训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recently, reinforcement learning (RL) has been widely applied during post-training for diffusion large language models (dLLMs) to enhance reasoning with block-wise semi-autoregressive generation. Block size has therefore become a vital factor in dLLMs, since it determines the parallel decoding granularity and affects the rollout trajectories during RL optimisation, e.g., GRPO. Instead of investigating the effect of block size during inference on individual domains, this paper studies block size from a domain conflict perspective for dLLM RL post-training in multi-domain scenarios. The main contributions are: (1) a formulation of domain block size conflict in multi-domain RL for dLLMs, which will largely affect the post-training effectiveness for rollout-based RL methods; (2) a novel dataset, Block-R1-41K is constructed with a best-improved training block size for each sample, which also induces a Block Size Conflict Score to quantitatively measure the domain conflict; (3) a new benchmark, Block-R1, for flexible RL post-training for dLLMs in both single and cross domain; and (4) a simple yet powerful cross-domain post-training method with sample-level best-improved training block sizes. Extensive experiments on 13 distinct datasets, 7 latest RL algorithms, and various different dLLM backbones are covered in Block-R1. The benchmark is open-sourced at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/Block-R1, with the dataset released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/dLLM-R1/Block-R1-41K.

</details>


### [134] [Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting: Multi-Identity Red-Teaming for Adversarial Discovery and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11730)
*Cristian Morasso,Anisa Halimi,Muhammad Zaid Hameed,Douglas Leith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PCAP方法，通过基于多样化攻击者人设的条件对抗搜索，显著提升了LLM红队测试的攻击成功率和提示多样性，并实现了从漏洞发现到自动对齐的闭环流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM自动化红队测试方法往往只发现狭窄的攻击切片，缺乏对多样化真实世界威胁的覆盖，导致生成的安全微调数据不足。

Method: 提出Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP)方法，将对抗搜索条件化于多样化的攻击者人设（如医生、学生、恶意行为者）和策略集，通过并行运行人设条件化搜索来探索现实的攻击场景，发现可迁移的越狱攻击并生成带有自动元数据跟踪的丰富防御数据集。

Result: 在GPT-OSS 120B模型上，PCAP将攻击成功率从57%提升至97%，同时生成2-6倍更多样化的提示覆盖各种真实场景。在PCAP生成的数据上微调轻量级适配器后，模型鲁棒性显著提升（召回率从0.36提升至0.99，F1从0.53提升至0.96），且假阳性率极低。

Conclusion: PCAP提供了一种实用的闭环方法，从漏洞发现到自动对齐，能够有效发现多样化的真实威胁并生成高质量的安全微调数据，显著提升模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated red-teaming for LLMs often discovers narrow attack slices, missing diverse real-world threats, and yielding insufficient data for safety fine-tuning. We introduce Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP), which conditions adversarial search on diverse attacker personas (e.g., doctors, students, malicious actors) and strategy sets to explore realistic attack scenarios. By running parallel persona-conditioned searches, PCAP discovers transferable jailbreaks across different contexts and generates rich defense datasets with automatic metadata tracking. On GPT-OSS 120B, PCAP increases attack success from 57\% to 97\% while producing 2-6$\times$ more diverse prompts covering varied real-world scenarios. Critically, fine-tuning lightweight adapters on PCAP-generated data significantly improves model robustness (recall: 0.36 $\rightarrow$ 0.99, F1: 0.53 $\rightarrow$ 0.96) with minimal false positives, demonstrating a practical closed-loop approach from vulnerability discovery to automated alignment.

</details>


### [135] [U-STS-LLM A Unified Spatio-Temporal Steered Large Language Model for Traffic Prediction and Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11735)
*Yichen Zhang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出U-STS-LLM框架，通过动态时空注意力偏置生成器引导大语言模型，首次在蜂窝网络流量分析中统一实现长期预测与高缺失率插值，达到最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜂窝网络时空流量分析中，预测与插值任务割裂发展；主流时空图神经网络（STGNN）存在模型专用化、计算开销大、泛化能力弱的问题；而直接适配大语言模型（LLM）的方法缺乏结构性指导，导致收敛不稳定且仅聚焦预测任务。因此亟需一种统一框架，有效融合LLM的能力与时空结构先验。

Method: 提出U-STS-LLM框架，核心创新为动态时空注意力偏置生成器，合成持久功能图与瞬态节点状态以显式引导LLM注意力；采用部分冻结骨干网络结合LoRA进行参数高效微调；引入门控自适应融合机制；在统一多任务目标下联合训练预测与插值任务。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，U-STS-LLM在长期预测与高缺失率插值任务上均达到新的最优性能，同时具备优异的训练效率与稳定性。

Conclusion: U-STS-LLM成功将大语言模型适配于结构化的非语言领域（蜂窝网络时空数据），通过显式引入时空结构指导实现了预测与插值任务的统一建模，为在具有结构化特征的领域利用基础模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: The efficient operation of modern cellular networks hinges on the accurate analysis of spatio-temporal traffic data. Mastering these patterns is essential for core network functions, chiefly forecasting future load to pre-empt congestion and imputing missing values caused by sensor failures or transmission errors to ensure data continuity. While deeply connected, forecasting and imputation have historically evolved as separate sub-fields. The dominant paradigm, Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (STGNNs), while effective, are often specialized, computationally intensive, and exhibit limited generalization. Concurrently, adapting large pre-trained language models (LLMs) offers a powerful alternative for sequence modeling, yet existing approaches provide weak structural guidance, leading to unstable convergence and a narrow focus on forecasting. To bridge these gaps, we propose U-STS-LLM, a unified framework built on a spatio-temporally steered LLM. Our core innovation is a Dynamic Spatio-Temporal Attention Bias Generator that synthesizes a persistent functional graph with transient nodal states to explicitly steer the LLM's attention. Coupled with a partially frozen backbone tuned via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and a Gated Adaptive Fusion mechanism, the model achieves stable, parameter-efficient adaptation. Trained under a unified multi-task objective, U-STS-LLM learns a holistic data representation. Extensive experiments on real-world cellular datasets demonstrate that U-STS-LLM establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both long-horizon forecasting and high-missing-rate imputation, while maintaining remarkable training efficiency and stability, offering a novel blueprint for harnessing foundation models in structured, non-linguistic domains.

</details>


### [136] [Online Continual Learning with Dynamic Label Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11742)
*Xinrui Wang,Shao-Yuan Li,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Alexandra Gomez-Villa,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DHOCL（动态层次在线持续学习）这一新问题设定，针对动态演化的层次结构提出HALO方法，通过自适应组合分类头与可学习的层次化原型，有效解决了混合粒度下的部分监督问题和粒度相关的干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线持续学习（OCL）方法大多假设扁平的标签空间，忽视了现实世界概念中层次化组织的结构，该结构在水平方向（兄弟类别）和垂直方向（粗细粒度类别）上都会演化。为更好地反映这一现实情境，需要研究能够处理动态演化层次结构的在线持续学习问题。

Method: 提出HALO（基于组织化原型的层次自适应学习）方法：1）自适应地组合互补的分类头；2）通过有组织的可学习层次化原型进行正则化约束；3）在分类体系演化过程中实现快速适应、层次一致性保持和结构化知识巩固。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HALO在层次准确率、错误严重程度和持续学习性能等指标上均持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: HALO成功解决了DHOCL设定下的两个核心挑战：混合粒度下的部分监督约束了可塑性并破坏了跨层级语义一致性，以及动态演化层次结构导致的粒度相关干扰加剧了灾难性遗忘问题，为动态层次化的在线持续学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Online Continual Learning (OCL) aims to learn from endless non\text{-}stationary data streams, yet most existing methods assume a flat label space and overlook the hierarchical organization of real\text{-}world concepts that evolves both horizontally (sibling classes) and vertically (coarse or fine categories). To better reflect this context, we introduce a new problem setting, DHOCL (Online Continual Learning from Dynamic Hierarchies), where taxonomies evolve across granularities and each sample provides supervision at a single hierarchical level. In this setting, we find two fundamental issues: (i) partial supervision under mixed granularities provides only point-wise signals over an evolving path-wise hierarchy, which constrains plasticity and undermines cross-level semantic consistency, and (ii) the dynamically evolving hierarchies induce granularity-dependent interference, destabilizing popular replay and regularization mechanisms and thereby exacerbating catastrophic forgetting. To tackle these issues, we propose HALO (Hierarchical Adaptive Learning with Organized Prototypes), which adaptively combines complementary classification heads, regularized by organized learnable hierarchical prototypes, enabling rapid adaptation, hierarchical consistency, and structured knowledge consolidation as the taxonomy evolves. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that HALO consistently outperforms existing methods across hierarchical accuracy, mistake severity, and continual performance.

</details>


### [137] [Learning Feature Encoder with Synthetic Anomalies for Weakly Supervised Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11749)
*Yingjie Zhou,Yuqin Xie,Fanxing Liu,Dongjin Song,Ce Zhu,Lingqiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督图异常检测方法SAWGAD，通过合成异常数据和多头检测机制学习对异常敏感的特征表示，显著提升了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督图异常检测面临的核心挑战是学习既能降低正常数据类内方差、又能对异常高度敏感的特征表示。现有自监督特征学习方法未针对图异常检测的独特需求进行专门设计，缺乏领域特定性，这促使作者探索更适用于图异常检测的特征学习策略。

Method: 提出基于多任务学习的弱监督图异常检测框架：1）通过多种扰动方式生成合成异常数据；2）为每种异常类型分配专门的检测头，确保学习到的特征对偏离正常模式的异常敏感；3）采用两阶段学习策略：预热阶段仅使用合成样本训练，随后进行全面训练阶段整合合成与真实数据，平衡两者影响。合成异常虽不能完美复现真实模式，但提供了有效的辅助训练数据。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在弱监督图异常检测任务上取得了优于现有竞争方法的性能表现。合成异常数据作为辅助信息有效提升了特征学习的质量。

Conclusion: 领域特定的特征学习策略对图异常检测至关重要。通过合成异常引导的多任务学习框架能够有效学习对异常敏感的鲁棒特征表示，为弱监督图异常检测提供了新的解决方案思路。

Abstract: Weakly supervised graph anomaly detection aims to unveil unusual graph instances, e.g., nodes, whose behaviors significantly differ from normal ones, given only a limited number of annotated anomalies and abundant unlabeled samples. A major challenge is to learn a meaningful latent feature representation that reduces intra-class variance among normal data while remaining highly sensitive to anomalies. Although recent works have applied self-supervised feature learning for graph anomaly detection, their strategies are not specifically tailored to its unique requirements, motivating our exploration of a more domain-specific approach. In this paper, we introduce a weakly supervised graph anomaly detection method that leverages a feature learning strategy tailored for graph anomalies. Our approach is built upon a multi-task learning scheme that extracts robust feature representations through synthesized anomalies. We generate synthetic anomalies by perturbing the normal graph in various ways and assign a dedicated detection head to each anomaly type, ensuring that learned features are sensitive to potential deviations from normal patterns. Although synthetic anomalies may not perfectly replicate real-world patterns, they provide valuable auxiliary data for effective feature learnin, much like features learned from ImageNet classification transfer to downstream vision tasks. Additionally, we adopt a two-phase learning strategy: an initial warm-up phase using only synthetic samples, followed by a full-training phase integrating both tasks, to balance the influence of synthetic and real data. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our method over its competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/yj-zhou/SAWGAD.

</details>


### [138] [Federated Client Selection under Partial Visibility: A POMDP Approach with Spatio-Temporal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11752)
*Qijun Hou,Yuchen Shi,Pingyi Fan,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空注意力机制的强化学习框架，用于解决联邦学习中部分可见性条件下的客户端选择问题，在异构和部分可见场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习客户端选择方法大多假设每轮通信中所有客户端对服务器完全可见，但在大规模或边缘部署场景中，由于通信、移动性或可用性限制，服务器仅能访问部分客户端（部分可见性），导致传统方法性能下降。

Method: 将部分可见性下的联邦客户端选择问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)，提出基于时空注意力机制的强化学习框架。该方法通过整合历史全局模型和客户端身份嵌入，同时捕获训练的时序上下文信息和客户端的持久特征。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在异构和部分可见设置下相比现有基线方法实现了更优的性能，验证了其在处理实际联邦学习系统中不完整观测挑战方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功解决了实际联邦学习系统中的部分可见性问题，为大规模和边缘部署场景下的客户端选择提供了有效解决方案，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Federated learning relies on effective client selection to alleviate the performance degradation caused by data heterogeneity. Most existing methods assume full visibility of all clients at each communication round. However, in large-scale or edge-based deployments, the server can only access a subset of clients due to communication, mobility, or availability constraints, resulting in partial visibility where only a subset of clients is observable for aggregation in each communication round. In this paper, we formulate federated client selection under partial visibility as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) and propose a Spatial-Temporal attention-based reinforcement learning framework. By integrating historical global models and client identity embeddings, the proposed method captures both the temporal contexts of training and the persistent characteristics of clients. Experimental results across multiple datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance compared to existing baselines in heterogeneous and partially visible settings, validating its effectiveness in addressing the challenges of incomplete observations in practical federated learning systems.

</details>


### [139] [One-Step Generative Modeling via Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11755)
*Jiaqi Han,Puheng Li,Qiushan Guo,Renyuan Xu,Stefano Ermon,Emmanuel J. Candès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了W-Flow框架，利用Wasserstein梯度流将扩散模型的多步采样压缩为单步生成，在ImageNet 256×256上实现SOTA的1.29 FID，采样速度提升约100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流方法虽然生成能力强，但采样过程需要多次迭代更新，计算成本高昂。因此需要一种方法在保持高生成质量的同时实现快速单步生成。

Method: W-Flow框架包含两个步骤：首先通过Wasserstein梯度流定义从参考分布到目标分布的演化过程，该过程最小化能量泛函（使用Sinkhorn散度实现基于最优传输的更新规则）；其次训练静态神经生成器将整个演化过程压缩为单步生成。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256生成任务上实现1.29 FID的新SOTA性能，相比相似FID分数的多步扩散模型，采样速度提升约100倍，同时改善了模式覆盖和域迁移效果。

Conclusion: Wasserstein梯度流为快速高保真生成建模提供了原则性且有效的基础，证明了单步生成可以在保持高质量的同时大幅提升采样效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models and flow-based methods have shown impressive generative capability, especially for images, but their sampling is expensive because it requires many iterative updates. We introduce W-Flow, a framework for training a generator that transforms samples from a simple reference distribution into samples from a target data distribution in a single step. This is achieved in two steps: we first define an evolution from the reference distribution to the target distribution through a Wasserstein gradient flow that minimizes an energy functional; second, we train a static neural generator to compress this evolution into one-step generation. We instantiate the energy functional with the Sinkhorn divergence, which yields an efficient optimal-transport-based update rule that captures global distributional discrepancy and improves coverage of the target distribution. We further prove that the finite-sample training dynamics converge to the continuous-time distributional dynamics under suitable assumptions. Empirically, W-Flow sets a new state of the art for one-step ImageNet 256$\times$256 generation, achieving 1.29 FID, with improved mode coverage and domain transfer. Compared to multi-step diffusion models with similar FID scores, our method yields approximately 100$\times$ faster sampling. These results show that Wasserstein gradient flows provide a principled and effective foundation for fast and high-fidelity generative modeling.

</details>


### [140] [Decomposing the Generalization Gap in PROTAC Activity Prediction: Variance Attribution and the Inter-Laboratory Ceiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11764)
*Thor Klamt,Wolfgang Nejdl,Ming Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过分解随机划分与留一靶点（LOTO）验证间的性能差距，发现实验室间测量方差是主要来源。LOTO AUROC在多种模型架构下均停滞在约0.67，少样本重训练结合ADMET特征可提升至0.705，并发布了PROTAC-Bench基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PROTACs（蛋白降解靶向嵌合体）的机器学习预测器在随机划分交叉验证下表现良好（AUROC 0.85-0.91），但在留一靶点（LOTO）验证下性能显著下降（AUROC约0.67）。这种泛化差距反映了模型在新靶点预测能力上的局限，而这恰恰是新药设计所依赖的核心能力。作者旨在分解这一差距的来源，识别其主导因素，从而改进模型的真实预测能力。

Method: 以PROTACs为测试对象，作者首先使用方差分解框架分析随机划分与LOTO验证间的性能差距，量化实验室间测量方差、二值化阈值选择等因素的贡献。随后，作者在八种已发表架构及ESM-2蛋白质语言模型（最大3B参数）上进行实验，引入SMILES级别去重，并执行21维、2000次试验的超参数优化。最后，提出k=5分层少样本重训练结合ADMET特征的方法，并采用后验Platt缩放进行校准。

Result: 方差分解表明，实验室间测量方差是主导因素，贡献至少0.124 AUROC，远超二值化阈值选择的0.05贡献。在所有模型架构与规模下，LOTO AUROC均停滞在约0.67，SMILES去重下亦然；大规模超参数优化无法突破该上限，且单种子最优配置在多种子验证下性能退化0.161 AUROC，符合选择偏倚预测。少样本重训练结合ADMET特征将LOTO AUROC从0.668提升至0.705，后验校准可恢复输出至良好校准阈值内。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了PROTAC预测器泛化差距的主要来源并非模型架构或超参数，而是数据本身的实验室间测量方差。这为领域设定了AUROC约0.67的性能上限。通过少样本重训练与校准策略可实现有限但实质性的改进。作者发布了PROTAC-Bench基准数据集、方差分解框架、靶点校准协议及评估代码，推动PROTAC预测模型的稳健评估与开发。

Abstract: Machine-learning predictors of biochemical activity often exhibit large random-split-to-leave-one-target-out generalisation gaps that have been documented but not decomposed. We frame this as an evaluation-science question and use targeted protein degradation as the empirical test bed. PROTACs (proteolysis-targeting chimeras) are heterobifunctional small molecules that induce targeted protein degradation, with more than forty candidates currently in clinical trials; published predictors report AUROC of 0.85 to 0.91 under random-split cross-validation, while the leave-one-target-out (LOTO) protocol of Ribes et al. reduces performance to approximately 0.67. Random splits reward within-target interpolation, whereas LOTO measures the novel-target prediction that de-novo design depends on. We decompose this gap and identify inter-laboratory measurement variance as the dominant component, anchored by a within-target cross-laboratory cascade bounding the inter-laboratory contribution at 0.124 AUROC, well above the 0.05 contribution from binarisation-threshold choice. Across eight published architectures and ESM-2 protein language models up to 3B parameters, LOTO AUROC plateaus near 0.67, with a comparable plateau under SMILES-level deduplication; a 21-dimensional 2000-trial hyperparameter optimisation cannot break this ceiling, and the rank-1 single-seed configuration regresses by 0.161 AUROC under multi-seed validation, matching a closed-form selection-bias prediction (Bailey and Lopez de Prado, 2014). Few-shot k=5 stratified per-target retraining combined with ADMET features lifts 65-target LOTO AUROC from 0.668 to 0.7050, and post-hoc Platt scaling recovers raw output to within the 0.05 well-calibrated threshold. We release PROTAC-Bench (10,748 measurements, 173 targets, 65 LOTO folds), the variance-decomposition framework, the per-target calibration protocol, and the evaluation code.

</details>


### [141] [Is Monotonic Sampling Necessary in Diffusion Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11773)
*Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性测试了扩散模型中非单调采样调度，发现所有90种配置下均无法超越单调基线，性能惩罚因模型架构差异巨大。研究提出了“调度敏感系数”作为新的诊断工具，证实单调采样的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型采样过程中噪声水平单调递减已成为六年来的既定规范，但这一假设从未被系统性验证。研究者希望探究单调采样是否具有理论必要性，还是仅作为惯例存在。

Method: 设计了四类结构化非单调调度方案，应用于三种架构不同的生成模型（DDPM、EDM、Flow Matching），在CIFAR-10数据集上进行NFE预算从10到200的广泛测试，并进行42单元超参数消融实验，共测试90种配置。

Result: 所有非单调调度均未改善单调基线性能。性能惩罚幅度跨越三个数量级：DDPM中持续显著，Flow Matching中中等，EDM中接近零。该差异被证实为模型的结构性属性而非噪声，研究者将其形式化为“调度敏感系数”，可作为架构无关的廉价诊断指标。

Conclusion: 研究为领域内对单调调度的默认依赖提供了实证依据。提出的调度敏感系数为扩散模型质量评估提供了补充FID等样本质量指标的新诊断工具，能揭示模型在临界噪声水平下是否收敛至贝叶斯最优去噪器。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate samples by iteratively denoising a Gaussian prior, traversing a sequence of noise levels that, in every published sampler, decreases monotonically. Six years of intensive work has refined nearly every aspect of this recipe, including the corruption operator, the training objective, the schedule shape, the architecture, and the ODE solver. Yet the assumption of monotonicity itself has never been systematically tested. Here we ask whether monotonic sampling is load-bearing or merely conventional. We design four families of structured nonmonotonic schedules and apply them to three architecturally distinct generative models, DDPM, EDM, and Flow Matching, across NFE budgets ranging from 10 to 200 function evaluations, plus a 42-cell hyperparameter ablation, on CIFAR-10. Across all 90 tested configurations, no tested nonmonotonic schedule improves on the monotonic baseline. The magnitude of the penalty, however, spans nearly three orders of magnitude: persistent and substantial in DDPM, intermediate in Flow Matching, and indistinguishable from zero in EDM. We show that this variation is not noise but a structural property of each trained denoiser, and we formalize it as the Schedule Sensitivity Coefficient, a cheap, architecture-agnostic diagnostic that provides evidence of non-convergence to the Bayes-optimal denoiser at the critical noise level. Our findings justify the field's tacit reliance on monotonic schedules and supply a new probe of diffusion model quality complementary to sample-quality metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance.

</details>


### [142] [Entropy Polarity in Reinforcement Fine-Tuning: Direction, Asymmetry, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11775)
*Jiazheng Zhang,Ziche Fu,Junrui Shen,Yunbin Zhao,Yunke Zhang,Zhiheng Xi,Long Ma,Chenxin An,Zhihao Zhang,Shichun Liu,Dingwei Zhu,Shihan Dou,Shaofan Liu,Han Li,Wiggin Zhou,Aiden Adams,Tao Gui,Fei Huang,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵极性（entropy polarity）概念，揭示了强化学习中策略熵变化的token级机制，并基于此提出PAPO算法，通过优势重加权自适应平衡探索与利用，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有熵感知方法主要通过全局目标调节熵，缺乏对采样策略更新如何在token级别重塑策略熵的机制理解，亟需建立熵力学的理论框架。

Method: 开发RLVR中熵力学的理论框架，推导熵变化的一阶近似，定义熵极性（signed token-level quantity），揭示结构不对称性（高频高概率token引发收缩，低概率样本或更强分布校正引发扩张）；提出PAPO算法，保留双极性分支，通过优势重加权和在线熵轨迹信号自适应重新分配优化压力。

Result: 熵极性可靠预测熵变化，正负极性分支在保持探索与强化利用方面互补；PAPO在数学推理和智能体基准上持续优于竞争基线，训练效率更高，奖励改进显著。

Conclusion: 熵极性为理解与控制策略熵提供了细粒度的token级理论工具；PAPO验证了双极性分支保留与自适应熵控制的有效性，为RLVR中的探索-利用平衡提供了新范式。

Abstract: Policy entropy has emerged as a fundamental measure for understanding and controlling exploration in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for LLMs. However, existing entropy-aware methods mainly regulate entropy through global objectives, while the token-level mechanism by which sampled policy updates reshape policy entropy remains underexplored. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework of entropy mechanics in RLVR. Our analysis yields a first-order approximation of the entropy change, giving rise to entropy polarity, a signed token-level quantity that predicts how much a sampled update expands or contracts entropy. This analysis further reveals a structural asymmetry: reinforcing frequent high-probability tokens triggers contraction tendencies, whereas expansive tendencies typically require lower-probability samples or stronger distributional correction. Empirically, we show that entropy polarity reliably predicts entropy changes, and that positive and negative polarity branches play complementary roles in preserving exploration while strengthening exploitation. Building on these insights, we propose Polarity-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO), which preserves both polarity branches and implements entropy control through advantage reweighting. With the empirical entropy trajectory as an online phase signal, PAPO adaptively reallocates optimization pressure between entropy-expanding and entropy-contracting updates. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic benchmarks show that PAPO consistently outperforms competitive baselines, while delivering superior training efficiency and substantial reward improvements.

</details>


### [143] [ROMER: Expert Replacement and Router Calibration for Robust MoE LLMs on Analog Compute-in-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11800)
*Wenyong Zhou,Yuannuo Feng,Yizhe Chen,Taiqiang Wu,Wendong Xu,Wenbo Qi,Zhengwu Liu,Wang Kang,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROMER框架，通过专家替换和对数校准解决模拟存内计算噪声对MoE大模型的负面影响，在真实芯片噪声条件下显著降低困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家(MoE)大模型在模拟存内计算(CIM)系统中面临硬件噪声干扰权重的问题，噪声会破坏专家负载均衡，导致路由决策失效，但该问题此前未被探索。

Method: 提出ROMER后训练校准框架：(1)用高频专家替换低激活专家以恢复负载均衡；(2)通过基于百分位的归一化重新校准路由器对数，稳定噪声环境下的路由决策。

Result: 在真实芯片噪声条件下，ROMER在DeepSeek-MoE、Qwen-MoE和OLMoE模型上分别实现困惑度降低58.6%、58.8%和59.8%，验证了跨架构的有效性。

Conclusion: ROMER成功解决了模拟CIM硬件噪声对MoE模型的负面影响，通过后训练校准恢复负载均衡并稳定路由，为MoE模型在噪声环境中的部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures achieve remarkable scalability by sparsely activating a subset of experts per token, yet their frequent expert switching creates memory bandwidth bottlenecks that compute-in-memory (CIM) architectures are well-suited to mitigate. However, analog CIM systems suffer from inherent hardware imperfections that perturb stored weights, and its negative impact on MoE-based LLMs in noisy CIM environments remains unexplored. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of MoE-based LLMs under noise model calibrated with real chip measurements, revealing that hardware noise critically disrupts expert load balance and renders clean-trained routing decisions consistently suboptimal. Based on these findings, we propose ROMER, a post-training calibration framework that (1) replaces underactivated experts with high-frequency ones to restore load balance, and (2) recalibrates router logits via percentile-based normalization to stabilize routing under noise. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ROMER achieves up to 58.6\%, 58.8\%, and 59.8\% reduction in perplexity under real-chip noise conditions for DeepSeek-MoE, Qwen-MoE, and OLMoE, respectively, establishing its effectiveness and generalizability across diverse MoE architectures.

</details>


### [144] [Stop Marginalizing My Dreams: Model Inversion via Laplace Kernel for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11804)
*Patryk Krukowski,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek,Marek Śmieja,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REMIX框架，通过结构化协方差建模来捕捉特征依赖关系，显著提升了无数据持续学习中伪样本的生成质量与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无数据持续学习（DFCIL）方法在模型反演时普遍假设特征分布服从对角协方差，忽略了特征间的相关性，导致生成的伪样本保真度不足，进而限制了知识的保留效果。作者旨在解决这一根本性局限，通过建模特征依赖关系来提升合成样本的质量。

Method: 提出了一种名为REMIX的结构化协方差建模框架。该方法利用拉普拉斯核参数化（Laplace kernel parameterization）来捕捉结构化的特征依赖关系。这种设计使得内存消耗随特征维度线性增长，计算成本仅增加一个对数因子，从而避免了传统全协方差建模中密集矩阵求逆和对数行列式计算的高昂开销，实现了可扩展的全协方差建模。

Result: 通过建模特征相关性，REMIX能够生成更具连贯性和保真度的合成样本。在标准的DFCIL基准测试中，该方法表现出一致的优越性，有效提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: 突破对角协方差假设的限制对于实现有效且可扩展的无数据持续学习至关重要，REMIX证明了结构化特征依赖建模是实现这一目标的关键要素。

Abstract: Data-free continual learning (DFCIL) relies on model inversion to synthesize pseudo-samples and mitigate catastrophic forgetting. However, existing inversion methods are fundamentally limited by a simplifying assumption: they model feature distributions using diagonal covariance, effectively ignoring correlations that define the geometry of learned representations. As a result, synthesized samples often lack fidelity, limiting knowledge retention. In this work, we show that modeling feature dependencies is a key ingredient for effective DFCIL. We introduce REMIX, a structured covariance modeling framework that enables scalable full-covariance modeling without the prohibitive cost of dense matrix inversion and log-determinant computation. By leveraging a Laplace kernel parameterization, REMIX captures structured feature dependencies using memory that scales linearly with the feature dimensionality, while requiring only an additional logarithmic factor in computation. Modeling these correlations produces more coherent synthetic samples and consistently improves performance across standard DFCIL benchmarks. Our results demonstrate that moving beyond diagonal assumptions is essential for effective and scalable data-free continual learning. Our code is available at https://github. com/pkrukowski1/REMIX-Model-Inversion-via-Laplace-Kernel.

</details>


### [145] [Fed-BAC: Federated Bandit-Guided Additive Clustering in Hierarchical Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11815)
*Satwat Bashir,Tasos Dagiuklas,Muddesar Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fed-BAC算法，通过加性聚类个性化和双层Bandit框架联合优化分层联邦学习中的聚类分配与客户端选择，在数据异构场景下相比基线精度提升最高达+35.5pp，收敛速度提升1.5至4.8倍，并仅需80%的客户端参与率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分层联邦学习方法缺乏在数据异构条件下联合优化聚类分配与客户端选择的机制，无法高效处理跨客户端的非独立同分布数据问题。

Method: Fed-BAC集成加性聚类个性化与双层Bandit框架：云端的上下文Bandit负责学习服务器到聚类的分配，边缘服务器的Thompson Sampling识别高贡献客户端；加性分解通过全局聚合网络实现群体间知识共享，聚类特定网络捕获分布变化。

Result: 在三个分类基准（CIFAR-10、SVHN、Fashion-MNIST）的中等（α=0.5）和严重（α=0.1）Dirichlet非IID划分下，Fed-BAC相比HierFAVG精度提升最高+35.5pp，相比IFCA提升+8.4pp；仅需80%客户端参与率；收敛速度快1.5至4.8倍；改善了跨服务器公平性；在5倍部署规模下得到验证，且异构性越严重优势越明显。

Conclusion: Fed-BAC证明了在数据异构环境下，联合优化聚类分配与客户端选择并采用加性聚类个性化策略能显著提升分层联邦学习的性能与效率，随着异构程度加剧，该方法的优势愈发显著。

Abstract: Hierarchical federated learning (HFL) leverages edge servers for partial aggregation in edge computing. Yet existing FL methods lack mechanisms for jointly optimizing cluster assignment and client selection under data heterogeneity. This paper proposes Fed-BAC, which integrates additive cluster personalization with a two-level bandit framework: contextual bandits at the cloud learn server-to-cluster assignments, while Thompson Sampling at each edge server identifies high-contributing clients. The additive decomposition enables the sharing of knowledge between groups through a globally aggregated network, while cluster-specific networks capture distribution variations. Across three classification benchmarks (CIFAR-10, SVHN, Fashion-MNIST) under moderate ($α= 0.5$) and severe ($α= 0.1$) Dirichlet non-IID partitioning, Fed-BAC achieves distributed accuracy gains of up to +35.5pp over HierFAVG and +8.4pp over IFCA, while requiring only 80% client participation, converging 1.5 to 4.8$\times$ faster depending on dataset and accuracy target, and improving cross-server fairness. These gains are further validated at 5$\times$ deployment scale on CIFAR-10. The advantage of Fed-BAC increases with heterogeneity severity, confirming that additive cluster personalization becomes increasingly valuable as data distributions diverge.

</details>


### [146] [More Edits, More Stable: Understanding the Lifelong Normalization in Sequential Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11836)
*Xin Ma,Wei Chen,Qi Liu,Derong Xu,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上揭示了终身归一化（LN）在长期模型编辑中维持稳定性的机制，证明其能产生参数更新的渐近正交性和有界范数，并据此提出StableEdit方法以增强稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身模型编辑面临灾难性遗忘和模型崩溃的挑战，虽然经验上发现终身归一化（LN）对长期稳定性至关重要，但其背后的机制仍是“黑盒”，缺乏理论解释。

Method: 对LN在终身编辑场景下进行理论分析，揭示其自增强稳定性循环机制；结合岭回归正则化，证明LN能使参数更新具有渐近正交性和有界范数；基于理论洞见提出StableEdit方法，通过显式预热阶段和完全白化来增强稳定性循环。

Result: 理论分析证明LN结合岭回归正则化可产生具有渐近正交性和有界范数的参数更新，直接缓解遗忘和系统性崩溃；实验验证了StableEdit在长时域编辑中具有良好的稳定性表现，且开销极小。

Conclusion: LN通过建立自增强稳定性循环机制，使参数更新保持渐近正交性和有界范数，是终身模型编辑稳定性的关键理论基础；StableEdit通过显式预热和完全白化强化这一机制，实现了具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Lifelong Model Editing aims to continuously update evolving facts in Large Language Models while preserving unrelated knowledge and general capabilities, yet it remains plagued by catastrophic forgetting and model collapse. Empirically, we find that recent editors resilient over long horizons share the same core strategy: Lifelong Normalization (LN), which normalizes value gradients using running statistics. Removing LN causes immediate performance collapse, and we observe a counter-intuitive positive cumulative effect where early edits can promote the success of future edits. Yet the mechanism of LN remains a "black box", leaving its precise role in lifelong stability poorly understood. In this work, we provide the first theoretical account of LN in the lifelong regime. Our analysis reveals a self-reinforcing stability loop and proves that, when combined with ridge-regularized regression, LN yields parameter updates with asymptotic orthogonality and bounded norms, directly mitigating forgetting and systemic collapse. Based on these insights, we derive StableEdit, which strengthens this stability loop via an explicit warm-up stage and full whitening, improving long-horizon stability at minimal overhead. Extensive experiments validate our theory and demonstrate competitive performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/MINE-USTC/StableEdit.

</details>


### [147] [Gradient Clipping Beyond Vector Norms: A Spectral Approach for Matrix-Valued Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11838)
*Alexander Yukhimchuk,Mladen Kolar,Martin Takáč,Sayantan Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱裁剪方法，通过对梯度矩阵的奇异值进行裁剪来处理重尾噪声，而非传统的向量范数裁剪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度裁剪将所有参数视为向量而忽略了现代架构的矩阵结构。实证发现数据离群点通常只放大层级梯度矩阵中的少量主要奇异值，而频谱其余部分基本不变。

Method: 提出谱裁剪方法，通过钳制超过阈值的奇异值同时保留奇异方向来稳定训练；引入基于移动平均或滑动窗口分位数的层级自适应阈值；使用随机截断SVD仅裁剪顶部r个奇异值以提高效率。

Result: 提供了非凸优化下谱裁剪SGD的收敛分析，获得重尾噪声的最优O(K^(2-2α)/(3α-2))收敛速率；在合成重尾设置和神经网络训练任务中展现出竞争性能。

Conclusion: 谱裁剪方法成功推广了经典梯度范数裁剪，可轻松集成到现有优化器中，在处理重尾噪声时表现优异，同时避免了大规模层的完全分解计算开销。

Abstract: Gradient clipping is a standard safeguard for training neural networks under noisy, heavy-tailed stochastic gradients; yet, most clipping rules treat all parameters as vectors and ignore the matrix structure of modern architectures. We show empirically that data outliers often amplify only a small number of leading singular values in layer-wise gradient matrices, while the rest of the spectrum remains largely unchanged. Motivated by this phenomenon, we propose spectral clipping, which stabilizes training by clamping singular values that exceed a threshold while preserving the singular directions. This framework generalizes classical gradient norm clipping and can be easily integrated into existing optimizers. We provide a convergence analysis for non-convex optimization with spectrally clipped SGD, yielding the optimal $\mathcal{O}\left(K^{\frac{2 - 2α}{3α- 2}}\right)$ rate for heavy-tailed noise. To minimize hyperparameter tuning, we introduce layer-wise adaptive thresholds based on moving averages or sliding-window quantiles of the top singular values. Finally, we develop efficient implementations that clip only the top $r$ singular values via randomized truncated SVD, avoiding full decompositions for large layers. We demonstrate competitive performance across synthetic heavy-tailed settings and neural network training tasks.

</details>


### [148] [Martingale-Consistent Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11846)
*Moritz Gögl,Hanwen Xing,Christopher Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于鞅一致性（Martingale Consistency）的自监督学习框架，解决了在信息逐步揭示（如部分观测）场景下粗细视图预测的连贯性问题，显著提升了模型的鲁棒性和校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习方法通常依赖不变性目标（invariance objectives）来拉近不同视图的表示，但在信息逐步变化或揭示的场景（如较短历史、缺失特征或部分观测图像）中，这种方法无法保证预测的连贯性。理想情况下，粗视图的预测应与精化后预测的期望值相匹配，这一原则由鞅理论形式化，但现有方法未对此进行约束。

Method: 提出了一个满足鞅一致性的自监督学习框架。与强制不变性的方法不同，该方法仅约束精化后预测的期望值，允许预测随信息揭示而更新，同时防止系统性漂移。研究提供了预测空间和潜在空间两种变体，并基于随机精化过程提出了一个无偏的两样本蒙特卡洛估计器进行优化。

Result: 在合成数据、真实时间序列、表格数据以及图像基准测试上，针对部分观测场景（包括半自监督和完全无标签设置）进行了评估。实验结果表明，该框架在部分观测条件下提高了鲁棒性和校准性能，并且在信息揭示过程中能够产生更稳定的表示。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了自监督学习中缺乏鞅一致性约束的空白，证明了在动态信息环境下，引入鞅一致性原则能有效提升模型性能，为处理部分观测问题提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is often deployed under changing information, such as shorter histories, missing features, or partially observed images. In these settings, predictions from coarse and refined views should be coherent: before refinement, the coarse-view prediction should match the average prediction expected after refinement. Martingales formalize this coherence principle, but standard SSL objectives do not enforce it. Unlike invariance objectives that pull views together, martingale consistency constrains only the expected refined prediction, allowing predictions to update as information is revealed while preventing systematic drift. We introduce a martingale-consistent SSL framework that closes this gap, with practical prediction- and latent-space variants and an unbiased two-sample Monte Carlo estimator based on stochastic refinement. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real time-series, tabular, and image benchmarks under partial-observation regimes, in both semi-self-supervised and fully label-free settings. Across these experiments, our framework improves robustness and calibration under partial observation, yielding more stable representations as information is revealed.

</details>


### [149] [GEAR: Granularity-Adaptive Advantage Reweighting for LLM Agents via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11853)
*Sijia Li,Yuchen Huang,Zifan Liu,Yanping Li,Jingjing Fu,Li Zhao,Jiang Bian,Ling Zhang,Jun Zhang,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GEAR框架，通过自适应粒度的信用分配改进LLM智能体的强化学习训练，在数学推理和工具使用任务上相比GRPO提升高达20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体强化学习训练通常依赖结果级奖励，仅提供粗粒度监督。虽然更细粒度的信用分配有望实现有效的策略更新，但在长周期轨迹中获取可靠的局部信用并将其分配到正确部分仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出粒度自适应优势重加权（GEAR）框架，利用自蒸馏产生的token级和片段级信号重塑轨迹级GRPO优势。通过比较在线策略学生模型与真实条件教师模型，获得参考引导的散度信号，用于识别自适应片段边界并调节局部优势权重。当学生模型偏离教师模型时，散度出现峰值，GEAR将这些峰值作为锚点形成自适应信用区域，保留对齐部分的token级分辨率，对偏离部分进行片段级优势调制。

Result: 在8个数学推理和智能体工具使用基准测试上，使用Qwen3 4B和8B模型进行实验，GEAR始终优于标准GRPO、仅自蒸馏基线以及token级或turn级信用分配方法。在GRPO基线准确率较低的基准上提升尤为显著，最高可达约20%。

Conclusion: 自适应重加权方案在更具挑战性的长周期设置中特别有效，能够通过细粒度信用分配显著提升LLM智能体的强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a widely used post-training approach for LLM agents, where training commonly relies on outcome-level rewards that provide only coarse supervision. While finer-grained credit assignment is promising for effective policy updates, obtaining reliable local credit and assigning it to the right parts of the long-horizon trajectory remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose Granularity-adaptivE Advantage Reweighting (GEAR), an adaptive-granularity credit assignment framework that reshapes the trajectory-level GRPO advantage using token- and segment-level signals derived from self-distillation. GEAR compares an on-policy student with a ground-truth-conditioned teacher to obtain a reference-guided divergence signal for identifying adaptive segment boundaries and modulating local advantage weights. This divergence often spikes at the onset of a semantic deviation, while later tokens in the same autoregressive continuation may return to low divergence. GEAR therefore treats such spikes as anchors for adaptive credit regions: where the student remains aligned with the teacher, token-level resolution is preserved; where it departs, GEAR groups the corresponding continuation into an adaptive segment and uses the divergence at the departure point to modulate the segment' s advantage. Experiments across eight mathematical reasoning and agentic tool-use benchmarks with Qwen3 4B and 8B models show that GEAR consistently outperforms standard GRPO, self-distillation-only baselines, and token- or turn-level credit-assignment methods. The gains are especially strong on benchmarks with lower GRPO baseline accuracy, reaching up to around 20\% over GRPO, suggesting that the proposed adaptive reweighting scheme is especially useful in more challenging long-horizon settings.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Parameter Aggregation: Semantic Consensus for Federated Fine-Tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11857)
*Amr Abourayya,Jens Kleesiek,Michael Kamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型行为而非参数聚合的联邦微调新范式，通过交换公共提示集上的生成输出来实现协作，将通信成本与模型大小解耦，实现了数量级的通信开销降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦大语言模型微调方法通常被建模为参数聚合问题，但即使是参数高效的方法也需要传输大量可训练权重、假设架构对齐、并依赖对模型参数的白盒访问。随着模型规模持续增长和部署环境日益异构，这些假设与实际约束越来越不匹配。

Method: 提出了一种新的联邦微调框架：客户端在私有数据上微调本地模型，并在共享的公共提示集上交换生成的输出；服务器将这些输出映射到语义表示空间，形成每个提示的语义共识，并返回伪标签用于进一步的本地微调。这种方法的通信量仅取决于公共提示预算和通信行为的大小，与模型大小无关。

Result: 该方法能够匹配强联邦微调基线的性能，同时大幅降低通信开销（例如，对于Llama3.1-405B，分析上降低了1006倍），并减少了运行时间和能耗。该方法自然地适应异构架构，并可直接应用于开放式文本生成任务。

Conclusion: 对于生成式基础模型，行为级共识提供了比参数聚合更适合联邦适应的抽象层次，为大规模、异构环境下的联邦学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models is commonly formulated as a parameter aggregation problem. However, even parameter-efficient methods require transmitting large collections of trainable weights, assume aligned architectures, and rely on white-box access to model parameters. As model sizes continue to grow and deployments become increasingly heterogeneous, these assumptions become progressively misaligned with practical constraints. We consider an alternative formulation in which collaboration is mediated through model behavior rather than parameters. Clients fine-tune local models on private data and exchange generated outputs on a shared, public prompt set. The server maps these outputs into a semantic representation space, forms a per-prompt semantic consensus, and returns pseudo-labels for further local fine-tuning. This formulation fundamentally changes the communication scaling of federated LLM fine-tuning. The amount of information exchanged depends only on the public prompt budget and the size of the communicated behaviors, independent of model size. As a consequence, the protocol naturally accommodates heterogeneous architectures and applies directly to open-ended text generation. We present a theoretical analysis and empirical results demonstrating that this approach can match strong federated fine-tuning baselines while substantially reducing communication by orders of magnitude (e.g., analytically by a factor of $1006$ for Llama3.1-405B), as well as reductions in runtime and energy consumption. These results suggest that, for generative foundation models, behavior-level consensus provides a more appropriate abstraction for federated adaptation than parameter aggregation.

</details>


### [151] [Information theoretic underpinning of self-supervised learning by clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11870)
*Josef Kittler,Sara Atito,Muhammad Awais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将深度聚类方法下的自监督学习建模为KL散度优化问题，为常见的蒸馏和中心化操作提供了理论支撑，揭示了批中心化是防止模式坍塌的数学简化形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习（SSL）已成为构建AI基础模型的关键工具，其进展主要源于经验性探索与争论，缺乏坚实的理论基础。本文旨在发展SSL的底层理论，特别是针对深度聚类方法进行理论化建模。

Method: 通过类比监督学习，将SSL形式化为KL散度优化问题。为防止模式坍塌，对教师分布施加优化约束，导出使用逆聚类先验的归一化方法，并证明利用Jensen不等式可将其简化为流行的批中心化（batch centering）过程。

Result: 从理论上证明了蒸馏和中心化等基于启发式的常用操作是优化问题的必然结果；批中心化被揭示为防止模式坍塌约束的数学简化；该理论框架既支持了现有成功的SSL方法，也为未来研究指明了方向。

Conclusion: 本研究为SSL中的关键启发式方法提供了严谨的理论基础，弥合了实践与理论间的差距，为理解和发展新的自监督学习算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is recognized as an essential tool for building foundation models for Artificial Intelligence applications. The advances in SSL have been made thanks to vigorous arguments about the principles of SSL and through extensive empirical research. The aim of this paper is to contribute to the development of the underpinning theory of SSL, focusing on the deep clustering approach. By analogy to supervised learning, we formulate SSL as K-L divergence optimization.
  The mode collapse is prevented by imposing an optimisation constraint on the teacher distribution. This leads to normalization using inverse cluster priors. We show that using Jensen inequality this normalization simplifies to the popular batch centering procedure. Distillation and centering are common {heuristics-based} practices in SSL, {but our work underpins them theoretically.} The theoretical model developed not only supports specific existing successful SSL methods, but also suggests directions for future investigations.

</details>


### [152] [LOFT: Low-Rank Orthogonal Fine-Tuning via Task-Aware Support Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11872)
*Lanxin Zhao,Bamdev Mishra,Pratik Jawanpuria,Lequan Lin,Dai Shi,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LOFT框架，通过将正交适配解耦为子空间选择和子空间内变换两个独立组件，揭示了支撑集选择是关键设计维度，并通过梯度引导的支撑集选择策略在多个任务上提升了参数高效微调的性能与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正交参数高效微调方法常将适配发生的子空间与该子空间内施加的变换这两个截然不同的设计选择混为一谈，忽视了支撑集选择作为核心设计轴的重要性。

Method: 提出LOFT（低秩正交微调）框架，将正交适配视为乘法子空间旋转，显式分离支撑集选择与变换参数化；基于一阶分析提出任务感知的梯度引导支撑集选择策略。

Result: 在语言理解、视觉迁移、数学推理和多语言分布外适配等任务上，LOFT在匹配参数量、内存和计算预算的前提下，恢复了基于主子空间的正交适配方法，同时梯度引导支撑集选择改善了效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 研究表明，原则性的支撑集选择是改进正交参数高效微调的重要方向，应将其作为独立的设计维度加以重视。

Abstract: Orthogonal parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) adapts pretrained weights through structure-preserving multiplicative transformations, but existing methods often conflate two distinct design choices: the subspace in which adaptation occurs and the transformation applied within that subspace. This paper introduces LOFT, a low-rank orthogonal fine-tuning framework that explicitly separates these two components. By viewing orthogonal adaptation as a multiplicative subspace rotation, LOFT provides a unified formulation that recovers representative orthogonal PEFT methods, including coordinate-, butterfly-, Householder-, and principal-subspace-based variants. More importantly, this perspective exposes support selection as a central design axis rather than a byproduct of a particular parameterization. We develop a first-order analysis showing that useful adaptation supports should be informed by the downstream training signal, motivating practical task-aware support selection strategies. Across language understanding, visual transfer, mathematical reasoning, and multilingual out-of-distribution adaptation, LOFT recovers principal-subspace orthogonal adaptation while gradient-informed supports improve the efficiency-performance trade-off under matched parameter, memory, and compute budgets. These results suggest that principled support selection is an important direction for improving orthogonal PEFT.

</details>


### [153] [Sobolev Regularized MMD Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11884)
*Chenyang Tian,Bharath K. Sriperumbudur,Arthur Gretton,Zonghao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sobolev正则化最大均值差异梯度流，通过引入见证函数的梯度惩罚项，成功解决了传统MMD流的非凸性问题，并提供了无需等周假设的全局收敛保证，且首次实现了在采样和生成建模两大任务中的统一应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大均值差异（MMD）梯度流在优化过程中存在目标函数非凸的问题，导致收敛性难以保证；现有工作通常需要依赖目标分布的等周假设，且梯度流方法往往仅适用于采样或生成建模单一场景，缺乏统一的框架。

Method: 提出了Sobolev正则化MMD（SrMMD）梯度流，在见证函数上引入梯度惩罚项进行正则化；该正则化策略缓解了MMD目标的非凸性；收敛分析基于核均值嵌入差异的正则性条件，而非传统的等周假设；在采样场景中使用Stein核实现应用。

Result: 在连续时间和离散时间设定下，均证明了SrMMD流在MMD度量下的全局收敛性；该收敛保证不依赖于目标分布的等周假设；在生成建模和采样两类任务的广泛实验中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SrMMD梯度流为采样和生成建模提供了一个统一的理论框架，突破了传统方法在收敛性分析和应用场景上的限制，为基于核方法的概率分布学习提供了新的理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: We propose Sobolev-regularized Maximum Mean Discrepancy (SrMMD) gradient flow, a regularized variant of maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) gradient flow based on a gradient penalty on the witness function. The proposed regularization mitigates the non-convexity of the MMD objective and yields provable \emph{global} convergence guarantees in MMD in both continuous and discrete time. A more surprising appeal is that our convergence analysis does not rely on isoperimetric assumptions on the target distribution. Instead, it is based on a regularity condition on the difference between kernel mean embeddings. A key highlight of the proposed flow is that it is applicable in both sampling (from an unnormalized target distribution) -- using Stein kernels -- and generative modeling settings, unlike previous works, where a gradient flow is suitable for only generative modeling or sampling but not both. The effectiveness of the proposed flow is empirically verified on a broad range of tasks in both generative modelling and sampling.

</details>


### [154] [Incentivizing Truthfulness and Collaborative Fairness in Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11889)
*Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,Jue Fan,Xiao Tian,Xinyi Xu,Patrick Jaillet,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能在贝叶斯模型中同时确保协作公平性和均衡时激励真实性的机制，通过结合半值（如Shapley值）与基于未知验证集的真实数据估值函数，解决了数据源操纵数据以获取不当收益的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作机器学习中的数据估值方法仅基于提交的数据进行公平奖励，但未验证或激励数据的真实性。数据源可通过提交重复或噪声数据来操纵估值以增加自身收益或阻碍他人，从而破坏协作学习的有效性和公平性。

Method: 提出一种结合半值（如Shapley值）和真实数据估值函数（DVF）的机制。DVF基于对数据源未知的验证集来评估数据质量。由于半值受他人数据影响，引入额外条件证明数据源通过提交反映其真实知识的数据集，可在联盟和半值中最大化期望数据价值。同时探讨了奖励预算有限或缺乏验证集时（F）和（T）条件的含义与适当松弛。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上验证了理论发现。机制能证明地确保（F）协作公平性，并在均衡状态下激励（T）真实性。理论分析表明数据源通过提交真实数据可最大化其期望数据价值。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现了在贝叶斯模型中同时保证协作公平性和真实性的机制，为协作机器学习中的数据估值提供了理论保障。研究还讨论了现实约束下的机制松弛，拓展了方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Collaborative machine learning involves training high-quality models using datasets from a number of sources. To incentivize sources to share data, existing data valuation methods fairly reward each source based on its data submitted as is. However, as these methods do not verify nor incentivize data truthfulness, the sources can manipulate their data (e.g., by submitting duplicated or noisy data) to artificially increase their valuations and rewards or prevent others from benefiting. This paper presents the first mechanism that provably ensures (F) collaborative fairness and incentivizes (T) truthfulness at equilibrium for Bayesian models. Our mechanism combines semivalues (e.g., Shapley value), which ensure fairness, and a truthful data valuation function (DVF) based on a validation set that is unknown to the sources. As semivalues are influenced by others' data, we introduce an additional condition to prove that a source can maximize its expected data values in coalitions and semivalues by submitting a dataset that captures its true knowledge. Additionally, we discuss the implications and suitable relaxations of (F) and (T) when the mediator has a limited budget for rewards or lacks a validation set. Our theoretical findings are validated on synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [155] [Procedural-skill SFT across capacity tiers: A W-Shaped pre-SFT Trajectory and Regime-Asymmetric Mechanism on 0.8B-4B Qwen3.5 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11907)
*Igor Strozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过跨三个Qwen3.5规模（0.8B、2B、4B）的实验，发现程序性技能SFT的提升效应在各规模间大致均匀（约+0.04至+0.075），但受基线能力W形轨迹影响，呈现"基线越弱、SFT增益越显著"的非对称模式，并纠正了此前因路径不匹配导致的错误结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确量化程序性技能监督微调（SFT）对不同规模大语言模型的贡献，澄清早期实验中因评估方法缺陷（如格式合规性偏差、路径不匹配）导致的误判（如"0.8B仅改善格式"、"4B收益递减"）。

Method: 构建353条包含任务+程序性技能块、Opus思维链和判定通过记录的演示语料；在200任务/40技能留出集上，对Qwen3.5三种规模（0.8B/2B/4B）进行SFT；采用匹配路径的纯LLM评分机制，并以Claude Haiku 4.5为前沿参照；通过负迭代序列（0.8B上五种配方变体）和跨家族验证（GPT-5.4）确保结论稳健。

Result: SFT可归因的程序性增量提升在各规模间大致均匀：0.8B/2B/4B分别为+0.070/+0.040/+0.075；后SFT增量的变化主要由W形预SFT基线轨迹主导；SFT在基线对程序处理困难的规模上绝对增益最大，呈现非对称模式；83.5%留出集使用的确定性ANSWER行提取器系统性地低估了自由形式结论；GPT-5.4跨家族验证Cohen's κ≥0.754，一致性≥93.25%。

Conclusion: 程序性技能SFT提升效应具有规模均匀性，其实际表现受基线能力轨迹调节；早期"格式优化仅限0.8B"、"4B收益收缩"等论断为路径不匹配伪影所致；本研究建立了可证伪预测（在8B/14B上验证），为理解SFT与模型规模的交互关系提供了修正框架。

Abstract: We measure procedural-skill SFT contribution across three Qwen3.5 dense scales (0.8B, 2B, 4B) on a 200-task / 40-skill holdout, with Claude Haiku 4.5 as a frontier reference. The corpus is 353 rows of (task + procedural-skill block, Opus chain-of-thought, judge-pass) demonstrations.
  \textbf{Main finding.} Under matched-path LLM-only scoring, the SFT-attributable procedural-$Δ$ lift is roughly uniform across sizes: $+0.070$ / $+0.040$ / $+0.075$ at 0.8B / 2B / 4B. Variation in post-SFT $Δ$ ($-0.005$, $+0.100$, $+0.065$) is dominated by a W-shaped pre-SFT base trajectory ($-0.075$, $+0.060$, $-0.010$, Haiku-4-5 at $+0.030$): the 5-step procedure hurts 0.8B and 4B, helps 2B, and helps frontier Haiku modestly. SFT works hardest in absolute terms where the base struggles with the procedure -- a regime-asymmetric pattern with a falsifiable prediction at 8B/14B.
  \textbf{Methodology.} (i) A bench format-compliance artifact: 83.5\% of the holdout uses a deterministic \texttt{ANSWER}-line extractor that under-counts free-form conclusions; an LLM-only re-judge reveals it was systematically biased against \CU. (ii) A negative-iteration sequence at 0.8B: five recipe variants cluster post-SFT \CU{} pass-rate within a 2\,pp band, constraining the absolute-pass-rate ceiling to base capacity rather than recipe.
  \textbf{Cross-family validation.} GPT-5.4 via OpenRouter on all 7 configurations (2800 paired episodes) agrees on the direction of every per-student finding: Cohen's $κ\geq 0.754$, agreement $\geq 93.25\%$.
  Earlier ``format-only at 0.8B'' and ``shrinking SFT at 4B'' framings were path-mismatch artifacts; this paper supersedes both (Appendix~\ref{sec:appendix-path}). Single-seed; threats in §\ref{sec:threats}.

</details>


### [156] [Delightful Gradients Accelerate Corner Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11908)
*Jincheng Mei,Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Delightful Policy Gradient (DG)算法，通过引入优势函数和动作惊奇度的乘积作为门控机制，解决了标准Softmax策略梯度在次优角落附近的“自陷”问题，实现了全局收敛和更快的逃离速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Softmax策略梯度方法虽然具有O(1/t)的渐近收敛率，但在策略空间的次优角落附近存在严重的“自陷”现象：具有负优势的动作会强化角落策略，最初甚至会将最优动作推向后方，导致逃离过程在瞬态行为上呈现指数级缓慢，这成为性能提升的瓶颈。

Method: 提出Delightful Policy Gradient (DG)算法，核心创新是对每个策略梯度项引入门控机制：将优势函数与动作惊奇度（surprisal，即负对数概率）相乘。这种设计使得：1) 在零温度极限下，定量上消除次优角落附近的陷阱机制；2) 在任意固定温度下，有害动作随其稀缺性被多项式抑制；3) 关键结构洞察是每个优于角落动作的“盟友”动作对逃离的贡献非负。结合角落不稳定性和单调值改进恒等式进行理论分析。

Result: 理论证明：1) 对于K臂老虎机，DG在零温度极限下逃离次优角落的时间界为初始概率比的对数级别；2) DG在老虎机和表格MDP中均能全局收敛到最优策略，保持O(1/t)渐近收敛率；3) 构造精确反例证明该表格机制在共享函数近似下可能失效；4) 在MNIST上下文老虎机实验中，使用共享参数神经网络的DG比标准策略梯度更快地从不良初始化中恢复，表明理论反例标记的是理论边界而非实践禁忌。

Conclusion: DG算法成功克服了标准策略梯度的自陷问题，在保持最优渐近收敛率的同时显著改善了瞬态性能。虽然在共享函数近似情形下存在理论反例，但实验结果表明该方法在实际深度强化学习场景中仍具有实用价值，为策略梯度方法的理论与实践提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Softmax policy gradient converges at $O(1/t)$, but its transient behavior near sub-optimal corners of the simplex can be exponentially slow. The bottleneck is self-trapping: negative-advantage actions reinforce the corner policy and can initially push the optimal action backward. We study \emph{Delightful Policy Gradient} (DG), which gates each policy-gradient term by the product of advantage and action surprisal. For $K$-armed bandits, we prove that the zero-temperature limit of DG removes this corner-trapping mechanism on a quantitative sector near any sub-optimal corner, yielding a first-exit escape bound logarithmic in the initial probability ratio. At every fixed temperature, the same local mechanism persists because harmful actions are polynomially suppressed as they become rare. A key structural insight is that every action better than the corner action is an \emph{ally}: its contribution to escape is non-negative. Combining corner instability with a monotonic value improvement identity, we prove that DG converges globally to the optimal policy in both bandits and tabular MDPs at an asymptotic $O(1/t)$ rate. We also show, via an exact counterexample, that this tabular mechanism can fail under shared function approximation. In MNIST contextual bandits with a shared-parameter neural network, DG nevertheless recovers from bad initializations faster than standard policy gradient, suggesting that the counterexample marks a boundary of the theory rather than a practical prohibition.

</details>


### [157] [Understanding Sample Efficiency in Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11911)
*Gaspard Oliviers,Elene Lominadze,Rafal Bogacz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“目标对齐”指标量化学习效率，理论证明预测编码（PC）比反向传播（BP）更高效，尤其在深层、窄网络和预训练网络中优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有小规模实验表明预测编码（PC）在样本效率和多种情境下优于反向传播（BP），但缺乏系统的理论解释。为填补该空白，需要量化并比较两者在深度网络中的学习效率。

Method: 提出“目标对齐”度量网络输出变化与输出预测误差的对齐程度，推导并在深度线性网络中实证验证其解析表达式；进而推导PC达到最优目标对齐的精确条件。

Result: 在深度线性网络中，PC的目标对齐持续高于BP，尤其在网络更深、更窄或预训练时优势更显著；推导出PC达到最优目标对齐的精确条件，并在非线性模型训练轨迹中验证其预测优势在部分假设被违背时仍成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为目标对齐提供了可解析的形式，从机理上解释了PC较BP更高的学习效率，并为PC的超参数设置提供了理论指导，以实现更有效的学习。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is an influential account of cortical learning. Much of recent work has focused on comparing PC to Backpropagation (BP) to find whether PC offers any advantages. Small scale experiments show that PC enables learning that is more sample efficient and effective in many contexts, though a thorough theoretical understanding of the phenomena remains elusive. To address this, we quantify the efficiency of learning in BP and PC through a metric called ``target alignment'', which measures how closely the change in the output of the network is aligned to the output prediction error. We then derive and empirically validate analytical expressions for target alignment in Deep Linear Networks. We show that learning in PC is more efficient than BP, which is especially pronounced in deep, narrow and pre-trained networks. We also derive exact conditions for guaranteed optimal target alignment in PC and validate our findings through experiments. We study full training trajectories of linear and non-linear models, and find the predicted benefits of PC persist in practice even when some assumptions are violated. Overall, this work provides a mechanistic understanding of the higher learning efficiency observed for PC over BP in previous works, and can guide how PC should be parametrised to learn most effectively.

</details>


### [158] [STAGE: Tackling Semantic Drift in Multimodal Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11919)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Xunkai Li,Yihan Sun,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAGE框架，通过协议优先策略解决多模态联邦图学习中跨客户端语义空间不一致问题，在8个数据集上实现SOTA性能并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习(MM-FGL)面临关键挑战：不同模态域的客户端在协作前就存在语义空间不一致问题，即使对同一概念，本地编码器也会产生不兼容的表示。这导致直接参数协调不可靠，引发两个下游问题：强制异构客户端表示进入朴素共享语义空间会产生虚假语义一致性，图消息传递可能放大邻域间的残留不一致性。

Method: 提出STAGE（协议优先框架），不依赖直接参数平均，而是构建共享语义空间。该方法首先将异构多模态特征翻译为可比较的表示，然后规范这些表示在本地图结构上的传播方式，从而改善跨客户端语义校准并降低图学习过程中不一致性放大的风险。

Result: 在8个多模态属性图数据集上，涵盖5个图中心和模态中心任务进行广泛实验。STAGE持续达到最先进性能，同时降低了每轮通信负载。

Conclusion: STAGE通过协议优先策略有效解决了多模态联邦图学习中的语义空间不一致问题，证明了翻译和规范机制在跨模态协作学习中的重要性，为MM-FGL提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Federated graph learning (FGL) enables collaborative training on graph data across multiple clients. As graph data increasingly contain multimodal node attributes such as text and images, multimodal federated graph learning (MM-FGL) has become an important yet substantially harder setting. The key challenge is that clients from different modality domains may not share a common semantic space: even for the same concept, their local encoders can produce inconsistent representations before collaboration begins. This makes direct parameter coordination unreliable and further causes two downstream problems: forcing heterogeneous client representations into a naively shared semantic space may create false semantic agreement, and graph message passing may amplify residual inconsistency across neighborhoods. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{STAGE}, a protocol-first framework for MM-FGL. Instead of relying on direct parameter averaging, STAGE builds a shared semantic space that first translates heterogeneous multimodal features into comparable representations and then regulates how these representations propagate over local graph structures. In this way, STAGE not only improves cross-client semantic calibration, but also reduces the risk of inconsistency amplification during graph learning. Extensive experiments on 8 multimodal-attributed graphs across 5 graph-centric and modality-centric tasks show that STAGE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing per-round communication payload.

</details>


### [159] [NOFE -- Neural Operator Function Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11970)
*Lars Uebbing,Harald L. Joakimsen,Siyan Chen,Georgios Leontidis,Kristoffer K. Wickstrøm,Michael C. Kampffmeyer,Sébastien Lefèvre,Arnt-Børre Salberg,Robert Jenssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NOFE框架，通过神经算子实现连续域的降维，在保持局部结构和采样独立性方面显著优于传统离散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降维方法将数据视为离散点云，忽略了现实世界过程中固有的连续域结构，导致无法在任意位置进行评估且对采样密度敏感。

Method: 提出Neural Operator Function Embedding (NOFE)框架，使用Graph Kernel Operator学习函数到函数的映射，实现与输入离散化无关的无网格评估，并将该方法理论建成为sheaf-to-sheaf映射的近似，推广了Sheaf Neural Networks到连续域。

Result: 在ERA5气候再分析数据集上，NOFE在局部结构保持方面显著优于基线方法（局部Stress: 0.111 vs PCA 0.398, t-SNE 0.773, UMAP 0.791）；在采样独立性方面，Patch Stitching Error相比UMAP降低最多20.0倍（59.0 vs 267.6）；同时保持有竞争力的全局结构保持能力（Stress-1: 0.379 vs PCA 0.268）。

Conclusion: NOFE成功解决了离散降维方法的关键限制，能够解析精细结构并生成平滑一致的嵌入，在不同采样密度下具有良好的泛化能力，为连续域数据降维提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Most dimensionality reduction methods treat data as discrete point clouds, ignoring the continuous domain structure inherent to many real-world processes. To bridge this gap, we introduce Neural Operator Function Embedding (NOFE), a domain-aware framework for continuous dimensionality reduction. NOFE learns function-to-function mappings via a Graph Kernel Operator, enabling mesh-free evaluation at arbitrary query locations independent of input discretization. We establish NOFE as approximation of sheaf-to-sheaf mappings, generalizing Sheaf Neural Networks to continuous domains. We evaluate NOFE across different datasets, comparing it against PCA, t-SNE, and UMAP. Our results demonstrate that NOFE significantly outperforms baselines in local structure preservation, achieving a local Stress of 0.111 compared to 0.398 for PCA, 0.773 for t-SNE, and 0.791 for UMAP for the ERA5 climate reanalysis dataset. NOFE also exhibits robust sampling independence, reducing the Patch Stitching Error by up to $20.0\times$ relative to UMAP (59.0 vs. 267.6 under regional normalization) and ensuring consistency across disjoint domain patches. While maintaining competitive global structure preservation (Stress-1: 0.379 vs. PCA's 0.268), NOFE resolves fine-grained structures and produces smooth, consistent embeddings that generalize across varying sample densities, addressing key limitations of discrete reduction methods.

</details>


### [160] [Towards Order Fairness: Mitigating LLMs Order Sensitivity through Dual Group Advantage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11974)
*Xu Chu,Guanyu Wang,Zhijie Tan,Xinrong Chen,Ziyu Li,Tong Mo,Weiping Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次利用强化学习缓解大语言模型的顺序偏差问题，提出DGAO方法同时提升模型准确性与顺序稳定性，并引入新评估指标揭示以往方法的伪稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在顺序偏差（order bias），即输入元素的排列顺序会影响模型性能，限制了其在上下文学习和检索增强生成（RAG）等场景的应用。现有方法要么通过统计或搜索寻找最优排列顺序增加推理开销，要么通过监督微调牺牲准确性导致模型陷入一致的幻觉错误，无法从根本上解决问题。

Method: 提出双组优势优化（DGAO）方法，利用强化学习计算并平衡组内相对准确度优势与组间相对稳定性优势。该方法奖励策略模型生成顺序稳定且正确的输出，惩罚顺序敏感或错误的响应。同时提出一致性率（Consistency Rate）和过度自信率（Overconfidence Rate）两个新指标，以揭示以往方法的伪稳定性并指导更全面的评估。

Result: 广泛实验表明，DGAO在实现优越顺序公平性的同时，在RAG、数学推理和分类任务上均提升了模型性能。新指标有效揭示了先前方法的伪稳定性问题。

Conclusion: DGAO首次成功将强化学习应用于缓解LLM顺序敏感性问题，实现了准确性与顺序稳定性的同步提升，为LLM的公平性研究提供了新范式和新评估标准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) suffer from order bias, where their performance is affected by the arrangement order of input elements. This unfairness limits the model's applications in scenarios such as in-context learning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Recent studies attempt to obtain optimal or suboptimal arrangements based on statistical results or using dataset-based search, but these methods increase inference overhead while leaving the model's inherent order bias unresolved. Other studies mitigate order sensitivity through supervised fine-tuning using augmented training sets with multiple order variants, but often at the cost of accuracy, trapping the model in consistent yet incorrect hallucinations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{D}ual \textbf{G}roup \textbf{A}dvantage \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{DGAO}), which aims to improve model accuracy and order stability simultaneously. DGAO calculates and balances intra-group relative accuracy advantage and inter-group relative stability advantage, rewarding the policy model for generating order-stable and correct outputs while penalizing order-sensitive or incorrect responses. This marks the first time reinforcement learning has been used to mitigate LLMs' order sensitivity. We also propose two new metrics, Consistency Rate and Overconfidence Rate, to reveal the pseudo-stability of previous methods and guide more comprehensive evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DGAO achieves superior order fairness while improving performance on RAG, mathematical reasoning, and classification tasks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hyalinesky/DGAO.

</details>


### [161] [Stochastic Minimum-Cost Reach-Avoid Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11975)
*Jingduo Pan,Taoran Wu,Yiling Xue,Bai Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了可达避让概率证书（RAPCs）和基于收缩的Bellman公式，解决了在随机环境中联合执行概率可达避让约束和成本优化的问题，实验证明了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全和约束强化学习方法通常无法在随机环境的学习设置中联合执行概率可达避让约束并优化成本，存在方法上的空白。

Method: 引入了可达避让概率证书（RAPCs）来识别满足随机可达避让约束的状态；基于RAPCs，开发了一种基于收缩的Bellman公式，作为将可达避让考虑因素整合到强化学习中的原则性替代方案，从而实现概率约束下的成本优化。

Result: 建立的算法能够几乎必然收敛到相对于所得目标的局部最优策略；在MuJoCo仿真器中的实验表明，所提方法在成本性能上有所改善，并且可达避让满足率持续更高。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了在随机环境中联合执行概率可达避让约束与成本优化的空白，所提方法在理论和实验上均证明了其有效性和优越性。

Abstract: We study stochastic minimum-cost reach-avoid reinforcement learning, where an agent must satisfy a reach-avoid specification with probability at least $p$ while minimizing expected cumulative costs in stochastic environments. Existing safe and constrained reinforcement learning methods typically fail to jointly enforce probabilistic reach-avoid constraints and optimize cost in the learning setting in stochastic environments. To address this challenge, we introduce reach-avoid probability certificates (RAPCs), which identify states from which stochastic reach-avoid constraints are satisfiable. Building on RAPCs, we develop a contraction-based Bellman formulation that serves as a principled surrogate for integrating reach-avoid considerations into reinforcement learning, enabling cost optimization under probabilistic constraints. We establish almost sure convergence of the proposed algorithms to locally optimal policies with respect to the resulting objective. Experiments in the MuJoCo simulator demonstrate improved cost performance and consistently higher reach-avoid satisfaction rates.

</details>


### [162] [QDSB: Quantized Diffusion Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11983)
*Tobias Fuchs,Florian Kalinke,Nadja Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量化扩散薛定谔桥（QDSB），通过锚点量化端点分布计算耦合，显著加速无仿真薛定谔桥的训练，同时保持样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无仿真薛定谔桥需要通过求解熵最优传输问题获得源域与目标域样本的耦合配对。在实际应用中，获取全局最优耦合不可行，通常在小批量上迭代求解熵最优传输问题，但该方法计算成本高昂且局部性会扭曲全局传输几何结构。

Method: 提出量化扩散薛定谔桥（QDSB）：1）在锚点量化的端点分布上计算端点耦合；2）通过单元采样将所得规划映射回原始数据点。该方法利用正则化最优耦合对锚点量化的稳定性，其误差由锚点近似质量控制。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，QDSB在样本质量上与现有基线方法相当，但所需训练时间大幅减少。证明了正则化最优耦合相对于锚点量化具有稳定性。

Conclusion: QDSB成功解决了无仿真薛定谔桥训练中耦合计算效率低下的问题，为源域和目标域分布仅通过未配对样本指定的生成模型学习提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning generative models in settings where the source and target distributions are only specified through unpaired samples is gaining in importance. Here, one frequently-used model are Schrödinger bridges (SB), which represent the most likely evolution between both endpoint distributions. To accelerate training, simulation-free SBs avoid the path simulation of the original SB models.
