Conclusion: CAAFC框架成功弥合了现有AFC系统与实际事实核查实践之间的差距，通过提供可操作的纠正和动态知识更新能力，显著提升了自动事实核查的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: With the vast amount of content uploaded every hour, along with the AI generated content that can include hallucinations, Automated Fact-Checking (AFC) has become increasingly vital, as it is infeasible for human fact-checkers to manually verify the sheer volume of information generated online. Professional fact-checkers have identified several gaps in existing AFC systems, noting a misalignment between how these systems operate and how fact-checking is performed in practice. In this paper, we introduce CAAFC (Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker), a frame-work designed to bridge these gaps. It surpasses SOTA AFC and hallucination detection systems across multiple benchmark datasets. CAAFC operates on claims, conversations, and dialogues, enabling it not only to detect factual errors and hallucinations, but also to correct them by providing actionable justifications supported by primary information sources. Furthermore, CAAFC can update evidence and knowledge bases by incorporating recent and contextual information when necessary, thereby enhancing the reliability of fact verification.

</details>


### [341] [Towards Affordable Energy: A Gymnasium Environment for Electric Utility Demand-Response Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12462)
*Jose E. Aguilar Escamilla,Lingdong Zhou,Xiangqi Zhu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了DR-Gym，一个开源的、与Gymnasium兼容的需求响应仿真环境，用于从电力公用事业角度训练和评估需求响应策略，解决了离线历史数据无法捕捉动态交互反馈循环的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气和波动的批发电力市场使住宅消费者面临灾难性财务风险，而配电级需求响应作为电网灵活性和能源可负担性的工具仍未得到充分利用。现有的离线历史智能电表和批发定价数据无法捕捉电力公用事业定价信号与客户接受度和适应度之间的动态、交互式反馈循环，这为强化学习优化需求响应顺序决策过程带来了挑战。

Method: 开发了DR-Gym，一个开源的、与Gymnasium兼容的在线环境，专注于市场级电力公用事业设置，提供丰富的观测空间。该模拟器包含校准至真实世界极端事件的制度切换批发价格模型，以及基于物理的建筑物需求概况。使用可配置的多目标奖励函数作为学习信号，以指定多样化的学习目标。

Result: 通过基线策略和数据快照展示了模拟器创建现实且可学习环境的能力，证明了其在训练和评估需求响应策略方面的有效性。

Conclusion: DR-Gym为需求响应研究提供了一个有效的工具，填补了现有设备级能源模拟器与市场级电力公用事业设置之间的空白，有助于解决离线数据在捕捉动态交互反馈循环方面的局限性。

Abstract: Extreme weather and volatile wholesale electricity markets expose residential consumers to catastrophic financial risks, yet demand response at the distribution level remains an underutilized tool for grid flexibility and energy affordability. While a demand-response program can shield consumers by issuing financial credits during high-price periods, optimizing this sequential decision-making process presents a unique challenge for reinforcement learning despite the plentiful offline historical smart meter and wholesale pricing data available publicly. Offline historical data fails to capture the dynamic, interactive feedback loop between an electric utility's pricing signals and customer acceptance and adaptation to a demand-response program. To address this, we introduce DR-Gym, an open-source, online Gymnasium-compatible environment designed to train and evaluate demand-response from the electric utility's perspective. Unlike existing device-level energy simulators, our environment focuses on the market-level electric utility setting and provides a rich observational space relevant to the electric utility. The simulator additionally features a regime-switching wholesale price model calibrated to real-world extreme events, alongside physics-based building demand profiles. For our learning signal, we use a configurable, multi-objective reward function for specifying diverse learning objectives. We demonstrate through baseline strategies and data snapshots the capability of our simulator to create realistic and learnable environments.

</details>


### [342] [Reward Hacking in Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12474)
*Anas Mahmoud,MohammadHossein Rezaei,Zihao Wang,Anisha Gunjal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于评分细则（rubric）的强化学习中的奖励黑客现象。通过跨模型族评审团进行评估，发现弱验证器会产生无法迁移的代理奖励增益，而更强的验证器虽能显著减少但无法完全消除利用行为。若评分细则未涵盖重要失败模式，即便更强的验证也无法阻止奖励黑客，强调了评分细则设计的核心地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在数学和编程等可验证奖励领域表现优异，但许多开放性任务依赖基于评分细则的奖励。研究者希望探究此类场景下的“奖励黑客”现象——即策略针对训练验证器优化，却在实际评估中失效，以及评分细则设计局限与验证器缺陷如何共同导致这一问题。

Method: 构建了一个分离两种分歧来源的分析框架：验证器失败（训练验证器认可的输出被参考验证器拒绝）与评分细则设计局限（即使是强验证器偏好的输出也被无细则法官评价更低）。在医学和科学领域进行实验，引入“自内化差距”（self-internalization gap）这一无验证器诊断指标，并使用跨模型族的三方前沿评审团进行评估。

Result: 弱验证器产生大量无法迁移到参考验证器的代理奖励增益，利用行为随训练增长并集中于部分满足复合标准、将隐式内容视为显式、主题匹配不精确等反复出现的失败模式。更强的验证器显著减少但未消除验证器利用。当评分细则遗漏重要失败模式时，更强验证无法阻止奖励黑客：基于细则的验证器偏好RL检查点，而无细则法官偏好基础模型；增益集中于完整性和存在性标准，而事实正确性、简洁性、相关性和整体质量下降。

Conclusion: 更强的验证可减少奖励黑客，但本身不能保证评分细则增益对应更广泛的质量提升。奖励黑客的根源不仅在于验证器强度，更在于评分细则是否完备。这提示未来研究需同时关注验证器改进与评分细则设计的系统性完善。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has enabled strong post-training gains in domains such as math and coding, though many open-ended settings rely on rubric-based rewards. We study reward hacking in rubric-based RL, where a policy is optimized against a training verifier but evaluated against a cross-family panel of three frontier judges, reducing dependence on any single evaluator. Our framework separates two sources of divergence: verifier failure, where the training verifier credits rubric criteria that reference verifiers reject, and rubric-design limitations, where even strong rubric-based verifiers favor responses that rubric-free judges rate worse overall. Across medical and science domains, weak verifiers produce large proxy-reward gains that do not transfer to the reference verifiers; exploitation grows over training and concentrates in recurring failures such as partial satisfaction of compound criteria, treating implicit content as explicit, and imprecise topical matching. Stronger verifiers substantially reduce, but do not eliminate, verifier exploitation. We also introduce a self-internalization gap, a verifier-free diagnostic based on policy log-probabilities, which tracks reference-verifier quality, detecting when the policy trained using the weak verifier stops improving. Finally, in our setting, stronger verification does not prevent reward hacking when the rubric leaves important failure modes unspecified: rubric-based verifiers prefer the RL checkpoint, while rubric-free judges prefer the base model. These disagreements coincide with gains concentrated in completeness and presence-based criteria, alongside declines in factual correctness, conciseness, relevance, and overall quality. Together, these results suggest that stronger verification reduces reward hacking, but does not by itself ensure that rubric gains correspond to broader quality gains.

</details>


### [343] [ToolCUA: Towards Optimal GUI-Tool Path Orchestration for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12481)
*Xuhao Hu,Xi Zhang,Haiyang Xu,Kyle Qiao,Jingyi Yang,Xuanjing Huang,Jing Shao,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolCUA智能体，通过合成GUI-工具轨迹和分层训练优化混合动作空间中的路径选择，在OSWorld-MCP上达到46.85%准确率，相对基线提升约66%，实现同规模模型最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUAs）在混合动作空间（原子级GUI操作与高级工具调用）中面临路径选择困境，难以判断何时继续GUI操作或切换至工具调用，导致执行路径次优。这一困难源于高质量交错GUI-工具轨迹稀缺、真实工具轨迹收集成本高且脆弱、以及缺乏轨迹级监督。

Method: 提出三阶段训练范式：1）交错GUI-工具轨迹扩展流程，重用静态GUI轨迹并合成工具库，无需人工标注或真实轨迹收集；2）工具引导的GUI强化微调（RFT），结合预热监督微调（SFT）与单轮强化学习，优化关键GUI-工具切换点的决策；3）在线智能体强化学习，在工具高效路径奖励引导下，于高保真GUI-工具环境中优化，鼓励合理工具使用和缩短执行路径。

Result: 在OSWorld-MCP基准测试中，ToolCUA达到46.85%准确率，相对基线提升约66%，在同规模模型中建立新的最优性能；相比纯GUI设置提升3.9%，验证了GUI-工具协调的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合动作空间训练是现实世界数字智能体的有前景范式，ToolCUA证明了通过合成轨迹和分层强化学习可以有效解决GUI-工具路径选择问题，为混合动作空间智能体设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Computer Use Agents (CUAs) can act through both atomic GUI actions, such as click and type, and high-level tool calls, such as API-based file operations, but this hybrid action space often leaves them uncertain about when to continue with GUI actions or switch to tools, leading to suboptimal execution paths. This difficulty stems from the scarcity of high-quality interleaved GUI-Tool trajectories, the cost and brittleness of collecting real tool trajectories, and the lack of trajectory-level supervision for GUI-Tool path selection. In this paper, we propose ToolCUA, an end-to-end agent designed to learn optimal GUI-Tool path selection through a staged training paradigm. We first introduce an Interleaved GUI-Tool Trajectory Scaling Pipeline that repurposes abundant static GUI trajectories and synthesizes a grounded tool library, enabling diverse GUI-Tool trajectories without manual engineering or real tool-trajectory collection. We then perform Tool-Bootstrapped GUI RFT, combining warmup SFT with single-turn RL to improve decisions at critical GUI-Tool switching points. Finally, we optimize ToolCUA with Online Agentic RL in a high-fidelity GUI-Tool environment, guided by a Tool-Efficient Path Reward that encourages appropriate tool use and shorter execution paths. Experiments on OSWorld-MCP show that ToolCUA achieves 46.85% accuracy, a relative improvement of approximately 66% over the baseline, establishing a new state of the art among models of comparable scale. It also improves by 3.9% over GUI-only settings, demonstrating effective GUI-Tool orchestration. The results further suggest that training in a hybrid action space is a promising paradigm for real-world digital agents. Open-sourced here: https://x-plug.github.io/ToolCUA/

</details>


### [344] [Empowering VLMs for Few-Shot Multimodal Time Series Classification via Tailored Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09395)
*Lin Li,Jiawei Huang,Qihao Quan,Dan Li,Boxin Li,Xiao Zhang,Erli Meng,Wenjie Feng,Jian Lou,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向少样本多模态时间序列分类的视觉语言智能体推理框架MarsTSC，通过自演化知识库和反思性智能体推理，在12个基准上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对少样本多模态时间序列分类中存在的样本稀缺、分布偏移以及模型推理可解释性不足等问题，现有方法难以动态适应新知识且易产生偏见。因此，亟需一种能够自我演化、持续优化知识表征并提供可解释推理决策的框架。

Method: 提出MarsTSC框架，构建包含三个协作智能体角色的自演化知识库：Generator负责基于推理执行可靠分类；Reflector诊断推理错误的根本原因，识别Generator忽略的时间特征；Modifier对知识库进行验证性更新以防止上下文崩塌。同时引入测试时更新策略，实现谨慎、连续的知识库精炼，缓解少样本偏差与分布偏移问题。

Result: 在12个主流时间序列基准上进行广泛实验，MarsTSC在6种视觉语言模型骨干网络上均取得显著且一致的性能提升，在少样本条件下超越经典及基础模型时间序列基线。同时，该方法能够生成可解释的推理依据，将每个分类决策与人类可读的特征证据相关联。

Conclusion: MarsTSC作为首个少样本多模态时间序列分类的视觉语言智能体推理框架，通过反思性智能体机制与动态知识库更新，有效解决了少样本偏差和分布偏移问题，在提升分类性能的同时增强了决策的可解释性，为时间序列分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the first VL$\underline{\textbf{M}}$ $\underline{\textbf{a}}$gentic $\underline{\textbf{r}}$easoning framework for few-$\underline{\textbf{s}}$hot multimodal $\underline{\textbf{T}}$ime $\underline{\textbf{S}}$eries $\underline{\textbf{C}}$lassification ($\textbf{MarsTSC}$), which introduces a self-evolving knowledge bank as a dynamic context iteratively refined via reflective agentic reasoning. The framework comprises three collaborative roles: i) Generator conducts reliable classification via reasoning; ii) Reflector diagnoses the root causes of reasoning errors to yield discriminative insights targeting the temporal features overlooked by Generator; iii) Modifier applies verified updates to the knowledge bank to prevent context collapse. We further introduce a test-time update strategy to enable cautious, continuous knowledge bank refinement to mitigate few-shot bias and distribution shift. Extensive experiments across 12 mainstream time series benchmarks demonstrate that $\textbf{MarsTSC}$ delivers substantial and consistent performance gains across 6 VLM backbones, outperforming both classical and foundation model-based time series baselines under few-shot conditions, while producing interpretable rationales that ground each classification decision in human-readable feature evidence.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [345] [ShardTensor: Domain Parallelism for Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11111)
*Corey Adams,Peter Harrington,Akshay Subramaniam,Mohammad Shoaib Abbas,Jaideep Pathak,Mike Pritchard,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ShardTensor：一种新的域并行范式，通过解耦输入数据空间维度与硬件约束，实现SciML对任意规模极值分辨率数据的并行处理与扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习（SciML）在处理极值分辨率数据时面临独特挑战，现有缓解方案通常无法有效扩展或会降低模型精度。尽管已有专用方法能处理大规模空间数据，但缺乏能在设备批次大小小于1时对输入数据进行并行化的通用框架。

Method: 引入ShardTensor——一种新颖的域并行范式，通过将输入数据的空间维度与硬件约束解耦，实现输入数据向任意尺寸的灵活扩展，从而支持在设备间对输入数据进行细粒度并行化。

Result: 在训练和推理阶段均展示了强扩展性和弱扩展性：强扩展性改善了延迟，弱扩展性提升了可处理的数据规模。此外，实现了多维并行化，验证了框架处理极端规模输入的能力。

Conclusion: ShardTensor成功打破了SciML在极端规模输入上的障碍，为高保真训练和推理提供了可扩展的通用并行化解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) faces unique challenges for extreme-resolution data, with mitigations that often fail to scale or degrade the accuracy of trained models. While some specialized methods have achieved remarkable results in training models or performing inference on massive spatial datasets with bespoke techniques, there is no generalized framework for parallelization over input data below batch size one per device. In this work we introduce ShardTensor: a novel paradigm of domain parallelism that enables flexible scaling of input data to arbitrary sizes. By decoupling the spatial dimensionality of input data from hardware constraints, ShardTensor enables scientific machine learning workloads to reach new levels of high fidelity training and inference. We demonstrate both strong and weak scaling of workloads during training and inference, showing improved latency with strong scaling and demonstrating the capacity to process higher data sizes with weak scaling. Additionally, we demonstrate multiple dimensions of parallelization, removing barriers to SciML on extreme-scale inputs.

</details>


### [346] [ReCoVer: Resilient LLM Pre-Training System via Fault-Tolerant Collective and Versatile Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11215)
*Ziyue Liu,Zhengyang Wang,Ruijie Zhang,Avinash Maurya,Hui Zhou,Paul Hovland,Sheng Di,Franck Cappello,Bogdan Nicolae,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReCoVer，一个针对大语言模型预训练的弹性容错系统，通过保持每次迭代的微批次数量不变，确保故障恢复后的梯度与无故障运行保持随机等价，在512 GPU规模下比检查点重启基线实现2.23倍的有效吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模GPU集群上预训练大型语言模型时，硬件故障已成为常态而非偶发事件，迫切需要弹性训练系统。然而现有框架要么仅针对特定并行方案设计，要么在故障恢复后会偏离无故障训练轨迹，影响模型收敛和训练效率。

Method: 提出ReCoVer系统，基于一个核心不变量：每次迭代保持微批次数量恒定，确保每次迭代的梯度与无故障运行保持随机等价。系统采用三层解耦协议设计：(1)容错集合通信层，隔离故障防止其跨副本传播；(2)步内细粒度恢复层，保留迭代内进度并防止梯度损坏；(3)多功能负载策略层，动态重新分配幸存GPU上的微批次配额。该设计与并行策略无关，可作为底层支撑直接集成到3D并行和HSDP中。

Result: 在多达512 GPU的端到端预训练任务上评估，ReCoVer成功在运行过程中丢失256个GPU的情况下保持与无故障参考一致的训练轨迹。与检查点重启基线相比，ReCoVer在连续故障后实现2.23倍更高的有效吞吐量。在234 GPU小时内，ReCoVer处理了74.9%更多的token，且随着训练时间延长优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: ReCoVer通过创新的容错设计，在不牺牲训练轨迹一致性的前提下实现了高效的故障恢复，显著优于传统的检查点重启方法。该系统的并行策略无关性使其具有良好的通用性，为大规模LLM预训练提供了实用的容错解决方案。

Abstract: Pre-training large language models on massive GPU clusters has made hardware faults routine rather than rare, driving the need for resilient training systems. Yet existing frameworks either focus on specific parallelism schemes or risk drifting away from a failure-free training trajectory. We propose ReCoVer, a resilient LLM pre-training system that upholds a single invariant: each iteration keeps the number of microbatches constant, ensuring per-iteration gradients remain stochastically equivalent to a failure-free run. The framework is organized as three decoupled protocol layers: (1) Fault-tolerant collectives that isolate faults from propagating across replicas; (2) in-step fine-grained recovery that preserves intra-iteration progress and prevents gradient corruption; (3) versatile-workload policy that dynamically redistributes microbatch quotas across the survivors. The design is parallelism-agnostic, integrating directly with both 3D parallelism and Hybrid Sharded Data Parallel (HSDP) as a drop-in substrate. We evaluate our implementation on end-to-end pre-training tasks for up to 512 GPUs, ReCoVer successfully preserves the training trajectory from a failure-free reference despite of 256 GPUs lost spread across the run. For comparison with checkpoint-and-restart baselines, ReCoVer demonstrates $2.23\times$ higher effective throughput after successive failures. This advantage results in ReCoVer processing 74.9% more tokens at 234 GPU-hours, with the gap widening as the training prolongs.

</details>


### [347] [Byzantine Consensus in Directed Graphs with Message Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11309)
*Nitin H. Vaidya,Lewis Tseng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向通信网络中基于消息认证机制（如数字签名）达成共识的问题，给出了同步系统中精确共识和异步系统中近似共识可解的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有向通信网络中，达成共识是一个关键问题。现有研究大多关注无向网络或缺乏消息认证的情况，而实际网络常具有有向性和消息认证能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索在有向图模型下，借助消息认证机制，达成精确共识与近似共识所需的最小网络条件。

Method: 本文采用图论和分布式计算理论相结合的方法。首先假设网络中存在消息认证机制（如数字签名）以验证消息完整性。然后，针对同步系统中的精确共识问题和异步系统中的近似共识问题，分别分析有向通信图的拓扑性质，推导出使问题可解的必要条件和充分条件，并证明这些条件是等价的。

Result: 对于同步系统中的精确共识问题和异步系统中的近似共识问题，本文分别确定了有向通信图需满足的必要且充分条件。这意味着在这些条件下，共识算法可以设计成功；而在条件不满足时，任何算法都无法保证共识达成。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在具备消息认证机制的有向通信网络中，共识问题的可解性完全取决于通信图的拓扑结构。本文给出的充要条件为评估和设计此类网络中的共识算法提供了理论基础。这些结果对理解有向网络的容错能力和共识性能具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider the problem of reaching consensus in communication networks that are modeled by directed graphs. We assume the existence of a message authentication mechanism (such as digital signatures) to verify the integrity of messages. We identify the necessary and sufficient conditions on the directed communication graph for the following problems to be solvable: (i) exact consensus in synchronous systems; and (ii) approximate consensus in asynchronous systems.

</details>


### [348] [MLCommons Chakra: Advancing Performance Benchmarking and Co-design using Standardized Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11333)
*Srinivas Sridharan,Andy Balogh,Bradford M. Beckmann,Brian Coutinho,Louis Feng,Sheng Fu,Sanshan Gao,Mehryar Garakani,Taekyung Heo,David Kanter,Josh Ladd,Ziwei Li,Winston Liu,Changhai Man,Dan Mihailescu,Spandan More,Joongun Park,Ashwin Ramachandran,Vinay Ramakrishnaiah,Saeed Rashidi,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Puneet Sharma,Phio Tian,William Won,Hanjiang Wu,Huan Xu,Jinsun Yoo,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chakra，一个用于分布式AI/ML工作负载性能基准测试和协同设计的开放、可移植生态系统，其核心是基于图的执行追踪（ET）表示，已被MLCommons及多家行业巨头采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能创新步伐快速，迫切需要一种敏捷的方法来观察、复现和优化生产AI系统中分布式机器学习工作负载的行为，并支持未来系统的高效软硬件协同设计。

Method: 提出了Chakra生态系统，核心组件为开放、可互操作的基于图的分布式AI/ML工作负载表示——Chakra执行追踪（ET），涵盖计算、内存、通信等关键操作、数据与控制依赖、时序和资源约束。配套提供工具集，支持ET的采集、分析、生成和采用，适用于广泛的模拟器、仿真器和回放工具。

Result: 在生产AI集群上采集并分析了Chakra ET，通过真实案例研究展示了其价值。Chakra已被MLCommons采纳，并获得NVIDIA、AMD、Meta、Keysight、HPE、Scala等多家企业的积极贡献和参与。

Conclusion: Chakra为分布式AI工作负载的性能基准测试和软硬件协同设计提供了开放、可移植、可互操作的解决方案，已在业界获得广泛采纳和验证。

Abstract: The fast pace of artificial intelligence~(AI) innovation demands an agile methodology for observation, reproduction and optimization of distributed machine learning~(ML) workload behavior in production AI systems and enables efficient software-hardware~(SW-HW) co-design for future systems. We present Chakra, an open and portable ecosystem for performance benchmarking and co-design. The core component of Chakra is an open and interoperable graph-based representation of distributed AI/ML workloads, called Chakra execution trace~(ET). These ETs represent key operations, such as compute, memory, and communication, data and control dependencies, timing, and resource constraints. Additionally, Chakra includes a complementary set of tools and capabilities to enable the collection, analysis, generation, and adoption of Chakra ETs by a broad range of simulators, emulators, and replay tools. We present analysis of Chakra ETs collected on production AI clusters and demonstrate value via real-world case studies. Chakra has been adopted by MLCommons and has active contributions and engagement across the industry, including but not limited to NVIDIA, AMD, Meta, Keysight, HPE, and Scala, to name a few.

</details>


### [349] [ChunkFlow: Communication-Aware Chunked Prefetching for Layerwise Offloading in Distributed Diffusion Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11335)
*Han Meng,Danny Willow Liu,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChunkFlow，一种通信感知的分层卸载运行时，通过自适应调度预取与集合通信，解决了PCIe节点上分层卸载中预取延迟难以隐藏的问题，实现了GPU内存与延迟的可调权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分层卸载技术依赖于将预取延迟隐藏在层计算之后，但在每GPU计算量较小或PCIe节点上预取与集合通信争用带宽时，该假设失效，导致性能下降。

Method: 建立一阶分析模型预测预取延迟能否被计算隐藏；基于该模型设计ChunkFlow运行时，在块粒度上自适应地让步于集合通信，并平滑地权衡GPU内存与预取量。

Result: 在两个通过PCIe连接的H100 GPU上运行三个代表性扩散Transformer的实验显示，ChunkFlow相比SGLang现有分层卸载实现了最高1.28倍的步进时间加速；在计算量足够大时，以几乎无性能损失为代价将峰值GPU内存降低高达49%；在小计算量场景下通过可调的内存-延迟权衡恢复了接近零的步进时间开销。

Conclusion: ChunkFlow通过通信感知的协同调度有效解决了分层卸载在PCIe环境下的局限性，实现了内存占用与推理速度的灵活权衡，提升了大型扩散Transformer在资源受限环境下的推理效率。

Abstract: Layerwise offloading reduces the GPU memory footprint of large diffusion transformer (DiT) inference by prefetching upcoming layers from host memory, but its effectiveness hinges on hiding prefetch latency behind per-layer computation. This assumption breaks down when the per-GPU compute workload is small. Moreover, on PCIe-only nodes, prefetch and inter-GPU collective communications such as all-reduce and all-to-all contend on the shared PCIe path, exposing prefetch latency even when compute would otherwise hide it. We revisit layerwise offloading as a co-scheduling problem between prefetch and communication, guided by a first-order analytical model that predicts when prefetch can be hidden by computation. Building on this model, we design ChunkFlow, a communication-aware, chunk-granular offloading runtime that adaptively yields to collective communication and smoothly trades GPU memory for prefetch volume. On three representative diffusion transformers running on two H100 GPUs over PCIe with Ulysses sequence parallelism, ChunkFlow delivers up to 1.28x step-time speedup over SGLang's existing layerwise offloading, reduces peak GPU memory by up to 49% over the no-offload baseline at near-identical step time once the workload is large enough, and exposes a tunable memory-latency tradeoff that recovers near-zero step-time overhead in the small-workload regime.

</details>


### [350] [GriNNder: Breaking the Memory Capacity Wall in Full-Graph GNN Training with Storage Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11517)
*Jaeyong Song,Seongyeon Park,Hongsun Jang,Jaewon Jung,Hunseong Lim,Junguk Hong,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了GriNNder，首个利用存储设备（NVMe SSD）在有限内存下实现全图GNN训练的系统。通过结构化存储卸载（SSO）框架协调GPU-主机-存储层级，在单GPU上实现了高达9.78倍于现有方法的加速，吞吐量可与分布式系统媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图GNN训练需要多GPU/服务器，硬件与通信成本高昂。现有单服务器方案仍受限于GPU和主机内存容量，无法应对大规模图数据。现代NVMe SSD容量大、带宽高，但直接套用其他领域的存储方法无法解决GNN特有的访问模式与数据依赖问题。

Method: 提出结构化存储卸载（SSO）框架管理GPU-主机-存储层级。具体包含三个核心机制：（i）基于分区感知的主机内存缓存策略，利用跨分区依赖性观察；（ii）针对梯度计算的重收集（regather）策略，消除冗余存储操作；（iii）轻量级图划分方案，降低现有图划分器的内存需求。

Result: 在多种模型和数据集上的实验表明，GriNNder相比最先进基线实现最高9.78倍加速，吞吐量可媲美分布式系统，使此前无法实现的大规模全图训练在单GPU上成为可能。

Conclusion: 通过协调GPU-主机-存储层级并针对性优化GNN的访问模式与数据依赖，GriNNder成功突破了单服务器内存限制，为资源受限环境下的大规模全图GNN训练提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Full-graph training of graph neural networks (GNNs) is widely used as it enables direct validation of algorithmic improvements by preserving complete neighborhood information. However, it typically requires multiple GPUs or servers, incurring substantial hardware and inter-device communication costs. While existing single-server methods reduce infrastructure requirements, they remain constrained by GPU and host memory capacity as graph sizes increase. To address this limitation, we introduce GriNNder, which is the first work to leverage storage devices to enable full-graph training even with limited memory. Because modern NVMe SSDs offer multi-terabyte capacities and bandwidths exceeding 10 GB/s, they provide an appealing option when memory resources are scarce. Yet, directly applying storage-based methods from other domains fails to address the unique access patterns and data dependencies in full-graph GNN training. GriNNder tackles these challenges by structured storage offloading (SSO), a framework that manages the GPU-host-storage hierarchy through coordinated cache, (re)gather, and bypass mechanisms. To realize the framework, we devise (i) a partition-wise caching strategy for host memory that exploits the observation on cross-partition dependencies, (ii) a regathering strategy for gradient computation that eliminates redundant storage operations, and (iii) a lightweight partitioning scheme that mitigates the memory requirements of existing graph partitioners. In experiments performed over various models and datasets, GriNNder achieves up to 9.78x speedup over state-of-the-art baselines and throughput comparable to distributed systems, enabling previously infeasible large-scale full-graph training even on a single GPU.

</details>


### [351] [State Twins: An Off-Chain Substrate for Agentic Reasoning over Decentralized Finance Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11522)
*Ian C. Moore*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了State Twin概念：一个类型化的、内存中、可重放的链上AMM池副本，作为DeFi协议智能体推理的底层支撑。该方案解耦了智能体推理与区块链时间，允许进行分叉、回放、分支和反事实推演等操作，解决了现有Agentic DeFi栈受区块确认延迟和gas限制的结构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic DeFi栈存在结构性问题：每个"假设性"查询都需要新的RPC读取或真实交易，导致智能体的有效行动空间受限于区块确认延迟和gas成本。作者认为这并非性能问题而是架构问题，缺失的是一个能够保留协议精确数学特性同时支持链上状态无法支持的操作（分叉、回放、分支、反事实推演）的链下底层支撑。

Method: 将每个AMM家族（Uniswap V2、V3、Balancer、Stableswap）形式化为离散时间受控动力系统，证明twin与链上状态之间差异的定量保真度界限。实现开源Python工具包DeFiPy v2，包含State Twin底层支撑和参考模型上下文协议服务器，将类型化分析原语作为LLM工具暴露。同一原语（一个Python类、一个调用模式）无需修改即可服务于笔记本量化分析、回测和LLM智能体。

Result: 提供了State Twin的开放架构实现，证明了twin与链上状态之间的定量保真度界限。展示了分叉评估的工作示例：单次实时RPC读取可在亚秒级墙钟时间内生成N个独立的内存twin，用于不同价格冲击场景分析。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于提供了Agentic DeFi底层支撑基质而非特定智能体实现，明确了Agentic DeFi底层支撑应有的规格说明，为解决智能体推理与链时间的结构性耦合问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce the State Twin: a typed, in-memory, replayable replica of an on-chain automated market maker (AMM) pool that serves as a substrate for agentic reasoning over decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols. Agentic DeFi stacks today couple reasoning to chain time, since every "what if?" query incurs a new RPC read or a real transaction, so the agent's effective action space is bounded by block confirmation latency and gas. We argue this coupling is a structural problem rather than a performance one, and that the missing layer is an off-chain substrate that preserves the protocol's exact mathematics while admitting the operations on-chain state cannot: forking, replay, branching, counterfactual rollout. We formalize each AMM family (Uniswap V2, V3, Balancer, Stableswap) as a discrete-time controlled dynamical system, prove a quantitative fidelity bound on the divergence between twin and chain, and give the open architecture used in DeFiPy v2, an open-source Python toolkit that ships the State Twin substrate and a reference Model Context Protocol server exposing typed analytical primitives as LLM tools. The same primitive (i.e., one Python class, one calling pattern) serves a notebook quant, a backtest, and an LLM agent without modification. We close with a fork-and-evaluate worked example: a single live RPC read seeds N independent in-memory twins under distinct price-shock scenarios, in sub-second wall-clock time. The contribution is the substrate, not a particular agent, which is what the specification of what an agentic DeFi substrate must look like

</details>


### [352] [NAVIS: Concurrent Search and Update with Low Position-Seeking Overhead in On-SSD Graph-Based Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11523)
*Jaeyong Song,Hongsun Jang,Changmin Shin,Seongyeon Park,Yong Jae Ryoo,Seo Jin Park,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了NAVIS，一种基于SSD的图向量搜索系统，通过优化位置查找开销，在高维工作负载下显著提升了并发搜索和更新的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于磁盘的图向量搜索（GVS）系统在高维工作负载下难以维持高并发的搜索和更新吞吐量。核心问题在于“位置查找”（position seeking）操作，即每次更新都需要进行完整的图遍历来定位邻居节点。这一操作比搜索查询更耗时，且受到两个系统性限制的放大：(1) 紧凑布局将每次边获取与完整的向量加载耦合；(2) 静态入口图导致入口点随着更新累积逐渐远离新插入区域。

Method: NAVIS通过三项核心技术降低位置查找开销：(1) 布局支持的选择性向量读取，打破紧凑页面的耦合关系同时保留局部性优势；(2) 动态轻量级入口图更新机制，复用并发更新已产生的遍历信息；(3) 入口图感知的边列表缓存，将容量集中在靠近刷新入口点的高复用路径上。

Result: 在多个大规模高维基准测试中，NAVIS将平均插入吞吐量提升高达2.74倍，平均并发搜索吞吐量提升高达1.37倍，同时将平均搜索延迟降低高达25.26%。

Conclusion: NAVIS有效解决了现有GVS系统中位置查找开销过大的问题，通过布局优化、动态入口图更新和智能缓存策略，实现了插入吞吐量和搜索性能的显著提升，为大规模向量数据库的高效服务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: On-disk graph-based vector search (GVS) has become the dominant approach for serving large-scale vector databases at high recall, but prior systems struggle to sustain concurrent search and update throughput on high-dimensional workloads. We find the main cause of this in position seeking, a full graph traversal that every update performs to locate neighbors before linking the new vector into the graph. Position seeking is fundamentally heavier than a search query, and its cost is further amplified by two systemic limitations of current GVS systems, packed layouts that couple every edge fetch to a full vector load, and a static entrance graph whose entry points drift away from newly inserted regions as updates accumulate.
  We present NAVIS, an on-SSD GVS system that drives down position-seeking overhead through (i) a layout-supported selective vector read that breaks the packed-page coupling without losing its locality benefits, (ii) a dynamic lightweight entrance graph update mechanism that reuses traversal information already produced by concurrent updates, and (iii) an entrance graph-aware edgelist cache that concentrates capacity on high-reuse paths near refreshed entry points. Across multiple large-scale high-dimensional benchmarks, NAVIS enhances average insertion throughput by up to 2.74x and average concurrent search throughput by up to 1.37x while reducing average search latency by up to 25.26%.

</details>


### [353] [GraphFlash: Enabling Fast and Elastic Graph Processing on Serverless Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11631)
*Chen Zhao,Parsa Poorsistani,Mohammad Goudarzi,Tawfiq Islam,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GraphFlash是一个基于无服务器基础设施的快速弹性图处理框架，通过子图中心编程模型和系统级优化，在执行时间和资源消耗上相比现有无服务器系统分别提升最多127倍和降低98%，使无服务器图处理变得实用且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图处理系统大多依赖静态配置的集群，在动态工作负载下弹性差且资源利用率低；而无服务器计算虽有自动扩展和细粒度计费优势，但现有无服务器图系统因低效的状态管理和通过外部存储的高通信开销而存在性能限制。

Method: GraphFlash采用子图中心编程模型，利用共享外部存储进行协调和通信，支持无状态的细粒度函数执行。它提供两种执行模式：资源受限环境下的轮转模式和资源充足时的高性能固定模式。针对无服务器限制，引入了分区感知的键聚合、函数内分区协同放置和超步感知激活等系统级优化。

Result: 在多种图算法和数据集上，GraphFlash相比现有兼容无服务器的系统执行时间提升最多127倍，在高资源配置下资源消耗降低最多98%；在资源受限情况下，相比先前无服务器解决方案实现最多48倍加速和99.97%的成本降低，且在大工作负载下性能可媲美传统分布式框架。

Conclusion: GraphFlash通过创新的编程模型和系统优化成功解决了无服务器图处理中的性能瓶颈，证明了无服务器图处理不仅可行，而且在性能和成本效益方面具有显著优势，为大规模图数据分析提供了一个实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph processing systems are essential for analyzing large-scale data with complex relationships, yet most existing frameworks rely on statically provisioned clusters, resulting in poor elasticity and inefficient resource utilization under dynamic workloads. Serverless computing offers automatic scaling and fine-grained billing, but existing serverless graph systems suffer from performance limitations due to inefficient state management and high communication overhead through external storage. We present GraphFlash, a fast and elastic graph processing framework built on serverless infrastructure. GraphFlash adopts a subgraph-centric programming model and leverages shared external storage for coordination and communication, enabling stateless, fine-grained function execution. It supports two execution modes: rotating mode for resource-constrained environments and pinned mode for higher performance when resources are sufficient. To address serverless limitations, GraphFlash introduces system-level optimizations, including partition-aware key aggregation, intra-function partition co-location, and superstep-aware activation. Across multiple graph algorithms and datasets, GraphFlash outperforms existing serverless-compatible systems by up to 127x in execution time and reduces resource consumption by up to 98% under higher-resource configurations, while matching the performance of traditional distributed frameworks on large workloads. Even with limited resources, it achieves up to 48x speedup and 99.97% cost reduction over prior serverless solutions, demonstrating that GraphFlash makes serverless graph processing practical and performant.

</details>


### [354] [Trade-offs in Decentralized Agentic AI Discovery Across the Compute Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11839)
*Patrizio Dazzi,Emanuele Carlini,Matteo Mordacchini,Saul Urso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在共享控制平面框架下比较了Chord、Pastry和Kademlia三种结构化覆盖网络，分析了其在代理发现场景下的可靠性、启动行为和控制平面开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨计算连续体（云、边缘及间歇性连接域）部署的代理系统需要有效的发现机制，而去中心化发现（如DHT）已成为新兴代理架构的设计方向。

Method: 在共享控制平面框架内，以Chord、Pastry和Kademlia为候选索引底座，通过4096节点静态对比基准和代表性4096节点节点变动（churn）基准进行实验评估。

Result: 刻画了三种覆盖网络在发现可靠性、启动行为和控制平面开销方面的差异及其在边缘到云环境中的运行特性。

Conclusion: 明确了各结构化覆盖网络在跨边缘到云的代理发现场景中所暴露的运行特点，为代理发现机制的选择提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Agentic systems deployed across the compute continuum need discovery mechanisms that remain effective across cloud, edge, and intermittently connected domains. In some emerging agentic architectures, decentralized discovery is already an active design direction, placing DHT-based lookup on the path toward agent directories. This paper studies the trade-offs among major structured-overlay families for agent discovery, comparing Chord, Pastry, and Kademlia as candidate indexing substrates within a shared control-plane framework. Using a benchmark subset centered on a 4096-node stationary comparison and a representative 4096-node churn benchmark, the paper characterizes how discovery reliability, startup behavior, and control-plane overhead vary across these overlays. The goal is to clarify the operating points they expose for agent discovery across edge-to-cloud environments.

</details>


### [355] [The Illusion of Power Capping in LLM Decode: A Phase-Aware Energy Characterisation Across Attention Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11999)
*Bole Ma,Ayesha Afzal,Jan Eitzinger,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在LLM服务的主导阶段（自回归解码），GPU功率限制实际上无效，因为内存受限的解码阶段功耗远低于GPU额定功率；通过SM时钟锁定替代功率限制，可节省高达32%的解码能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率限制被视为LLM服务中标准的GPU能耗控制手段，且表面上看似乎有效（吞吐量下降、功率读数降低）。作者旨在揭示这种表面现象背后的真实情况，特别是在主导生产服务的自回归解码阶段。

Method: 在NVIDIA H200 GPU上，对四种注意力范式（GQA、MLA、Gated DeltaNet和Mamba2）进行实验分析。通过识别功率限制在内存受限的解码阶段失效的问题，提出SM时钟锁定方法，并识别三种架构相关的DVFS行为类别。

Result: 解码阶段仅消耗137-300W（GPU额定700W），功率限制从未触发；固件启动的时钟节流会干扰测量。SM时钟锁定的方法普遍优于功率限制，可在极小吞吐量损失下恢复高达32%的解码能耗；新型注意力机制在生产批次大小下可将总请求能耗相对GQA减半。

Conclusion: 功率限制对LLM解码阶段无效，因为内存带宽饱和而非计算成为瓶颈；SM时钟锁定是更优的能耗控制策略；新型注意力架构呈现"预填充高成本、解码高效率"的能耗模式。

Abstract: Power capping is the standard GPU energy lever in LLM serving, and it appears to work: throughput drops, power readings fall, and energy budgets are met. We show the appearance is illusory for the phase that dominates production serving: autoregressive decode. Across four attention paradigms -- GQA, MLA, Gated DeltaNet, and Mamba2 -- on NVIDIA H200, decode draws only 137--300\,W on a 700\,W GPU; no cap ever triggers, because memory-bound decode saturates HBM bandwidth rather than compute and leaves power headroom untouched. Firmware-initiated clock throttling compounds the illusion: these deviations can corrupt any throughput measurement that attributes them to the cap. SM clock locking dissolves both confounds. By targeting the lever that is actually on the critical path, clock locking Pareto-dominates power capping universally, recovering up to 32\% of decode energy at minimal throughput loss. We identify three architecture-dependent DVFS behavioural classes and characterise a common energy pattern across novel attention replacements: a heavy prefill cost recouped by efficient decode, eventually halving total request energy relative to GQA at production batch sizes.

</details>


### [356] [AB-Sparse: Sparse Attention with Adaptive Block Size for Accurate and Efficient Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12110)
*Di Liu,Ruitian Wang,Chen Chen,Mingliang Gong,Yongjie Yuan,Han Zhao,Yu Feng,Quan Chen,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AB-Sparse框架，通过自适应的注意力头块大小分配和无损块中心点量化，解决了长上下文大模型中KV cache加载的瓶颈问题，在保持吞吐量的同时将准确率提升了5.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型上下文长度增加，KV cache加载成为注意力计算的关键瓶颈。现有块稀疏注意力方法对所有注意力头采用统一块大小，隐含假设模型行为同质化。本文分析发现该假设存在缺陷：不同注意力头对块粒度的敏感度差异巨大，统一块大小会导致准确率次优。

Method: 提出AB-Sparse训练无关的算法-系统协同设计框架。核心方法包括：(1) 跨注意力头的轻量级自适应块大小分配以提高准确率；(2) 无损块中心点量化以补偿额外内存开销；(3) 开发定制GPU内核支持可变块大小的高效执行。

Result: 实验结果表明，AB-Sparse在无吞吐量开销的情况下，相比现有块稀疏注意力基线实现了高达5.43%的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 证明了注意力头具有异质性特征，自适应块大小分配优于统一分配方案。AB-Sparse成功实现了准确率与吞吐量的平衡，为长上下文大语言模型的注意力优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models scale to longer contexts, loading the growing KV cache during attention computation becomes a critical bottleneck. Previous work has shown that attention computation is dominated by a small subset of tokens. This motivates block sparse attention methods that partition the KV cache into fixed-size blocks and selectively compute attention over those blocks exhibiting high importance. However, these methods assign a uniform block size across all attention heads, implicitly assuming homogeneous behavior throughout the model. Our analysis reveals that this assumption is flawed: attention heads exhibit widely varying sensitivity to block granularity, and uniformity leads to suboptimal accuracy. We present AB-Sparse, a training-free algorithm-system co-designed framework that improves accuracy while preserving throughput. AB-Sparse introduces lightweight adaptive block size allocation across attention heads to improve accuracy. To compensate for the additional memory overhead, it further employs lossless block centroid quantization. In addition, custom GPU kernels are developed to support efficient execution with variable block sizes. Evaluation results demonstrate that AB-Sparse achieves an accuracy improvement of up to 5.43% over existing block sparse attention baselines without throughput overhead.

</details>


### [357] [NCCLZ: Compression-Enabled GPU Collectives with Decoupled Quantization and Entropy Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12396)
*Jiamin Wang,Zhijing Ye,Xiaodong Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出NCCLZ，一种支持压缩的GPU集合通信库，通过分层解耦量化和熵编码实现最高9.65倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多节点GPU科学计算和分布式深度学习应用中，集合通信是主要性能瓶颈，尤其是节点间带宽受限且大消息传输时。现有压缩方案依赖MPI栈、省略熵编码或紧耦合压缩器，限制了压缩率、灵活性和通信计算重叠。

Method: 提出NCCLZ，将量化置于接口层，将熵编码嵌入NCCL原语，使用轻量级设备端选择器选择编码策略，并通过重叠压缩与通信来减少开销。

Result: 在科学数据集、训练梯度和合成工作负载上的实验表明，相比NCCL实现高达9.65倍加速，相比现有压缩辅助集合通信库实现高达3.34倍提升。

Conclusion: 通过分层解耦量化和熵编码并深度集成到NCCL中，有效解决了大消息集合通信的性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Collective communication is a major bottleneck for multi-node GPU workloads in scientific computing and distributed deep learning, especially when inter-node bandwidth is limited. Although NCCL provides optimized GPU-centric collectives, large messages can still dominate end-to-end performance. Existing compression-enabled collective libraries either rely on MPI-based stacks that cannot fully exploit NCCL, omit entropy coding, or tightly couple full compressors with communication primitives, limiting compression ratio, flexibility, and communication-computation overlap. This paper presents NCCLZ, a compression-enabled GPU collectives that decouples quantization and entropy coding and integrates them at different layers of the stack. NCCLZ places quantization at the interface, embeds entropy coding into NCCL primitives, uses a lightweight device-side selector to choose coding strategies, and overlaps compression with communication to reduce exposed overhead. Experiments on scientific datasets, training gradients, and synthetic workloads show up to 9.65x speedup over NCCL and up to 3.34x improvement over prior compression-assisted collective libraries.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [358] [Agents Should Replace Narrow Predictive AI as the Orchestrator in 6G AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11516)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模态大语言模型（LLMs）或领域适配的大型电信模型（LTMs）作为AI-RAN的认知操作系统，以实现第5级自主6G网络，解决现有狭窄模型的知识孤岛和意图鸿沟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI-RAN架构依赖分散的深度神经网络（DNNs）和深度强化学习（DRL）智能体，这些狭窄模型存在知识孤立、对分布外动态严重脆弱、无法弥合高层非结构化操作指令与刚性数值网络配置之间的语义鸿沟（即意图鸿沟）等根本性缺陷。

Method: 将LLMs/LTMs定位为RAN智能控制器（RIC）内的认知操作系统，使其协调而非替代现有狭窄模型，将人类意图动态转化为具体策略，并利用检索增强生成（RAG）自主诊断复杂的多供应商网络异常。

Result: 提出了一个将LLMs作为认知操作系统的AI-RAN架构框架，明确了实现这一架构转变所需的关键基础研究方向：通过网络驱动反馈的持续对齐（RLNF）、极低至8位以下的边缘量化、神经符号验证以遏制幻觉，以及防御对抗性提示注入的安全编排框架。

Conclusion: 为实现第5级自主6G网络，机器学习社区需优先开展针对电信严格约束的基础研究，特别是模型对齐、量化压缩、验证机制和安全防护四个关键领域，以支持LLMs在电信网络中的实际部署。

Abstract: This position paper argues that to achieve Level 5 autonomous 6G networks, the next generation of Artificial Intelligence in Radio Access Networks (AI-RAN) should transition away from fragmented, narrow predictive models and instead adopt multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) as central reasoning agents. Current AI-RAN architectures rely on disjointed Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) agents that operate in isolated domains. These narrow models suffer from siloed knowledge, severe brittleness to out-of-distribution dynamics, and a fundamental inability to bridge the intent gap the semantic disconnect between high-level, unstructured operator directives and rigid numerical network configurations. We propose elevating LLMs, or domain-adapted Large Telecom Models (LTMs), to act as the cognitive operating system situated within the RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC), the control and orchestration layer of AI-RAN. In this architecture, LLMs do not replace narrow models but orchestrate them as executable subroutines, dynamically translating human intent into concrete policies and utilizing Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to autonomously diagnose complex, multi-vendor network anomalies. To make this architectural shift a reality, we call upon the machine learning community to prioritize critical foundational research tailored to the strict constraints of telecommunications, specifically focusing on continuous alignment via network-driven feedback (RLNF), extreme sub-8-bit edge quantization, neuro-symbolic verification to curb hallucinations, and securing orchestration frameworks against adversarial prompt injections.

</details>


### [359] [Avoiding Cross-Datacenter Collective Congestion via Disaggregated Buffering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11852)
*Mariano Scazzariello,Noga H. Rotman,Dima Gavrilenko,Sajy Khashab,Alexander Shpiner,Matty Kadosh,Marco Chiesa,Dejan Kostic,Mark Silberstein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Spillway机制，通过交换机分离缓冲区缓存丢弃数据包，解决跨数据中心LLM训练中集合通信与数据中心内流量冲突导致的拥塞崩溃问题，迭代时间最高减少14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数万GPU规模的LLM训练场景下，跨数据中心的集合通信不可避免。现有拥塞控制环路响应时间为数毫秒，无法及时处理跨数据中心集合通信与目的数据中心内部流量冲突引发的严重丢包和拥塞崩溃，导致性能严重下降。

Method: 设计透明的网络内机制Spillway，在目的数据中心的交换机分离缓冲区中缓存被丢弃的数据包，待拥塞缓解后进行排出。该机制无需修改终端主机或训练框架，对上层应用透明。

Result: 大规模端到端仿真和硬件原型实验表明，Spillway能够消除集合通信冲突导致的性能下降，将迭代时间降低最高14%，有效避免拥塞崩溃。

Conclusion: Spillway为跨数据中心LLM训练中集合通信冲突问题提供了一种高效、透明的网络层解决方案，无需修改现有训练框架和主机配置即可显著提升性能。

Abstract: LLM training at the scale of tens of thousands of GPUs now spans multiple datacenters (DC), making cross-DC collectives over long-haul links unavoidable. A critical and overlooked bottleneck arises when these collectives collide with intra-DC traffic at the destination - a common pattern in real workloads. The multi-millisecond congestion control loop is too slow to react, triggering severe packet loss and congestion collapse.
  We present Spillway, a transparent in-network mechanism that buffers dropped packets in switch-disaggregated buffers in a destination data center and drains them once congestion subsides. Through large-scale end-to-end simulations and a hardware prototype, we show that Spillway eliminates performance degradation from collective collisions, reducing iteration time by up to 14 %, without changes to end hosts or training frameworks.

</details>


### [360] [Energy Consumption in Next Generation Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11899)
*Urooj Tariq,Rishu Raj,Merim Dzaferagic,Daniel Kilper*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过基于事务的能耗模型分析了下一代RAN架构，发现处理能耗占主导地位，且基带处理(BBP)的部署位置显著影响整体能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线接入网(RAN)是移动网络中能耗最大的部分，随着未来网络向更高密化方向发展，理解其能耗方式和来源至关重要。O-RAN作为一种支持多厂商环境、分层智能控制和大规模部署的解决方案应运而生，因此需要研究下一代RAN架构中的能耗特性以指导未来网络设计。

Method: 采用基于事务的能耗模型(transaction-based energy models)，该模型包含处理和传输两个能耗组件。通过评估基带处理(BBP)在不同网络节点间的部署位置以及不同网络密化程度下的能耗变化来进行分析。

Result: 研究结果表明：处理能耗在总能耗中占主导地位；基带处理(BBP)的位置对整体能效有强烈影响；这些发现可为平衡灵活性、成本和可持续性的未来RAN部署设计提供指导。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来RAN部署设计提供了重要见解，有助于在网络架构设计中平衡灵活性、成本和可持续性，推动绿色通信网络的发展。

Abstract: The radio access network (RAN) accounts for the largest share of energy consumption in mobile networks, making it essential to understand how and where this energy is used, particularly as future networks move toward higher levels of densification. Open radio access networks (O-RAN) have emerged as a promising approach to support this evolution through open interfaces that enable a multivendor environment, support for hierarchical intelligent controls, and simplified, cost-effective radio units that facilitate large-scale deployments. This paper examines the energy consumption in next-generation RAN architectures through transaction-based energy models. The model captures both processing and transmission energy components and evaluates how energy use varies with the placement of baseband processing (BBP) across network nodes and with different levels of network densification. Results indicate that processing energy dominates total consumption and that the location of BBP strongly influences overall energy efficiency. These insights can inform the design of future RAN deployments that balance flexibility, cost, and sustainability.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [361] [Sieve: Dynamic Expert-Aware PIM Acceleration for Evolving Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11277)
*Jungwoo Kim,Rubens Lacouture,Genghan Zhang,Gina Sohn,Qizheng Zhang,Swapnil Gandhi,Christos Kozyrakis,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sieve框架，通过运行时调度器动态划分专家执行位置（GPU vs PIM），解决了现代MoE模型双峰分布导致的现有PIM系统效率下降问题，实现了1.3x-1.6x的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MoE模型呈现出日益明显的双峰专家分布特征（少数专家处理大量token，多数专家处理极少token），导致算术强度差异巨大。现有PIM系统采用静态规则卸载内存受限操作，未考虑负载不均衡和GPU间通信的综合影响，从而显著降低了系统效率。

Method: 提出一个运行时调度器，基于token-to-expert的实时分布，联合考虑互连开销、内存带宽、GPU吞吐量和PIM吞吐量，动态划分专家在GPU和PIM之间的执行位置。在此基础上构建Sieve运行时框架，在保持专家并行性所要求的跨设备依赖关系的同时，重叠GPU计算、PIM计算以及设备内/设备间通信。

Result: 在基于Ramulator 2.0的周期精确模拟器上进行评估，与最先进的MoE PIM系统相比，Sieve在Qwen3.5-397B-A17B、GPT-OSS-120B和Qwen3-30B-A3B模型上分别实现了1.3x、1.3x和1.6x的吞吐量与交互性提升。

Conclusion: 针对MoE模型的双峰分布特性，动态的、感知运行时负载的GPU-PIM协同调度策略能够显著优于静态卸载方案，有效提升大规模MoE模型的推理吞吐量和交互性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a dominant architecture for scaling large language models (LLMs). However, the execution characteristics of MoE inference are changing rapidly and increasingly mismatch the assumptions underlying existing Processing-in-Memory (PIM) systems. Prior PIM systems for LLMs rely on static rules to offload memory-bound operations to PIM, without accounting for the combined effects of load imbalance and inter-GPU communication. Meanwhile, modern MoE models activate fewer experts out of increasingly many, creating a bimodal expert distribution: a small set of experts receives many tokens, while a long tail of experts receives only one or a few.
  We identify a trend in modern MoE models toward increasingly bimodal token-to-expert distributions, quantify the resulting disparity in arithmetic intensity across experts, and show that this disparity dramatically reduces the efficiency of state-of-the-art PIM systems for LLMs. To address this problem, we propose a scheduler for serving MoE models on multi-GPU systems with attached HBM-PIM stacks. Our scheduler partitions expert execution between GPU and PIM based on runtime token-to-expert distributions, while jointly considering interconnect overhead, memory bandwidth, GPU throughput, and PIM throughput. Moreover, we propose Sieve, a runtime framework that employs the scheduler to coordinate execution across GPUs and their attached HBM-PIM stacks. Sieve overlaps GPU computation, PIM computation, and intra- and inter-device communication while preserving cross-device dependencies induced by expert parallelism. Sieve is evaluated on our cycle-accurate simulator based on Ramulator 2.0. Compared to state-of-the-art PIM systems for MoE, Sieve improves both throughput and interactivity by 1.3x, 1.3x, and 1.6x on Qwen3.5-397B-A17B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Qwen3-30B-A3B, respectively.

</details>


### [362] [Heterogeneous SoC Integrating an Open-Source Recurrent SNN Accelerator for Neuromorphic Edge Computing on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12217)
*Michelangelo Barocci,Vittorio Fra,Enrico Macii,Gianvito Urgese*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构SoC设计，将ReckOn脉冲神经网络加速器与RISC-V微控制器（X-HEEP）及ARM处理器集成，在FPGA上实现了低成本的神经形态系统，验证了分类准确性与在线学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在能效和并行计算方面具有优势，但专用数字神经形态硬件的流片成本高昂，限制了其普及；FPGA作为灵活且具成本效益的替代平台，可促进开源硬件设计的发展。

Method: 设计了一个异构片上系统（SoC），将ReckOn循环SNN加速器与传统处理器集成，包括开源RISC-V微控制器X-HEEP和Zynq Ultrascale系统中的ARM处理器，并在FPGA上实现以验证与流片版本的等价性。

Result: 通过FPGA实现成功复现了流片版ReckOn的分类结果，验证了准确性和物理实现特性的等价性；在Braille数字数据集上评估了在线学习能力，展示了系统在神经形态任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于FPGA的异构SoC方案为数字神经形态系统提供了一种成本效益高且灵活的实现途径，有助于推动开源神经形态硬件的普及与应用。

Abstract: The growing popularity of Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) and their applications has led to a significant fast-paced increase of neuromorphic architectures capable of mimicking the spike-based data processing typical of biological neurons. The efficient power consumption and parallel computing capabilities of the SNNs lead researchers towards the development of digital accelerators, which exploit such features to bring fast and low-power computation on edge devices. The spread of digital neuromorphic hardware however is slowed down by the prohibitive costs that the silicon tape out of circuits brings, that's why targeting Field Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs) could represent a viable alternative, offering a flexible and cost-effective platform for implementing digital neuromorphic systems and helping the spread of open-source hardware designs. In this work we present an heterogeneous System-on-Chip (SoC) where the operations of ReckOn, a Recurrent SNN accelerator, are managed through the integration with traditional processors. These include the RISC-V-based, open-source microcontroller X-HEEP and the ARM processor featured in Zynq Ultrascale systems. We validate our design by reproducing the classification results through the implementation on FPGA of the taped-out version of ReckOn in order to check the equivalence of the accuracy and the characteristics in terms of physical implementation. In a second set of experiments, we evaluate the online learning capability of the solution in classifying a subset of the Braille digit dataset recently used to compare neuromorphic frameworks and platforms.

</details>


### [363] [Enhancing Instruction Prefetching via Cache and TLB Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12433)
*Alexandre Valentin Jamet,Georgios Vavouliotis,Marti Torrents,Dimitrios Chasapis,Marc Casas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出IP-CaT框架，通过联合优化TLB和缓存管理来提升L1I预取效率，在服务器负载上实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代服务器负载具有巨大的指令足迹，严重压力处理器前端，L1指令缓存(L1I)预取对维持性能至关重要。然而，现有L1I预取器存在两个关键限制：(1)跨页边界的L1I预取需要在发射前进行地址转换，转换延迟降低了预取时效性；(2)L1I预取获取的代码行重用行为高度异构，部分行被多次重用，而另一些则是到达即失效。

Method: 提出指令预取中心缓存和TLB管理(IP-CaT)框架，包含两个组件：(1)转换预取缓冲区(tPB)——与二级TLB(sTLB)共置的小型结构，存储跨页L1I预取获取的页表项，减少转换开销；(2)三模态指令预取替换策略(TIPRP)——基于决策树的L2缓存替换策略，专门针对L1I预取获取的缓存行。

Result: 在105个当代服务器负载上评估IP-CaT与三个先进L1I预取器(EPI、FNL+MMA、Barca)的组合效果。IP-CaT+EPI相比单独EPI实现8.7%的几何平均加速。IP-CaT优于先进的指令TLB预取、先进TLB替换(CHiRP)，以及先进的代码感知、预取感知和通用缓存替换策略(包括Emissary、SHiP++和Mockingjay)。

Conclusion: IP-CaT是首个联合优化TLB和缓存管理以提升L1I预取的微架构框架，有效解决了跨页预取的地址转换延迟和预取行异构重用问题，在现代服务器负载上实现了一致且显著的性能改进。

Abstract: Modern server workloads exhibit massive instruction footprints that heavily pressure the processor front-end, making L1 instruction (L1I) prefetching critical for sustaining performance. However, this paper shows that current L1I prefetchers fail to reach their full potential due to two key limitations. First, L1I prefetches crossing page boundaries require address translation before issuance, and translation latency reduces prefetch timeliness. Second, the reuse behavior of code lines fetched by L1I prefetches is highly heterogeneous: while some lines are reused many times, others are dead-on-arrival.
  This paper introduces Instruction Prefetch-Centric Cache and TLB Management (IP-CaT), the first microarchitectural framework jointly optimizing TLB and cache management for L1I prefetching. IP-CaT consists of two components: (i) the translation Prefetch Buffer (tPB), a small structure colocated with the second-level TLB (sTLB) that stores page table entries fetched by page-crossing L1I prefetches, reducing translation overheads; and (ii) the Trimodal Instruction Prefetch Replacement Policy (TIPRP), a decision-tree-based L2 cache replacement policy specialized for lines fetched by L1I prefetches.
  We evaluate IP-CaT with three state-of-the-art L1I prefetchers: EPI, FNL+MMA, and Barca. Across 105 contemporary server workloads, IP-CaT consistently improves performance. For example, IP-CaT+EPI achieves an 8.7% geomean speedup over EPI alone. We further show that IP-CaT outperforms state-of-the-art instruction TLB prefetching, advanced TLB replacement (CHiRP), and state-of-the-art code-aware, prefetch-aware, and general-purpose cache replacement policies, including Emissary, SHiP++, and Mockingjay.

</details>
